### [1] [Anchored Inertial Proposals with Objective Verification for Diffusion-Prior Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25551)
*Minwoo Kim,Seunghyeok Shin,Dabin Kim,Hongki Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种"锚定惯性校正"规则，将无训练扩散先验逆问题中的测量引导拆分为"提议生成"与"接受验证"两阶段，在不增加去噪器调用的情况下提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无训练扩散先验在逆问题中很强，但反向采样中的测量引导是局部性的：激进更新虽改善即时数据拟合，却会破坏后续去噪过程。作者希望缓解这一"即时拟合与长期去噪"之间的矛盾。

Method: 在每个校正步上，以去噪器预测为锚点冻结一个干净空间目标函数，由测量拟合项与依赖噪声级的近端项组成；用由测量曲率确定步长与动量的重球（heavy-ball）动力学生成干净状态候选；再由独立的验证器用同一目标评估候选，若未通过则以去噪器预测为回退。两阶段源自同一条整轨迹目标（逐步考虑），被接受的干净状态重新加噪以继续采样。方法适用于像素与潜空间扩散，需可微测量算子，无需重训练，且校正循环内不额外调用去噪器。

Result: 理论分析了目标验证可带来局部非增性与位移控制；在自然图像基准上取得有竞争力的重建质量。

Conclusion: 将引导更新拆为"提议—验证"并以统一目标约束，可在不增加计算开销（去噪器调用）的前提下稳定无训练扩散逆问题采样，兼顾即时拟合与后续去噪。

Abstract: Training-free diffusion priors are powerful for inverse problems, but measurement guidance during reverse sampling is local: aggressive updates can improve immediate data fit while disrupting later denoising. We introduce an anchored inertial correction rule that separates proposal generation from acceptance. At each corrected step, the denoiser prediction anchors a frozen clean-space objective combining measurement fit with a noise-level-dependent proximal term. Heavy-ball dynamics with step size and momentum computed from the measured curvature generate a clean-state candidate; a separate verifier evaluates it with the same objective, with the denoiser prediction retained as fallback; both stages are derived from one objective for the whole trajectory, considered one step at a time. The accepted clean state is re-noised to continue sampling. The rule applies to pixel and latent diffusion with differentiable measurement operators, requires no retraining, and adds no denoiser calls inside the correction loop. We analyze how objective verification yields local nonincrease and displacement control. Across natural-image benchmarks, the method achieves competitive reconstruction quality. Code is available at https://github.com/Seunghyeok0715/PIVOT2026.

</details>


### [2] [Contrast Where It Matters: Visual Evidence-Guided Decoding for Mitigating Hallucinations in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25524)
*Yiheng Li,Wenhao Wang,Xingyi Li,Mingda Jia,Zichang Tan,Yinhang Tang,Zhen Lei,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEGD：一种免训练的对比解码框架，先定位与查询相关的视觉证据，并在解码全程引导主/辅两支使用同一证据位置，从而抑制LVLM幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM幻觉源于语言先验压过视觉证据，或预测依赖无关/误导性图像区域；现有免训练对比解码只改动辅助输入或计算方式，无法保证任一支在解码时使用与查询相关的视觉证据。

Method: 1) 聚合具有强视觉响应且空间结构一致的定位头，定位查询相关视觉证据；2) 在辅助图像中选择性削弱该证据；3) 生成时把两支的"视觉载体头"引导至同一证据位置——主支强化完整证据、辅支锚定削弱版本；4) 依据分布散度自适应融合/对比两支预测。

Result: 在多种LVLM与多类基准上，VEGD一致性地降低幻觉。

Conclusion: 通过显式识别并全程引导查询相关视觉证据的使用，VEGD在无需训练的前提下有效缓解LVLM幻觉，且具备跨模型、跨基准的通用性。

Abstract: Despite strong multimodal capabilities, large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate when linguistic priors outweigh visual evidence or when predictions are grounded in irrelevant or misleading image regions. Training-free contrastive decoding mitigates hallucinations by contrasting the original prediction with an auxiliary one that exposes linguistic priors. Yet existing methods focus on altering the auxiliary input or computation and do not ensure that either branch uses query-relevant visual evidence during decoding. We propose Visual Evidence-Guided Decoding (VEGD), a training-free framework that identifies query-relevant visual evidence and guides its use throughout contrastive decoding. VEGD locates this evidence by aggregating localization heads with strong visual responses and coherent spatial structure, then selectively weakens it in the auxiliary image. During generation, VEGD directs visual-carrier heads in both branches toward the same evidence location, strengthening access to intact evidence in the main branch while anchoring the auxiliary branch to its weakened counterpart. An adaptive distribution fusion strategy then combines or contrasts their predictions according to distribution divergence. Experiments across diverse LVLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that VEGD consistently reduces hallucinations.

</details>


### [3] [Mesh-Free Keypoint Learning for Category-Level 6D Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25513)
*Junghoon Seo,Hakjin Lee,Jaehoon Sim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MORSEPose 直接用标准位姿与尺寸标注构造“位姿-尺寸包络”来筛选物体支撑点，替代传统的到配准表面距离选点方式，在多级别 RGB-D 特征与自适应关键点上取得与表面监督相当的类别级 6D 位姿精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级 6D 位姿估计需要将未见实例的部分 RGB-D 观测映射到类别共享的规范帧，物体感知重建可帮助自适应关键点保留可见表面几何。但常见重建目标依赖“到配准实例表面的距离”来选点，该方式依赖高质量实例表面配准，构造不一致且对位姿/尺度误差敏感。

Method: 将观测 3D 点映射到归一化物体坐标（NOCS），保留落在带容差的“位姿-尺寸包络”内的点作为重建支撑；该包络直接由标准位姿与度量尺寸标注导出，跨相机帧和度量尺度保持一致；并将其与多级别 RGB-D 特征、自适应关键点及可学习的类别上下文相融合。

Result: 在 REAL275 与 HouseCat6D 上，包络监督在点级与任务级均与基于表面的监督高度吻合；在边界扰动与视角变化下保持稳定；类别级位姿精度在各基准上具有竞争力。理论上，当标注范围包住物体表面时，包络保守地包含传统表面距离选择器的支撑集。

Conclusion: 用位姿-尺寸包络替代表面距离选点，可在不依赖实例表面配准的前提下提供一致、鲁棒的物体感知重建监督，是一种简洁且可跨帧、跨尺度泛化的类别级 6D 位姿估计方案。

Abstract: Category-level 6D pose estimation relates partial RGB-D observations of unseen instances to a canonical frame shared across a category. Object-aware reconstruction helps adaptive keypoints retain the geometry of the visible surface. Common reconstruction targets select observed points by their distance to a registered instance surface. We present MORSEPose, which derives this support directly from standard pose and metric-size annotations. We map observed 3D points to normalized object coordinates and retain those inside a tolerancedilated pose-and-size envelope. This construction is consistent across camera frames and metric scales. When the annotated extent bounds the object surface, the envelope conservatively contains the support of a conventional surface-distance selector. We integrate this target with multi-level RGB-D features, adaptive keypoints, and learnable category context. Experiments on REAL275 and HouseCat6D show that the envelope closely matches surface-based supervision at both point and task levels, remains stable under boundary perturbations and viewpoint changes, and supports competitive category-level pose accuracy across benchmarks.

</details>


### [4] [Carry the Fine Print: Budget-Aware Visual Token Compression for Document OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25506)
*Yixuan Huang,ShengFan Zhang,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"选择与补偿"(Select and Compensate)两阶段框架：在冻结OCR骨干的前提下，用自适应分辨率+全局重要性+局部对比选锚点，再用邻近被丢弃内容及其相对位置补偿锚点，半量视觉token下达到接近无损的整页OCR效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉token压缩通常把显著性(salience)或相似性当作转录重要性的代理，但完整文档OCR打破这一假设：小符号与重复条目视觉上不显眼却对转录不可或缺，因此需重新设计压缩准则，将"选择哪些token"与"该token需承载哪些证据"解耦。

Method: 两阶段框架。选择阶段：结合自适应编码分辨率、全局重要性与局部对比度决定保留哪些token作为锚点(anchors)。补偿阶段：在不增加token数的前提下，用邻近被丢弃内容及其相对位置信息增强锚点。OCR骨干保持冻结，原生坐标与结构token均保留。在OmniDocBench五类骨干上评测整页转录，在MPDocBench上评测文档重建，并与降低输入分辨率、学习式全局选择器及RTPrune/DivPrune/HiPrune等适配方法对比。

Result: 半量视觉token下，输入自适应配置在Ovis上达95.42 OmniDocBench Overall（未压缩96.09）；配对评测显示在两个基准上均优于单纯降分辨率。原输入网格下，75%保留率时与未压缩模型差距仅0.17分，25%保留率时超出学习式全局选择器4.70分。固定200页Ovis负载、50%预算下仅3个输出触及长度上限（RTPrune/DivPrune/HiPrune适配为30–34个），批处理完成快35–40%。

Conclusion: 在完整文档OCR场景中，"选择"与"补偿"解耦可在激进压缩下保持高保真转录，并显著降低输出长度溢出与推理时延，验证了以转录重要性而非视觉显著性为压缩依据的有效性。

Abstract: Visual token compression often treats salience or similarity as a proxy for transcription importance. Complete document OCR breaks this assumption: small symbols and repeated entries can be indispensable to the transcript despite being visually inconspicuous. We present Select and Compensate, a two-stage framework built on a simple idea: choosing where to retain a token need not determine all the evidence it carries. Selection combines adaptive encoding resolution with global importance and local contrast to choose which tokens to retain as anchors. Compensation enriches these anchors with nearby discarded content and its relative position, without adding tokens. The OCR backbone remains frozen, and native coordinates and structural tokens are preserved. We evaluate complete page transcription across five backbones on OmniDocBench and document reconstruction on MPDocBench. With half the original visual tokens, our input-adapted configuration reaches 95.42 OmniDocBench Overall on Ovis versus 96.09 for the uncompressed model, and paired evaluations show gains over simply reducing the input resolution on both benchmarks. With the original input grid, our method stays within 0.17 points of the uncompressed model at 75% retention and exceeds a learned global selector by 4.70 points at 25%. On a fixed 200-page Ovis workload at the same 50% visual-token budget, only 3 of its outputs hit the length limit, versus 30-34 for the evaluated RTPrune, DivPrune, and HiPrune adaptations, and it finishes the batch 35-40% sooner. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [5] [Real Cameras are Radiometric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25265)
*Ties Harder,Felix Seegräber,David Nakath,Hendrik Sauer,Kevin Köser,James Pritts*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"辐射度相机"模型，将任意中心几何相机模型提升为辐射度模型，保留原有投影并加入有限孔径、自由形式对焦面和通用渐晕函数；通过逆渲染标定，在多视图辐射度重建中联合估计焦点面与渐晕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中心几何相机模型仅描述投影几何，无法刻画孔径、对焦与渐晕等辐射度现象；而几何标定依赖特征检测，特征偏差会引入误差。作者希望用统一的辐射度模型直接扩展多视图几何到多视图辐射度。

Method: 将任意中心几何相机模型"提升"为辐射度相机：保持其投影，增加有限孔径、自由形式对焦面与通用渐晕函数。标定采用逆渲染，直接在传感器相对辐照度上进行，且复用几何标定所用的标准标靶图像；该方法不使用特征点。对焦面因此可将散焦建模从薄透镜推广到任意中心相机，并在首个多视图辐射度重建中用于恢复纹理的聚焦反射率；渐晕被联合估计。

Result: 在留出测试集上辐射度相机重投影误差低于几何标定（后者吸收特征检测偏差）；联合估计的渐晕能准确复现经漫射器测得的平场；多视图辐射度重建可从散焦图像恢复聚焦反射率。

Conclusion: 由于每个标准相机模型都能提升为辐射度模型，辐射度相机可直接把生成式场景模型从多视图几何推广到多视图辐射度，并能准确估计光照、衰减等现象。

Abstract: We propose the radiometric camera, a model that lifts any central geometric camera model to a radiometric one. The lifted model keeps the projection of the geometric model and adds a finite aperture, a free-form focus surface and a generic vignetting function. We calibrate it by inverse rendering directly on the relative irradiance of the sensor, from the same images of standard calibration targets used in geometric calibration. Geometric calibration absorbs the bias of its feature detections. The proposed radiometric calibration uses no features, and our radiometric cameras have lower reprojection errors on held-out test sets. The focus surface extends defocus modeling from the thin lens to any central camera, and we use it in a first multi-view radiometric reconstruction, which recovers the in-focus reflectance of a texture from defocused images. The vignetting is estimated jointly and accurately reproduces a flat field measured through a diffuser. Because every standard camera model lifts to a radiometric one, the radiometric camera naturally and directly extends generative scene models beyond multi-view geometry to multi-view radiometric ones, where phenomena such as lighting and attenuation can be accurately estimated.

</details>


### [6] [SegAnyFruit: Learning Generalizable and Edge Deployable Fruit Instance Segmentation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25475)
*Yanan Wang,Yuzheng wang,Yibin Ying,Zhenghao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SegAnyFruit (SAF) 数据构建与渐进式训练框架，将基础模型先验蒸馏进轻量免提示分割器，实现跨物种/果园/成像条件的零样本水果实例分割并可在边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实农业场景中部署水果实例分割需在严格机载算力与标注预算下泛化到未见物种、果园与成像条件；现有基础模型（如 SAM 3）性能强但难以在边缘端实用部署。

Method: 三阶段渐进训练：(1) 通用掩码预训练；(2) 基于自建 WebFruits 数据集的自动标注网络图像大规模中训练；(3) 在 PaperFruits 上进行人工核验的农业领域精调后训练。总计 733K 图像、9.66M 全果掩码、165 种水果；输出免提示轻量分割器（SAF-RF-DETR 系列）。

Result: 在七个基准上，零样本 SAF-RF-DETR-XL 平均 mask AP50:95 达 69.06（较 SAM 3 提升 +4.76）且可在边缘设备部署；SAF 初始化在五个农业域上提升 few-shot 至 full-shot 的适应效果并缓解灾难性遗忘；系统性分析了训练阶段、数据规模、模型容量与生成式增强的作用，并验证流程可迁移至水下、细胞与人体分割。

Conclusion: 数据构建加渐进式蒸馏训练可有效将基础模型先验转化为轻量、免提示、可边缘部署的专用分割器，兼具零样本泛化与少样本适应能力，且该流程具有跨领域可迁移性；数据集、代码与模型将开源。

Abstract: Deploying fruit instance segmentation in real agricultural settings requires generalizing across unseen species, orchards, and imaging conditions under strict onboard compute and annotation budgets. We introduce SegAnyFruit (SAF), a data construction and progressive training framework that distills foundation-model priors into lightweight, prompt-free segmenters. SAF combines generic mask pre-training, broad mid-training on automatically annotated web imagery from our curated WebFruits dataset, and human-verified agricultural refinement post-training on PaperFruits, together spanning 733K images and 9.66M whole-fruit masks across 165 fruit types. Across seven benchmarks, zero-shot SAF-RF-DETR-XL reaches 69.06 average mask AP50:95 (+4.76 over SAM 3) while remaining deployable on edge devices. SAF initialization also boosts few-shot to full-shot adaptation across five agricultural domains while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we systematically analyze training stages, data scale, model capacity, and generative augmentation, and validate our pipeline's transferability to underwater, cell, and human segmentation. We will release our datasets, code, and SAF models.

</details>


### [7] [ActMem: From Latent Memory Reasoning to Grounded Robotic Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25461)
*Linfeng Dong,Jianwei Niu,Jiuyi Zhao,Ruibing Feng,Xinghao Wu,Wu Yang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu,Mu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActMem：解耦"记忆推理"与"感知接地"的上下文记忆VLA框架，用潜空间记忆思维链（Memory CoT）条件化流匹配动作专家，并在长历史下高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强VLA存在两极问题：要么仅检索过去观测而不推理其含义，要么在共享上下文中推理完整历史，从而挤占当前感知信息。认知科学将二者视为不同系统——记忆负责重构过去经验以决定"做什么"，感知负责指导"怎么做"。

Method: 基于原生支持视频理解的大型视觉语言模型构建单骨干框架：1) 从带时间戳的交互历史中推断当前子任务，并将推理过程的隐状态保留为Latent Memory Chain-of-Thought（Memory CoT）；2) 该Memory CoT作为条件输入给flow-matching动作专家；3) Perception Attention使动作专家可直接关注当前视觉输入，实现记忆推理与感知接地的解耦；4) Efficient Memory CoT Inference在相邻决策间复用计算，不改输出以加速长历史推理。

Result: RoboMemArena上任务成功率71.1%、完成成功率80.8%，分别领先最强基线7.5与8.7个百分点；MIKASA-Robo平均成功率73.8%，为对比方法中最佳；真实世界外生、内生与无痕记忆任务平均成功率68.8%，较最强记忆增强VLA高10.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式解耦记忆推理与感知接地，并以潜空间CoT传递历史语义，可在单一VLM骨干内同时提升长时序任务性能与推理效率；多基准与真机实验验证其优于现有记忆增强VLA，代码与权重将开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising results in real-world long-horizon manipulation, where memory plays a crucial role.
  However, existing memory-augmented VLAs either retrieve past observations without reasoning about what they imply, or reason over the full
  history in a shared context where it crowds out current perception. Cognitive science treats them as distinct systems: memory reconstructs past
  experience to decide what to do, while perception guides how to act. We therefore propose ActMem, an in-context memory framework that
  decouples memory reasoning from perceptual grounding within a single vision-language backbone. Built on a vision-language model with native
  video understanding, ActMem infers the current subtask from a timestamped interaction history and keeps the hidden states formed during this
  inference as a Latent Memory Chain-of-Thought (Memory CoT), which conditions a flow-matching action expert. Perception Attention further lets
  the expert attend directly to the current view. To keep inference fast under long histories, Efficient Memory CoT Inference reuses computation
  across consecutive decisions without changing the output. ActMem achieves 71.1% task success and 80.8% completion success on RoboMemArena,
  surpassing the strongest baseline by 7.5 and 8.7 percentage points, and 73.8% average success on MIKASA-Robo, the best among all compared
  methods. In the real world, it reaches 68.8% average success on exogenous, endogenous, and traceless memory tasks, 10.0 percentage points above
  the strongest memory-augmented VLA. Code and checkpoints will be released at https://github.com/LigphiDonk/ActMem and
  https://huggingface.co/donk111/ActMem.

</details>


### [8] [RemoteVLPG: How to Obtain a Specific VLM for Language-Guided Path Generation on Remote Sensing Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25458)
*Junzhe Dang,Mengmeng Zhang,Yuxiang Zhang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何为遥感图像上“语言引导的起终点路径生成”任务获得任务专用视觉语言模型（VLM），从架构实现、预训练数据构建与评测基准三方面展开，并验证了自建预训练数据训练的模型零样本性能可超过部分先进商业VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向遥感图像的路径生成缺乏任务专用VLM的系统性研究：现有工作缺少对不同架构方案的比较、缺少可扩展的预训练数据构造手段，也缺少分层评测的基准与指标，导致难以判断如何高效构建此类模型。

Method: (1) 实现三种广泛使用的架构方案并进行性能对比；(2) 设计可扩展的预训练数据构造方法，构建具有一定多样性的有效预训练数据集；(3) 构建三个支持分层评估的基准，并设计三项面向路径生成的评价指标；(4) 开展大量实验进行系统性探究。

Result: 在该预训练数据集上训练的方法取得良好的零样本性能，其零样本表现可超过部分先进商业VLM；并给出三种架构方案的性能比较与分层评测结果。代码与数据集已发布于 https://github.com/jzdang/RemoteVLPG 。

Conclusion: 通过架构比较、可扩展预训练数据构造与分层基准评测三方面协同，可有效获得遥感图像语言引导路径生成的任务专用VLM；自建预训练数据能带来超越部分商业模型的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: We study how to obtain a task-specific Vision-Language Model (VLM) for language-guided path generation between start and end points on remote sensing images. We explore this question from three aspects. First, we implement three widely used architectural approaches and compare their performance. Second, we design a scalable method for constructing pre-training data and build an effective pre-training dataset with a degree of diversity. Methods trained on this dataset achieve promising zero-shot performance. Third, we construct three benchmarks that support stratified evaluation and design three metrics for path generation. We investigate this problem through extensive experiments. The results show that the zero-shot performance of methods trained on the constructed pre-training dataset can exceed that of some advanced commercial VLMs. Code and Dataset can be available at https://github.com/jzdang/RemoteVLPG

</details>


### [9] [Raptor: Rejection-aware Adaptive Tree based on Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25436)
*Qishu Hu,Haozhao Wang,Zicong Hong,Yufeng Zhan,Yuanqing Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Raptor：一种免训练框架，利用拒绝反馈联合调控树状推测解码的候选覆盖与草稿开销，在视觉语言模型上实现最高2.39倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视觉上下文使树状推测解码的推测计算成本上升，候选覆盖不足与冗余扩展成为除树拓扑自适应外的关键问题；而仅凭接受长度无法区分这两种情形，二者需要不同的资源分配决策。

Method: 提出Raptor免训练框架，从拒绝反馈中提取互补的拒绝感知信号，分别刻画推测进展、前沿覆盖与冗余扩展；据此对树深度、分支宽度与草稿预算进行有界、动量平滑的联合更新，并配合执行控制以抑制无效草稿并在适当时机逐步恢复扩展。

Result: 在两个VLM、七个多模态基准上的大量实验表明Raptor有效，相对自回归解码平均加速最高达2.39倍。

Conclusion: 以拒绝反馈驱动的多维信号协同调控候选覆盖与草稿投入，可有效提升长视觉上下文下树状推测解码的效率，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Tree-structured speculative decoding accelerates vision-language models (VLMs) by verifying multiple candidate continuations in parallel. However, long visual contexts increase the cost of speculative computation, making candidate coverage and redundant expansion important considerations beyond tree topology adaptation. We propose Raptor, a training-free framework that uses rejection feedback to jointly regulate candidate coverage and drafting effort. Our key insight is that acceptance length alone cannot distinguish insufficient candidate coverage from excessive deep expansion, and these conditions require different allocation decisions. Raptor therefore extracts complementary rejection-aware signals to characterize speculative progress, frontier coverage, and redundant expansion. These signals jointly guide bounded, momentum-smoothed updates of tree depth, branching width, and drafting budget, together with execution control that limits unnecessary drafting and progressively restores expansion when appropriate. Extensive experiments with two VLMs across seven multimodal benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of Raptor, achieving up to 2.39x average speedup over autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [10] [Decision Boundaries Matter: Graph-Regularized Residual Adaptation for Cross-Domain Fake Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25406)
*Shichuang Xie,Qishuai Ding,Tong Qiao,Liquan Shen,Guorui Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出图正则残差适配(GraRA)，仅用无标签目标数据修正源检测器决策边界，在OpenMMSec未见域达84.36%准确率、91.34% AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪造图像检测器虽学到可迁移特征，但在未见域上因决策边界不匹配而性能下降；分析表明冻结取证编码器提取的特征仍具判别性，问题在于源域训练得到的决策边界未充分利用这些信息。而无源数据、无目标标签条件下修正边界面临源预测不可靠导致错误适配的风险。

Method: 构建紧凑残差子空间：由不确定性加权后的目标特征生成，并约束其与源分类方向正交以确定需更新的分类器方向；用源分数锚点经目标图扩散生成软标签作为更新引导；以邻域一致性正则化约束子空间内的残差学习；最终将修正项合并进源分类器，仅需32个优化系数且无额外推理分支。

Result: 在OpenMMSec多个未见域上取得84.36%准确率与91.34% AUC，较源检测器分别提升4.33与3.50个百分点；分析显示可靠源预测与局部一致邻域有助于引导残差学习，可纠正简单阈值平移无法解决的排序错误。

Conclusion: GraRA以极低参数开销实现无源数据、无标签的目标域决策边界精修，验证了特征可迁移而边界需适配的观点。

Abstract: Despite learning transferable features, fake image detectors may struggle on unseen domains due to mismatched decision boundaries. Our analysis shows that features extracted by the frozen forensic encoder remain discriminative, but the source-trained decision boundary does not fully exploit this information. However, correcting this boundary without source data or target labels remains challenging, as unreliable source predictions may lead to incorrect adaptation. To address this issue, we propose Graph-Regularized Residual Adaptation (GraRA), which refines the source decision boundary using only unlabeled target data. To determine which classifier directions to update, GraRA constructs a compact residual subspace from uncertainty-weighted target features and keeps it orthogonal to the source classification direction. To guide these updates, it uses source-score anchors to generate soft labels through target-graph diffusion, while neighborhood consistency regularizes residual learning within the constrained subspace. The learned correction is merged into the source classifier, requiring only 32 optimized coefficients and no additional inference branches. On diverse unseen OpenMMSec domains, GraRA achieves 84.36\% accuracy and 91.34\% AUC, improving over the source detector by 4.33 and 3.50 percentage points, respectively. Further analysis shows that reliable source predictions and locally consistent neighborhoods help guide residual learning, allowing GraRA to correct ranking errors that a simple threshold shift cannot address. \textcolor{cvprblue}{Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [11] [HOPD: Historical On-Policy Distillation for SAR MLLM Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25391)
*Yuxuan Li,Derrick Goh Xin Deik,Zheng-Peng Duan,Qianru Sun,Weijie Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出历史同策略蒸馏（HOPD），将各源域采集阶段的LoRA状态归档为持久教师模型，通过同策略蒸馏与增强视图回放缓解SAR持续学习中的视觉漂移、类别偏置与历史数据稀缺问题，在SARVQA-CL基准上取得最优最终平均分54.51。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续SAR解译要求多模态大模型在学习新数据源的同时保留旧任务能力，但采集与地理变化导致视觉证据漂移、区域集中的类别引发答案偏置、历史图像稀缺，使知识保留困难。

Method: HOPD在每源域巩固前归档其LoRA状态作为持久教师，由一个持续演化的学生模型接受多个教师监督；教师对学生自生成前缀进行同策略监督，将历史行为迁移至学生变化的生成上下文；并用原始视图教师指导增强后的历史学生视图（保持空间坐标）以丰富稀疏回放；最终合并为无需源身份标识的单一部署适配器。

Result: 在自建的源增量评测协议SARVQA-CL上，HOPD最终平均分为54.51，较最强基线高2.96分；在所有评测任务上最终源宏平均分均优于共享记忆的Replay-SFT，且在不同源顺序下均有一致增益。

Conclusion: 源采集历史本身可作为监督信号，用于在持续SAR自适应中保留早期任务性能；归档式教师+同策略蒸馏+增强回放是有效的知识保持范式。

Abstract: Continual SAR interpretation requires multimodal large language models to learn incoming sources while retaining earlier task abilities. This is difficult when acquisition and geographic shifts alter visual evidence, regionally concentrated categories bias answers, and historical images are scarce. We address this problem with Historical On-Policy Distillation (HOPD), which reuses source-acquisition LoRA states as persistent teachers for one evolving student. Archiving each state before consolidation preserves a stable source reference. The teachers supervise student-generated prefixes, transferring historical behavior to the student's changing generation contexts. To enrich sparse replay, original-view teachers guide augmented historical student views while preserving spatial coordinates. This knowledge is consolidated into a single deployed adapter that requires no source identity. On SARVQA-CL, our source-incremental evaluation protocol, HOPD achieves the highest final mean score of 54.51 among the compared methods, exceeding the strongest baseline by 2.96 points. It also improves the final source-macro score in every evaluated task over shared-memory Replay-SFT, with gains across source orders. These results support source-acquisition history as supervision for retaining earlier task performance during continual SAR adaptation. Code and data: https://github.com/zcablii/HOPD.

</details>


### [12] [Branch Caching After Step Distillation in Four- and Eight-Step Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25248)
*Mingyu Liang,Dingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在蒸馏图像生成器的少步去噪中复用中间特征，通过固定分支缓存与标量校正，在保持学生噪声调度下实现约1.5倍加速并提升保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏后的图像生成器剩余去噪步数很少，单步计算代价高；作者希望在不改变采样网格的前提下，通过复用步间特征节省时间，同时避免朴素复用带来的质量损失。

Method: 采用固定分支缓存，替换选定的attention与MLP评估，同时保留学生的噪声调度。利用同一初始噪声的一条完整轨迹与一条raw-cache轨迹，为每类分支和每个复用位置求解一个标量校正量。

Result: 1024²下，一次内部复用使四步Qwen-Image-Lightning去噪时间从1.279s降至0.977s；三次复用在八步Lightning与八步Hyper-FLUX上分别取得1.51×与1.53×加速。在DrawBench全部200条提示上，固定复用位置时校正使同种子图像保真度较raw复用分别提升0.66dB与0.87dB（两个Lightning学生）；Hyper-FLUX上PSNR差0.01dB，LPIPS由0.1625改善至0.1546。

Conclusion: 分支缓存结合标量校正可在保持学生原有采样网格的情况下有效加速蒸馏生成器，并在多数设置下优于无校正的raw复用。

Abstract: Can a distilled image generator save time by reusing features between its few remaining denoising steps? We study a fixed branch cache that replaces selected attention and MLP evaluations while preserving the student's noise schedule. One full trajectory and one raw-cache trajectory from the same initial noise supply a scalar correction for each branch type and reuse position. At $1024^2$, one internal reuse reduces four-step Qwen-Image-Lightning denoising time from 1.279 to 0.977 seconds. Three reuses yield $1.51\times$ speedup on eight-step Lightning and $1.53\times$ on eight-step Hyper-FLUX. We evaluate image quality on all 200 DrawBench prompts. With the reuse positions held fixed, correction improves mean same-seed image fidelity over raw reuse by 0.66 and 0.87 dB on the two Lightning students. On Hyper-FLUX the PSNR difference is 0.01 dB, while LPIPS improves from 0.1625 to 0.1546. These results measure the effect of branch caching and correction while retaining each student's original sampling grid.

</details>


### [13] [What a Calibrated Probability Hides: Belief-Level Post-Training for Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25168)
*Xinye Li,Zirui Song,Huaxing Liu,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Zixiang Xu,Yinjun Liu,Zhewen Zhang,Xiaojun Chang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Belief-Grouped Policy Optimization（BGPO），将校准决策模型预测中的不确定性分解为"观点分歧"与"认知不确定"两类，用贝叶斯规则为观测选择分配信念权重，从而在不使用信念标签的情况下学习信念分组并产生校准的聚合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准后的决策模型只能预测每个答案被选择的频率，但同一预测可能来自两种成因：自信个体之间的真实分歧（disagreement），或共享同一信念者内部的认知不确定（uncertainty）。二者应对策略不同——分歧需要识别"谁的观点适用"，不确定需要收集更多证据。标准后训练每次只用一个观测选择对单一预测打分，学到的是池化分布，无法区分这两种不确定性来源。

Method: BGPO 让策略提出一组信念（belief）列表并估计其流行度（prevalence），为每个信念预测一个选择分布。训练时用贝叶斯规则估计每个观测选择由各信念生成的概率，以此作为权重调节该选择对各信念对数得分奖励的贡献。为发现稀有信念，策略在观测少量选择后提出信念列表，仅当能较好预测留出选择时才保留。推理时仅使用问题本身，按流行度加权组合各信念分布，得到校准的聚合预测。

Result: 在涵盖观点、对话安全、艺术品引发的情绪、照片美学等基准上，BGPO 在不同模型初始化下均取得最低的条件、最差组和整体 Jensen-Shannon 散度；在文本与图像任务上均改善条件散度。在具有已知信念的合成图像任务上，BGPO 对潜在分组的恢复比使用信念标签训练的基线更准确。

Conclusion: 通过将预测不确定性显式分解为信念分歧与信念内不确定，并借助贝叶斯责任度加权与留出验证的信念发现机制，BGPO 无需信念标签即可学到可解释的信念分组结构，并提升预测的校准性与分组鲁棒性。

Abstract: A calibrated decision model can predict how often each answer is chosen. The same prediction can reflect disagreement among people who are confident in their choices or uncertainty among people who share a belief. Disagreement calls for identifying whose perspective applies, while uncertainty calls for gathering more evidence. Standard post-training scores a single prediction against one observed choice at a time, learning the pooled distribution without distinguishing these two sources of uncertainty. We introduce Belief-Grouped Policy Optimization (BGPO). The policy proposes a list of beliefs, estimates their prevalence, and predicts a choice distribution for each. During training, BGPO uses Bayes' rule to estimate how likely each belief was to generate an observed choice. These estimates weight the choice's contribution to each belief's log-score reward. To discover rare beliefs, the policy also proposes lists after seeing a few observed choices and retains them only if they predict held-out choices well. At inference, the policy uses only the question and combines the belief-specific distributions by prevalence to produce a calibrated aggregate prediction. On benchmarks covering opinions, conversation safety, emotions elicited by artwork, and photo aesthetics, BGPO achieves the lowest conditional, worst-group, and overall Jensen-Shannon divergence across model initializations. It improves conditional divergence on both text and image tasks. On a synthetic image task with known beliefs, BGPO recovers the underlying groups more accurately than baselines trained with belief labels.

</details>


### [14] [TALD: Transferability-Aware Logit Disentanglement for Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25126)
*Hui Li,Pengfei Yang,Xinyi Feng,Junhong Liu,Tianyang Zheng,Hui Zeng,Le Dong,Kaibing Zhang,Mengting Zhao,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TALD：两阶段迁移感知Logit解耦框架，缓解跨模态蒸馏中的负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态知识蒸馏中，源模态监督与目标模态判别特性冲突时会产生负迁移。

Method: 在类对齐logit空间进行两阶段处理：由残差解耦器将教师与独立训练的目标模态参考学生的logit分解为模态通用与模态特定成分，解耦受任务相关"自益性"与"跨模态排他性"准则引导；样本自适应调制器调节解耦约束与样本级融合权重；最后将模态通用成分自适应融合以监督最终学生。

Result: 在视觉、音频、文本五个基准上取得最大的平均精度增益与最高正迁移频率；10个迁移方向×5次运行中，84%的评测优于无蒸馏训练（基线最高70%），并具有较优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 通过解耦并选择性融合模态通用知识，TALD有效提升跨模态蒸馏的迁移性与稳定性，代码将开源。

Abstract: Cross-modal knowledge distillation can incur negative transfer when source-modality supervision conflicts with the target modality's discriminative characteristics. We propose TALD, a two-stage framework for Transferability-Aware Logit Disentanglement in class-aligned logit space. Guided by task-dependent self-benefiting and cross-modal exclusivity criteria, a Residual Disentangler decomposes logits from a teacher and an independently trained target-modality reference student into modality-general and modality-specific components. A Sample-Adaptive Modulator regulates decomposition constraints and sample-specific fusion weights. The learned modality-general components are then adaptively fused to supervise the final student. Across five benchmarks spanning vision, audio, and text, TALD achieves the largest mean accuracy gain and highest positive-transfer frequency among the compared methods. Across ten transfer directions with five runs each, TALD improves over training without distillation in 84% of evaluations, compared with at most 70% for the baselines, while offering a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. We plan to release the code.

</details>


### [15] [World-delta: Continually Integrating Observations for Incremental 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25802)
*Haonan Wang,Hanyu Zhou,Xiaobin Hu,Luxin Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 World-delta 增量式三维场景生成框架，通过不确定性感知的持久世界建模与 delta 引导扩散，实现对三维世界的持续扩展与观测驱动修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维生成方法多为固定输入的一次性合成，难以随新观测持续扩展与修订场景；迭代式方法把生成内容与真实观测融合进单一表示，将生成内容视为既定结构而非可修正的预测，导致早期生成误差在新证据下仍持续存在并累积。

Method: 框架包含三个组件：(1) 视觉证据编码，将输入图像中的几何与语义线索整合为统一的世界 token；(2) 观测校准世界建模，维护持久隐世界状态，区分观测支持的内容与临时补全假设，由新证据驱动选择性世界 delta 修正冲突假设并保留可靠内容；(3) 增量世界扩散生成，利用 delta 派生掩码将扩散限制在需更新区域、冻结未变区域，仅解码受影响的三维块并整合进现有场景。

Result: 实验表明 World-delta 在增量式三维场景生成、观测驱动修正、场景一致性以及连续场景更新中的更新效率方面均优于基线。

Conclusion: 将生成内容显式建模为可修正假设并仅对增量区域进行扩散与解码，可在保证场景一致性的同时实现持续世界扩展与修正，并减少冗余生成。

Abstract: Existing 3D generation methods synthesize visually compelling scenes from fixed inputs, yet struggle to continually expand and revise a 3D world as new observations arrive. Iterative approaches often fuse real observations and generated content into a single representation, treating generated content as established structure rather than revisable predictions. Consequently, early generation errors can persist and accumulate despite new evidence. We propose World-delta, an incremental 3D scene generation framework combining uncertainty-aware persistent world modeling with delta-guided diffusion for continual scene expansion and correction. Our framework comprises three components: 1) Visual Evidence Encoding integrates geometric and semantic cues from incoming images into unified world tokens. 2) Observation-Calibrated World Modeling maintains a persistent latent world state that distinguishes observation-supported content from provisional completion hypotheses. New evidence drives selective world deltas to revise conflicting hypotheses while preserving well-supported content. 3) Incremental World Diffusion Generation uses delta-derived masks to restrict diffusion to regions requiring updates, freezing unchanged regions. Only affected 3D chunks are decoded and integrated into the existing scene. Together, these components enable continual world expansion and revision while reducing redundant generation and preserving scene consistency. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of World-delta in incremental 3D scene generation, observation-driven correction, scene consistency, and update efficiency over successive scene updates.

</details>


### [16] [GeoPillars: A Large-Kernel Pillar Detector for Single-Sweep LiDAR Under Point Sparsity, and a Decomposition of Its Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25787)
*Chaitanya Dumbre,Shubham Sarkar,Akash Mohapatra,Ghanasham Patil,Sheetal Girase*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoPillars 提出一种在每 0.4 m 柱体仅保留 8 点的强预算下的单帧 LiDAR 3D 检测器，在 CARLA 数据上达到 81.33 BEV AP@0.7，但消融显示增益主要来自检测头分辨率提升，零样本迁移到真实 nuScenes 完全失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极端点预算（每柱 8 点、丢弃 46.6% 有效点）下提升单帧 LiDAR 3D 检测精度，并检验所提出几何特征与注意力等组件的真实贡献与跨域泛化能力。

Method: 基于 pillar 的单帧检测框架，引入几何 pillar 特征、柱内分组向量注意力、BEV 网格上可学习的绝对位置先验、大核主干、表面法向辅助监督与全分辨率检测头；在 123k 合成 CARLA 扫描上训练，并做逐级组件分解与七模型对比。

Result: BEV AP@0.7 达 81.33，同预算下超 CenterPoint 13.76 点，在其原生更密预算下超 1.91 点；但验证集按帧而非按场景划分，属分布内结果。单提升检测头分辨率即贡献 13.76 点中的 10.19，编码器/主干/颈部/位置先验联合贡献 3.37，法向监督仅 0.20（在运行间波动范围内）。剔除航向翻转后各模型朝向误差相近，但 GeoPillars 航向反转频率约为分辨率匹配基线的一半，且为七模型中速度最慢。零样本在 6,743 帧真实 nuScenes 关键帧上无模型迁移成功，仅保留 CARLA 精度的 3.7%–12.2%；数据采集缺陷导致强度恒定，所有模型仅从几何学习。

Conclusion: 该方法的精度提升主要源自检测头分辨率而非新颖组件，且存在明显的仿真到现实域差距与效率代价；评估协议非场景独立，结果需谨慎解读，代码与权重已公开。

Abstract: GeoPillars is a pillar-based 3D detector for single-sweep LiDAR under a hard budget of eight points per 0.4 m pillar, a budget that discards 46.6% of in-range points. It adds geometric pillar features, grouped vector attention within each pillar, a learned absolute positional prior on the bird's-eye-view (BEV) grid, a large-kernel backbone, auxiliary surface-normal supervision and a full-resolution detection head. Trained on 123k synthetic CARLA scans and evaluated on cars, it reaches 81.33 BEV AP@0.7, 13.76 points above CenterPoint at the same input budget and 1.91 points above CenterPoint at its native, denser budget. These are in-distribution figures, because the validation split is per-frame rather than scene-disjoint. A rung-by-rung decomposition shows that the new components explain little of the gain. Raising the detection-head resolution alone recovers 10.19 of the 13.76 points, the joint change of encoder, backbone, neck and positional prior adds 3.37, and the normal-supervision branch adds 0.20, which is within run-to-run variation. With heading reversals folded out, orientation error is similar for all seven models, although GeoPillars reverses headings about half as often as a resolution-matched baseline. It is also the slowest of the seven models. Evaluated zero-shot on 6,743 real nuScenes keyframes, no model transfers, and each retains 3.7% to 12.2% of its CARLA accuracy. A collection defect left LiDAR intensity constant, so every model learned from geometry alone. Code and checkpoints are publicly available.

</details>


### [17] [AdaptReg: Skip-level FiLM Adapter for Task-Specific Adaptation of a Frozen Registration Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25761)
*Shuyue Shi,Rong Yuan,Lin Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdaptReg：在完全冻结的配准基础模型uniGradICON上，通过身份保持初始化的轻量适配分支（对解码器跳跃连接施加FiLM逐体素仿射调制，由冻结TotalSegmentator语义特征生成并以病灶体积保持损失监督），实现PSMA-PET/CT纵向配准中的病灶体积保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前列腺癌治疗反应评估依赖纵向PSMA-PET/CT配准，但需求超越通用对齐精度：形变必须保持病灶体积，使MTV与TLG的变化反映真实治疗反应而非配准伪影。

Method: 冻结uniGradICON，仅在解码器跳跃连接上引入FiLM式逐体素仿射调制(γ, β)；调制参数由冻结TotalSegmentator编码器提取的解剖语义特征生成；采用身份保持初始化；以病灶体积保持损失进行监督训练。

Result: 官方Codabench验证集(20对)：DSC 0.7585、HD95 6.33 mm、MTV/TLG误差1.68%/2.11%、非微分同胚体素0.0022%；较uniGradICON+实例优化基线DSC提升0.05，MTV/TLG误差降低70.9%/67.7%。隐藏测试集(160对)：CT DSC 0.816、PET DSC 0.598、MTV/TLG误差4.02%/3.46%，在PSMAReg评测16支队伍中排名第4。

Conclusion: 以冻结基础模型加轻量语义引导适配的方式，可有效将通用配准模型特化为体积保持的纵向PET/CT配准，在保持高形变正则性（近零非微分同胚体素）的同时显著降低肿瘤定量指标误差。

Abstract: Longitudinal PSMA-PET/CT registration is a key step in assessing treatment response in prostate cancer, and it requires more than generic alignment accuracy: the deformation must preserve lesion volume, so that measured changes in metabolic tumor volume (MTV) and total lesion glycolysis (TLG) reflect treatment response rather than registration artifacts. We propose AdaptReg, which adapts a frozen registration foundation model to such task-specific requirements through a lightweight trainable adapter branch with identity-preserving initialization. Keeping uniGradICON fully frozen, we apply FiLM-style per-voxel affine modulation ($γ$, $β$) to its decoder skip connections; the modulation is generated from anatomical semantic features of a frozen TotalSegmentator encoder and supervised by a lesion volume-preservation loss. On the official Codabench validation set (20 pairs), AdaptReg achieves DSC 0.7585, HD95 6.33 mm, MTV/TLG errors of 1.68%/2.11%, and 0.0022% non-diffeomorphic voxels, improving on the uniGradICON + instance optimization baseline by $+0.05$ DSC and reducing the MTV/TLG errors by 70.9%/67.7%. On the hidden official test set (160 pairs), AdaptReg achieves CT DSC 0.816, PET DSC 0.598, and MTV/TLG errors of 4.02%/3.46%, ranking fourth among 16 teams in the final PSMAReg evaluation. Code: https://github.com/shishuyue/AdaptReg

</details>


### [18] [CGM: Chaos-Guided Graph Attention and Expert Routing for Autism Stereotypy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25730)
*Hafsa Aslam,Yue Li,Saba Aslam,Abdur Rasool,Qiao Pan,Muhammad Rizwan,Ovidiu Bagdasar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混沌引导图注意力与专家混合模型（CGM），以逐关节点、逐帧的不规则性张量结构化驱动计算，在自闭症刻板行为视频分析中达到0.930平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有骨架图注意力仅按内容为关节点赋权，使抖动的手腕与静止的手腕权重相当；而刻板行为的定义在于运动展开的不可预测性而非运动幅度。作者发现幅度类运动学描述在刻板片段中均失效，仅角速度的窗口化样本熵上升（Hedges' g=+1.05），因此需要让不规则性直接参与模型计算。

Method: CGM耦合两种机制：(1) 在图注意力logit上加入学习到的成对项，将注意力集中于不规则关节点，并把剩余边权重向联合不规则的关节点对重排序；(2) 以同一信号为条件的门控将每帧路由至四个专家中的两个。采用按受试者分组的五折交叉验证。

Result: 116个片段上达到0.930平衡准确率，较图注意力基线提升+0.187；零化张量后94%片段注意力发生重排；组件排序在公开MMASD数据集上复现；无重训练的Panoptic Studio留出会话上保持0.86特异度（无混沌基线≤0.35）；消融中仅混沌调制贡献+0.132；严重度指数分半信度0.87，组间Hedges' g=+1.08。

Conclusion: 以不规则性作为结构化先验驱动注意力与专家路由是有效的，且具备跨数据集可迁移性与个体内一致性，可支持干预期间仅凭视频的刻板行为监测。

Abstract: Skeleton graph attention for autism video analysis weights joints by content alone, giving an erratic wrist the same standing as a still one. Stereotypy, however, is defined by how unpredictably movement unfolds rather than how much of it occurs: in our cohort, every amplitude-based kinematic descriptor falls in stereotyped clips, while windowed Sample Entropy of angular velocity alone rises (Hedges' g = +1.05). We compute this irregularity as a per-joint, per-frame tensor that structurally drives computation. The proposed chaos-guided graph attention and mixture-of-experts model (CGM) couples two mechanisms. First, a learned pairwise term added to the graph-attention logit concentrates attention on irregular joints and re-ranks the remaining edge weight toward jointly irregular pairs; its unconstrained coefficient neither vanishes nor inverts, and zeroing the tensor realigns attention in 94% of clips. Second, a gate conditioned on the same signal routes each frame to two of four experts, whose weights track frame-level irregularity. Under subject-grouped five-fold cross-validation on 116 clips, CGM reaches 0.930 balanced accuracy, exceeding the graph-attention baseline by +0.187; the component ordering replicates on the public MMASD dataset, and on held-out Panoptic Studio sessions, CGM keeps 0.86 specificity without retraining, against at most 0.35 for chaos-free baselines. In ablation, routing without irregularity yields no reliable gain, whereas chaos modulation alone contributes +0.132. A severity index from the same tensor reaches 0.87 split-half reliability, separates the cohorts with Hedges' g = +1.08, and stays consistent within children across therapy recordings, supporting video-only monitoring of stereotypy during intervention.

</details>


### [19] [Beyond Entity Recognition: Vision-Centric Knowledge Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25680)
*Jiatong Ma,Yuchen Liu,Longteng Guo,Yichen Yuan,Yuze Wu,Dongze Hao,Ru Zhen,Yanhao Zhang,Haonan Lu,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VC-VQA 基准，系统评估 MLLM 在细粒度视觉知识获取与多阶段推理上的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多将视觉信息仅用于实体识别、依赖文本外部知识，忽视了视觉信息在知识推理中的来源与参与阶段。

Method: 按视觉信息来源（内部/外部图像）与参与阶段（检索前/检索后）显式建模视觉作用；构建带可追溯文本与视觉证据的受控多模态知识库；在无外部知识、给定证据、知识库检索、开放网络搜索四种设置下评测 21 个代表性 MLLM。

Result: 当前模型在视觉中心知识推理上仍存在困难；细粒度视觉知识获取是主要瓶颈；智能体式检索体现多步规划与跨模态知识整合的价值。

Conclusion: VC-VQA 为评估 MLLM 的视觉中心知识推理提供了更具挑战性的基准，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: We propose VC-VQA, a large-scale benchmark for vision-centric knowledge reasoning, aiming to systematically evaluate the capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in fine-grained visual knowledge acquisition and complex multi-stage reasoning. Unlike existing benchmarks that primarily treat visual information as entity recognition and rely on text-based external knowledge, VC-VQA explicitly models the role of visual information by considering both its source (internal vs. external images) and stage of involvement (pre-retrieval vs. post-retrieval). Specifically, VC-VQA requires models to retrieve external visual evidence, identify visual clues before retrieval, and localize, verify, or match retrieved knowledge against the internal image. We further construct a controlled multimodal knowledge base with traceable textual and visual evidence for each sample. We evaluate 21 representative MLLMs under four settings: no external knowledge, with provided evidence, knowledge base retrieval, and open-web search. Experimental results lead to three key findings: current models still struggle with vision-centric knowledge reasoning; fine-grained visual knowledge acquisition remains the primary bottleneck; and agentic retrieval highlights the value of multi-step planning and cross-modal knowledge integration. Overall, VC-VQA provides a more challenging benchmark for evaluating vision-centric knowledge reasoning in MLLMs. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/CASIA-IVA-Lab/VC-VQA.

</details>


### [20] [Physical Laws without Physics Loss? Probing Learned Dynamics in Video World Models through Forced Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25626)
*Yutao Li,Chen Wang,Wangmeng Zuo,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过"非线性受迫响应"实验（激励晃动悬挂物体支撑点）检验视频世界模型是否真正习得物理定律，而非仅模仿运动；发现六个模型均能复现线性响应（含共振），五个能捕捉高阶非线性动力学（含弱软化现象），并据此提出改进视频训练与生成的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体运动与外观指标无法判断视频世界模型是习得物理规律还是单纯模仿运动，需要更严格的判据来检验其物理理解能力。

Method: 提出双视觉-数值条件（dual visual-numeric conditioning），在仅使用流匹配损失（flow-matching loss）的情况下适配六个图生视频骨干网络，预测受迫响应运动；设计基于动力学与几何泰勒展开的三级测试，并进行反事实实验，扩展到双摆与摇摆块任务。

Result: Wan能准确预测运动并泛化到未见的激励、物体、位姿与场景；反事实测试显示超越训练视频回放。三级测试中，六个模型均复现线性响应（含共振），五个捕捉高阶动力学（含非线性软化），而软化在训练数据中仅占摆动的平方位移0.74%；双摆与摇摆块表明持续动力学比短暂碰撞被学得更忠实，但状态估计或传播出错时学到的动力学可能不显现。

Conclusion: 视频世界模型可在无物理损失下习得相当程度的物理规律（含训练中极稀疏的非线性特征），但能力依赖正确的状态估计与传播；这些测试可转化为改进视频训练与生成的策略。

Abstract: Do video world models learn physical laws without a physics loss, or merely imitate motion? Overall motion and appearance scores cannot settle it. We investigate this through nonlinear forced response: an excitation shakes the support of a suspended object. We introduce dual visual-numeric conditioning and adapt six image-to-video backbones to predict this motion using only the flow-matching loss. Wan predicts motion accurately and generalizes to unseen excitations, objects, poses, and scenes. Counterfactual tests suggest capabilities beyond replaying training videos. But what laws have they learned? We design three tiers of tests using Taylor expansions of the dynamics and geometry. All six reproduce the linear response, including resonance; five capture higher-order dynamics, including nonlinear softening. Remarkably, softening is barely present in training: only 0.74% of the swing's squared displacement. Extensions to a double pendulum and a rocking block show sustained dynamics are learned more faithfully than brief impacts. Yet learned dynamics may fail to appear when state estimation or propagation goes wrong. We turn these tests and findings into strategies to improve video training and generation.

</details>


### [21] [Online Mistake Detection in Instruction-Conditioned Procedural Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25567)
*Amit Feldman,Ilan Shimshoni,Rotem Dror*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在线、指令条件化的自我中心视频错误检测任务，比较模块化流水线与联合模型，并重划分EgoPER数据集以包含错误视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 助手监控需在错误仍可挽回时发现错误，因此必须在线、结合任务指令文本判断动作；已有在线检测器不使用指令文本，而指令条件化方法多为离线，二者存在空白。

Method: 仅用观测视频、任务指令和历史预测，在线识别并时间定位已完成的动作尝试，分类为正确、修改、失误(slip)、纠正、添加，遗漏步骤在视频结束后判定；对比模块化流水线（步骤识别、时间定位、执行验证分离）与单步联合预测模型；重划分EgoPER使各划分均含错误视频。

Result: 步骤分割F1@0.5相近（93.6% vs 92.1%）；联合模型二分类错误检测F1@0.5更高（84.1% vs 80.7%），在slip上增益最大（56.4% vs 37.5%）；组件级分析表明模块化方法分数较低源于阶段间误差传播；在含错误测试视频上，两种设计均优于已发布的AEM与ESTANet检查点。

Conclusion: 在线指令条件化错误检测可行，联合建模优于模块化流水线；阶段间误差传播是模块化方法的瓶颈。

Abstract: An assistant monitoring a person performing a procedure is most useful when it can detect mistakes while there is still time to recover. This requires judging actions against task instructions as the video unfolds, identifying the attempted step, locating it in time, and assessing its execution. Existing online detectors do not use the instruction text, while instruction-conditioned methods operate offline. In this paper, we study online, instruction-conditioned mistake detection in egocentric videos. Using only observed video, task instructions, and prior predictions, our model identifies and localizes completed action attempts and classifies them as correct, modified, slip, corrections, or additions; omitted steps are determined after the video ends. We compare a modular pipeline, with separate step identification, temporal localization, and execution verification, to a joint model that predicts all outputs in a single step. To support supervised learning from mistakes, we repartition EgoPER to include erroneous videos in all splits. The approaches achieve similar step-segmentation F1@0.5 (93.6% and 92.1%), while the joint model achieves higher binary mistake-detection F1@0.5 (84.1% vs. 80.7%), with its largest gain on slips (56.4% vs. 37.5%). Component-level analysis suggests that error propagation between stages contributes to the modular approach's lower detection score. On test videos containing mistakes, both designs outperform released AEM and ESTANet checkpoints under our evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [22] [QFT-Based Hybrid PointNet-Quantum Neural Networks for LiDAR Point-Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25578)
*Anurag Kulkarni,Nouhaila Innan,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建PointNet-量子混合架构用于LiDAR点云分类，系统评测五种傅里叶处理电路配置在理想仿真与IBM FakeManilaV2噪声模型下的性能与复杂度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将QFT作为量子特征变换引入混合量子-经典学习框架具有潜力，但其电路配置如何影响预测性能、量子资源开销与噪声鲁棒性尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 采用经典特征提取＋振幅编码＋变分量子电路的三段式PointNet-量子网络；对比No-QFT、Full-QFT-After、QFT-no-swap、IQFT-After、Trainable-QFT五种配置；在KITTI、nuScenes、PandaSet上用三个随机种子评估分类性能、类级行为、电路复杂度及对FakeManilaV2噪声的敏感性。

Result: 理想仿真下IQFT-After在KITTI取得最高加权F1（0.857±0.033），No-QFT为0.838±0.023；去掉QFT末尾SWAP门可在保持Pauli-Z测量特征（至多一个可被分类头吸收的固定置换）的同时降低电路复杂度；噪声条件下排序改变，IQFT-After退化更明显，No-QFT与Trainable-QFT更鲁棒；各类别性能差异依赖数据集。

Conclusion: 傅里叶处理配置同时影响分类行为与噪声敏感性，凸显电路结构设计与硬件感知评估在三维点云混合量子学习中的关键作用。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical learning architectures provide a promising framework for integrating quantum feature transformations into complex data-processing tasks. Among these transformations, the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT) offers a structured approach to feature processing, motivating an investigation of how its circuit configuration influences predictive performance, quantum resource requirements, and noise robustness. We present a hybrid PointNet-quantum neural network architecture for LiDAR point-cloud classification, combining classical feature extraction, amplitude encoding, and a variational quantum circuit. Five Fourier-processing configurations are systematically evaluated: No-QFT, Full-QFT-After, QFT-no-swap, IQFT-After, and Trainable-QFT. Experiments on KITTI, nuScenes, and PandaSet across three random seeds assess classification performance, class-level behavior, circuit complexity, and sensitivity to the IBM FakeManilaV2 noise model. Under ideal simulation, IQFT-After achieves the highest weighted F1-score on KITTI (0.857 $\pm$ 0.033), compared with 0.838 $\pm$ 0.023 for No-QFT. Removing the final SWAP gates in the QFT circuit reduces circuit complexity while preserving the Pauli-Z measurement features up to a fixed permutation that can be absorbed by the classification head. Under quantum noise, the circuit ranking changes, with IQFT-After exhibiting greater performance degradation and No-QFT and Trainable-QFT showing stronger robustness. Per-class analysis further identifies dataset-dependent differences in minority-class performance. These results demonstrate that Fourier-processing configuration influences both classification behavior and noise sensitivity, highlighting the role of circuit structure and hardware-aware evaluation in hybrid quantum learning for three-dimensional point-cloud data.

</details>


### [23] [Do Deep Stereo Models Learn Shape Priors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25113)
*Linbo Tang,Aneel Damaraju,Todd Zickler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究深度立体模型在局部线索微弱时是否能产生符合简单形状先验的物体边界，发现现有SOTA模型存在系统性边界误差，并提出联合预测视差与距离场的生成式立体模型改善边界定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉系统中物体边界定位至关重要，微小边界误差会改变推断出的物理表面几何。因此作者关注在局部图像与视差线索微弱时，深度立体模型是否能利用简单形状先验获得一致的边界。

Method: 构建了一个合成立体数据集：物体取自高度受限的形状族，并随机化深度、倾斜、颜色与纹理。在该数据集上评估多种SOTA立体模型（含微调与从头训练）。随后提出一个简单生成式立体模型，联合预测视差与表示物体边界的距离场。

Result: 尽管形状空间简单，多个SOTA立体模型仍保留系统性边界误差，即便微调或从头训练也难以消除。所提生成式模型改善了边界定位，其生成轮廓更贴合底层形状空间。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了当前立体架构在局部证据微弱时边界预测的局限性，并支持进一步研究生成式模型能否更好地结合区域分组证据与全局几何结构。

Abstract: Localizing object boundaries is important for embodied visual systems, since small boundary errors can change the inferred geometry of physical surfaces. We study whether deep stereo models produce boundaries consistent with simple shape priors when local image and disparity cues are weak. We create a synthetic stereo dataset consisting of objects drawn from a highly constrained family of shapes, with randomized depth, slant, color and texture. Despite the simplicity of this shape space, we find that several state-of-the-art stereo models retain systematic boundary errors, even when finetuned or trained from scratch on the dataset. We then introduce a simple generative stereo model that jointly predicts disparity and a distance field representing object boundaries. The model improves boundary localization and produces contours that adhere more closely to the underlying shape space. These results expose limitations in the boundary predictions of current stereo architectures when local evidence is weak, and motivate further study of whether generative models can better combine regional grouping evidence with global geometric structure.

</details>


### [24] [Infer3D: Inverse Generative Modeling Enables Robust 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25061)
*Amani Kiruga,Mark Hamilton,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,William T. Freeman,Vincent Sitzmann,Yilun Du,Ayush Tewari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Infer3D，一种受人类生成式视觉启发的逆向生成建模框架：通过优化生成模型的隐变量以匹配单目输入，并用冻结的 3D lifting 网络约束优化空间，实现优于前馈网络的 OOD 三维重建，并引入自适应测试时计算分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能在高度陌生或极端视角下理解三维场景，认知科学认为这源于自顶向下的生成式推理；而当前先进的 3D 重建网络依赖摊销式前馈推理，速度快但本质是模式匹配，遇到训练分布外（OOD）场景条件时性能显著退化。

Method: 将重建表述为逆向生成建模：在推理时优化生成模型（生成式图像先验）的隐变量，使其生成的观测最匹配单目输入。为避免退化的平面/塌缩输出，将生成式图像先验与一个冻结的 3D lifting 网络耦合，把优化空间限制在有效的、多视角一致的三维流形上。此外提出自适应推理策略，依据输入歧义度分配测试时计算：对模糊的 OOD 样本投入更多算力，对分布内输入高效路由。

Result: 在多种受控合成任务与真实世界任务上，Infer3D 的 OOD 重建精度优于在同等或更大数据集上训练的前馈网络；自适应推理策略在提升 OOD 表现的同时保持对分布内输入的推理效率，模拟了人类处理混淆刺激需更长感知时间的现象。

Conclusion: 以生成式逆向推理替代纯摊销前馈映射，可显著提升 3D 重建的分布外泛化能力；结合 3D 一致性约束与按需分配测试时计算，为鲁棒三维视觉提供了兼顾精度与效率的新范式。

Abstract: Humans can understand the 3D structure of scenes even from highly unfamiliar or extreme viewpoints. Cognitive science suggests this generalization stems from a top-down generative inference strategy. In contrast, state-of-the-art 3D reconstruction neural networks typically rely on amortized, feedforward inference. While fast, this pattern-matching approach degrades when faced with out-of-distribution (OOD) scene conditions unseen during training. Inspired by the generative nature of human vision, we propose Infer3D, an inverse generative modeling framework that recovers the latent variables of a generative model to best match the monocular input. To prevent degenerate flat outputs, we couple the generative image prior with a frozen 3D lifting network that constrains the optimization space to a valid, multi-view-consistent 3D manifold. Across a variety of controlled synthetic and real-world tasks, Infer3D demonstrates superior OOD reconstruction accuracy compared to feedforward networks trained on equal or larger datasets. Furthermore, mirroring how human perception requires more time to process confusing stimuli, we introduce an adaptive inference strategy that expends more test-time compute on ambiguous OOD examples while efficiently routing in-distribution inputs. Project website: https://amanikiruga.github.io/infer3d-site/

</details>


### [25] [TAP: Tapping the Discriminative Potential of Frozen Representations for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25478)
*Zihao Wang,Yu-Wei Zhan,Xiao-Qian Liu,Zhen-Duo Chen,Weitong Liu,Xin Luo,Xin-Shun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TAP框架，在冻结的CLIP嵌入空间中持续累积类信息，用轻量优化开发预训练表示的判别潜力，实现类增量学习的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型虽能提供可迁移表示，但顺序微调会带来优化开销并破坏旧类特征结构，因此作者希望冻结编码器、仅通过累积类信息来挖掘表示的判别潜力。

Method: TAP包含三个模块：类条件视觉分布建模（保留类均值与类内散度，融合类特定与共享协方差以稳定分布估计）、类自适应语义校准（用当前任务图像学习文本模板的类特定权重）、视觉-语义后验融合（以加权专家乘积对所有已见类做预测，无需保留旧任务图像）。

Result: 在两种增量协议下的九个数据集上，TAP达到SOTA，在对比方法中取得最高的平均增量准确率与最终阶段准确率。

Conclusion: 冻结预训练表示并累积视觉-语义类信息，是一种高效且能保护旧类特征结构的类增量学习范式。

Abstract: Pre-trained models provide transferable representations for class-incremental learning (CIL). However, sequentially adapting these representations introduces optimization costs and may disrupt old-class feature structure. This motivates us to explore whether the discriminative potential of these representations can be further exploited by continually accumulating class information while keeping the encoders frozen. To this end, we propose TAP, a new and novel framework for tapping the discriminative potential of frozen representations in class-incremental learning. It accumulates visual and semantic class information in the frozen CLIP embedding space with only lightweight optimization. TAP comprises three modules: class-conditional visual distribution modeling, class-adaptive semantic calibration, and visual-semantic posterior fusion. The first module retains class means and within-class scatters, combining class-specific and shared covariances to stabilize distribution estimation. The second module learns class-specific weights over text templates from current-task images, adapting text representations to downstream classes. The third module combines the visual and semantic posteriors through a weighted product of experts for prediction over all seen classes without retaining images from previous tasks. Across nine datasets under two incremental protocols, TAP achieves state-of-the-art performance, attaining the highest average incremental and final-stage accuracies among the compared methods. The code is provided as an ancillary file.

</details>


### [26] [TuringPAI: A Foundation for On-Device Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25474)
*Qiman Wu,Hanlin Chen,Yuhua Wei,Mingyuan Wang,Shaman Tang,Minghao Li,Rui Xin,Xingye Liu,Zhichao Yang,Yiqi Yu,Hongyan Pei,Xiaowen Tian,YingYing Liu,Shiwen Liu,Jiahui Hu,Yizhao Wang,Yuheng Zhang,Anhua Liu,Yue He,Shuang Su,Da Zhu,Hua Zhou,Pengfei Diao,Haojie Yang,Weichao Huang,Hang Zhang,Honggou Yang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TuringPAI 系列面向端侧物理 AI 的模型：TuringPAI-VL（0.65B 共享视觉-语言骨干）、TuringPAI-Drive（自动驾驶）与 TuringPAI-Embodied（机器人操作），在有限车载算力下实现理解与动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理 AI 需要模型同时具备空间关系与动态环境理解能力，并在有限端侧算力下把观测转化为动作；现有方案缺乏可共享、轻量的视觉-语言基础来同时支撑驾驶与操作任务。

Method: 构建 0.65B 的 TuringPAI-VL，采用 HybridViT-MLQ 架构：卷积局部聚合与 Transformer 全局建模相结合；Multi-scale Latent Queries 在同一视觉 Transformer 内融合空间特征与观测历史，形成紧凑结构化接口输入语言模型；训练混合通用视觉-语言数据与几何、跨视角、时序监督；该模型作为两个下游 VLA 模型的共享初始化。

Result: TuringPAI-VL 在定量几何感知上优于 Qwen2-VL-2B；TuringPAI-Drive 使用 MLQ 接口时各 ADE/FDE 指标与全 token HybridViT 基线差距在 1.2% 以内，内部仿真聚合得分相当，基于 XNRT 在单颗 NVIDIA Orin-X 上以 12 Hz 生成轨迹；TuringPAI-Embodied 在 LIBERO 四个套件上平均成功率 96.8%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了在实用端侧约束下，可用共享视觉-语言基础统一支撑自动驾驶与机器人操作，MLQ 接口在显著压缩视觉 token 的同时保持性能并满足实时性。

Abstract: Physical AI requires models that understand spatial relationships and dynamic environments and translate observations into actions under limited onboard compute. We present TuringPAI, a family of models for on-device Physical AI comprising TuringPAI-VL for shared vision-language understanding, TuringPAI-Drive for autonomous driving, and TuringPAI-Embodied for robotic manipulation. TuringPAI-VL is a 0.65B model built on HybridViT-MLQ, which combines convolutional local aggregation with Transformer-based global modeling. Multi-scale Latent Queries integrate spatial features and observation history within the same visual Transformer, providing a compact, structured interface to the language model. Training combines general vision-language data with geometric, cross-view, and temporal supervision. TuringPAI-VL supports general multimodal understanding and improves quantitative geometric perception over Qwen2-VL-2B, while providing a shared initialization for the two downstream VLA models. For autonomous driving, TuringPAI-Drive maintains all evaluated ADE/FDE metrics within 1.2\% of a full-token HybridViT baseline using the MLQ interface and achieves comparable aggregate scores in an in-house simulation evaluation. Using XPeng Native Runtime (XNRT) on a single NVIDIA Orin-X SoC, TuringPAI-Drive generates trajectories at 12\,Hz in on-vehicle operation. For robotic manipulation, TuringPAI-Embodied achieves a 96.8\% average success rate across the four LIBERO suites. These results demonstrate a shared vision-language foundation for driving and robotic manipulation under practical on-device constraints.

</details>


### [27] [A Wolf in Sheep's Pixels: Visual Camouflage Attacks via HDR Gain Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25469)
*Qi Shen,Yujia Gong,Yue Zhao,Tong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DRJanus 利用 SDR 处理与 HDR 显示之间的差异，将两幅独立图像嵌入同一 JPEG：机器读取的 SDR 基底层安全无害，HDR 查看器重建出的画面则是有害内容，从而绕过多种商用审核服务与视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化安全审核只读取图像的 SDR 基底层，而 HDR 显示设备会结合辅助增益图重建图像外观，二者之间存在感知鸿沟。该鸿沟使得"机器看到的内容"与"人看到的内容"可能不一致，从而形成视觉信任缺口。

Method: 提出 DRJanus 视觉伪装攻击：逆向 HDR 重建过程，通过辅助增益图的设计，在单个 JPEG 中嵌入两幅独立选定的彩色图像；精确保留所选 SDR 基底层，同时重建 HDR 目标。方法为闭式构造，无需查询或针对目标模型进行优化。

Result: 400 幅图像上 HDR 重建平均 PSNR 43.03 dB；在 100 幅色情内容图像基准上，绕过全部 6 个商用审核服务与 9 个视觉语言模型的检测；五类智能体中，每类在每种威胁设定下评估 120 个任务，审核规避成功率 31.67%–100%，任务操纵成功率 95%–100%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示视觉信任的根本缺口：保障机器所读取的内容安全，并不等于保障用户所看到的内容安全。作者进一步探讨了跨视图一致性校验作为潜在缓解手段。

Abstract: An image can pass automated safety checks while showing harmful content to its human viewer. We introduce DRJanus, a visual camouflage attack that exploits the gap between standard dynamic range (SDR) processing and high dynamic range (HDR) display. Automated systems often read only an image's SDR base, whereas HDR viewers reconstruct its appearance using an auxiliary gain map. DRJanus inverts this reconstruction to embed two independently chosen color images in a single JPEG, without querying or optimizing against the target model. It preserves the chosen SDR base exactly and reconstructs HDR targets at an average PSNR of 43.03 dB across 400 images. On a benchmark of 100 sexual-content images, DRJanus evades detection by all six tested commercial moderation services and nine vision-language models. Across five agents, each evaluated on 120 task cases per threat setting, attack success ranges from 31.67% to 100% for moderation evasion and from 95% to 100% for task manipulation. We also explore cross-view consistency checks as a mitigation. These findings expose a gap in visual trust: securing what a machine reads does not secure what a user sees. A demo is available at https://qshen0629.github.io/DRJanus/

</details>


### [28] [Lossless Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25454)
*Tongda Xu,Yu Zhang,Jun Hu,Huijuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无损潜扩散模型LLDM：不改动扩散模型与训练目标，而是构造一个可逆（无损）变换，将像素映射到更利于扩散建模的潜表示，从而在保证像素精确重建的同时显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有实现"保像素精确重建"的图像扩散训练方法通常在像素空间直接建模，需要改动扩散训练目标或网络架构，代价高且限制通用性。作者希望在不修改扩散模型本身的前提下获得无损重建能力。

Method: 采用"改表示而非改模型"的范式：设计残差潜变换（residual latent transformation）与不均匀潜缩放（uneven latent scaling）构成的无损（可逆）变换，把像素映射为更易被扩散建模的潜空间；并提出语义秩优先级（SRP）指标解释其有效性。

Result: 在生成质量上大幅优于现有像素空间扩散模型，甚至超过若干广泛使用的有损VAE；且SRP与下游生成性能呈强相关。

Conclusion: 无损可逆潜变换是替代"改目标/改架构"的一条有效路径，可在保持像素精确重建的同时达到甚至超越有损VAE的生成质量；SRP可作为分析潜空间适扩性的诊断指标。

Abstract: We study the problem of training image diffusion models while preserving pixel-exact reconstruction. Prior works typically address this problem by directly modeling images in pixel space, requiring modifications to the diffusion training objective or model architecture. In this work, we introduce a different paradigm: rather than modifying diffusion models, we construct a lossless transformation that maps pixels into a representation more amenable to diffusion modeling. We term our approach the Lossless Latent Diffusion Model (LLDM). We construct LLDM using residual latent transformation and uneven latent scaling, and explain its effectiveness through a proposed metric, semantic rank priority (SRP). Empirically, LLDM substantially outperforms prior pixel-space diffusion models in generation quality and even surpasses several widely used lossy VAEs. Furthermore, we show that SRP is strongly correlated with downstream generative performance.

</details>


### [29] [MoGloss: Joint Motion and Pseudo-Gloss Pretraining for American Sign Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25333)
*Rui Hong,Jana Košecká*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段运动编码器预训练方法（先掩码运动重建，再联合掩码预测伪手语单元与运动），在ASL翻译与孤立词识别上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语翻译与识别可从预训练中获益，但现有方法缺乏结构化的中间语言表征，且缺少统一的跨数据集评测基准，难以公平比较。

Method: 两阶段预训练：第一阶段学习重建被掩码的运动；第二阶段联合掩码预测由大语言模型从字幕生成的伪手语单元（pseudo-gloss）与运动。构建约1,100小时、来自五个公开数据集的美国手语语料，为所有片段估计2D姿态，并定义统一评测协议；在同一训练/测试划分下复现SOTA翻译方法，并基于该预训练策略构建翻译模型。

Result: 在How2Sign、ASL STEM Wiki和OpenASL上优于Uni-Sign、SHuBERT及YouTube-ASL基线；伪手语单元与运动联合预训练的收益随翻译数据减少而增大；同一编码器构建的分类器在ASL Citizen孤立词识别上取得SOTA，并在WLASL2000上优于Uni-Sign复现结果。

Conclusion: 结构化伪手语单元与运动的联合掩码预训练是有效的运动编码器预训练范式，能同时提升手语翻译与识别性能，并在数据稀缺场景下优势更明显。

Abstract: Sign language translation and recognition have been shown to benefit from various pretraining approaches. We propose a novel two-stage pretraining approach for the motion encoder, which first learns to reconstruct masked motion, followed by joint masked prediction of pseudo-gloss units and motion. Pseudo-glosses serve as an intermediate structured representation of natural language text and are obtained from the captions by a large language model. We curate a corpus of about 1,100 hours of American Sign Language from five public datasets, estimate 2D pose for all clips and define an evaluation protocol. We then reproduce state-of-the-art translation methods and evaluate them using the same train and test splits. We propose a translation model built on our pretraining strategy, and this model outperforms the previous state-of-the-art methods Uni-Sign and SHuBERT, as well as the YouTube-ASL baseline, on the How2Sign, ASL STEM Wiki and OpenASL datasets. Furthermore, the gain from the joint pretraining using motion and pseudo-gloss grows as translation data shrinks. A classifier built on the same encoder also achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on ASL Citizen isolated sign recognition and outperforms our reproduction of Uni-Sign on WLASL2000.

</details>


### [30] [Fast and Accurate Body Estimation in Pixels, World Space and Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25801)
*Jinkun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAB 是一个直接从图像预测人体骨架关节与稠密表面点的实时人体估计器，兼顾推理效率与恢复保真度，并支持接触估计、世界坐标运动估计与场景对齐，以及面向 Unitree G1 的人到人形机器人重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法只预测参数化网格模型参数，难以兼顾推理效率与身体恢复保真度；同时缺少将人体与场景（接触）衔接、以及向人形机器人迁移运动的统一方案。

Method: FAB 直接预测骨架关节与稠密表面点：在图像中预测稠密 2D/3D 体点及参数化 MHR 头部，然后用一次学习式拟合加两步 Gauss-Newton 拟合 SMPL。ViT-L 学生模型 FAB-L 由微调后的 SAM 3D Body 蒸馏而来。网络同时预测脚跟、脚尖、手与膝的接触，用于连接人体与场景；并估计世界坐标运动，融合可见人体周围的静态场景深度，基于接触进行人体-场景对齐。面向真机迁移，加入 in-model G1 头部与 FAB-Retarget（读取稠密点或拟合网格的运动级重定向器），将运动映射到 Unitree G1。

Result: 在 RTX 4070 Ti SUPER 上实现单人的检测到拟合实时运行，速度约为 SAM 3D Body eager-PyTorch 纯人体路径的 4.6 倍，同时在多数相机坐标系基准上精度更优；FAB-Retarget 生成的参考动作比 SOMA、GMR、UMR、OmniRetarget 等专用重定向器更贴合人物，也显著优于用序列 IK 重定向的 Fast SAM 3D Body。

Conclusion: FAB 以"骨架+稠密点"替代纯参数回归，在实时性与精度间取得更好平衡，并将接触、世界坐标运动与场景对齐、人形机器人重定向整合进统一框架。

Abstract: We present FAB (Fast and Accurate Body estimation), a human body estimator that jointly improves inference efficiency and body-recovery fidelity. Rather than predicting only parametric mesh-model parameters, FAB directly predicts human skeleton joints and dense surface points. In images, it predicts dense 2D and 3D body points alongside a parametric Momentum Human Rig (MHR) head, then fits SMPL in one learned pass followed by two Gauss-Newton steps. Its ViT-L student, FAB-L, distilled from a fine-tuned SAM 3D Body, runs in real time for one person on an RTX 4070 Ti SUPER, from detection to a fitted body, at 4.6x the speed of SAM 3D Body's eager-PyTorch body-only path, while improving accuracy on most camera-space benchmarks. The same network predicts heel, toe, hand, and knee contacts, which connect the body to the scene. FAB also estimates world-space motion, fuses static-scene depth around the visible person, and aligns body and scene using predicted contacts. For human-to-humanoid real-to-sim transfer, an in-model G1 head and FAB-Retarget, a motion-level retargeter that reads dense points or the fitted mesh, map motion to a Unitree G1. FAB-Retarget produces references that follow the person more closely than specialized retargeters including SOMA, GMR, UMR, and OmniRetarget, and substantially more closely than Fast SAM 3D Body retargeted with our sequence IK. Project page: https://www.jinkuncao.com/FAB.

</details>


### [31] [Towards High-Fidelity Learned Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25731)
*Bumyoon Kim,Yongseong Kim,Inhyuk Jeong,Byeungwoo Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种"搜索式"学习的帧间编解码器，通过块运动搜索+学习块变换搜索，在10-bit 4:2:2专业质量范围内显著超越现有学习编解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业视频工作流的质量（如ProRes 4:2:2高档）远超现有学习视频编解码器的覆盖范围；现有学习编码器为固定潜变量容量的前馈网络（≤0.25），缺乏传统编码器那样的搜索能力，且量化对质量贡献有限（≤1 dB），需要弥补这一差距。

Method: 构建衡量"专业质量范围覆盖率"的评测：下限为VVC QP37，上限为ProRes 4:2:2；帧间编码统一以原始帧作帧内参考。提出的学习帧间编码器像传统编码器一样进行搜索：搜索块运动并由学习网络精化；在潜变量容量最高为1的学习块变换间搜索，以率失真代价选择划分、skip决策与量化系数。并与四种近期学习编解码器（含固定16×16残差块、容量0.25的变体）对比。

Result: 在10-bit YCbCr 4:2:2测试序列上，四种现有学习编解码器仅覆盖24%–38%的质量范围，去掉量化最多提升1 dB；所提编解码器在全部六条序列上达到范围上端；当所有残差块固定为16×16（容量0.25）时，同一编解码器覆盖率仍达55%，无论是否使用量化。

Conclusion: 在编码器中引入搜索（运动、划分、变换、skip与量化系数的率失真决策）比单纯增大潜变量容量或取消量化更能提升质量覆盖，使学习编解码器迈向专业级质量。

Abstract: Professional video workflows operate at qualities that learned video codecs do not reach. We measure how much of a professional quality range a codec covers, from the lowest quality of the standard test conditions, VVC at QP 37, to the highest 4:2:2 grade of the production codec ProRes. To compare inter-frame coding alone, every codec receives original frames as references at the intra positions. On 10-bit YCbCr 4:2:2 test sequences, four recent learned codecs cover 24 to 38% of this range. Their encoders are feed-forward networks with a fixed latent capacity, the number of latent values per sample, of at most 0.25, and removing their quantization raises the quality by at most 1 dB. We propose a learned inter-frame codec whose encoder searches, as the encoders of conventional codecs do. It searches block motion, which a learned network refines, and it searches among learned block transforms whose latent capacity ranges up to 1, selecting the partition, the skip decisions, and the quantized coefficients by a rate-distortion cost. Our codec reaches the upper end of the range on all six sequences. With all residual blocks fixed to 16x16, whose latent capacity is 0.25, the same codec covers 55% of the range, with or without quantization.

</details>


### [32] [DACA: Dual-Anchor Collaborative Agent for Zero-Shot 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25722)
*Jiaming Liu,Linghe Kong,Guixu Zhang,Huaxi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双锚点协作智能体DACA，将3D视觉定位重构为语义-几何双锚点协同推理范式，通过双向信息交换与闭环优化实现零样本SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D-VG智能体虽摆脱对预处理点云的依赖，但采用单向流水线式工具调用架构，将语义理解与几何重建割裂，导致误差累积且无法自主从失败中恢复。

Method: DACA由两个互补专用工具构成：语义锚点工具持有目标视觉语义特征，通过光流与VLM局部验证实现高效目标跟踪；几何锚点工具持有目标3D空间几何特征，通过体素化与多关键点投影进行全局空间推理；二者经标准化工具调用协议双向交换信息，自主决策下一步感知-动作，形成闭环优化。

Result: 在ScanRefer与Nr3D基准上，零样本DACA分别取得73.6%（Acc@0.25）与70.4%的整体准确率，显著超越最新零样本3D-VG方法。

Conclusion: DACA以双锚点协同推理替代单向流水线架构，验证了语义与几何双向闭环交互在3D视觉定位中的有效性，为具身智能与人机交互提供基础能力支撑。

Abstract: 3D visual grounding (3D-VG) is a task that bridges the natural language and 3D space, serving as a foundational capability for embodied intelligence and human-computer interaction. Although recent 3D-VG agents overcome the reliance on pre-processed 3D point clouds, they essentially employ a unidirectional, pipeline-style tool-calling architecture. This architecture separates semantic understanding from geometric reconstruction, leading to error accumulation and an inability to autonomously recover from failures. To address this flaw, this paper proposes the Dual-Anchor Collaborative Agent (DACA), which reinterprets 3D-VG as a semantic-geometric dual-anchor collaborative reasoning paradigm. DACA comprises two complementary specialized tools: the semantic anchor tool holds the visual semantic features of the target and achieves efficient target tracking via optical flow and VLM local verification; the geometric anchor tool holds the 3D spatial geometric features of the target and performs global spatial reasoning via voxelization and multi-keypoint projection. The two components exchange information bidirectionally via a standardized tool call protocol, autonomously deciding on the next perception-action step to form a closed-loop optimization. Experiments on the ScanRefer and Nr3D benchmarks demonstrate that zero-shot DACA achieves an overall Acc@0.25 of 73.6\% on ScanRefer and 70.4\% overall accuracy on Nr3D, far surpassing the latest zero-shot 3D-VG methods. The code and models are available at https://github.com/liujia99/DACA

</details>


### [33] [LG-FiLM: Local-Global Mixture-of-Experts Modulation for RAW-to-RGB Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25639)
*Fei Li,Xilei Cao,Shichang Zhai,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LG FiLM：在共享编解码器的瓶颈处放置小型仿射专家混合，由局部邻域证据与全局场景描述子预测各专家的缩放、平移与混合权重，实现场景自适应的显式神经 ISP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像信号处理不止重建图像，还需按场景决定降噪强度、色彩平衡与色调曲线（逆光天空与室内人像所需曲线不同）。而多数神经 ISP 只学习单一固定变换并施加于所有场景，缺乏场景自适应能力。

Method: 将适配显式嵌入网络：保留单一共享编码器-解码器，仅在瓶颈处引入小型仿射专家混合（mixture of affine experts）；专家参数（scale、shift）及混合权重由局部邻域证据和全局场景描述子共同预测；重建始终在单一共享通路中进行，仅切换处理模式。

Result: 在 Fujifilm UltraISP、Zurich RAW to RGB 以及基于逆 ISP 构建的 COCO 衍生基准三个数据集上评估；在主基准上取得对比条目中最优重建保真度，并可迁移至另外两个数据集。

Conclusion: 场景自适应的处理模式切换可在不增加多条重建通路的前提下，通过瓶颈处的条件化仿射专家混合高效实现，并获得更优且可迁移的重建保真度。

Abstract: An image signal processor does more than reconstruct an image. It also has to decide how strongly to denoise, how to balance color, and which tone curve maps the scene onto the display, and these settings depend on the scene itself, the curve that suits a backlit sky is not the one that suits an indoor portrait. Most neural ISPs learn a single fixed transformation and apply it to every scene. We instead make the adaptation an explicit part of the network. Our model, LG FiLM, keeps one shared encoder decoder and places a small mixture of affine experts at its bottleneck; the experts scales, shifts, and mixing weights are predicted from local neighborhood evidence together with a global scene descriptor. Reconstruction stays in a single shared pathway and only the processing mode is switched. We evaluate LG FiLM on three datasets, Fujifilm UltraISP, Zurich RAW to RGB, and a COCO derived benchmark built with an inverse ISP. The model reaches the best reconstruction fidelity of the compared entries on the primary benchmark and transfers to the other two.

</details>


### [34] [CaT-Movie: Balancing Global Planning and Local Rendering for Long Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25620)
*Jinpeng Lu,Haoyuan Shi,Yuanshen Guan,Jiyuan Xia,Bin Wu,Yinda Chen,Zhiwei Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CaT-Movie 提出"全局故事规划器 + 局部窗口渲染器"的多镜头长视频生成系统，并构建 350K 电影镜头数据集与 CaT-MBench 基准，在跨镜头角色一致性与整体评分上取得最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多镜头视频生成器单次仅能渲染有限时长，长片需由局部片段拼接，导致叙事推进不连贯、视觉身份（人物外观）跨片段漂移。目标是实现时间维度上连贯的长时程电影生成。

Method: 系统由两部分组成：(1) 全局故事规划器——用 LLM 扩展用户请求以保留/推断/补全故事细节，并用确定性代码维护分镜脚本、编译自包含的镜头提示，并从已生成历史中索引参考帧；(2) 局部窗口渲染器——采用 reference-routed sparse self-attention 与 shot-wise cross-attention，从索引帧路由身份信息，同时隔离各镜头的文本条件。数据方面构建了 350K+ 带语义状态转移、实体 grounding 与多粒度请求标注的电影镜头数据集，并建立 CaT-MBench 基准。

Result: 在 CaT-MBench 上，CaT-Movie 取得最高平均分与最一致的角色表现；评测中仅当剪辑开启新机位时才计入跨镜头一致性，避免重复构图被误判为一致。

Conclusion: 通过规划与渲染解耦、显式身份路由与严格的基准评测协议，可有效缓解长片生成的叙事漂移与身份漂移问题。项目主页：https://jinplu.github.io/CaT-Movie/ 。

Abstract: Current multi-shot video generators render only limited durations per pass, requiring long movies to be stitched from local segments across which narrative progression and visual identity drift. To synthesize long-horizon movies with Coherence across Time, we present CaT-Movie, a multi-shot generation system consisting of a global story planner and a local window renderer. The planner expands user requests via a large language model to preserve, infer, and complete story details, while deterministic code maintains the resulting storyboard across shots to compile self-contained shot prompts and index reference frames from generated history. To execute this guidance, the renderer applies reference-routed sparse self-attention and shot-wise cross-attention to route identities from the indexed frames while isolating per-shot textual conditions. Given the scarcity of high-quality open-source multi-shot data, we construct a dataset of over 350K film shots annotated with semantic state transitions, entity grounding, and multi-granularity requests. We further build CaT-MBench, a benchmark on held-out films with the same annotations. It gives every baseline its native format and counts cross-shot consistency only when a cut opens a new camera setup, preventing repeated framing from passing as consistency. On CaT-MBench, CaT-Movie attains the highest average score and the most consistent characters among all methods. The project page is available at https://jinplu.github.io/CaT-Movie/.

</details>


### [35] [Query-Aware vs. Query-Agnostic Training-Free Video Token Pruning in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25537)
*Divya Kothandaraman,Timo Kumkel,Jaclyn Pytlarz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对21种免训练、LLM前置的视频token选择方法做受控对比，结论是：默认应保持视觉覆盖，仅在任务与预算需要选择性证据检索时引入结构化查询相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型需处理大量视觉token，token剪枝对高效推理很重要；现有免训练剪枝方法在是否、如何利用用户查询上存在分歧，查询条件化的实际价值尚不明确，因此需要系统性受控研究。

Method: 固定Qwen-3-VL骨干、token预算与解码设置，在免训练、pre-LLM阶段比较21种剪枝原则，涵盖查询无关、浅层查询条件化、结构化查询条件化与整体化四类策略；并提出结构化查询剪枝（将问题分解为语义锚点）及Pareto变体（结合锚点相关性与查询无关的视觉多样性）。

Result: 在Video-MME、MVBench、LongVideoBench上，原始问题嵌入的相关性不可靠，比最优方法低8–16分；查询分解得到的锚点可避免该失效。但查询访问并非天然占优，强查询无关覆盖方法在全场对比中仍有竞争力甚至更优。相较仅去冗余基线，结构化选择在紧预算下的证据特定与长上下文任务上收益最大；在广泛任务上，整体化选择器优于单纯相关性或冗余性。

Conclusion: 提出实用设计规则：默认保留视觉覆盖，当任务类型与token预算需要选择性证据检索时再叠加结构化相关性；剪枝可降低计算成本与内存占用。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models process large numbers of visual tokens, making token pruning important for efficient inference. Training-free pruning methods differ in whether and how they use the user query, and the value of query conditioning remains unclear. We present a controlled study of training-free, pre-LLM video token selection. Using a shared Qwen-3-VL backbone, token budgets, and decoding settings, we compare 21 pruning principles spanning query-agnostic, shallow query-conditioned, structured query-conditioned, and holistic strategies. To instantiate the last two categories, we introduce Structured Query Pruning, which decomposes the question into semantic anchors, and a Pareto variant, which combines anchor relevance with query-agnostic visual diversity. Across Video-MME, MVBench, and LongVideoBench, relevance computed from the raw question embedding is unreliable (8--16 points below the best methods), whereas anchors derived from query decomposition avoid this failure. However, query access is not inherently advantageous: strong query-agnostic coverage methods remain competitive or superior in the full-field comparison. Relative to a redundancy-only baseline, structured selection helps most on evidence-specific and long-context tasks at tight budgets; on broad tasks, the holistic selector improves over either relevance or redundancy alone. Further, pruning reduces cost and memory. These results support a practical design rule: preserve visual coverage by default, and add structured relevance when the task and token budget demand selective evidence retrieval.

</details>


### [36] [Persona Lenses: What Makes a Vision-Language Model See Through Its Persona's Eyes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25508)
*Yoonho Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"persona lenses"（文本透镜、像素透镜、权重透镜）来放大视觉语言模型中的人格提示对模拟注视点的影响，并系统比较三种透镜与模型规模的作用，发现透镜选择取决于成本与模型大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型已可用于模拟用户在网页上的注视位置，但仅靠提示中的人格（persona）只能部分影响模拟注视，人格信息的"抵达程度"有限。作者希望量化并增强这种影响，明确人格在何种干预方式下能有效塑造注视行为。

Method: 将"persona lens"置于模型与页面之间，定义三种透镜：文本透镜（两步提示，模型先写出该人格会关注什么）、像素透镜（在与人格兴趣匹配的区域上打标记）、权重透镜（以注视停留奖励为门控，保持扫描路径自然）。用"reach"指标衡量同一模型在两个仅差单一属性的MatrAIx人格间的注视差异。在生成页面与真实页面上，使用Qwen3-VL（4B、32B）和Qwen3.5-9B，对三种透镜互相比较、与模型规模比较，并与无透镜的32B基线比较。

Result: 文本透镜对人格的区分度强，但对不应改变注视的画像变化不鲁棒；像素透镜区分度较弱，但不受与注视无关属性的影响；在4B与9B上，权重透镜能在未参与训练的留出兴趣上区分人格，且在新页面上无需额外步骤；32B模型仅用基础提示即可区分人格。

Conclusion: 不同透镜在区分强度与鲁棒性之间存在权衡，大模型自身的规模可替代显式透镜。实际应用中透镜的选择应依据计算成本与模型规模综合决定。

Abstract: Vision-language models have been used to simulate where a user looks on a web page. However, a persona in the prompt reaches the simulated gaze only partly. We measure this reach as the difference in one model's gaze between two MatrAIx personas that differ in a single attribute. We introduce persona lenses, which place the persona between the model and the page: a text lens (a two-step prompt in which the model first writes what the persona would look at), a pixel lens (marks on the region matching the persona's interests) and a weight lens (a dwell reward gated to keep the scanpath natural).
