Using Qwen3-VL (4B and 32B) and Qwen3.5-9B on generated and live pages, we compare the lenses with one another and with scale, the 32B model with no lens. It has been confirmed that the text lens separates the personas strongly but is not robust to profile changes that should not move gaze. The pixel lens separates the personas less but is unaffected by attributes unrelated to gaze. At 4B and 9B, the weight lens separates the personas on interests held out from training, with no extra step on a new page. The 32B model separates the personas with the base prompt alone. Therefore, the choice of a lens depends on cost and model size.

</details>


### [37] [Limits of local curvature for constructing search subspaces in segmentation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25822)
*Maxim Bolshim,Alexander Kugaevskikh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文考察由局部曲率构造的子空间能否代表语义分割模型在有限步长后梯度更新场的变化，指出评估搜索子空间应同时衡量其对梯度场变化与局部曲率响应的表征精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作常用局部曲率子空间近似梯度更新，但缺乏对"有限步后梯度场变化"与"局部曲率响应"表征精度的直接、对照性评估，作者希望厘清这类子空间到底能多好地刻画实际更新。

Method: 在50张图像、两个分割模型上，计算固定子空间内达到的损失增量与全空间损失增量之比（随搜索半径变化）；在10张图像上，以相同秩16对比两种子空间：一种在初始梯度上加入广义Gauss-Newton方向，另一种加入初始梯度的曲率响应方向；采用相同初始状态、相同实际步长与测量向量，并用固定像素权重的分解分析有限步余项。

Result: 子空间内/全空间损失增量比随搜索半径增大而下降。加入广义Gauss-Newton方向后，场变化的平均相对表征误差从0.922降至0.874（模型一）、从0.819降至0.763（模型二）。同秩下加入初始梯度响应方向几乎消除了局部响应的表征误差，但场变化平均误差并未降低。分解显示有限步余项在范数上与场变化相当，且对步后场平方投影误差的对角贡献最大。

Conclusion: 局部曲率子空间对局部响应刻画良好，但对有限步后梯度场变化的表征仍有限；评估搜索子空间须分别度量其对场变化与局部响应的表征精度，不能仅凭单一指标判断。

Abstract: We examine how well subspaces constructed from local curvature represent the change in the gradient update field after a finite step in semantic segmentation models. The local response is the action of the curvature operator on the actual input displacement. In a study of 50 images and two models, we measure the ratio of the loss increase attained in a fixed subspace to that attained in the full space. This ratio decreases as the search radius increases. On ten images from the same sample, we compare the one-dimensional subspace spanned by the initial gradient with two rank-16 subspaces for both models. The two equal-rank subspaces are assessed at the same initial states, using the same actual steps and measured vectors. Adding generalised Gauss-Newton directions to the initial gradient reduces the mean relative error in representing the field change from 0.922 to 0.874 for the first model and from 0.819 to 0.763 for the second. At the same rank, including the response direction for the initial gradient nearly eliminates the error in representing the local response. The mean error in representing the field change does not decrease. A decomposition with fixed pixel weights shows that the finite-step remainder is comparable in norm to the field change and makes the largest diagonal contribution to the squared projection error of the field after the step. Evaluation of a search subspace should measure how accurately it represents the field change and the local response.

</details>


### [38] [UniReFP: Robust Unified Fingerprinting for Vision Models against Cross-Task Repurposing Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25555)
*Ziye Geng,Guang Yang,Yihang Chen,Lingfeng Yao,Xing Shi,Miao Pan,Xin Fu,Changqing Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文首次提出"跨任务模型再利用攻击"会使现有模型指纹方法失效，并提出统一的模型指纹框架UniReFP，在共享的类级语义空间而非任务特定输出空间中构造所有权证据，从而在模型被跨任务复用时仍能有效验证所有权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型指纹方法依赖模型原始任务特定的输出空间，当受保护模型被改造并复用到其他任务（输出格式异构）时，指纹失效；同时还需抵御常见的模型修改攻击及其组合。

Method: UniReFP通过"再利用"受保护模型构造代理分类器，使指纹生成独立于原任务相关输出空间；优化分组指纹，使同组指纹在代理分类器上产生一致的语义响应与中间表示，而在独立训练的参考分类器上响应分散；验证阶段采用任务感知的语义投影机制，将异构嫌疑模型输出映射为统一语义存在向量，并通过组内一致性度量判定所有权。

Result: 大量实验表明UniReFP在跨任务再利用攻击、常见模型修改攻击及二者组合场景下均持续取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 将所有权证据嵌入共享类级语义空间是提升模型指纹跨任务鲁棒性的有效途径，UniReFP为异构任务下的统一所有权验证提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We identify, for the first time, a new model modification attack--the cross-task model repurposing attack--that can render fingerprints generated by existing model fingerprinting approaches inapplicable. To address this challenge, we propose UniReFP--a novel unified model fingerprinting framework applicable to diverse vision models across different tasks--that utilizes ownership evidence in a shared class-level semantic space rather than original task-specific output space, enabling effective ownership verification when a protected model is repurposed across tasks with heterogeneous output formats. Specifically, UniReFP constructs a surrogate classifier by repurposing the protected model, enabling fingerprints to be generated independently of the original task-dependent output space of the protected model. Moreover, it optimizes grouped fingerprints to encode ownership evidence: fingerprints within the same group are encouraged to induce consistent semantic responses and intermediate representations on the surrogate classifier, while producing dispersed responses on independently trained reference classifiers. During verification, UniReFP uses a task-aware semantic projection mechanism to map heterogeneous suspect-model outputs into unified semantic-presence vectors, and determines ownership by measuring inner-group consistency. Extensive experimental results validate the effectiveness of UniReFP, consistently showing superior performance under cross-task repurposing attacks, commonly studied model modification attacks, and even their combinations.

</details>


### [39] [Fusing Multimodal CNN Features with Structurally-Masked Transformers for Hierarchical Sub-Pixel Tree Species Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25824)
*Mattia Ferrari,Lorenzo Bruzzone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种层级感知多模态深度学习框架，融合PRISMA高光谱、高分辨率全色与DEM数据，用结构掩码Transformer约束宽类-细粒度树种的分类一致性，实现亚像元森林物种组成制图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载高光谱森林物种精细制图困难：树冠光谱高度相似，天然林分存在亚像元异质性；传统CNN依赖空间上下文，在破碎或混交林中易引入噪声。

Method: 多分支编码器：EfficientNetV2骨干的一维CNN提取光谱特征，孪生2D CNN分别提取空间与地形特征；接入新型结构掩码Transformer，以二值转移张量条件化slot嵌入并约束推理，强制宽森林类别与细粒度物种分布间的分类学一致性。

Result: 在意大利三个生物地理区（阿尔卑斯、中部与南部亚平宁）野外样地验证中，持续优于现有多标签与排序类方法，尤其在从属物种识别及其相对优势度估计上表现突出；双向学习使亚像元组成特征反哺宏观林型预测，地形先验有效消解复杂地形下的光谱歧义。

Conclusion: 该知识驱动方法在所评估的PRISMA场景中支持区域尺度生物多样性监测与森林表征。

Abstract: Precise forest species mapping using spaceborne hyperspectral data is challenging due to high spectral similarity and the inherent sub-pixel heterogeneity of natural forest stands. While traditional deep learning models exploit spatial context through convolutional architectures, these approaches can introduce noise in fragmented or mixed-species forests. In this paper, we propose a hierarchy-aware, multimodal deep learning framework to resolve sub-pixel forest composition by integrating PRISMA (PRecursore IperSpettrale della Missione Applicativa) hyperspectral imagery, high-resolution panchromatic data, and digital elevation models. The architecture features a multi-branch encoder combining an EfficientNetV2-based one-dimensional convolutional neural network (CNN) for spectral signatures and twin two-dimensional CNNs for spatial and topographic features, coupled with a novel structurally-masked Transformer. By using a binary transition tensor to condition the Transformer slot embeddings and constrain inference, the model enforces taxonomic consistency between broad forest categories and fine-grained species distributions. The framework is validated across three diverse Italian biogeographical regions (the Alps and the Central and Southern Apennines) using field-surveyed plots. The proposed method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-label and ranking-based paradigms, particularly in identifying subordinate species and their relative dominance. The bidirectional learning mechanism allows sub-pixel compositional features to refine macroscopic forest type predictions, while the integration of topographic priors effectively resolves spectral ambiguities in complex terrains. This knowledge-driven method supports regional-scale biodiversity monitoring and forest characterization within the evaluated PRISMA scenarios.

</details>


### [40] [SCCV-6D: Sparse Canonical Coordinate Voting for Holistic Multi-View RGB-D 6D Pose Estimation in Robotic Bin Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25819)
*Nico Leuze,Samed Dogan,Maximilian Hoh,Henry Schaub,Alfred Schöttl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCCV-6D，一种全稀疏的多视角 RGB-D 6D 位姿估计器，通过单次整体式（holistic）前向在高分辨率下同时预测任意数量物体的类别、平移与旋转，在 IPD 上取得 0.826 AP、单场景 1.13 s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业料箱抓取（bin picking）场景存在杂乱堆叠、部分遮挡、反光与弱纹理等问题，需要高精度位姿；多阶段流水线精度高但内存与运行开销难以满足实际部署约束，而现有整体式方法依赖无差别下采样或降低分辨率来控制成本，牺牲了精度。

Method: 1) 多视角 RGB-D 全稀疏重建，达到毫米级精度的三维空间表示；2) 分阶段热图机制剪除背景，并在最高分辨率下对任意数量目标同时预测类别、平移偏移与旋转；3) 旋转建模为规范坐标投票：各点带置信度投票至物体的规范坐标，配合对称性感知的加权 Procrustes 闭式求解，并借助偏移投票实现簇内点恢复，从而省去耗时的后处理阶段；4) 复用热图分支中任务监督的注意力先验，构造注意力门控的蒸馏目标，对师生特征对齐进行重加权。

Result: IPD 测试集上达到 0.826 AP，单场景耗时 1.13 s，显著快于领先方法；MV-YCB 上达到 96.1 ADD-S；在 ROBI 密集料箱场景中仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 以全稀疏整体式管线替代多阶段流程，在保持高分辨率与高精度的同时显著降低内存与时间开销，并通过规范坐标投票与注意力门控蒸馏去除后处理环节，适用于工业料箱抓取的实时部署。

Abstract: Industrial bin picking requires pose estimates that are accurate under clutter, partial occlusion, and the reflective, texture-less nature of manufactured parts. Most recent approaches to 6D pose estimation follow multi-stage pipelines. These achieve high accuracy, but incur memory and runtime footprints that do not cope with the operational constraints of many real-world industrial deployments. Holistic pipelines estimate poses in a single pass, with cost controlled through indiscriminate subsampling or lowered resolution. Continuing this line, we present SCCV-6D, a fully sparse multi-view RGB-D estimator. We reconstruct the 3D space at millimeter precision, prune background via a staged heatmap mechanism, and predict class, translation offset and rotation at highest resolution for an arbitrary number of objects simultaneously. Rotation is formulated as canonical coordinate voting. Points vote for their canonical object coordinates with an associated confidence, enabling closed-form pose recovery with a symmetry-aware weighted Procrustes solve. Along an in-cluster point recovery induced by offset votes, this removes time-consuming post-processing stages. We reuse the task-supervised attention priors from the heatmap formulation for a novel attention-gated distillation objective that reweights teacher-student feature alignment. On the IPD test split we report competitive 0.826 AP at 1.13 s per scene, significantly faster than the leading entries. On MV-YCB our approach reaches 96.1 ADD-S, and remains competitive even on the densely packed bins of ROBI.

</details>


### [41] [Interpreting DeepStack-Style Vision-Language Models: Do Input or Deeper-Layer Visual Tokens Drive Answers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25713)
*Yucheng Du,Yi Nian,Ruixue Lian,Yue Zhao,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统检验DeepStack式视觉语言模型中"后置视觉注入"（later entries）的作用：发现其在图像冲突时往往主导答案，且在主入口被削弱时影响增强、能纠正错误；并据此提出分割推理中的低比特传输方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型在输入层已获得完整图像（main entry），却仍在深层再次注入视觉token（later entries）。这些重复入口究竟贡献什么、是否冗余，缺乏因果层面的证据；这既关系到对多模态架构的理解，也关系到推理效率优化（如分割推理中的传输开销）。

Method: 采用"冲突探针"因果实验：构造仅在某物体颜色上不同的两组视觉入口版本，观察答案跟随哪一入口。步骤包括：(1) 真实照片上测试DeepStack与Qwen3-VL-8B/32B的跟随倾向；(2) 合成图像上保留冲突、仅降低主入口颜色饱和度，测量后置特征对两色相对分数的边际影响，并纳入Granite Vision；(3) 同时退化两个入口、仅恢复后置入口，测试后置特征能否纠错（条纹方向任务，施加相同模糊）；(4) 用"单入口干净"时的准确率预测模型可承受丢失哪个入口，并在GQA属性题上验证；(5) 将被预测为冗余的入口置零以测损失，并据此在分割推理中仅传输主入口特征（更高精度/更低位宽）。

Result: 照片上原始DeepStack跟随后置入口，Qwen3-VL-8B/32B跟随主入口。合成图上仅削弱主入口饱和度后，未改动的后置特征对两色相对分数的影响在四个模型（含Granite Vision）中均增强，且在无冲突准确率仍为100%的轻度去饱和下即成立。纠错实验中，同一模糊使两个Qwen模型在条纹方向上损失50个百分点准确率；恢复后置入口后32B回升48点、8B仅8点。"单入口干净"的准确率成功预测各模型可丢失的入口，6条预测中5条在新GQA属性题上成立。置零另一入口时，Qwen3-VL-8B仅损0.3点（仅主入口），DeepStack在任一入口单独使用时损失至多3.45点。基于此的分割推理中，DeepStack仅以更高精度发送主入口特征，比全入口统一精度少49%字节而准确率相近。

Conclusion: 后置视觉入口并非冗余复制，而是携带独立且可因果影响输出的信息：在入口间冲突时可能主导答案，在信息被削弱时可补偿，并能纠正另一入口造成的错误；不同模型的入口依赖程度存在结构差异（DeepStack偏后置、Qwen3-VL偏主入口）。这种可预测的入口冗余性可直接用于分割推理的通信压缩，在保持精度的同时显著降低传输量。

Abstract: DeepStack-style vision-language models feed visual tokens to the language model at its input, and again at deeper layers. We call these the main entry and the later entries. What do the later entries add when the model already has the image? We give the entries versions that differ in one object's color and see which one the answer follows. On photographs, the original DeepStack follows the later entries, while Qwen3-VL-8B/32B follow the main entry. Does weakening the main entry give the later features more influence? On synthetic images, we keep this conflict and reduce color saturation at the main entry only. The unchanged later features gain a stronger effect on the relative scores of the two colors, in all four models, including Granite Vision. This holds even under mild desaturation, when accuracy without the conflict stays at 100%. To test whether later features can also correct errors, we degrade both entries and restore only the later ones. The same blur costs both Qwen models 50 percentage points of accuracy on stripe orientation. The 32B model recovers 48 points and the 8B model only 8. Accuracy with one clean entry predicted which entry each model can lose, and five of six tested predictions held on new GQA attribute questions. With the other entry zeroed, Qwen3-VL-8B loses only 0.3 points on its main entry alone, and DeepStack at most 3.45 points on either entry alone. In split inference on these questions, DeepStack can then send only its main-entry features at higher precision. This takes 49% fewer bytes than all entries at a shared precision, at similar accuracy.

</details>


### [42] [Slide2PPT: Native PowerPoint Reconstruction with Localization-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25708)
*Baoliang Chen,Xinlong Bu,Lingyu Zhu,Xiangjie Sui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、模型无关的 Slide2PPT 框架，使视觉语言模型能够将幻灯片图像重建为具有原生对象结构的可编辑 PPTX；并构建 Slide2PPT-Bench 基准。结果优于直接代码生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到幻灯片的可编辑重建需要同时恢复视觉外观与原生对象结构。现有方法存在两类问题：视觉上逼真的图表/表格缺乏可编辑的数据系列与单元格；直接代码生成则将内容预测与渲染实现耦合，难以迁移到不同骨干模型。

Method: 1）统一类型化接口：模型只预测对象记录（object records），由固定渲染器物化其原生属性，实现免训练、免架构改动地接入开源/闭源 VLM 骨干。2）定位引导的局部精修（localization-guided refinement）：将视觉差异映射回对象记录进行修正；区域更新保留未选中记录，整页更新处理跨区域关系。3）支持记录级精修与目标对象编辑（结构与属性级），以渲染反馈驱动迭代接受。

Result: 在开源与闭源骨干上，PPTX 生成可靠性显著高于直接代码生成；科学图表上的布局与文本恢复更优；提供超越几何图元的原生图表与表格接口。基准分别评测文件可生成性、渲染一致性与原生对象可用性。

Conclusion: 将内容预测与渲染解耦的类型化记录范式可有效提升图像到 PPTX 的原生可编辑重建质量，并具备跨骨干的通用性；配套基准支持对受控元素、真实幻灯片与科学图表的系统评估。

Abstract: Image-to-slide reconstruction must recover both visual appearance and native object structure for subsequent editing. Yet visually faithful charts and tables may lack editable series and cells, and direct code generation couples content prediction to rendering implementation. We present Slide2PPT, a model-agnostic, training-free framework that equips both open- and closed-source vision-language models with native PowerPoint reconstruction capabilities. Through a unified typed interface, the model predicts object records while a fixed renderer materializes their native properties, enabling integration with different backbones without task-specific training or architectural changes. Localization-guided refinement then connects visual discrepancies to object records for correction. Regional updates preserve unselected records, while whole-slide updates address cross-region relationships. Record refinement and targeted object editing support structural and attribute-level changes, with rendered feedback governing iterative acceptance. To evaluate this framework across diverse reconstruction demands, we introduce Slide2PPT-Bench, covering controlled elements, real slides, and scientific figures. Evaluation separately measures file production, rendered agreement, and native-object availability. Experiments across open- and closed-source backbones demonstrate substantially more reliable PPTX generation than direct code generation, improved layout and text recovery on scientific figures, and native chart and table interfaces beyond geometric primitives.

</details>


### [43] [LIFT: Lifting Language-Controlled Image Fusion to Free-Viewpoint 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25696)
*Jingning Tian,Hao Zhang,Yumin Huang,Zizhuo Li,Xingyu Jiang,Erdun Zhao,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LIFT框架，将多模态图像融合从2D固定视角扩展到语言控制的三维自由视角融合（L3D-IF），实现跨视角一致、语义可控的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态图像融合仅在固定视角下由配对观测生成单一融合图像，难以支持全面场景感知和对多样感知目标的适应。

Method: 1) Semantic Modality Binding（SMB）：解析语言指令识别语义目标及其模态偏好，并将其锚定到共享高斯场景的对应区域，建立持久的语义—空间对应关系以支持跨视角一致控制；2) Language-Conditioned Gaussian Appearance（LCGA）：结合自适应多模态混合与语言/视角条件化的外观细化，实现场景级外观建模，并在纯模态端点保持源保真；3) 构建Multi-View Language-Supervision（MVLS）数据集，将语言指令与标定视角关联到调制后的融合图像，提供指令依赖融合的监督。

Result: 实验表明LIFT可在连续视角下实现连贯的多模态融合、准确的语义目标定位，以及在多样语言指令下一致的区域融合控制。

Conclusion: LIFT将多模态融合推广至语言引导的三维自由视角范式，为面向多样感知目标的可控融合提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion traditionally generates a single fused image from paired observations at a specific viewpoint, limiting comprehensive scene perception and adaptability to diverse perceptual objectives. To overcome these limitations, we present LIFT, a novel framework for our proposed language-controlled 3D image fusion (L3D-IF) paradigm that extends multimodal image fusion from 2D images to free-viewpoint 3D scenes, enabling language-guided fusion tailored to diverse perceptual objectives. First, LIFT formulates Semantic Modality Binding (SMB), which interprets language instructions to identify semantic targets and their associated modality preferences and grounds these targets in corresponding regions of a shared Gaussian scene, establishing persistent semantic-to-spatial correspondences for consistent fusion control across viewpoints. Second, Language-Conditioned Gaussian Appearance (LCGA) is developed to achieve scene-wide multimodal appearance modeling by combining adaptive multimodal blending with language- and viewpoint-conditioned appearance refinement, enabling spatially adaptive realization of diverse fusion appearances while preserving source fidelity at pure-modality endpoints. Finally, we construct a Multi-View Language-Supervision (MVLS) dataset that associates language instructions and calibrated viewpoints with modulated fused images, providing effective supervision for learning instruction-dependent fusion from finite multi-view observations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LIFT achieves coherent multimodal fusion across continuous viewpoints, accurate semantic target localization, and consistent regional fusion control under diverse language instructions.

</details>


### [44] [Minimal Memory for Correctable Visual Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25675)
*Xiyang Zhang,Yuanhe Tian,Hongzhi Wang,Yan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究"视觉延续"中场景状态的可修正性问题：在无法回放历史图像时，仅保留足够当前渲染的摘要会丢失日后修正所需的候选信息。作者建立了稀疏与密集的匹配界，并提出Coherent Archive Memory (CAM)来存储可精确更新的几何摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉延续任务需要从过去的图像和已知动作生成未来帧，并维护一个可修改（revisable）的场景状态。当物体重新出现或后续视角改变先前解释时，这一状态必须可修正。但一旦历史图像无法回放，仅保留"足以当前渲染"的摘要，就会丢失修正后所需的候选项。因此需要刻画：为支持后续修正究竟需要多少比特信息。

Method: 理论侧：在稳定观测与有界分歧条件下，针对稀疏与密集情形建立匹配的比特下界与上界；对静态流在给定平滑度与间隔尺度下分析修正开销。方法侧：提出Coherent Archive Memory (CAM)，以裁剪式扩展（clipped expansion）与坐标分配下的精确更新存储几何摘要，再利用历史存在性约束为所有请求帧选择一个状态。

Result: 在所述平滑度与间隔尺度下，静态流的修正至少带来一维的对数开销，高维则为多项式开销。穷尽的摄影测试显示，联合见证（joint witnesses）在严格最坏真实图像风险下取得增益；受控的神经延续实验能够恢复在当前视角中被隐藏的位置。

Conclusion: 可修正场景状态存在内在的信息代价：在当前渲染摘要之外，修正需额外（对数或多项式）开销。CAM通过保留几何摘要与历史存在性约束，在理论界内实现了可精确修正的记忆机制，并在图像风险与隐藏位置恢复上得到验证。

Abstract: Given past images and known actions, visual continuation generates future frames from one evolving scene state. Keeping this state revisable matters when an object reappears or a later view changes an earlier interpretation. Once past images cannot be replayed, a summary sufficient for current rendering can lose candidates needed after correction. We establish matching sparse and dense bounds on the bits needed for later correction under stable observations and bounded disagreement. For static streams at the stated smoothness and margin scales, correction incurs at least logarithmic overhead in one dimension and polynomial overhead in higher dimensions. Coherent Archive Memory (CAM) stores geometric summaries with exact updates under clipped expansion and coordinate assignments, then uses historical existence constraints to select one state for all requested frames. Exhaustive photographic tests show strict worst-truth image-risk gains from joint witnesses, while controlled neural continuations recover positions hidden in the current view.

</details>


### [45] [SEAF: Selective Semantic Enrichment and Visual Evidence Fusion for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25672)
*Faxian Wan,Xiaocui Yang,Yifan Cao,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双分支框架SEAF，用冻结MLLM生成实体背景与图像描述，分别用于语义增强和视觉证据对齐融合，在MMSD3.0多图讽刺检测上超越所有对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态讽刺检测需文本、视觉与上下文证据，多图场景下图像提供互补线索；MLLM可生成辅助信息，但其输出混杂有用背景知识与冗余视觉描述，需选择性利用。

Method: SEAF将生成字段分角色使用：实体背景进入语义分支，图像描述提供对齐统计。语义分支融合原文、冻结MLLM背景与OCR文本；对齐视觉分支融合全局/局部视觉特征及跨模态与图文间相似度统计。提出Text-Anchored Smoothed Aggregation（TASA），按图像与原文相似度加权并保留低字面相似图像的贡献；两分支分类得分相加完成融合。

Result: 在多图MMSD3.0基准上，SEAF优于所有对比方法。

Conclusion: 对MLLM生成信息进行角色化分工并辅以文本锚定的平滑加权聚合，可有效整合上下文语义与视觉证据，提升多图多模态讽刺检测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection requires textual, visual, and contextual evidence, particularly when multiple images provide complementary cues. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate auxiliary information from a post's text and images, but their outputs may mix useful background knowledge with redundant visual descriptions. We propose SEAF, a dual-branch framework for selective semantic enrichment and visual evidence fusion. SEAF assigns generated fields to distinct roles: entity background enriches semantic input, while image descriptions supply alignment statistics. The semantic branch combines the original post text, background from a frozen MLLM, and optical character recognition (OCR) text. The aligned visual branch combines global and local visual features with statistics of cross-modal and inter-image similarity. Text-Anchored Smoothed Aggregation (TASA) weights images by their similarity to the original post text while preserving contributions from images with low literal similarity. Summing the branch classification scores integrates contextual semantics with visual evidence. Experiments on the multi-image MMSD3.0 benchmark show that SEAF outperforms all compared methods.

</details>


### [46] [VidToC: Rethinking Video Token Compression for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25574)
*Zihan Xiao,Boshen Xu,Pei Fu,Jianzhong Ju,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VidToC 是一个受控评测框架，系统比较视频视觉 token 压缩算法、LLM 骨干与时间编码策略，覆盖免训练与微调设定及三类互补视频理解任务，揭示了任务相关的压缩行为并刻画了压缩位置、时间编码与骨干选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 MLLM 从图像扩展到视频时，海量视觉 token 带来显著内存与延迟开销，催生了大量视觉 token 压缩方法与更高效的 LLM 架构；但现有压缩方法在训练设置、任务和模型上各不相同，难以公平比较，同时混合 Mamba-Transformer 骨干的兴起也使压缩策略能否跨骨干迁移成为未知问题。

Method: 提出 VidToC 受控评测框架，对代表性压缩算法、LLM 骨干与时间编码策略进行统一评测，覆盖免训练与微调两种设定，并在三个互补的视频理解任务上进行实验。

Result: 实验揭示压缩行为具有任务依赖性，并刻画了压缩插入位置、时间编码方式与骨干架构选择对性能的具体影响。

Conclusion: 研究结论为在不同任务与训练设定下设计与选择视频 token 压缩策略提供了实证性指导。

Abstract: Extending multimodal large language models (MLLMs) from images to videos faces substantial memory and latency challenges due to the massive number of visual tokens. These challenges have driven the development of numerous visual token compression methods and more efficient LLM architectures. However, existing compression methods are often evaluated with different training setups, tasks, and models, making fair comparison difficult. Meanwhile, the growing use of hybrid Mamba-Transformer architectures raises questions about whether compression strategies transfer across backbones. We present VidToC, a controlled evaluation framework for representative compression algorithms, LLM backbones, and time encoding strategies, covering training-free and fine-tuned settings on three complementary video understanding tasks. Our results reveal task-dependent compression behavior and characterize the effects of compression placement, time encoding, and backbone choice. These findings provide empirical guidance for designing and selecting video token compression strategies across tasks and training settings.

</details>


### [47] [CANDOR: Contrastive Self-Denoising Rewards for Label-Free Text-to-Image Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25457)
*Junhyeok Lee,Byung-Hoon Kim,Kyu Sung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CANDOR内在奖励：用"无条件/条件去噪误差的对数比"衡量提示词对去噪的帮助程度，作为Flow-GRPO的奖励，无需外部奖励模型即可提升文本渲染、美学与人类偏好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成器的强化学习后训练依赖外部奖励模型，需要标注偏好或辅助评估器，且易发生奖励作弊。已有的"内在奖励"（生成器自身去噪准确率，即绝对自置信度）在提示保真度上只是糟糕的代理：同一提示内的绝对置信度被图像复杂度主导，对正确样本的排序接近随机水平。

Method: 提出CANDOR（Contrastive Alignment from Null-referenced Denoising lOg-Ratio）：在共享噪声探针下，计算无条件去噪误差与条件去噪误差的对数比，度量提示词使模型更好地去噪其自身样本的程度。该比值抵消了同时放大两种误差的、与提示无关的难度因素。将其作为流匹配模型的唯一 Flow-GRPO 奖励。

Result: 在同提示成对比较中，CANDOR在组合生成与文本渲染任务上分别以60%和63%的准确率选出更优样本。作为唯一奖励，相比基座模型与先前的绝对自置信度奖励，提升文本渲染、美学和人类偏好分数；文本渲染与偏好增益无法通过更强 classifier-free guidance 复现。在基于外部奖励训练的公开 Flow-GRPO 适配器上，CANDOR进一步提高了PickScore与ImageReward。

Conclusion: 去噪误差的对数比（而非绝对自置信度）是提示保真度的有效内在信号；CANDOR可作为独立奖励替代外部奖励模型，也可与外部奖励互补叠加使用。

Abstract: Post-training text-to-image generators with reinforcement learning usually relies on external reward models that need annotated preferences or auxiliary evaluators and are prone to reward hacking. We show that the generator's own denoising accuracy, used by a recent intrinsic reward, is a poor proxy for prompt fidelity. Within a same-prompt group, this absolute self-confidence is dominated by image complexity and ranks correct samples above incorrect ones near chance. We introduce CANDOR (Contrastive Alignment from Null-referenced Denoising lOg-Ratio), the log-ratio of unconditional to conditional denoising errors under shared noise probes, which measures how much the prompt helps the model denoise its own sample. It cancels prompt-independent difficulty that scales both errors and ranks the better of two same-prompt samples higher in 60% and 63% of pairs on compositional generation and text rendering. As the only Flow-GRPO reward for a flow-matching model, CANDOR improves text rendering, aesthetic and human-preference scores over the base model and a prior absolute self-confidence reward, with text-rendering and preference gains that stronger classifier-free guidance does not reproduce. On public Flow-GRPO adapters trained on external rewards, CANDOR further raises PickScore and ImageReward, so it can complement those rewards. Code is available at https://github.com/jhlee0619/candor.

</details>


### [48] [VisualTalk-Bench: Evaluating Question-Driven Avatar-Led Video Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25444)
*Xin Zhang,Tujie Xu,Zhiyi Chen,Xiaodong Zhang,Sicheng Yang,Minglei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VisualTalk-Bench 基准与 Avatar-O Agent 模块化实现，用于评估"由虚拟人主导、基于问题驱动的视频讲解"，并揭示事实准确性与视觉质量并不能保证准确的指向（pointing）行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成与虚拟人合成技术进步，使得通过视觉解释回答用户问题成为可能（需正确作答、将信息转为视觉素材、并以虚拟人语音与动作讲解）。但现有基准很少评估虚拟人是否真正指向所讲的视觉内容，缺乏对该能力的系统性评测。

Method: 构建 VisualTalk-Bench，覆盖金融与中英文教育场景，含 800 条筛选参考视频与 100 条独立评测请求；定义五项评测维度：内容事实性、语音-视觉一致性、视觉质量、运动平滑度、虚拟人-内容指向。另提出 Avatar-O Agent，一个带显式交互规划的模块化参考实现，并与六个 agent 进行对比实验及 26 人用户研究。

Result: 六种 agent 实验表明事实精度与视觉质量不能保证指向准确；26 名参与者对四种 agent 的对比显示 Avatar-O Agent 在视觉引导、感知指向准确性与讲解有效性上获得最高平均评分。

Conclusion: 端到端视频生成器难以将手势与具体视觉目标对齐，显式交互规划的模块化设计可有效提升指向与讲解效果；数据与代码将公开，为该任务提供评测基础。

Abstract: Advances in video generation and avatar synthesis enable new ways to answer user questions through visual explanations. This task requires a system to formulate a correct answer, translate relevant information into visual material, and deliver the explanation through an avatar's speech and actions. Existing benchmarks rarely assess whether avatars point to the visual content being discussed. We introduce VisualTalk-Bench for question-driven avatar-led video explanations in finance and Chinese and English education. It includes 800 curated reference videos and 100 independent evaluation requests, assessing Content Factuality, Speech-Visual Consistency, Visual Quality, Motion Smoothness, and Avatar-Content Pointing. Yet end-to-end video generators often struggle to align gestures with specific visual targets. To explore this challenge, we develop Avatar-O Agent, a modular reference implementation with explicit interaction planning. Experiments across six agents show that factual precision and visual quality do not guarantee accurate pointing. A 26-participant study comparing four agents finds that Avatar-O Agent receives the highest average ratings for visual guidance, perceived pointing accuracy, and explanation effectiveness. We will release the data and code.

</details>


### [49] [Wearable Logo, Silent Hijack: Stealthy Cross-Modal Semantic Attacks on Visible--Infrared Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25437)
*Qike Zhang,Jiaju Han,Xuelian Shi,Xiang Chen,Dehe Zhao,Banglong Nong,Wenhao Zheng,Lei Wang,Jiahuan Long,Jiujiang Guo,Chengyin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LoSA：以自然存在的logo为载体，在不改变其轮廓的前提下，通过优化内部外观与放置位置，对可见光-红外视觉语言模型实施跨模态协同定向语义攻击的黑盒框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: logo在日常生活中普遍存在，其可识别形状与语义联想使其易于融入真实环境且不显眼。作者追问：现有logo能否在保持原有轮廓不变的情况下充当载体，对VIS-IR视觉语言模型实施协同的定向语义攻击。难点在于需在固定形状内实现表达力强的对抗扰动，同时兼顾两种模态的外观差异与模型响应差异。

Method: 基于分数的黑盒框架。采用轮廓场（contour field）与低频图谱模态（graph spectral modes）构建内部颜色布局；使用冻结的外观适配器（frozen appearance adapter）耦合可见光与红外外观；以粒子群优化（PSO）联合优化共享设计参数（内部外观与放置位置），并保持剪影不变。

Result: 在四个数据集上，LoSA在同时达到Top-1定向攻击成功率方面一致优于所评估的基线；跨架构实验、视觉自然度评分以及图像描述/视觉问答迁移分析进一步刻画了其有效性与局限。

Conclusion: 来自真实环境的常见视觉符号可作为保持形状的载体，实现跨成像模态的协同定向语义攻击，揭示了物理世界中logo类语义线索对多模态模型的安全隐患。

Abstract: Logos appear naturally on clothing and daily necessities, where their recognizable shapes and semantic associations make them familiar visual cues. This familiarity helps them blend into real-world environments and remain visually inconspicuous. We ask whether an existing logo can serve as a carrier for coordinated targeted semantic attacks on visible-infrared (VIS-IR) vision-language models (VLMs) without changing its original outline. This setting requires expressive adversarial variation within a fixed shape while accounting for different appearance characteristics and model responses across modalities. We propose Logo-guided Semantic Attack (LoSA), a score-based black-box framework that optimizes a logo's interior appearance and placement while preserving its silhouette. LoSA uses a contour field and low-frequency graph spectral modes to structure the interior color layout, a frozen appearance adapter to couple VIS and IR appearances, and particle swarm optimization to jointly optimize shared design parameters. Experiments across four datasets demonstrate that LoSA consistently outperforms the evaluated baselines in simultaneous Top-1 targeted success. Cross-architecture experiments, visual naturalness ratings, and captioning/VQA transfer analyses further characterize its effectiveness and limitations. These findings reveal that familiar visual symbols from real-world environments can serve as shape-preserving carriers for coordinated targeted semantic attacks across imaging modalities.

</details>


### [50] [Zero to Four Views: Unified Panorama Generation and Preference RL for Unobserved Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25389)
*Rui Hong,Xugang Ye,Jana Košecká,Kai Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的全景图生成模型，可在文本+0~4个透视视角条件下生成等距柱状全景图，采用两阶段微调（相机槽位ID可变的token拼接 + 未覆盖区域GRPO强化学习），单视角下在Laval Indoor上超越360Anything。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景图可作为多镜头视频生成的共享背景，但现有全景生成器仅支持纯文本或单一输入图像，缺乏对多视角条件输入的灵活统一支持；且监督训练只在GT噪声隐变量上计算损失，从未评价模型自身生成并解码的全景图，导致未被任何条件视角覆盖的区域质量欠佳。

Method: 基于预训练文本到图像rectified-flow transformer进行两阶段微调：第一阶段将每个条件视角连同其相机槽位ID追加到token序列，训练中视角数K∈{0,...,4}可变，一套权重适配所有输入设置；第二阶段针对无视角覆盖区域优化，提出Uncovered-Region GRPO（UR-GRPO），以视觉语言模型在该区域裁剪块上的成对比较偏好作为奖励进行强化学习。另提供可选变体，将各视角相机方向写入位置编码，使用户可控制视角在输出中的位置。

Result: 单视角条件下，在与360Anything相同数据和评测代码下，于Laval Indoor超越该此前最优方法，在SUN360室内子集上结果互有胜负；第二阶段对每种条件视角数量均带来提升。

Conclusion: 单一模型即可支持0~4个视角的灵活条件全景生成，UR-GRPO能有效改善未覆盖区域质量；位置编码注入相机方向提供可控性，为多镜头视频生成的场景背景构建提供实用方案。

Abstract: Panoramas can serve as shared scene backgrounds for multi-shot video generation, but most existing panorama generators take text alone or a single input image. We present a single model that generates a full equirectangular panorama from a text caption and K in {0,...,4} perspective conditioning views taken by a four-camera rig at one viewpoint. We fine-tune a pretrained text-to-image rectified-flow transformer in two stages. In the first stage, each conditioning view is appended to the token sequence together with the ID of its camera slot, and the number of views varies during training, so one set of weights serves every input setting. In the second stage, we improve the region not covered by any conditioning view. Supervised training computes its loss on noisy latents of the ground truth and never scores a panorama the model has generated and decoded, so we propose Uncovered-Region GRPO (UR-GRPO), a reinforcement-learning recipe that rewards the candidates a vision-language model prefers in pairwise comparisons on crops around this region. Because the slot ID alone does not tell the model which way the rig points, we also offer an optional variant that writes each view's camera direction into its position encoding, which lets the user decide where each view appears in the output. With one conditioning view, our model outperforms the previous state of the art, 360Anything, trained on the same data and evaluated with the same code, on Laval Indoor and gives mixed results on an indoor subset of SUN360; the second stage improves the results for every number of conditioning views.

</details>


### [51] [Fold the Loop: Decoding-Time KV Memory for Looped Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25369)
*Kun Zhou,Wenmian Yang,Zhensheng Wang,Yanzeng Li,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FoldCache，在循环视觉语言模型中沿递归轴压缩视觉 KV 缓存，将同一模块内连续递归步分成组并共享组末视觉 KV，在保持精度的同时将前缀 KV 内存减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环 VLM 通过重复应用权重共享模块增强视觉推理，但解码期 KV 缓存随递归深度线性增长，每步都生成各自的 KV 状态，导致缓存中存有多份相同图像的副本，限制了固定内存预算下可用的图像尺寸与批大小。

Method: 观察到精确 prefill 之后，早期递归步仍需访问视觉信息，但可在同一模块内复用最终的视觉 KV。据此将每个模块内的递归步分组，仅保留每组最后一步的视觉 KV 供组内成员解码时共享；共享严格限制在模块边界内，问题与答案 KV 每步独立保存。分组粒度控制压缩程度，并可叠加 token 驱逐与量化进一步降内存。

Result: 在 LoopVL 上，FoldCache 将前缀 KV 内存约减半，同时在五个开放式基准上与完整缓存精度持平；相同 GPU 显存预算下支持最高 1.7 倍于完整缓存的批大小，更激进的 FoldCache-Lite 可达 2.6 倍。

Conclusion: 视觉 KV 在递归轴上的冗余可被安全折叠，为循环 VLM 提供了一种精度无损、与现有压缩手段正交的解码内存优化方案。

Abstract: Looped vision-language models (VLMs) deepen visual reasoning by repeatedly applying weight-tied modules. However, their decoding memory grows with recurrent depth and limits the image size and batch size that fit in a fixed memory budget. Each recurrent step produces its own key-value (KV) states, so the cache holds several copies of the same image. We find that, after an exact prefill, earlier steps still need access to visual information but can reuse the final visual KV within the same module. We propose FoldCache, which compresses visual KV along the recurrent axis while preserving the full prefill computation. FoldCache partitions recurrent steps within each module into groups and retains the final visual KV of each group for its members to share during decoding. Sharing stays within module boundaries, while question and answer KV remain separate for each step. The grouping granularity controls the degree of compression, and folding can be combined with token eviction and quantization to further reduce memory use. On LoopVL, FoldCache approximately halves prefix KV memory while matching full-cache accuracy on five open-ended benchmarks. These memory savings support batch sizes up to 1.7 times those of a full cache under the same GPU memory budget, and up to 2.6 times with the more aggressive FoldCache-Lite.

</details>


### [52] [Search or Scoring? Diagnosing Why Visual World-Model Planners Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25347)
*Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Chenhao Wu,Zixiang Ni,Yingguang Yang,Feng Zhang,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Jifeng Zhu,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将视觉世界模型规划器的失败拆分为"搜索失败"与"打分失败"，通过执行候选动作、用规划器自身代价给已知解打分、比较预测与真实结果的代价来区分二者，并提出 DemoProp（按预测误差大小选择热启动或直接平均示范窗口），在四种仿真环境和三种世界模型上验证诊断能指导补救选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DINO-WM、JEPA-WM 等视觉世界模型依赖优化器搜索"预测代价最低"的动作，规划可能因两类原因失败：优化器没搜到好的动作（搜索失败），或代价在预测上把坏动作排到第一（打分失败）。二者所需补救不同（加大搜索 vs 改进模型），但规划器不会记录发生了哪一类，因此需要一种可操作的诊断手段，并据此决定如何利用检索到的示范。

Method: 诊断：执行优化器给出的候选动作、用规划器自身代价对已知解打分、对比预测结果与真实结果上的代价，从而判别两类失败。理论上给出一个误差模型说明两类失败各自的产生条件。方法 DemoProp：预测误差小时以示范窗口做搜索热启动（warm start）；误差大、优化器会选到被高估动作时，不经模型直接对示范窗口取平均。实验覆盖四个仿真环境、三种世界模型，并在 PushT 上仅退化模型以检验使用方式是否翻转。

Result: 在规划器可表示目标的短任务上，PushT 与 Wall 主要因搜索失败，Metaworld 与 RoboCasa 主要因打分失败——代价在真实结果上可靠（Metaworld AUROC 0.94）但在预测上接近随机（0.54）。四倍搜索仅在搜索失败处降低真实代价（DINO-WM 上明显）；在打分失败处，无模型平均示范窗口解决 168 个新 Metaworld 任务中的 127 个，而让模型挑选最多 87 个。PushT 中仅退化模型即翻转两种用法优劣，预先冻结的规则随之切换，解决 960 个新回合中的 678 个，最佳固定用法为 530。

Conclusion: 两类规划失败可被实证区分，且诊断结果能预测哪种补救有效：搜索失败受益于更大搜索，打分失败受益于绕开模型的示范平均。基于误差大小的 DemoProp 规则无需事后调参即可自适应，性能显著优于任何固定用法。

Abstract: Visual world models such as DINO-WM and JEPA-WM plan with an optimizer that searches for actions whose predicted outcome has the lowest cost, its distance to a goal image. A plan can thus fail in two ways: the optimizer never tries a good action that the cost would prefer (a search failure), or the cost, computed on predictions, ranks a bad action first (a scoring failure). The two need different remedies, more search or a better model, but a planner does not record which one occurred. We tell them apart by executing the optimizer's candidates, scoring a known solution with the planner's own cost, and comparing costs on predicted and real outcomes. An error model explains when each arises and so how to use retrieved demonstration windows (DemoProp): start the search from them (a warm start) when prediction errors are small, and average them without the model when large errors make the optimizer pick overrated actions. Across four simulated environments and three world models, on short tasks whose goals the planner can represent, PushT and Wall fail mostly by search, while Metaworld and RoboCasa fail by scoring because the cost, sound on real outcomes (AUROC 0.94 on Metaworld), is at chance on predictions (0.54). The diagnosis tells which remedy pays off: a four times larger search lowers the real-outcome cost only where failures come from search (clearly for DINO-WM), and where they come from scoring, averaging the windows without the model solves 127 of 168 new Metaworld tasks, against at most 87 when the model chooses among them. Within PushT, degrading only the model flips which use is better, and our rule, frozen in advance, follows the flip and solves 678 of 960 new episodes, against 530 for the best fixed use.

</details>


### [53] [VideoRNG: Relightable Neural Gaussians from Generative Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25338)
*Bowen Xue,Saeed Hadadan,Fabrice Rousselle,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Zahra Montazeri,Miloš Hašan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoRNG 提出两阶段方法，从文本或单图生成完整可重光照的三维资产，不依赖网格或解析材质模型：视频扩散模型先生成已知位姿/相机/光照下的 RGBA 转台序列，再由大型重建模型将视频 VAE 潜帧直接映射为数百万各向异性高斯（rtRNG），配合共享方向解码器与随机高斯光线追踪实现新视角与新光照下的渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示受限：表面网格与紧凑的基于物理材质难以表达由精细几何或复杂光传输产生的外观；而现有 Gaussian-splatting 生成器通常把固定光照烘焙进外观，无法重光照。作者希望摆脱网格与解析材质模型的预设，直接从文本或单图生成完整、可重光照的 3D 资产。

Method: 两阶段流程：(1) 由预训练视频扩散骨干微调的视频生成器，在已知位姿、相机与光照条件下产生结构化 RGBA 转台序列；(2) 由预训练视频扩散骨干初始化的前馈大型重建模型，将所选转台帧的视频 VAE 潜表示直接映射为带学习神经外观特征的数百万各向异性高斯。共享方向解码器与随机高斯光线追踪将该表示（rtRNG）在新视角与新光照下转为辐射亮度。此外引入深度锚定的外观训练以在学几何的同时保留高频外观，并通过前景 token 选择与代价感知分布式采样使百万级高斯重建可行。

Result: 能够生成毛发、羽毛、蛛网等复杂资产，支持交互式渲染、重光照并集成到场景中。

Conclusion: 以“视频生成 + 大型重建”的两阶段范式结合可重光照神经高斯表示（rtRNG），可在不预设网格或解析材质的前提下生成完整、可重光照且可交互渲染的三维资产，拓展了对精细几何与复杂光传输类物体的表达能力。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D assets remains constrained by classical representations: surface meshes and compact physically based materials struggle to express objects whose appearance arises from fine geometry or complex light transport, while existing Gaussian-splatting generators typically bake fixed illumination into appearance. We introduce VideoRNG, a two-stage method that generates complete, relightable 3D assets from a text prompt or a single image without prescribing a mesh or analytic material model. A video generator fine-tuned from a pretrained video diffusion backbone first produces a structured RGBA turntable sequence under a known pose, camera, and illumination. A feed-forward large reconstruction model initialized from a pretrained video diffusion backbone then maps the video-VAE latent frames of selected turntable frames directly to millions of anisotropic Gaussians with learned neural appearance features. A shared directional decoder and stochastic Gaussian ray tracer turn this representation, which we call ray-traced Relightable Neural Gaussians (rtRNG), into radiance under novel viewpoints and illumination. To preserve high-frequency appearance while learning geometry, we introduce depth-anchored appearance training, and we make million-Gaussian reconstruction practical through foreground token selection and cost-aware distributed sampling. VideoRNG generates complex assets such as fur, feathers, and spider webs that can be interactively rendered, relit, and integrated into scenes.

</details>


### [54] [VoxForge: A Unified Foundation Model for Dense 3D Features Across MRI, CT and PET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25309)
*Rushil Anirudh,Jayaraman J. Thiagarajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VoxForge——一个统一的3D医学基础模型编码器，通过融合3D架构、自蒸馏与跨模态课程学习，从许可开放的MRI/CT数据中学习可迁移特征；在冻结评估下于MRI/CT的分割、分类、配准及未见过的PET任务上达到最优或并列最优平均排名，并展现出极高的标签效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用模型仍需为局部任务与全局任务分别使用不同骨干网络，专才模型局限于单一模态；跨模态训练又可能弱化细粒度解剖结构，且两者通常依赖端到端微调才具竞争力。因此需要一个真正可复用、冻结即可用的统一3D医学基础模型。

Method: 采用单一编码器，整合近期3D架构进展、自蒸馏（self-distillation）与跨模态课程（cross-modal curricula），从许可开放的MRI与CT体数据中学习可迁移特征；评估时严格冻结特征，并与SOTA通用模型和专才模型对比。

Result: 在MRI与CT的分割、分类、配准各任务组中平均排名最优或并列最优；在从未见过的PET模态上同样有效；单一编码器即可同时服务局部与全局任务并达到专才模型水平；标签效率极高——在TotalSegmentator CT上仅用十分之一标签即匹配最优模型表现。

Conclusion: 统一单一编码器可同时覆盖多模态、局部与全局任务，且冻结即具备竞争力，证明可复用3D医学基础模型在无需微调、低标注条件下切实可行。

Abstract: A unified 3D medical foundation model promises reusable, frozen features that serve a wide variety of tasks such as segmentation, classification and registration on MRI, CT and PET as well as specialist models do. Generalist models still need separate backbones for localized and global tasks, specialist models are confined to a single modality, with the concern that spanning modalities under-emphasizes fine anatomy, and both typically rely on end-to-end fine-tuning to be competitive. We present VoxForge, a single encoder that integrates recent advances in 3D architecture, self-distillation and cross-modal curricula to learn transferable features from permissively licensed MRI and CT volumes. We evaluate it strictly frozen, the test a reusable foundation model must pass, against state-of-the-art generalist and specialist models on segmentation, classification and registration across MRI and CT, and on PET, which it never sees. VoxForge has the best or tied-best average rank in every task group, serves localized and global tasks across modalities at the level of state-of-the-art specialists, and is extremely label-efficient, matching top-performing models on TotalSegmentator CT with a tenth of the labels.

</details>


### [55] [CalibCache: Cache-to-Full Feature Dynamics Calibration for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25296)
*Mingyu Liang,Dingkun Xu,Jingwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CalibCache，一种离线标定特征加速度映射的轻量方法，用于降低 Diffusion Transformer 特征缓存的近似误差，在少步采样下兼顾质量与加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformer 迭代采样计算开销大；特征缓存虽能减少完整网络评估次数，但其近似误差在短采样调度下更严重——完整计算步更少、采样网格更粗，使基于历史外推的缓存方法（如 TaylorSeer）可靠性下降。

Method: CalibCache 将特征加速度定义为跨采样步的二阶有限差分，并校正缓存与完整计算特征动态之间的系统性偏差。在每个设置下仅使用一对完整计算轨迹与朴素缓存轨迹，通过中心化最小二乘离线拟合标量加速度映射；采样时直接应用该映射，无需额外完整网络评估或在线优化。

Result: 在 FLUX.1-dev、Qwen-Image（DrawBench）及 DiT-XL/2（ImageNet-256）上，20 步采样、缓存间隔 N=3,4,5 时均优于 TaylorSeer。N=5 时 FLUX.1-dev LPIPS 从 0.563 降至 0.429，相对全计算采样取得 2.86× 去噪加速；DiT-XL/2 FID 从 16.39 降至 10.37。

Conclusion: 通过离线标定特征加速度的系统性偏差，可在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升短调度、大缓存间隔下的缓存采样质量，为扩散 Transformer 的特征缓存提供了一种低成本、即插即用的误差校正方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers generate high-quality images, but iterative sampling remains computationally expensive. Feature caching reduces the number of full network evaluations, yet its approximation error can become more severe under short sampling schedules, where fewer full-compute steps and a coarser sampling grid can make history-based extrapolation less reliable. In this paper, we propose CalibCache, a lightweight offline calibration method that corrects systematic discrepancies between cached and full-compute feature dynamics. Specifically, CalibCache calibrates feature acceleration, defined as the second-order finite difference across sampling steps. Using only a single pair of full-compute and naive-cache trajectories at each setting, CalibCache fits scalar acceleration maps offline by centered least squares. These maps are then applied during sampling without additional full-network evaluations or online optimization. Across FLUX.1-dev and Qwen-Image on DrawBench and DiT-XL/2 on ImageNet-256, CalibCache consistently improves fidelity or generation quality over TaylorSeer under 20-step sampling at cache intervals $N=3,4,5$. At $N=5$, it reduces FLUX.1-dev LPIPS from $0.563$ to $0.429$ while achieving a $2.86\times$ denoising speedup over full-compute sampling, and lowers DiT-XL/2 FID from $16.39$ to $10.37$ relative to TaylorSeer.

</details>


### [56] [Beyond Restoration: Content-Degradation Factor Unfolding for Real-World Concealed Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25239)
*Rihan Zhang,Fengyang Xiao,Yuhan Chen,Sina Farsiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出因子展开网络FUN，将Retinex扩展为“内容-退化”分解，以内容因子R作为唯一语义证据来源、退化因子L生成空间可靠性图进行校准，通过统一能量交替更新构建由粗到细网络，无需干净图像监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退化条件下的可靠视觉感知需要区分任务相关内容与退化引起的变异，在隐藏目标分割（COS）中前景线索本就微弱。核心挑战不只是提升可见性，而是在退化变异中识别可靠的内容证据。RGB空间分割易将内容与退化线索纠缠，基于复原的方法主要关注图像恢复与任务交互，缺乏显式的因子分离与可靠性评估。

Method: 以Retinex的空间对齐基础为出发点，将其扩展为“内容-退化”成像形成模型，并赋予任务非对称的因子角色。因子展开网络（FUN）：内容因子R作为分割的唯一直接语义证据来源；退化因子L刻画退化相关变异并生成空间可靠性图以校准该证据。构建统一能量函数，耦合观测形成与可靠性加权的前景-背景分离，其交替更新展开为无需干净图像监督的由粗到细网络。

Result: 在清晰与退化条件及多种分割任务上的大量实验表明，方法具有稳健性能与良好泛化能力。代码将开源。

Conclusion: FUN的关键在于“为感知而分解退化”而非去除退化：通过显式因子化区分内容证据与退化效应并评估其可靠性，可在无干净图像监督下实现稳健的退化条件分割与跨任务泛化。

Abstract: Reliable visual perception under degradation requires distinguishing task-relevant content from degradation-induced variations, especially in concealed object segmentation (COS), where foreground cues are inherently weak. The central challenge is not merely improving visibility, but identifying reliable content evidence amid degradation-induced variations. RGB-space segmentation often entangles content and degradation cues, while restoration-based methods primarily focus on image recovery and task interaction. This motivates explicit factorization to distinguish content evidence from degradation effects and assess its reliability. Retinex provides a spatially aligned basis for this separation but remains illumination-centric. We therefore extend it to content-degradation formation with task-asymmetric factor roles. The Factor Unfolding Network (FUN) uses the content factor R as the sole source of direct semantic evidence for segmentation, while the degradation factor L captures degradation-related variations and generates a spatial reliability map to calibrate this evidence. A unified energy couples observation formation with reliability-weighted foreground-background separation, and its alternating updates yield a coarse-to-fine network without clean-image supervision. In this way, FUN factorizes degradation for perception rather than removing it. Extensive experiments across clean and degraded conditions and diverse segmentation tasks demonstrate robust performance and generalization. Code will be released.

</details>


### [57] [CoRelate: Coordinated Human-Object Motion Synthesis through Interaction Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25166)
*Zhiwen Qiu,Daisy Lin,Diyu Zhou,Jialin Wu,Rosita Ji,Saleh Kalantari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoRelate扩散框架，通过显式交互关系（手-部件关系）建模与任务反馈，协调全身人-物（刚性与铰接物体）运动生成，实现接触建立/保持与任务完成的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-物运动合成需人协调参与使物体到达目标，但“到达目标”不等于建立并维持接触，而为改善接触所做的调整又可能干扰任务完成，二者存在冲突，缺乏统一协调机制。

Method: 以手-部件关系（参与手、部件相对参考位置、参与时间段）表示交互意图，既作生成条件又作交互实现度量；在每个去噪步用与生成器联合训练的时间修正器，融合关系偏差、任务残差与当前预测的运动特征，联合更新全序列的人与物体运动；生成后再以时序运动校正优化局部片段修复残余接触失败，同时保持物体轨迹与受保护的人体运动。

Result: 实验表明物体目标满足度、接触F1及完整交互成功率均优于对比基线；消融验证了关系条件的收益及任务反馈与关系反馈的互补作用。

Conclusion: 显式交互关系条件与任务/关系双反馈的联合去噪修正，可有效协调人-物运动中的接触与任务目标，提升完整交互成功率。

Abstract: Human-object motion synthesis requires coordinated human participation as objects move toward specified goals. However, reaching object targets does not ensure that contact is established and maintained, while adjustments that improve contact may interfere with task completion. We introduce CoRelate, a diffusion framework that coordinates full-body human-object motion through explicit interaction relations and task feedback for rigid and articulated objects. We represent intended participation through hand-part relations that specify the participating hand, its part-relative reference position, and participation interval. These relations serve both as generation conditions and as references for measuring interaction realization. At each denoising step, a temporal refiner trained jointly with the generator combines relation deviations, task residuals, and motion features from the current prediction to jointly update human and object motion across the sequence. After generation, temporal motion correction addresses remaining contact failures by optimizing local motion segments while preserving object trajectories and protected human motion. Experiments demonstrate improved object-goal satisfaction, contact F1, and complete interaction success over the evaluated baselines. Ablation studies support the benefits of relation conditioning and the complementary roles of task and relation feedback.

</details>


### [58] [AesDistill: Distilling Generative Aesthetics for Image Color Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25635)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Chengkun Yue,Huiqi Liang,Haoyang Li,Zihan Deng,Qingwen Zeng,Pengfei Ye,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 AesDistill 框架，将大型生成模型的"生成式美学"蒸馏到图像色彩增强任务中，构建了 64,644 张编辑图像的 AesDistill 数据集，并训练出仅 1.13M 参数的学生模型，在保持语义结构的同时在美学评估上超越所有 20 个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有色彩增强方法以专家标注为监督目标，但这些目标仅反映少数专家的偏好、覆盖有限的美学选择，无法保证在不同审美与场景下获得更高的美学质量；而大型生成模型虽具备丰富视觉知识、能产生生动编辑，却会改变源图像的语义结构。因此需要一种既能利用生成式美学知识、又能保持语义结构的方法。

Method: 1) 使用 4 个领先的开源大型生成模型生成 64,644 张编辑图像作为教师目标，耗费约 3,360 H100 GPU 小时，构建 AesDistill 数据集；2) 提出 AesDistill Student（1.13M 参数模型），通过置信度加权监督学习教师的色彩变化，同时保持源图像语义结构。

Result: 使用 12 个美学评估模型与 4 个图像质量评估模型进行评估：在联合评估中，每个教师模型的目标均高于 FiveK 与 PPR10K 专家目标的均值；在两个数据集上，AesDistill Student 优于全部 20 个基线方法，排名仅次于 4 个教师模型。

Conclusion: 从生成模型中蒸馏美学知识可使紧凑的学生模型超越基于专家目标训练的方法，表明生成式教师目标在美学丰富度与覆盖面上优于有限的专家标注。

Abstract: Image color enhancement aims to improve visual appeal, but previous methods often learn to reproduce ground-truth expert targets. These targets reflect the preferences of a few experts and cover a limited range of aesthetic choices. Matching them does not ensure stronger aesthetic quality across different tastes and scenes. By contrast, large generative models learn rich visual knowledge from large image collections and often produce vivid, appealing image edits. However, direct generation can change the source image's semantic structure along with its colors. In this paper, we introduce AesDistill, a framework for distilling generative aesthetics into image color enhancement. We use 4 leading open-source large generative models to produce 64,644 edited images as teacher targets at a cost of about 3,360 H100 GPU-hours, forming the AesDistill Dataset. AesDistill also includes AesDistill Student, a 1.13M-parameter model that learns teacher color changes through confidence-weighted supervision while preserving the source image's semantic structure. We evaluate AesDistill with 12 aesthetic and 4 image-quality assessment models. Every teacher's targets rank above the FiveK and PPR10K expert target means in the joint assessment. Across both datasets, AesDistill Student outperforms all 20 baseline methods and ranks directly behind the 4 teachers. These results demonstrate that distilling aesthetics from generative models enables a compact student to outperform methods trained on expert targets.

</details>


### [59] [Keel: A Frontier Model Teaches a Vision-Language Model by Rewriting Its Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25257)
*Jiajun Fan,Neil He,Bangji Yang,Qinglong Zheng,Ziwen Wang,Chaoran Cheng,Changyi Yang,Hanqi Mao,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Keel，首个面向VLM图像任务的智能体式权重编辑方法：仅告知失败症状，由前沿多模态大模型作为编辑智能体定位权重中的循环成因、直接编辑并验证，在免训练、不改变模型规模的前提下消除小型推理VLM在思考模式下的重复循环问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型推理VLM（如Qwen3.5）在思考模式下处理图像问题时易陷入重复循环，耗尽token预算而丢失答案；现有补救措施要么只在解码层面干预、不改变模型本身，要么需人工告知修改位置，而问题根源实际写在权重中。

Method: Keel以失败清单与可观测工具（展示失败图像及VLM注意力位置）辅助前沿多模态LLM作为编辑智能体自主定位原因、编辑权重并测试；在多个编辑器中仅保留能显著降低失败率且不损害未见问题准确率的编辑。方法无需训练，保持原有模型规模与每token开销，并可作用于已被编辑过的权重。

Result: 独立编辑器在原始权重上均将循环溯源至中间层注意力输出，且循环时较小VLM不再参考图像。在Qwen3.5-0.8B与2B上，跨视觉数学到GUI grounding的六个图像基准中，编辑消除了大部分循环、缩短了回答，平均准确率显著高于思考模式；2B模型在练习题中未出现的GUI grounding任务上收益最大。与预算强制结合后，在最佳权重上Keel在两模型上取得最佳平均准确率，在较大模型上显著优于所有免训练基线。

Conclusion: 循环的成因可定位于权重层面，通过智能体式权重编辑即可有效修复推理VLM的循环失效，且与解码期干预（预算强制）互补，为VLM修复提供免训练、低成本、可迁移的新范式。

Abstract: Can a frontier model repair a vision-language model (VLM) by rewriting its weights? Small reasoning VLMs such as Qwen3.5 need such a repair: in thinking mode they fall into loops on many image questions, repeating themselves until the token limit, spending the whole budget and losing the answer. The usual remedies act on the decoding, leaving the looping model as it was, or must be told what to change. Yet the cause is written in the weights. Keel, the first agentic weight-editing method for VLMs on image tasks, finds and removes it there. Told only the symptom, an editor, a frontier multimodal LLM acting as an agent, locates the cause, edits the weights and tests them, with a failure census and instruments showing it the failing images and where the VLM attends; among several editors, the harness keeps only edits that clearly reduce the failure without lowering accuracy on questions no editor saw. The repair needs no training, keeps the model's size and per-token cost, and applies to already-edited weights. The editors expose where the loops live: from the original weights, independent editors on both models trace them to the attention outputs of the middle layers, and the smaller VLM stops consulting the image inside a loop. On Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-2B, across six image benchmarks from visual mathematics to GUI grounding, the edit removes most loops on every benchmark and shortens answers, and the edited models are significantly more accurate than the thinking models on average; the larger one gains most on GUI grounding, a task absent from the practice questions. Because the repair lives in the weights, it combines with budget forcing where held-out questions favor it: on the best available weights, Keel has the best average accuracy on both models, significantly above every training-free baseline we compare on the larger one.

</details>


### [60] [SkillLens: Action-Guided Dense Visual Dynamics for Proficiency Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25712)
*Deokhyun Ahn,Hyukjin Kim,Jae-Ho Han,Bumsub Ham,Heeseung Choi,Ig-Jae Kim,Haksub Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkillLens利用原子动作描述作为时序引导来定位技能相关窗口，提取密集视觉动力学特征，并以高-低原型边际打分，经CORAL序数校准融合自我/外部视角线索，在Ego-Exo4D上达到0.542准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类熟练度估计困难，因为技能是高层且常具主观性的属性，无法仅由动作身份捕获；多模态观测（自我-外部视频、注视、姿态、惯性）与抽象熟练度标签之间存在难以跨越的语义鸿沟。

Method: 提出SkillLens框架：以原子动作描述作为时序引导（而非直接语义监督）定位技能相关时间窗口；将每个窗口汇总为动作动力学特征，并针对同一活动-动作类别的高低熟练度执行样本，用高-低原型边际进行打分；再通过CORAL序数校准将自我/外部视角PDM线索与全局自我-外部-注视基线融合。

Result: 在Ego-Exo4D数据集上取得0.542的准确率。

Conclusion: 以原子动作描述提供时序引导并结合原型边际打分与序数校准，可有效弥合传感器证据与抽象熟练度标签之间的鸿沟，验证了该框架在熟练度估计任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Human proficiency estimation is challenging because skill is a high-level and often subjective property that is not captured by action identity alone. Existing approaches leverage multimodal observations such as ego-exo video, gaze, pose, and inertial signals, but the gap between sensor-level evidence and abstract proficiency labels remains difficult to bridge. We present SkillLens, a proficiency estimation framework that uses atomic action descriptions as temporal guidance, rather than direct semantic supervision, to localize proficiency-relevant windows for dense visual-dynamics extraction. Each window is summarized into an action-dynamics feature and scored by a high-low prototype margin against higher- and lower-proficiency executions from the same activity-action category. Fusing these ego/exo PDM cues with a global ego-exo-gaze baseline through CORAL ordinal calibration, our method achieves 0.542 accuracy on Ego-Exo4D. Our project page is available at https://skill-lens.net/

</details>


### [61] [VisAlloc: Allocating Visual Supervision for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25069)
*Haoran Xu,Tian Chen,Yuhe Wu,Yanbo Zhao,Junchi Liao,Hongyu Wang,Jiaze Li,Wentao Wang,Yanfeng Li,Rui Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisAlloc：样本级视觉过滤 + token级反事实重加权的双层on-policy自蒸馏框架，在八个benchmark上以4B/9B模型取得81.44/84.92的宏平均，超越Vision-OPD复现基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD可提升MLLM视觉推理，但并非所有样本都能同等受益于特权视觉引导，且均匀的token监督会稀释有视觉依据的信号，需要同时做样本级筛选与token级加权。

Method: 样本级：固定成对教师过滤器，仅保留“以证据为中心的正裁剪答对、空间位移负裁剪答错”的样本。token级：两个EMA教师在学生自生成响应上打分，以绝对对数概率差衡量视图敏感性；对具敏感性或教师分布分歧的token追加监督，并施加共享的frozen-prior、模板bigram、师生散度门控；用逆频率重分配在微批内保持额外权重总量不变，正教师为唯一蒸馏目标。

Result: 八个benchmark上4B与9B模型宏平均分别为81.44与84.92，较Vision-OPD复现分别提升0.97与0.52分。

Conclusion: 双层（样本+token）分配策略可在不增加推理成本（仅用全图学生）的前提下有效提升MLLM视觉推理，验证了监督资源的差异化分配优于均匀蒸馏。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers a promising approach to improving visual reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, not all samples benefit equally from privileged visual guidance, and uniform token supervision can dilute visually grounded signals. We propose VisAlloc, a dual-level framework combining sample-level visual filtering with token-level counterfactual reweighting. A fixed paired-teacher filter retains samples answered correctly from an evidence-centered positive crop but incorrectly from a spatially displaced negative crop. At the token level, both EMA teachers score the same student-generated response, and their absolute log-probability differences measure view sensitivity. Additional supervision targets tokens admitted by sensitivity or teacher-distribution disagreement, with shared frozen-prior, template-bigram, and student--teacher divergence gates. Inverse-frequency redistribution preserves the total additional weight within each micro-batch, while the positive teacher remains the sole distillation target. Inference requires only the full-image student. Across eight benchmarks, our 4B and 9B models achieve macro-averages of 81.44 and 84.92, outperforming Vision-OPD reproductions by 0.97 and 0.52 points, respectively. Code on GitHub: https://github.com/Kaelyn01/VisAlloc

</details>


### [62] [DAWN: Robust Neural Blind Deconvolution via Alternating Wiener Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25621)
*Yida Pan,Yixuan Jia,Chaoyan Huang,Qing Qu,Ismail Alkhouri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAWN 是一个无需训练数据的 DIP 框架，通过在模型输入中直接注入基于模型的估计并用网络自身先验精化该估计，配合图像与核网络的自编码正则，在强噪声下显著提升盲图像去卷积的图像与模糊核质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲图像去卷积（BID）是病态逆问题，需从单张退化观测中联合恢复清晰图像与模糊核。在摄影与显微成像等常见的高噪声场景下，现有方法易出现噪声过拟合与核估计不准；而基于深度图像先验（DIP）的无训练数据方法多把模型-based 估计作为额外损失项引入，仅作外部引导，未能充分利用这些估计所携带的信息。

Method: 提出 DAWN：无训练数据的 DIP 框架，在"将基于模型的估计直接注入模型输入"与"用网络自身先验精化该不完美估计"之间交替进行；为提供更可靠的注入核估计，对图像网络和核网络同时施加自编码正则。

Result: 在多种高斯与泊松噪声水平的自然图像与科学图像上，DAWN 在图像与核质量上均优于 SOTA；在 Levin 数据集 σ=0.1 时相对次优基线最高提升 +4.5 dB。在无任何训练图像的情况下，于 FFHQ 上仍与在大规模同分布数据上训练的 SOTA 扩散模型高度可比。

Conclusion: 将模型估计作为输入注入而非仅作损失约束，能更充分挖掘其信息；配合双网络自编码正则可获更可靠核估计，使无训练数据的 DIP 方法在高噪声 BID 中达到领先性能。

Abstract: Blind image deconvolution (BID) is an ill-posed inverse problem that aims to jointly recover a sharp image and blur kernel from a single degraded observation. Under nontrivial and high noise, which is common in photography and scientific imaging such as microscopy, BID remains particularly challenging, as existing methods suffer from noise overfitting and inaccurate kernel estimation. Existing training-data-free learning methods for BID, such as those based on the deep image prior (DIP), often utilize external guidance by including model-based estimates as additional loss terms, which underexploits the information these estimates carry especially under nontrivial noise. To address these challenges, we propose DAWN, a training-data-free DIP framework that alternates between injecting model-based estimates directly into the model input and refining this imperfect estimate with the network's own prior. To provide more reliable kernel estimates for these injections, we apply autoencoding regularization to both the image and kernel networks. On natural and scientific images under varying levels of Gaussian and Poisson noise, DAWN outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both image and kernel quality (achieving up to +4.5 dB over the next best baseline on the Levin dataset at $σ= 0.1$). Without any training images, DAWN remains highly competitive with a state-of-the-art diffusion model on FFHQ, despite the latter being trained on a massive number of in-distribution images.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Resemblance: Image Fusion Reimagined](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25453)
*Hao Zhang,Yumin Huang,Linfeng Tang,Junjun Jiang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生成式图像融合新范式 ApexFuse，摆脱"源图像相似度"约束，转向学习高质量场景表征的通用分布，并借助人类反馈与 LMM 构建参考分布、以 FLUX 生成先验实现融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像融合缺乏真值融合图像，现有深度方法只能通过要求融合结果同时接近多张源图像来间接定义目标。但不同模态成像特性差异会带来相互竞争的约束，削弱互补信息的有效整合。

Method: 构建 human-LMM-in-the-loop 流程，利用大型多模态生成模型与迭代人类反馈，将抽象融合目标转化为可靠视觉参考，从而定义理想融合结果的目标分布；再通过目标分布引导的适配，把预训练 FLUX 的生成先验迁移到图像融合，实现以多模态观测为条件的高质量场景重建。

Result: 实验表明 ApexFuse 能协调融合显著热目标、自然色彩与细节纹理，视觉质量及语义分割、目标检测性能均优于源相似度类方法；并可从红外-可见光融合泛化到多曝光、多聚焦、可见光-深度融合，无需额外微调。

Conclusion: 以分布学习替代源相似度约束的生成式融合范式可行且有效，兼具融合质量提升与跨任务零微调泛化能力。

Abstract: Image fusion often lacks ground-truth fused images, forcing existing deep methods to indirectly define fusion objectives by requiring the fused image to simultaneously resemble multiple source images. However, the distinct imaging characteristics of different modalities can impose competing constraints on the fused result, potentially limiting the effective integration of their complementary information. To address this long-standing challenge facing the image fusion community, we propose ApexFuse, a novel generative image fusion paradigm that moves beyond source-resemblance constraints toward learning a general distribution of high-quality scene representations. Specifically, we develop a human-LMM-in-the-loop pipeline that leverages large multimodal generative models and iterative human feedback to translate abstract fusion objectives into reliable visual references, thereby defining the target distribution of ideal fusion outcomes. Through target-distribution-guided adaptation, we transfer the rich generative priors of pretrained FLUX to image fusion, enabling high-quality scene reconstruction conditioned on multimodal observations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ApexFuse harmoniously integrates salient thermal targets, natural colors, and fine textures, achieving superior visual quality as well as semantic segmentation and object detection performance over previous source-resemblance methods. Moreover, ApexFuse exhibits remarkable cross-task generalization, extending from infrared-visible fusion to multi-exposure fusion, multi-focus fusion, and visible-depth fusion without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [64] [Watertight Mesh Optimization of Textured Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25414)
*Nathaniel Burgdorfer,Philippos Mordohai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MODL直接在封闭三角网格上联合优化几何与视角相关外观，通过拉普拉斯预条件优化与曲率自适应重网格化，在无分割、无几何监督、无alpha混合条件下实现新视角合成与高内在网格质量的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像重建3D方法往往长于某一目标而牺牲另一目标，且常忽视重建表面的内在质量（如网格质量），难以同时兼顾真实感渲染、几何精度与可用3D资产。

Method: 提出MODL框架：直接在watertight三角网格上联合优化几何与视角相关外观；采用拉普拉斯预条件优化实现平滑顶点更新；引入曲率自适应重网格化以保留细节并保持三角形形状良好；优化受多视图归一化互相关（NCC）约束，不使用分割、几何监督或alpha混合。

Result: 在物体重建数据集上，MODL在新视角合成上具竞争力，同时生成几何准确的模型并具备更优的内在网格质量。

Conclusion: 直接网格优化有望统一照片级渲染、几何重建与实用3D资产创建。

Abstract: When reconstructing high-quality 3D assets from images, existing approaches often excel at one objective while compromising another, and frequently overlook the intrinsic quality of the reconstructed surfaces. We present MODL, a framework that jointly optimizes geometry and view-dependent appearance directly on a watertight triangle mesh. Our approach combines Laplacian-preconditioned optimization for smooth vertex updates with curvature-adaptive remeshing to preserve fine details and well-shaped triangles. The triangle mesh is optimized subject to multi-view normalized cross-correlation constraints, but without segmentation, geometric supervision or alpha-blending. Evaluations on object reconstruction datasets demonstrate that MODL achieves competitive novel-view synthesis while producing geometrically accurate models with superior intrinsic mesh quality. Our results highlight the potential of direct mesh optimization to unify photorealistic rendering, geometric reconstruction, and practical 3D asset creation.

</details>


### [65] [A Unified Framework for Image Manipulation Localization Under Multi-Type Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25371)
*Songlin Li,Hui Ma,Zhiqing Guo,Dan Ma,Gaobo Yang,Xin Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniIML：统一弱标注的师生框架，借助OSC候选选择与可靠性加权蒸馏，实现多种弱标注联合训练的图像篡改定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督图像篡改定位方法依赖与特定标注类型绑定的训练策略，难以在单一模型内联合使用多种弱标注，限制了可用训练数据规模与性能上限。

Method: 提出UniIML师生框架：将多种弱标注映射为统一表示以引导SAM生成多个候选掩码；提出Ordinal Safe Consensus（OSC），先用统一表示中的可信弱标注约束过滤候选，再分别按与弱标注一致性、SAM预测质量分、跨视角稳定性三准则排序并聚合，并通过逐一剔除准则的留一排序评估选择稳定性，取被选最频繁的候选为最终掩码，其相对选择频率量化为稳定性；由所选候选预测概率构造软伪标签，基于选择稳定性、候选间像素一致性、预测确定性估计像素级监督权重；学生在弱标注定义区域受直接监督，其余区域通过可靠性加权蒸馏学习软伪标签。

Result: 实验表明UniIML在平均性能上优于现有最先进的IML方法。

Conclusion: 统一弱标注表示结合OSC候选选择与可靠性加权蒸馏，可在单一模型中有效整合异构弱监督并取得领先的平均定位性能。

Abstract: Existing weakly supervised image manipulation localization (IML) methods typically rely on annotation-specific training strategies, hindering the joint use of diverse annotations within a single model. This limits the scale of usable training data and constrains further progress. We propose UniIML, a teacher-student framework that maps diverse weak annotations into a unified representation to guide the Segment Anything Model (SAM) in generating multiple candidate masks. However, SAM is designed for general-purpose segmentation and has limited sensitivity to manipulation traces; its predicted quality scores alone may therefore fail to reliably identify the candidate that best delineates the manipulated region. We introduce Ordinal Safe Consensus (OSC), which first filters candidates using trusted weak-annotation constraints encoded in the unified representation. It then ranks the remaining candidates separately by consistency with weak annotations, SAM-predicted quality scores, and cross-view stability, aggregating these rankings for an initial selection. To assess how changes in the evaluation criteria affect candidate selection, OSC repeats ranking and selection while omitting one criterion at a time. The most frequently selected candidate is adopted as the final mask, and its relative selection frequency quantifies selection stability. We construct soft pseudo-labels from the selected candidate's predicted probabilities and estimate pixel-level supervision weights based on selection stability, pixel-level agreement among candidates, and predictive certainty. The student receives direct supervision in regions defined by weak annotations and learns from soft pseudo-labels elsewhere through reliability-weighted distillation, enabling unified training across weak annotation types. Experiments show that UniIML achieves superior average performance compared with state-of-the-art IML methods.

</details>


### [66] [ScaleDiag: Diagnosing the Limits of Test-Time Scaling in GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25297)
*Chen Liu,Zhen Fang,Xin Yu,Dadong Wang,Ling Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScaleDiag 诊断框架，将 GUI grounding 的视觉缩放、指令缩放与采样缩放三类测试时扩展策略解耦分析，发现各策略的有效区间远窄于通常假设，且瓶颈性质各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过视觉缩放、指令缩放、采样缩放提升 GUI grounding，但尚不清楚这些策略分别解决哪个瓶颈、收益在何处饱和，缺乏受控的归因分析。

Method: 构建受控诊断框架 ScaleDiag，分别独立操控三类因素：目标尺度与周边上下文（视觉输入）、目标特异性信息与辅助描述（指令信息）、候选覆盖与选择（预测采样）；覆盖 5 个基准、最多 8 个视觉语言模型进行评测。

Result: (1) 视觉缩放受目标占屏比支配而非固定裁剪比例，目标占裁剪视图约 0.32% 即可挽救全屏错误，继续放大反而有害；(2) 指令缩放取决于可识别目标的信息，颜色、尺寸、形状等真值属性常无效甚至有害，外观与空间描述贡献超过 90% 的可达增益；(3) 重复采样下正确坐标常被生成但难以选中，存在 5.1–11.0 分 oracle 余量，几何共识几乎无增益。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展受限于彼此不同的运行区间，并非单调受益于更强的视觉聚焦、更丰富的指令或更多采样；GUI grounding 的提升需针对具体瓶颈设计策略。

Abstract: GUI grounding maps a screenshot and a natural language instruction to the coordinates of the intended interface element. Existing approaches improve grounding through visual scaling, instruction scaling, and sampling scaling, yet it remains unclear which grounding bottlenecks these strategies address and where their gains saturate. To address this gap, we introduce ScaleDiag, a controlled diagnostic framework that isolates the effects of visual input, instruction information, and prediction sampling on GUI grounding by independently varying target scale and surrounding context, target-specific information and auxiliary descriptions, and candidate coverage and selection, respectively. We evaluate ScaleDiag across five benchmarks and up to eight vision-language models and find that each scaling strategy operates within a much narrower regime than commonly assumed. (1) Visual scaling is governed by target occupancy rather than a fixed crop ratio, with full-screen errors typically rescued once the target occupies only 0.32% of the cropped view, while further zooming eventually becomes harmful. (2) Instruction scaling is driven by information that identifies the target rather than description richness itself, as even ground-truth-derived attributes such as color, size, and shape are often neutral or harmful, while appearance and spatial descriptions account for over 90% of the achievable gain. (3) Repeated sampling exposes a different limitation, as correct coordinates are frequently generated but remain difficult to select, leaving 5.1--11.0 points of oracle headroom while geometric consensus provides almost no improvement. Together, these results show that test-time scaling is constrained by distinct operating regimes rather than benefiting monotonically from more visual focus, richer instructions, or additional samples. Our code is available at https://github.com/YenanLiu/ScaleDiag.

</details>


### [67] [Persistent Identity in Person Tracking: Online Enrollment and Registered Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25283)
*Ratha Siv,Matei Mancas,Bernard Gosselin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将PoseTReID扩展为画廊式外观库匹配与残差冲突守卫的SVM识别，并在多视频上以全框GT评估：画廊式与SVM在两组合数据集上平均IDF1/HOTA领先独立跟踪器，但优势依赖事后新增的GTA编译、且逐视频仍有落后，SVM利用约20%帧的全部GT裁剪（含未来外观）缺乏部署公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估PoseTReID体系在身份保持（IDF1/HOTA）上的真实收益：一方面检验在线外观库（Gallery）匹配与残差冲突守卫下注册SVM识别的效果，另一方面考察其相对StrongSORT等独立跟踪器的优势是否稳健、是否源于评估集与信息可得性的不对等。

Method: 以PoseTReID为基座，加入在线外观库匹配（Gallery AIN、COPE两变体）与用于注册SVM识别的残差冲突守卫；统一使用提供的GT人体框，按视频等权平均全框评估的IDF1/HOTA；在六个SportsMOT片段与十一个视频两套队列上对比StrongSORT等独立跟踪器；SVM使用约20%帧的全部GT裁剪（含未来外观）作为注册；另设六片段persistent-only评分与匹配对照，统计正确-P覆盖率与编码器尝试次数；并做姿态门控消融。

Result: 六片段上StrongSORT单独最优（79.44/84.72%），高于两个Gallery变体；十一视频上Gallery AIN达83.29/85.44%、COPE达84.66/86.38%，超过有完整结果的独立跟踪器，但该领先依赖两个事后加入的GTA开发编译，在原始九视频上消失。注册SVM在完整结果中两队列均最优，AIN在十一视频上为95.72/94.82%；MOT20-03上AIN IDF1 96.80%，对90.72%的评估GT观测赋予正确注册名。Gallery与独立跟踪器无逐视频标注注册。两路线均可跨片内标题卡恢复身份，但Gallery在三视频（HOTA为四）落后SORT，两个SVM在MOT16-04/MOT20-03落后SORT。六片段persistent-only下Gallery相对匹配对照平均IDF1 +2.39个百分点，但正确-P覆盖率更低、编码器尝试次数为5.02倍；姿态门控效果混合，守卫的指标贡献未被单独分离。

Conclusion: 外观库匹配与注册SVM识别在平均IDF1/HOTA上看似显著领先，但优势高度依赖事后增设的GTA编译与信息占优（GT框、全帧裁剪含未来外观、逐视频标注注册），在原始数据与逐视频比较中并不稳健，且代价为更低覆盖率与更高计算开销。作者据此对结论持保留：守卫的独立效应与姿态门控收益尚未厘清，需在对称信息与预注册评估协议下复验。

Abstract: We extend PoseTReID with online appearance-bank matching (Gallery) and a residual-conflict guard for registered SVM recognition. All methods use supplied ground-truth (GT) person boxes. We average all-box assessment IDF1/HOTA equally across videos. On six SportsMOT clips, StrongSORT leads standalone trackers (79.44/84.72%) and exceeds both Gallery variants. Over eleven videos, Gallery AIN (83.29/85.44%) and COPE (84.66/86.38%) exceed standalone trackers with complete results. This lead depends on two retrospectively added GTA development compilations and is absent on the original nine videos. Registered SVMs lead both cohorts among complete results, with AIN at 95.72/94.82% over eleven videos. They use every GT crop from approximately 20% of frames, including future appearances. On MOT20-03, registered AIN reached 96.80% IDF1 and assigned the correct enrolled name to 90.72% of assessment GT observations. Gallery and standalone trackers receive no per-video labelled enrollment. Both identity routes restore identities across internal title cards, but Gallery trails SORT on three videos (four in HOTA) and both SVMs trail SORT on MOT16-04/MOT20-03. Under six-clip persistent-only scoring, native Gallery gains 2.39 percentage points in mean IDF1 over a matched control, with lower correct-P coverage and 5.02 times as many encoder attempts. Pose gating is mixed; the guard's metric effect is not isolated.

</details>


### [68] [MV-JEPA: Post-Training Video Encoders for Cross-View Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25172)
*Yangchen Zhao,Ali Payani,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多视角预测式后训练（MV-JEPA），在不改变编码器结构与单视角推理接口的前提下，增强JEPA视频编码器的跨视角实例与粗粒度时间对应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA式视频/图像编码器仅在单张图像或单个视频片段内做潜空间预测，未显式利用同一物体或同一时刻的成对相机视角，因而学到的表征难以可靠支持跨相机视角的匹配。

Method: 采用共享编码器独立处理被掩码的上下文视角；引入仅在训练期存在的预测器，联合预测被掩码上下文区域的潜变量token以及一个完全留出的视角。将跨视角预测与通用图像/视频数据上的单视角掩码预测交替进行，以保留预训练能力。全程不修改编码器架构，单视角推理接口亦不变。

Result: 仅单卡9,000次后训练更新即在多个数据集上提升跨视角实例检索与粗粒度跨相机时刻检索，并在AE2上进一步改善零样本动作阶段检索与冻结特征分类；冻结探针评估显示运动识别、图像识别与稠密语义分割性能基本保持。

Conclusion: 多视角预测式后训练可在不改动编码器架构与单视角推理接口的情况下，强化JEPA视频编码器的实例级与粗粒度时间对应能力，且不显著损害原有通用表征能力。

Abstract: JEPA-based video encoders learn strong representations for video and image understanding through prediction in latent space. However, their standard pretraining predicts features within individual images or video clips, without explicitly exploiting paired camera views of the same object or moment. Consequently, the learned representations may not reliably support matching across different camera viewpoints. To solve this problem, we introduce multi-view predictive post-training to strengthen cross-view instance and temporal correspondence while retaining independent single-view inference. A shared encoder processes masked context views independently, and a training-time predictor jointly predicts latent tokens for masked context regions and a fully held-out view. We alternate cross-view prediction with single-view masked prediction on general image and video data to help retain the encoder's pretrained capabilities. After only 9,000 post-training updates on a single GPU, MV-JEPA improves cross-view instance retrieval and coarse cross-camera moment retrieval on various datasets, with further gains in zero-shot action-phase retrieval and frozen-feature classification on AE2. Frozen-probe evaluations further indicate that post-training largely preserves motion recognition, image recognition, and dense semantic segmentation performance on the evaluated benchmarks. Together, these results demonstrate that multi-view predictive post-training can strengthen instance and coarse temporal correspondence in JEPA video encoders without modifying the encoder architecture or single-view inference interface.

</details>


### [69] [Suppressing the Shortcut Hidden Behind the Gains of VideoLLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25776)
*Youngwoo Shin,Jongsuk Kim,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VETO，将视频大模型SFT中的"文本捷径"干扰作用在微调更新上而非数据上，抑制捷径并保持分布内收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VideoLLM在SFT中会学到仅依赖训练数据的文本规律作答，绕过视频输入，形成捷径。该捷径难以在数据层面定义，且与训练epoch、数据规模和骨干网络无关；它隐藏在分布内精度上升背后，却无益于该上升，并使SFT在分布偏移下损失视频带来的收益。

Method: 提出Video-Evidence Training via Orthogonal projection (VETO)：在写入捷径的微调更新处进行干预。先校准基础模型激活中"纯文本所占子空间"，再将更新投影到其正交补上，从而在捷径形成过程中加以抑制，无需显式定义捷径。

Result: 在七个VideoLLM上，VETO保持SFT在训练分布上的收益并抑制捷径；在六个分布偏移基准上，增益超过SFT，且模型基于完整输入而非捷径作答。

Conclusion: 通过在更新空间（而非数据空间）施加正交投影约束，可有效抑制VideoLLM的文本捷径学习，提升分布偏移下的泛化，且适用于多种骨干模型。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is the stage in which Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) advance their capability to reason over video. However, the VideoLLM also learns to answer from textual regularities of its training data, a shortcut that bypasses the video. The regularities behind the shortcut are hard to define at the data level, and the shortcut forms regardless of training epoch, data size, or backbone. The shortcut is hidden behind a rise in accuracy within the training distribution, yet it is not required for the rise and even costs SFT the gain the video offers under distribution shift. We therefore propose Video-Evidence Training via Orthogonal projection (VETO), which intervenes on the fine-tuning update where the shortcut is written rather than on the data. VETO calibrates the subspace that the text alone occupies in the activations of the base model and projects the update onto its orthogonal complement. The VideoLLM is thus left to ground its answer in the full input, with the shortcut suppressed as it forms and never defined. Across seven VideoLLMs, VETO preserves the gain of SFT on the training distribution and suppresses the shortcut. On six benchmarks under shift it gains more than SFT and answers from the full input rather than the shortcut. Code is available at https://github.com/Youngwoo-git/VETO.

</details>


### [70] [SAM 3 Sees More Than It Detects: Promptable Concept Maps Beyond Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25747)
*Yu-Huan Wu,Yun Liu,Liangli Zhen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"可提示概念图"(PCM)任务与PCM-Bench基准，并给出UNVEIL方法：复用SAM 3语义路径、LoRA微调末级模块并加独立存在性解码器，在7个类无关密集预测任务上均取得最佳成绩且不改变SAM 3原输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可提示分割器只回答"物体是什么"，而机器人、监控和图像编辑等应用还需要回答类无关问题，如什么最显著或处于伪装状态、光如何被遮挡/透射/反射、结构位于何处。作者希望将SAM 3的可提示概念分割从名词实例扩展到由短名词短语命名的概念图，并允许"none"作为答案。

Method: 构建PCM-Bench，按所提问题对七类任务的标准测试集分组。诊断发现SAM 3"看到的比检测到的多"：语义路径能响应许多此类概念，但低的图像级存在性分数会阻断实例路径的全部检测。据此提出UNVEIL：复制SAM 3语义路径，用LoRA微调其最后若干模块，并训练一个独立的存在性解码器，从经核验的负样本中学习概念是否出现；每次查询仅输入一个文本短语。

Result: 同一套权重既可映射区域类概念（显著目标、伪装目标、阴影、透明物体、镜子），也可映射细结构（边缘、骨架）及普通物体类别。与SAM 3并列运行时，SAM 3自身输出保持不变。在七项任务上，UNVEIL均取得所比较的可提示模型中的最高分，超过SAM 3官方微调方案及基于多模态大模型的流水线。
