Conclusion: 通过解耦"概念存在性判断"与"概念区域映射"，并复用SAM 3的语义表征，UNVEIL以单一文本短语查询统一处理多类类无关密集预测任务，兼顾性能与对原模型的非侵入性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Promptable segmenters answer what a thing is. Dense prediction tasks also ask class-agnostic questions, such as what stands out or hides, how light is blocked, transmitted or reflected, and where structure lies. Robots, surveillance systems and image editors build on their answers. We study promptable concept maps (PCM), which extend SAM 3's promptable concept segmentation from noun instances to maps of concepts named by short noun phrases such as `"transparent object", with "none" as an answer. PCM-Bench groups the standard test sets of seven such tasks by the question they ask. On it, we find that SAM 3 sees more than it detects. Its semantic path responds to many of these concepts, yet a low image-level presence score can block every detection of its instance path. We present UNVEIL, which trains a copy of SAM 3's semantic path for these maps, with LoRA on copies of its last blocks. A separate presence decoder learns from verified negatives whether a concept occurs. With one text phrase per query, UNVEIL maps region concepts such as salient and camouflaged objects, shadows, transparent objects and mirrors. The same weights map thin structures such as edges and skeletons as well as object categories. Run next to SAM 3, it leaves SAM 3's own outputs unchanged. On each of the seven tasks, UNVEIL reaches the highest score among the compared promptable models, including SAM 3's official fine-tuning and pipelines built on multimodal LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/yuhuan-wu/UNVEIL.

</details>


### [71] [What's in the box? Revealing Object Layouts Inside Opaque Boxes via Contactless Vibration Excitation and Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25728)
*Ethan Turok,Matan Kichler,Shai Bagon,Mark Sheinin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WhisperBox 通过远程激发并测量箱体表面振动，学习振动到隐表示的映射并解码为箱内物体的二维占用图，实现无接触的"透视"布局推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 箱内物体改变箱体机械响应并留下位置线索，作者希望完全远程地揭示不透明箱体内部的空间布局而不需接触或直接成像。

Method: 用扬声器非接触激发振动，在可见壁上以激光点阵+相机测量各点散斑图案位移；构造 WhisperBox 模型，将振动编码为隐表示并解码为箱内物体占用图。

Result: 在多种箱体及单/多物体场景中恢复布局，泛化到未见实例、位置、朝向、重量及激光与激励源变化；分析表明信息频段不必与箱体共振重合，且隐空间捕获物体位置并支持算术运算。

Conclusion: 远程振动感知可用于推断不可见空间布局，展示了在非接触、非光学成像条件下进行内部结构感知的潜力。

Abstract: Can we reveal what is inside an opaque box by observing its surface vibrations? Objects inside a box alter its mechanical response, providing cues about their hidden locations. We introduce a method that uses these cues to predict a box's 2D occupancy layout entirely remotely. A nearby speaker excites vibrations, which we measure on one visible wall using a grid of laser points and a camera measuring each point's speckle-pattern shifts. Our model, WhisperBox, learns to encode these vibrations into a latent representation and decode it into an occupancy map of the box's contents. The method requires no physical contact to excite or sense vibrations. We demonstrate layout recovery across various boxes and single- and multiple-object scenes, with generalization to unseen object instances, positions, orientations, and weights, as well as changes in laser placement and excitation source. Our analysis reveals that informative frequency bands need not coincide with the box's resonances and that the learned latent space captures object placement and supports arithmetic operations. These results demonstrate the potential of remote vibration sensing to infer spatial layouts hidden from view.

</details>


### [72] [SteerTrace: Tracing How Target Attacks Steer World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25719)
*Yukun Dai,Tianshi Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Xin Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SteerTrace: 对世界动作模型（WAM）的通用定向对抗攻击，并通过逐干预的“定向效应”显著度进行内部机理追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 同时预测未来视频与动作，其“想象未来”常被视为安全信号；需检验该信号能否被低成本扰动劫持，并厘清攻击效果在模型内部的承载位置。

Method: 每模型一条 ℓ∞ 有界（4/255）、跨帧/任务/视角共享的扰动，定向推向攻击者指定动作；用带符号定向效应量化各干预（恢复未来 token、当前帧 token、视频专家 FFN 神经元、残差方向）移除或复现的比例；在 4 个家族 8 个模型（FastWAM、Faster-WAM、OpenWAM、LingBot-VA）与 LIBERO、RoboTwin 上评测，并对比同预算噪声与 BadWAM。

Result: 任务成功率趋近 0，动作显著朝目标偏移，同预算噪声与 BadWAM 几乎无定向位移。标准攻击下，恢复未来 token 移除 61–94% 定向效应（5 个动作专家关注未来 token 的模型）；约束未来预测贴近干净预测时，效应转依赖当前帧 token，3 个模型中恢复当前帧 token 移除 87–99%，未来 token 仅 9–15%。恢复视频专家第 10–29 层仅 0.36% 的 FFN 神经元即移除 72–96%；残差方向 2–16 仅占 11–18% 位移能量，但移除后取消大部分定向效应。

Conclusion: WAM 的想象未来不是可靠安全信号：单一微小、通用、共享的扰动即可定向操控执行动作，且效应集中于少量 FFN 神经元与少数残差方向，依赖路径随攻击约束在“未来 token”与“当前帧 token”间迁移。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict future videos and actions with one backbone, and their imagined future is often seen as a safety signal. We introduce SteerTrace, a targeted universal attack on WAMs that also traces its effect inside the model. One $\ell_\infty$-bounded (4/255) perturbation per model, shared across frames, tasks, and camera views, pushes the executed action toward an attacker-chosen target. On eight WAMs from four families (FastWAM, Faster-WAM, OpenWAM and LingBot-VA) on LIBERO and RoboTwin, it drives task success close to zero and moves the attacked action toward the target. Same-budget noise and BadWAM produce little action shift in the target direction. We use the signed targeted effect to measure how much of the effect each intervention removes or reproduces. Under the standard attack, restoring future tokens removes 61-94% of the targeted effect in the five models whose action experts attend to them. When the attack is constrained to keep the predicted future close to the clean prediction, the targeted effect can become more dependent on current-frame tokens. In three of these five models, restoring current-frame tokens removes 87-99% of the effect, compared with 9-15% for future tokens. In six models, restoring just 0.36% of the FFN neurons in layers 10-29 of the video expert removes 72-96% of the targeted effect. Residual directions 2-16 hold only 11-18% of the shift energy, yet removing them cancels most of the targeted effect.

</details>


### [73] [D3-Cache: Accelerating Diffusion Models with Dynamic Mode Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25689)
*Yanbo Li,An Xiang,Xiaoxin Hu,Feng Wang,Cheng-zhong Xu,Kejiang Ye,Xitong Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: D3-Cache：将动态模态分解（DMD）用于扩散采样，仅对调度器消耗的速度做无训练缓存预测，并配合horizon-aware门控决定跳步，在四个图像/视频模型上实现2.6–3.8×加速且保持高保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散 Transformer 采样质量高但每步都需完整网络评估，成本高；现有免训练缓存方法依赖逐模型校准的跳步准则或维护逐层预测历史，通用性差、开销大。

Method: 对近期精确速度历史拟合 DMD，只预测调度器实际消费的速度量；设置 horizon-aware 门控：每次真实网络评估时将该步输出与预测值比对，误差越大则越早再次评估，并对连续跳步数设上限；无需训练、无需逐模型离线校准曲线，内存开销可忽略，四个模型共享同一历史长度与风险容忍度。

Result: 在四个 SOTA 图像与视频模型上取得 2.6–3.8× 加速，且输出与基础模型保持高保真度；单一超参数配置跨模型通用。

Conclusion: 基于 DMD 的速度级预测加误差驱动的自适应门控，是一种免训练、低内存、跨模型通用的扩散采样加速方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers generate high-quality images and videos, but every sampling step requires a full network evaluation. Training-free caching reduces this cost by reusing or forecasting network outputs across steps, but common approaches use per-model calibrated skip criteria or maintain per-layer prediction histories. We present D3-Cache, which applies Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD) to Diffusion sampling: it fits DMD to a short history of exact velocities and forecasts only the velocity that the scheduler consumes. A horizon-aware gate decides when to skip: whenever the network is evaluated, its output is compared with the forecast for that step, and the larger the error, the sooner the network is evaluated again, up to a cap on consecutive skipped steps. D3-Cache requires no training or per-model offline calibration curve and adds negligible memory. It shares one history length and risk tolerance across the four evaluated models. On four state-of-the-art image and video models, D3-Cache achieves speedups of 2.6-3.8x with high fidelity to the base model output.

</details>


### [74] [What One Sample Cannot See: Why Fewer Denoising Steps Can Score Higher on Physics Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25632)
*Yutao Li,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出Physics-IQ以单个真实视频比对的评测会奖励"多未来分布塌缩"，提出Kernel Physics-IQ以两独立采样消除该激励，实证两步采样在官方分数上升而在新指标下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型作为世界模型的评测存在悖论：减少去噪步数虽使视频模糊或提前静止，Physics-IQ物理分数反而升高。作者认为根源在于场景可有多条合理未来，而Physics-IQ仅与单一录制比对，其分数在数学上可奖励生成未来间变异度的降低，即便分布整体更不准确。

Method: 先给出精确分解，证明Physics-IQ分数可分解出对生成未来多样性的奖励项；据此提出Kernel Physics-IQ，以核（分布）度量替代单样本匹配，只需每个条件两个独立样本即可在期望意义上去除塌缩激励；并在结果概率已知的受控物理系统中验证指标偏好真实分布而非塌缩预测。

Result: 两步采样在全部五个图生视频模型上均降低Kernel Physics-IQ；CogVideoX官方分数上升0.055而Kernel Physics-IQ下降0.131；改变采样调度表可揭示官方分数未捕捉的差异；受控系统中新指标正确偏好真实分布。

Conclusion: 世界模型的评判不应只看单条视频与既成事实的接近程度，还应衡量其预测对"可能发生之事"的表征忠实度；单参考比对指标存在结构性偏差，需采用分布层面的核度量。

Abstract: When a storm can head north or south, a forecast down the middle may score better under squared error than either plausible path. The same paradox appears when video generators are tested as world models: fewer denoising steps can raise physics scores even as videos blur or stop moving early. Why? Physics-IQ compares each generated video with one recording, although a scene can have multiple valid futures. An exact decomposition shows its scores can reward reduced variation among generated futures, even when their distribution becomes less accurate. We introduce Kernel Physics-IQ to remove this incentive in expectation, needing only two independent samples per condition. Two-step sampling lowers Kernel Physics-IQ in all five image-to-video models. For CogVideoX, the official score rises by 0.055 while ours falls by 0.131. Changing the sampling schedule reveals further differences official scores miss. In controlled physical systems with known outcome probabilities, our metric favors the true distribution over collapsed predictions. In short, a world model should be judged not only by how closely one video matches what happened, but by how faithfully its predictions represent what could have happened.

</details>


### [75] [ContextRenderer: Dynamic-Context Video Generation for Interactive Game Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25838)
*Runze Wang,Fei Zha,Bing He,Ke Xu,Zhongyuan Wu,Yuxuan Xie,Wenzheng Wu,Zhongyuan Liu,Youcheng Cai,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ContextRenderer，一个自回归视频扩散渲染框架，用于交互式游戏世界模型，兼顾动态条件跟随与跨代理泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式游戏世界模型将世界演化与视觉生成解耦，视频模型需把引擎提供的场景表示渲染为细节观测。但现有生成式渲染器在持续游玩中难以同时做到跟随动态变化的条件与跨不同代理表示及目标风格保持视觉连续性。

Method: 1) Dynamic-Context Learning（DCL）：块级交互监督；2) Recurrent History Compression（RHC）：在有限 token 预算内压缩历史，实现条件更新跟随与视觉连续；3) Cross-Representation Flow Alignment（CRFA）：借助共享高保真重建，连接代理重建与外观、交互变化；4) 构建交互中心数据集 WorldPrism（含代理构建世界、AAA 游戏与开源视频，提供渲染结果与块级标注）。

Result: 在 GWM-Bench 与 Solar-Bench 上重建与视觉质量均有提升；定性结果显示在变化提示与主体参考下可实现数分钟级的交互渲染。

Conclusion: ContextRenderer 通过动态上下文学习、循环历史压缩与跨表示流对齐，在有限 token 预算下兼顾条件跟随与视觉连续性，并展现出跨代理表示的泛化能力。

Abstract: Interactive game world models separate executable world evolution from visual generation, using video models to render engine-provided scene representations into detailed observations. During sustained gameplay, however, existing generative renderers struggle to follow changing conditions while preserving visual continuity across diverse proxies and target styles. We therefore introduce ContextRenderer, an autoregressive video diffusion framework designed for both dynamic conditioning and cross-proxy generalization. It couples Dynamic-Context Learning (DCL) for chunk-level interaction supervision with Recurrent History Compression (RHC) to follow condition updates while preserving visual continuity within a bounded token budget. Across proxy representations, Cross-Representation Flow Alignment (CRFA) connects proxy reconstruction with appearance and interaction variation through a shared high-fidelity reconstruction. WorldPrism, our interaction-centric dataset constructed from agent-built worlds, AAA gameplay, and open-source videos, supplies the corresponding renderings and chunk-level annotations for these learning tasks. Experiments on GWM-Bench and Solar-Bench show improved reconstruction and visual quality, while qualitative results demonstrate minute-long interactive rendering under changing prompts and subject references. Project page: https://rainzor.github.io/contextrenderer/

</details>


### [76] [What Does Anti-Hallucination Training Buy? Saying Less, Cheap Pruning, and the Rest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25814)
*Lingkai Bu,Qian Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文拆解偏好训练降低物体幻觉的增益构成，发现大部分"超越少说"的增益可被一个仅11特征的冻结事后探针（500张标注图拟合）复现；该探针可替代训练信号做标签，且删句更精准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好训练（如DPO）被普遍用于降低视觉语言模型的物体幻觉，新方法不断报告更低的CHAIR分数，但"分数下降"具体由什么构成并不清楚：可能只是"少说物体"（brevity），也可能是模型真的变得更准。作者要拆解这一增益的来源。

Method: 1) 对每个被训练系统，与其基座模型将caption截断到相同物体召回率后的结果比较，剥离"少说"带来的那部分增益；2) 将剩余增益与一个刻意弱化的、冻结的11特征事后探针（仅用500张标注图拟合）所能复现的量比较；3) 所有判据在分析代码中预先指定，并在被评判输出生成前冻结；4) 覆盖8个公开方法（LLaVA-1.5-7B）+ SENTINEL（LLaVA-1.5与Qwen2.5-VL）+ 自训DPO检查点（Qwen2.5-VL、InternVL2.5），数据集为COCO、AMBER、Object HalBench；5) 进一步用该度量给训练信号定价，并用人类判断检验探针删句正确性与CHAIR评估器精度。

Result: 探针在8个方法中7个（COCO）、8个（AMBER）、6个（Object HalBench）上复现了至少2/3的"超越少说"增益，RLAIF-V是唯一一致的例外；SENTINEL在两骨干上同样达标。自训DPO在Qwen2.5-VL上成立，InternVL2.5在COCO上4种训练设计中仅2种成立。同召回下，基座+探针在COCO 8/9、AMBER 9/9以及两个新骨干上优于截断，且可落地为真实删句。训练信号定价：用探针标签或物体级CLIP标签的DPO分别回收真值标签DPO"超越少说"增益的0.70与0.87（Qwen2.5-VL），InternVL2.5上探针为0.73，均优于等长随机标签，COCO上CLIP标签优于探针标签。人类判断显示探针删除的句子比截断删除的更常是错的；但CHAIR评估器精度低于预设阈值，作者将此列为局限。

Conclusion: 偏好训练报告的幻觉降低，在剥离"少说"效应后，其大部分剩余增益可被一个极弱的冻结11特征探针复现，说明这些提升在很大程度上可由简单的物体存在性后验解释，而非复杂的模型改进；该度量也能为训练信号定价，但CHAIR评估器本身精度不足构成局限。

Abstract: Preference training is the standard recipe for reducing object hallucination in vision-language models, and each new method reports a lower CHAIR score. We ask what that reduction consists of. Every trained system is compared with its base model's caption truncated to the same object recall, which removes the part of the gain that comes from saying less; the remainder is compared with what a deliberately weak, frozen, eleven-feature post-hoc probe fitted on 500 annotated images can reproduce. All criteria were specified in the analysis code and frozen before the outputs they judge existed. For eight public methods on LLaVA-1.5-7B, the probe reproduces at least two thirds of what training removes beyond saying less for 7 of 8 methods on COCO, 8 of 8 on AMBER and 6 of 8 on Object HalBench, with RLAIF-V the consistent exception; a later public method released for both LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen2.5-VL (SENTINEL) meets the same bar on both. On our own DPO checkpoints the accounting holds on Qwen2.5-VL but fails on InternVL2.5 on COCO (2 of 4 training designs). At matched recall, the base model plus the probe beats truncation for 8 of 9 LLaVA-1.5 systems on COCO and 9 of 9 on AMBER, on the two newer backbones, and when realized as actual sentence deletion. The same measure prices training signals: DPO on the model's own samples labelled by the probe or by an object-level CLIP score recovers 0.70 and 0.87 of the beyond-brevity gain of ground-truth-labelled DPO on Qwen2.5-VL (the probe 0.73 on InternVL2.5), both beat length-matched random labels, and CLIP labels buy more than probe labels on COCO. Human judgements show that the sentences the probe deletes are wrong more often than those truncation would delete; a human check of the CHAIR evaluator's precision falls below our pre-set bar, which we report as a limitation.

</details>


### [77] [TwoPass: Draft-Guided Few-Step Video Prediction for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25553)
*Yijun Liu,Shijia Ge,Tongtong Feng,Xin Wang,Yuan Meng,Yuwei Liu,Zhi Wang,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TwoPass：自条件训练与推理框架，保持标准 flow-matching 目标，用自生成草稿作条件并在推理时复用草稿迭代精化，4 NFE 达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件视频预测器每个未来帧均从纯噪声去噪，未利用自身估计，导致预测质量（尤其被操作物体）受限，且需大量网络调用。

Method: 1) Self-Generated Draft Training：在同一噪声状态下，用共享网络给出带梯度的分离（detached）干净视频估计作为条件；2) Refinement-Only Late Adapter：存在草稿时施加专用残差变换；3) 推理端 Draft-Reuse Iterative Refinement：每次速度预测同时产生求解器更新与下一步草稿；训练中采样离散求解器查询噪声水平并更新预训练骨干。

Result: 在 Bridge 与 Language Table 上取得 SOTA，仅需 4 NFE 每视频块，远少于现有预测器的 50–59 次调用。

Conclusion: 自条件（草稿条件化）+ 草稿复用迭代精化可显著提升动作条件视频预测质量并将采样开销降至极低，为机器人规划与策略评估提供高效预测器。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video prediction lets robots anticipate the visual consequences of their actions for planning and policy evaluation. Existing predictors denoise each future from noise without using their own estimate of it, which limits the quality of the predicted future, in particular of the manipulated objects, and requires many network calls. We present TwoPass, a self-conditioned training-and-inference framework that keeps the standard flow-matching objective. Self-Generated Draft Training supplies a detached clean-video estimate from the shared network as conditioning at the same noisy state. A Refinement-Only Late Adapter applies a dedicated residual transformation when a draft is present. At inference, Draft-Reuse Iterative Refinement obtains both a solver update and the next draft from each velocity prediction, requiring as few as 4 function evaluations (NFE) per video chunk. Training samples discrete solver-query noise levels and updates the pretrained backbone. Extensive experiments on Bridge and Language Table show that TwoPass achieves state-of-the-art results with 4 NFE, far fewer than the 50-59 calls of existing predictors. Code: https://github.com/PlantPotatoOnMoon/TwoPass.

</details>


### [78] [EgoPass-Bench: Identifying Objects That May Affect the Wearer's Passage in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24995)
*Hongyu Wang,Chuhan Li,Congjin Xu,Xuandong Zhao,Hua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EgoPass-Bench 基准，评估多模态基础模型对自中心视频中"观察者—实体交互"的理解，发现模型运动感知强但交互理解与相关实体发现弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对多模态基础模型（MFM）的自中心环境评测集中于观察者定位、运动轨迹与动作可行性，对"观察者与实体之间动态交互的发现与解释"缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建 EgoPass-Bench：用 Ray-Ban Meta（第二代）智能眼镜采集并人工审核 284 段评估视频，设计 2,201 个问题、覆盖五类任务（运动感知、指定实体的交互判断、无目标线索的相关实体发现）；评测 8 种逐帧配置与 6 种原生视频配置，并设置两人人类基线与 always-empty 基线。

Result: 最强模型 GPT-6 Astra 在 394 组同目标运动比较中正确率 91.37%，但仅 66.50% 正确识别通行约束变化；在 574 道实体发现题上落后两人人类基线 23.65±7.45 个百分点（配对 95% CI），同时高出 always-empty 基线（36.76%）至少 18.9 个百分点。进一步分析显示模型常纳入既无合理物理冲突也不构成通行约束的实体。

Conclusion: 准确的运动识别并不保证可靠的相关实体发现，模型中"运动感知—交互理解"存在显著鸿沟，指向自中心交互推理的关键能力缺口。

Abstract: Understanding an egocentric environment requires reasoning about how surrounding entities affect the observer's actions. Existing evaluations of multimodal foundation models (MFMs) examine observer localization, motion trajectories, and action feasibility, but leave the discovery and interpretation of evolving observer-entity interactions insufficiently assessed. We introduce EgoPass-Bench, a benchmark for observer-relative interaction understanding in real-world egocentric video. EgoPass-Bench comprises 284 manually reviewed evaluation clips recorded using Ray-Ban Meta (Gen 2) smart glasses and 2,201 distinct questions across five tasks assessing motion perception, interaction judgments about specified entities, and relevant-entity discovery without target cues. Our evaluation of eight frame-based and six native-video configurations reveals a pronounced discrepancy between motion perception and interaction understanding. The strongest evaluated model, GPT-6 Astra, correctly recognizes object motion in 91.37% of 394 comparisons involving the same targets, but correctly identifies changes in passage constraints in only 66.50%. Across all 574 entity-discovery questions, it trails the two-person human baseline by $23.65 \pm 7.45$ percentage points (paired 95% CI), while exceeding the always-empty baseline (36.76%) by at least 18.9 percentage points. Further analysis reveals frequent inclusion of entities that neither pose a plausible physical conflict nor constrain passage, showing that accurate motion recognition does not ensure reliable relevant-entity discovery.

</details>


### [79] [Accord: Universal Online Metric Localization and Dense Mapping from one Camera and an IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25279)
*Jaafar Mahmoud,Arthur Movsesyan,Cuong Huynh,Mikhail Iumanov,Sergey Zagoruyko,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Accord，从任意带 IMU 的单目相机在线构建稠密度量（metric）地图：用 VIO 滤波器提供度量位姿，用深度基础模型在关键帧窗口内预测深度，并以"多视图一致性"为内核校正逐图尺度误差，最终在因子图中融合子图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密度量与度量尺度难以兼得：VIO 能给出度量运动但几何稀疏；前馈深度模型能给出稠密结构但不保证度量尺度。现有方法多针对特定数据集/配置调参，泛化性不足。

Method: 1) VIO 滤波器输出度量位姿；2) 以位姿为条件的深度基础模型对短关键帧窗口预测深度，并引入上下文关键帧提升预测质量；3) 将每个关键帧窗口锚定到由滤波位姿三角化得到的度量 3D 点上；4) 利用共视图像间的双向稠密深度一致性估计并校正每张图的尺度误差，同时保留锚点建立的度量尺度；5) 将所得子图在因子图中融合。

Result: 单一建图配置、无需按数据集/平台调参，且每张地图仅用 Accord 自身轨迹对齐，在五个公开相机-IMU 平台及新建的 Phone-rig/GoPro 数据集上，均取得 5 cm 阈值下最优或次优的平均 F-score。

Conclusion: 多视图一致性（Accord）可作为 SLAM 中几何自一致性的有效来源，在保持 VIO 度量尺度的前提下实现稠密度量建图并具备跨平台泛化能力；代码与手机支架数据集已开源。

Abstract: Dense metric mapping from a single camera and IMU remains challenging: visual-inertial odometry (VIO) provides metric motion but sparse geometry, whereas feed-forward depth models predict dense structure without ensuring metric scale. We present Accord, which constructs an accurate online dense metric map from any camera with an IMU. A VIO filter publishes metric poses, a depth foundation model conditioned on them predicts depth for short keyframe windows, and a factor graph fuses the resulting submaps. Recent work has explored several approaches to this problem. What is essential is the multi-view agreement (Accord), which is considered in SLAM as a source of geometric self-consistency. Accord anchors each keyframe window to metric 3D points triangulated from the filter poses, then exploits bidirectional dense depth agreement between co-visible images to estimate and correct per-image scale errors while preserving the metric scale established by the anchors. Context keyframes further improve depth prediction, and the resulting submaps are fused in a factor graph. We evaluate a single mapping configuration across five public camera-IMU setups and a new Phone-rig/GoPro dataset. With nothing tuned per dataset/setup and every map aligned only by Accord's own trajectory, Accord achieves consistently best/second-best mean F-score at 5 cm on every platform. Accord is open-source along with the phone-rig dataset for the benefit of the research community

</details>


### [80] [Characterizing Age-Conditioned Affective Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25593)
*Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Lin Gu,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 年龄提示词会改变VLM对图像情绪的评分，但这种改变几乎不预测不同年龄人群对同一图像的真实评分差异；人口学个性化评测必须在单刺激层面引入人类对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已知年龄提示词能改变视觉语言模型的情感评分，但不清楚这种改变是否对应真实人群中不同年龄个体的评分差异，即模型的人口学个性化是否具有刺激层面的效度。

Method: 在900张OASIS图像上，用青年、中年、老年三种年龄提示评估17个VLM；同时由822名被试提供评分，估计图像级年龄差异。先检验无年龄线索下模型与人类平均效价的对应关系，再以图像愉悦度为协变量，比较模型年龄效应与人类年龄效应的相关性，并与估计的理论上限（约0.595）对照；另外测试提供其他图像的人类评分能否提升一致性，并在600张留出图像上评估。

Result: 无年龄线索时，模型与人类平均效价高度一致（Pearson r中位数0.891）。老年提示下17个模型中有16个给出更低的平均效价。但控制图像愉悦度后，模型与人类年龄效应的相关仅为-0.030至0.093，远低于0.595的上限。在54张被判为愉悦的敏感图像上差异最明显：模型平均降低0.845分，而老年人比年轻人高0.443分。加入其他图像的人类评分可改善年龄差异方向的一致性，但图像级保真度仍较低（r_part=0.105，600张留出图像）。

Conclusion: VLM的年龄条件化情感评分虽随提示词系统偏移，但不反映真实年龄群体的刺激级差异，且方向在敏感图像上甚至相反。基础模型的人口学个性化评测需以个体刺激层面的人类数据为基准，而非仅比较提示词引起的平均偏移。

Abstract: An age prompt can change how a vision-language model (VLM) rates the emotion evoked by an image. Does the change predict how people of different ages rate that same image? We evaluate 17 VLMs on 900 OASIS images using young-, middle-, and older-adult prompts and estimate image-specific age differences from ratings by 822 participants. Without an age cue, models closely track average human valence (median Pearson $r=0.891$). Under an older-adult prompt, 16 of 17 models assign lower valence on average than under a young-adult prompt. Yet after adjusting for image pleasantness, correlations between model and human age effects range from $-0.030$ to $0.093$, against an estimated ceiling of $0.595$. The discrepancy is clearest on 54 sensitive images rated as pleasant. Across models, the older prompt reduces valence by $0.845$ points on average, while older participants rate these images $0.443$ points higher than younger participants. Providing human ratings from other images improves agreement on the direction of age differences, although image-specific fidelity remains modest on 600 held-out images ($r_{\mathrm{part}}=0.105$). These results show why evaluations of demographic personalization in foundation models need human comparisons at the level of individual stimuli. Project page available at https://shahriyar-zaman.github.io/age-conditioned-emotion-reasoning/

</details>


### [81] [DiLE: Discrimination-Aware Latent-Space Learning for Universal Multimodal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25419)
*Ben Kang,Junda Wang,Yuxuan Yuan,Hang Li,Yuhan Liu,Xin Chen,Wanting Geng,Tong Lin,Pei Fu,Dong Wang,Jian Luan,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DiLE 框架，通过任务引导与在线策略检索反馈的隐空间学习，使通用多模态嵌入保留区分易混样本的判别性证据，在 MMEB-v2、UVRB、MRMR 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型增强了通用多模态嵌入模型的输入理解能力，但强理解并不保证嵌入中保留区分相关项与易混淆项所需的证据，导致检索判别力不足。

Method: （1）用少量可学习 query 在紧凑隐空间中编码任务相关信息，并以推理指令与辅助答案生成监督塑造表示；（2）设计共享-私有专家适配，兼顾跨任务知识共享与任务特化表示；（3）引入 on-policy 难负例蒸馏：学生检索易混候选，用反向 KL 对齐其基于嵌入的相似度分布与教师细粒度相关性分布，并周期性用学生快照刷新候选。

Result: 在覆盖两代骨干的四种模型配置下，于 MMEB-v2、UVRB、MRMR 三个基准取得 SOTA，跨模态与跨任务均有效。

Conclusion: 将任务引导与在线检索反馈结合于隐空间学习，可显式保留判别性证据，提升通用多模态嵌入的检索区分能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have greatly improved input understanding in universal multimodal embedding models. However, this understanding does not ensure that their embeddings preserve the evidence needed to distinguish relevant items from confusable alternatives. To preserve essential discriminative evidence in embeddings, this paper proposes DiLE, a discrimination-aware latent-space learning framework that combines task guidance with on-policy retrieval feedback. Specifically, a few learnable queries are used to encode task-relevant information in a compact latent space. Reasoning instructions and auxiliary answer-generation supervision shape these representations to support both task understanding and retrieval discrimination. To address task-specific discriminative needs, we design shared-private expert adaptation that integrates cross-task knowledge sharing with task-specific representation learning. Building on this, the on-policy hard-negative distillation is introduced: the student retrieves confusable candidates, and a reverse KL divergence objective aligns its embedding-based similarity distribution with the teacher's fine-grained relevance distribution over these candidates. Candidates are periodically refreshed using updated student snapshots, tying teacher supervision to the student's evolving retrieval behavior and confusions. Across four model configurations that span two backbone generations, DiLE achieves state-of-the-art performance on MMEB-v2, UVRB, and MRMR, demonstrating its effectiveness across modalities and tasks.

</details>


### [82] [Bilateral Evidence Selection and Reasoning for Mammography Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25418)
*Zhengbo Zhou,Dooman Arefan,Shandong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BESR（Bilateral Evidence Selection and Reasoning），将视觉token的证据保留与双侧对比推理相连接：用双侧引导的token选择器在共享token预算下联合优先保留病灶外观、局部与对侧上下文，并用双侧奖励鼓励模型真正利用对侧对比证据；在CBIS-DDSM构建的双侧数据集与VinDr-Mammo上，在受限视觉token预算下提升了证据保留与诊断性能，并降低prefill延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉-语言模型处理高分辨率图像时存在大量正常解剖与冗余信息，需定位微小、细微病灶；现有视觉token压缩虽提升效率，但仅保留显著区域会丢弃诊断解释所需的上下文。该上下文可跨图像延伸——双侧乳腺摄影中对侧乳腺提供患者特异性解剖参照。然而，仅“保留”证据并不保证模型有效“使用”证据，存在保留与利用之间的缺口。

Method: 提出BESR框架，包含两个协同组件：(1) 双侧引导的token选择器（bilateral-guided token selector），在共享token预算约束下，同时优先选择病灶外观及其局部上下文与双侧（对侧）上下文；(2) 双侧奖励（bilateral reward），通过奖励机制促使模型生成受益于所保留对比证据的评估，从而将证据保留与比较性判断相连。

Result: 在基于CBIS-DDSM构建的双侧数据集与VinDr-Mammo数据集上，于受限视觉token预算条件下，证据保留率与诊断性能均有提升；推理基准测试显示prefill延迟降低。

Conclusion: 视觉token压缩不应只追求显著区域保留，而需兼顾跨图像诊断上下文，并确保被保留的证据在推理中被有效利用；联合设计证据选择与对比推理奖励可在效率约束下同时改善诊断表现与推理效率。

Abstract: Medical vision--language models (VLMs) often need to identify small, subtle lesions within high-resolution images containing extensive normal anatomy and redundant visual information. Visual-token compression improves efficiency, but preserving salient regions alone can discard the context needed for diagnostic interpretation. This context can extend across images: in bilateral mammography, the opposite breast provides a patient-specific anatomical reference. Yet retaining such evidence does not ensure that the model uses it effectively. We propose Bilateral Evidence Selection and Reasoning (BESR), which connects evidence preservation with comparative assessment through a bilateral-guided token selector and a bilateral reward. Under a shared token budget, the selector jointly prioritizes lesion appearance and its local and bilateral context, while the reward encourages assessments that benefit from the preserved comparison. Experiments on a bilateral dataset constructed from CBIS-DDSM and on VinDr-Mammo demonstrate improved evidence retention and diagnostic performance under constrained visual-token budgets. Inference benchmarking further shows reduced prefill latency.

</details>


### [83] [MIR-Depth: Multi-Range Refinement for Single-View RGB-D Depth Completion of Transparent Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25417)
*Xinzhe Fu,Qi Shao,Yuanfei Xie,Taoyu Wu,Zhihao Cao,Long Hu,Hong Liang,Xianzhi Li,Anh Nguyen,Baoru Huang,Rongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIR-Depth，通过多范围代价体假设与选择性循环精化，从单张RGB-D图像与目标掩码恢复透明物体精确可见表面深度，误差优于ReMake等方法并支持6-DoF抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明物体因镜面反射导致深度缺失、折射使非零深度返回携带背景深度，原始测量不可靠；在固定采样预算下，单视图单区间精化难以兼顾大折射偏移与局部精细分辨率，存在精度权衡。

Method: 1）构建坐标感知多范围假设：3D聚合的全局逆深度体，以及围绕先验引导初始深度的、沿深度轴过滤的逐像素局部体；2）选择性循环精化：引入越界查找掩码与有效性掩码softmax融合，用多尺度ConvGRU迭代更新逆深度；3）空间门控校正：对全分辨率残差施加逐像素学习门控，保留初始几何且不将原始传感器读数作为硬约束。

Result: 在TransCG、TODD、TRansPose、ClearPose与HouseCat6D上，误差低于ReMake、DFNet、BP-Net与PSD；实机实验验证可为6-DoF抓取位姿预测提供准确表面几何。

Conclusion: 多范围假设与掩码循环融合有效缓解单视图采样预算下的精度权衡，门控校正避免原始读数误差传播，方法在多样数据集与真实机器人上具备实用性。

Abstract: Recovering accurate visible-surface depth from a single RGB-D image and an object mask is essential for robotic grasping and manipulation of transparent objects. Specular reflections cause missing depth, while refraction corrupts nonzero returns with background depth, making raw measurements unreliable as surface anchors. Under a fixed sampling budget, refining an initial single-view prediction within a single search interval either misses large refraction offsets or sacrifices fine local resolution. To resolve this single-view trade-off, MIR-Depth constructs Coordinate-Aware Multi-Range Hypotheses: a 3D-aggregated global inverse-depth volume and depth-axis-filtered pixel-wise local volumes around a prior-guided initial depth. Selective Recurrent Refinement then introduces out-of-bounds lookup masking and validity-masked softmax fusion to iteratively update inverse depth from these volumes using a multi-scale ConvGRU. Finally, Spatially Gated Correction applies a learned per-pixel spatial gate to the full-resolution residual, preserving accurate initial geometry without imposing raw sensor readings as hard constraints. Experiments on TransCG, TODD, TRansPose, ClearPose, and HouseCat6D show that MIR-Depth achieves lower error than ReMake, DFNet, BP-Net, and PSD. Real-robot evaluations confirm accurate surface geometry for 6-DoF grasp pose prediction. Code and pretrained models will be publicly available.

</details>


### [84] [Text Lives in the Guidance: Preventing Silent Text Rewriting in Cached Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25382)
*Kun Zhou,Wenmian Yang,Zhensheng Wang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 特征缓存会静默改写扩散Transformer渲染的文字，源于CFG引导项误差；提出GuideCache仅对预测引导误差最大的token重算双分支，2.4x FLOPs下恢复全计算文本保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT已能在图像中渲染清晰长文本，而特征缓存是主流加速手段。但现有缓存仅用图像级指标（ImageReward、CLIP Score）评估，无法检验渲染文本是否仍正确。作者发现缓存会悄悄改写文字：Z-Image上TaylorSeer在2.9倍FLOPs削减下将词准确率从62%降到46%，而图像级指标几乎无变化；对比同等计算下的少步采样几乎不掉文本，说明损失来自缓存对跳过步骤的预测方式。误差沿CFG分解后，损失主要归于引导项（条件与无条件预测之差），缓存对文本的引导外推最差。因此需要以文本保真度为核心重新审视并改进缓存加速。

Method: 先沿无分类器引导（CFG）分解缓存误差，定位引导项为主要误差源；并验证仅校正文本区域的引导即可恢复大部分丢失文本。据此提出GuideCache：利用缓存已存储的引导项，估计各token的预测引导误差，仅对误差最大的token重算条件与无条件两个CFG分支，从而以稀疏重算替代全局重算。

Result: 在2.4倍FLOPs削减下，GuideCache在Z-Image与Qwen-Image上恢复全计算时的提示词保真度；Z-Image上其文字渲染效果与ToCa、TeaCache、FasterCache相当或更优，且输出更接近全模型结果。GuideCache兼容任意阶数的缓存，并在叠加少步采样时以更高加速比保留更多文本。

Conclusion: 缓存加速的文本退化根因可归因于CFG引导项的token级误差，对其做稀疏校正即可高效修复。作者据此主张：面向文本渲染模型的加速方法应以所渲染的文字作为评估依据，而非仅依赖图像级指标。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) now render long, legible text in images, and feature caching is a standard way to accelerate them. Caches are judged by image-level metrics, which do not check whether the rendered text is still correct. We find that caching silently rewrites this text. On Z-Image, TaylorSeer at 2.9x fewer FLOPs lowers word accuracy from 62% to 46%, while ImageReward and CLIP Score barely change. Sampling with fewer steps at the same compute keeps almost all of this text, so the loss comes from how the cache predicts the skipped steps. Decomposing the cache error along classifier-free guidance (CFG), we trace this loss mainly to the guidance term, the difference between the conditional and unconditional predictions, which caches extrapolate worst on text. Correcting the guidance inside the text alone recovers most of the lost text. We therefore propose GuideCache, which recomputes both CFG branches only on the tokens with the largest predicted guidance error, using the guidance terms that the cache already stores. At 2.4x fewer FLOPs, GuideCache restores full-compute prompt fidelity on Z-Image and Qwen-Image, and on Z-Image it renders text as well as or better than ToCa, TeaCache, and FasterCache while staying much closer to the full model's output. GuideCache works with caches of any order, and on top of fewer sampling steps it keeps more text than step reduction at a higher speedup. These results suggest that acceleration methods for text-rendering models should be evaluated on the text they render.

</details>


### [85] [SceneAnything: Agentic Reconstruction of Large-Scale Mesh Scenes from a Single Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25340)
*Bowen Xue,Saeed Hadadan,Fabrice Rousselle,Zahra Montazeri,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Milos Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SceneAnything：一个自动化系统，通过软件框架协调智能体、预训练视觉模型与图形工具，从真实或生成视频重建可编辑网格场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视频重建可编辑 3D 场景需同时匹配可见结构并合理补全不可见区域；直接让智能体编写场景构建程序易在布局、尺度与光照上出错。

Method: 采用显式重建协议组织流程：以共享的相机、深度与分割估计作为组件构建依据；场景主控智能体规划环境并向作者智能体下达任务，作者编写参数化 Blender 程序；基于可见性与拟合的 LOD 规则选择构建层级，不可见区域补全并标记为推断；装配后拟合光照并施加有支撑的运动约束，修订经程序化检查与独立视觉对比；被接受的程序与经验跨场景复用。

Result: 在 100 个场景上评估；子集对比显示原视角保真度优于直接智能体重建，新视角内容相似度高于所对比的视频到 3D 方法。

Conclusion: 模块化多智能体搭配图形工具与视觉模型的重建协议可生成可编辑、可复用的高质量场景；将开源框架、智能体指令、可复用库与场景。

Abstract: Reconstructing an editable 3D scene from video requires matching visible structure while plausibly completing unseen surfaces. Agents can write scene-construction programs, but direct reconstruction attempts often produce errors in layout, scale, and lighting. We introduce SceneAnything, an automated system for reconstructing mesh scenes from real and generated videos, enabling text-to-scene construction when paired with video generation. A software harness coordinates agents, pretrained vision models, and graphics tools through an explicit reconstruction protocol. Shared camera, depth, and segmentation estimates ground component construction: a scene-owner agent plans the environment and briefs authors, who write parameterized Blender programs. A visibility- and fit-based level-of-detail rule selects the construction level, while unseen regions are completed and marked as inferred. Assembled scenes use fitted lighting and supported motion, and revisions undergo programmatic checks and independent visual comparisons. Accepted programs and lessons are reused across scenes. We evaluate on 100 scenes; subset comparisons show improved original-view fidelity over direct-agent reconstruction and higher novel-view content similarity than the compared video-to-3D methods. We will release the harness, agent instructions, reusable libraries, and scenes.

</details>


### [86] [Harnessing Medical Visual-Language Models with Agentic Evidence Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25840)
*Qiming Huang,Hanze Dong,Jipeng Zhang,Mike Zheng Shou,Xinxing Xu,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Mask-Evidence MedVQA基准（2.5K样本、四类医学图像）以分离“证据使用”与“分割质量”，揭示直接使用工具的两大局限（证据呈现方式影响行为、掩码带来证据权衡），并提出TUNE证据管理框架，在多个医学VQA基准上平均提升9.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言智能体广泛依赖分割工具，但现有工作多把“拿到正确掩码”视为工具使用的终点，忽视了智能体能否有效利用该证据。现有医学VQA数据集无法同时满足三点：分离证据使用与分割质量、覆盖多种医学图像类型、允许同一掩码以不同方式呈现。

Method: 构建Mask-Evidence MedVQA基准：2.5K样本、四类图像，每题配对与答案相关的真值掩码及多种渲染方式。基于该基准诊断直接工具使用的问题，进而提出TUNE（Tool Use is Not Enough）证据管理框架，显式管理“获取何种证据、如何呈现证据、是否接受由此产生的答案更新”。

Result: 在同一固定掩码下，不同渲染方式会显著改变模型行为；高亮相关区域可暴露此前遗漏的诊断证据，但附加视觉线索也会诱发错误关联，即使真值掩码也会在修正部分答案的同时将原本正确的答案变为错误。在三个公开医学VQA基准上，证据管理使微调后的Qwen3.5-9B主干平均准确率提升4.0个百分点；完整TUNE在七个基准上平均优于直接工具使用9.1个百分点，并与现有医学VLM及智能体取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 仅获取正确掩码并不充分，智能体还需决定证据的呈现方式以及是否据此更新答案。证据管理是提升医学多模态智能体可靠性的关键环节，TUNE框架为此提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Medical vision-language agents increasingly rely on segmentation tools, and recent work has focused on acquiring the right mask, often treating a good mask as the end point of tool use. But do these agents use the resulting evidence effectively? To answer this question, we need a benchmark that separates evidence use from segmentation quality, spans multiple medical image types, and allows the same mask to be presented in different ways. Existing medical VQA datasets do not jointly provide these properties. We therefore construct Mask-Evidence MedVQA, a 2.5K-example benchmark spanning four image types, where each question is paired with an answer-relevant ground-truth mask and multiple possible renderings. Our study reveals two limitations of direct tool use. First, evidence presentation matters. Different renderings of the same fixed mask can substantially change model behavior. Second, masks introduce an evidence trade-off. Highlighting a relevant region can expose diagnostic evidence the model previously missed, but the added visual cue can also induce incorrect associations. As a result, even a ground-truth mask can correct some answers while turning initially correct ones into incorrect ones. Together, these findings show that acquiring the right mask is not enough. An agent must also decide how to present the evidence and whether it should change the answer. We therefore introduce TUNE (Tool Use is Not Enough), an evidence-management harness that explicitly manages what evidence to acquire, how to present it, and whether to accept the resulting answer update. Across three public medical VQA benchmarks, evidence management improves the fine-tuned Qwen3.5-9B backbone's mean accuracy by 4.0 percentage points. Full TUNE improves over direct tool use by 9.1 points on average across seven benchmarks and achieves competitive results against existing medical VLMs and agents.

</details>


### [87] [Rank2Replace: VLM-Ranked Slot Replacement for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25834)
*Bo-Han Feng,Keng-Te Liao,Shou-De Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出训练时增强方法 Rank2Replace，利用预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）评估对象槽的任务相关性，并按排名做跨图像同秩槽替换，从而提升小模型在分布偏移下的OOD泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像预测器易依赖虚假相关，获得高分布内精度但在分布偏移下泛化差。作者希望将VLM中关于"任务相关证据"的知识迁移给小模型，且不依赖环境标签或数据集特定的替换调参。

Method: 用VLM对以对象为中心的槽（slot）按任务相关性排序，将另一图像中同排名的槽嵌入替换进来（rank-dependent replacement），保留原图标签，鼓励模型依赖保留的任务证据而非偶然线索。该方法可与现有OOD方法组合，并支持置换不变预测器；VLM仅在训练时使用，推理高效。评测统一采用冻结槽嵌入，覆盖4个数据集、6种划分、14种OOD训练方法及5种排序VLM、6种提取器-预测器组合。

Result: 默认交叉熵配置取得宏平均average-group准确率80.43%、worst-group准确率65.28%，无需环境标签与数据集特定替换调优。槽损坏（slot corruption）对全部14种OOD方法均提升两项宏平均指标；与槽排序及IRM结合达81.96%和72.68%。消融实验支持排序与替换设计的必要性，并在多种VLM和提取器-预测器组合上取得增益；置换不变预测器较未增强版本两项指标均提升。

Conclusion: VLM引导的按排名槽替换是一种通用、无需环境标签的训练时增强，可显著改善worst-group鲁棒性并与现有OOD方法互补；推理阶段不引入VLM开销。

Abstract: Spurious correlations can give image predictors high in-distribution accuracy but poor generalization under distribution shift. We introduce $\textbf{Rank2Replace}$, which transfers knowledge of task-relevant evidence from pretrained vision--language models (VLMs) to small predictors through training-time augmentation for out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. The VLM ranks object-centric slots by task relevance and guides rank-dependent replacement with same-rank slot embeddings from another image. This augmentation retains the original image's label, encouraging reliance on preserved task evidence as incidental cues change, and supports combination with existing OOD methods. Across four datasets and six splits under shared frozen slot embeddings, the default cross-entropy configuration reaches macro-averaged average-group and worst-group accuracies of $80.43\%$ and $65.28\%$ without environment labels or dataset-specific replacement tuning. Slot corruption improves both macro-averaged metrics for all fourteen OOD training methods. Combining it with slot ranking and IRM reaches $81.96\%$ and $72.68\%$. Controlled ablations support the ranking and replacement design, with gains across five ranking VLMs and six extractor--predictor combinations. Permutation-invariant predictors further improve both macro-averaged metrics over their unaugmented counterparts while using VLM guidance only during training for efficient inference.

</details>


### [88] [What Places an Action Boundary? Structure-Aware Attribution for Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25815)
*Ruoshan Lan,Zongru Shao,Chieh Tsai,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构感知归因框架（按Shapley-Shubik原理对随机证据恢复顺序取平均），并引入cell结构化恢复顺序与边界锚定参考，使TAL中边界定位的证据归因更忠实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序动作定位（TAL）中边界估计是主要误差来源，理解"什么决定边界放置"需要对时序证据做忠实归因；现有归因方法未充分考虑TAL的时序结构，也未针对边界定位定制。

Method: 基于Shapley-Shubik原理对随机证据恢复顺序的贡献取平均；利用检测器感受野限制单独排序的时间步以降低复杂度；用软梯度路径近似每个顺序；针对边界预测设计cell结构化恢复顺序与边界锚定参考（选择性移除相邻证据）。并改进忠实性评测，考察连续时序证据的联合效应与边界位移方向。

Result: 在THUMOS14与EPIC-KITCHENS-100上，配合TriDet与ActionFormer，归因忠实性优于现有方法；归因分析与受控干预显示类别分数变化不能可靠反映边界位移，说明分类所依赖的内容对边界定位信息不足。

Conclusion: 结构感知归因可更忠实地解释TAL检测器的时序证据与边界决策，并揭示分类与定位证据的解耦；代码与评测工件将公开。

Abstract: Temporal action localization (TAL) detectors must identify actions and estimate their temporal boundaries, but localization remains a major source of detection errors. While understanding what determines boundary placement requires faithful attribution of temporal evidence, existing attribution methods have not sufficiently addressed the temporal structure of TAL or the need to tailor attribution to boundary localization. We introduce a structure-aware attribution framework that averages contributions across random evidence-restoration orders following the Shapley-Shubik principle. We make it tractable by using the detector's receptive field to limit which time steps are ordered individually, and by approximating each order with a soft gradient path. For boundary predictions, we further introduce cell-structured restoration orders and boundary-anchored references that selectively remove adjacent evidence. We also adapt faithfulness evaluation to assess the joint effects of contiguous temporal evidence and the direction of boundary displacement. Experiments on THUMOS14 and EPIC-KITCHENS-100 with TriDet and ActionFormer demonstrate improved attribution faithfulness over existing methods. Attribution analysis and controlled interventions further reveal that class-score changes do not reliably track boundary displacement, providing input-level evidence that classification relies on content less informative about boundary placement. Code and evaluation artifacts will be publicly released.

</details>


### [89] [Identity World Model: Recognizing People in an Imagined Latent World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25204)
*Dingqiang Ye,Kartik Narayan,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出身份世界模型 IDWorld，在潜空间中一次性"想象"人物在统一规范条件（同衣物、固定视角、裸轮廓）下的反事实特征并自适应融合，用于鲁棒的全身体识别，性能达 SOTA 且单帧仅 5.9 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身体识别方法在真实场景变化下脆弱，因其将身份绑定到衣物、纹理、姿态、视角等瞬态线索；作者认为鲁棒识别器不应仅"学会忽略"这些线索，而应"想象"人物在这些条件改变后会被如何表征。

Method: 借鉴潜空间世界模型（预测动作后场景特征）的思想：单次前向传播中预测同一身份在不同条件下（同一套衣物、固定视角、裸轮廓等共享规范条件）的反事实特征，无需渲染像素；随后对每种想象条件按每个 probe-gallery 对进行自适应加权融合。

Result: 潜空间想象与像素级显式想象精度接近，但速度快 11 倍以上（5.9 ms/帧）；在 CCPG、CCGR-MINI 上取得 SOTA，在 MEVID 上优于对比方法，在 CASIA-B* 与 SUSTech1K 上泛化最佳，在 CCVID 上取得最佳 mAP。

Conclusion: 身份世界模型为鲁棒全身体识别提供了一条简单而有效的路径；代码将开源。

Abstract: Whole-body human recognition has advanced rapidly with large vision models. However, existing methods remain fragile under real-world variations, as they tie identity to transient cues such as clothing, texture, pose, or viewpoint. We argue that a robust recognizer should not merely learn to ignore these cues, but imagine how a person would be represented if they changed. To this end, we propose IDWorld, an identity world model: just as a latent world model predicts the features of a scene after an action, IDWorld predicts how a person would be represented under a changed condition. In a single forward pass, IDWorld imagines these counterfactual features under shared canonical conditions, such as the same outfit, a fixed view, or a bare silhouette, without rendering any pixels. Compared with explicit imagination in pixels, this latent imagination keeps nearly the same accuracy while being over 11x faster, at 5.9 ms per frame. Since each imagined condition has its own strengths, IDWorld then fuses them adaptively, weighting each condition for every probe-gallery pair. Extensive experiments show that IDWorld achieves state-of-the-art results on CCPG and CCGR-MINI, outperforms the compared methods on MEVID, and generalizes best to CASIA-B* and SUSTech1K, with the best mAP on CCVID. These results suggest identity world models offer a simple yet effective path to robust recognition. Code will be released.

</details>


### [90] [Phantom Success, Silent Failure: Auditing the Success Checkers of Simulated Manipulation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25179)
*Zirui Song,Jiadong Zhang,Zheng Lu,Xiaosong Ma,Xiaojun Chang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LLM流水线SimAudit，用于审计机器人操作基准中的成功判定器，发现虚假成功与静默失败两类缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖人工编写的成功判定器（success checker）以二值标签评估策略表现。通过专家演示只能证明判定器接受某一种完成任务的方式，但学习策略可能中途停止或以不同方式完成任务，导致判定器错误接受不完整执行（虚假成功）或错误拒绝符合指令的执行（静默失败），而此类判定器缺陷缺乏系统审计。

Method: 提出SimAudit：基于LLM的审计流水线。步骤为：(1) 同时分析任务指令与判定器代码，识别二者可能产生分歧的场景；(2) 在仿真中复现这些场景；(3) 运行原始判定器；(4) 通过多视角渲染进行视觉验证，判断实际行为是否满足指令，并进行去重。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、RoboCasa365与RoboDojo上，183个仿真测试用例均产生预测的判定器结果；经视觉验证与去重后，在49个任务中发现61个不同缺陷，包括40个虚假成功与21个静默失败。RoboCasa365中缺陷多为接受不完整执行，RoboDojo中多为拒绝有效执行，RoboTwin 2.0中两类错误均常见。

Conclusion: 验证成功判定器须同时测试不完整执行与专家演示之外的有效替代方式；当前基准的判定器存在系统性缺陷，亟需更严格的评测协议。

Abstract: Simulated manipulation benchmarks measure policy performance using hand-written success checkers that label each episode as a success or failure. Passing an expert demonstration shows that a checker accepts one way of completing the task. Learned policies, however, may stop after partial progress or complete the task in a different way. A checker may then accept an incomplete execution (phantom success) or reject an execution that satisfies the instruction (silent failure). In this paper, we introduce SimAudit, an LLM-based pipeline that audits success checkers against task instructions. SimAudit analyzes the instruction and checker code to identify scenarios where the two may disagree, recreates those scenarios in simulation, and runs the original checker. Multi-view renderings are then used to verify whether the observed behavior satisfies the instruction. Across RoboTwin 2.0, RoboCasa365, and RoboDojo, 183 simulated test cases produced the predicted checker verdict. Visual verification and deduplication yielded 61 distinct defects across 49 tasks, including 40 phantom successes and 21 silent failures. Most defects in RoboCasa365 accepted incomplete executions, most in RoboDojo rejected valid executions, and both error types were common in RoboTwin 2.0. These findings suggest that validating success checkers requires testing both incomplete executions and valid alternatives to expert demonstrations, and motivate more rigorous evaluation protocols for future benchmarks.

</details>


### [91] [Recurrent Vision Language Models Solve the Shell Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25210)
*Dingqiang Ye,Jiacong Xu,Wen Zhang,Yiqun Mei,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLMs在"藏球"视频跟踪任务上的瓶颈源于固定深度带来的串行计算上限；作者提出RecurrentVLM，让每层注意力在时间维度上循环复用自身输出的KV，实现单token回答达100%/99.6%，并在仅训练30次交换的情况下泛化到50次交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能轻松追踪被遮挡物体，但VLM在shell game（球藏在相同杯子下并不断交换）任务上接近随机；思维链模型在长序列上失败，微调只能覆盖训练中见过的长度。作者认为根本限制是串行计算：每次交换都需与上一次结果复合，而固定深度的VLM只有固定次数的状态转移。

Method: 提出RecurrentVLM：将预训练VLM的每个注意力层在时间上递归化，利用该层自身输出计算已完成帧组的key和value，使层深转化为沿时间的循环状态更新，从而用单个答案token完成长序列推理。

Result: 单答案token下VET-Bench达100%、MET-Bench达99.6%；最多训练30次交换即可在50次交换游戏上达99%。观察到多种现象：长度泛化在某个"顿悟时刻"突然出现；无递归时每次交换需消耗一层注意力；球的状态在前四层注意力中形成；递归以牺牲早期时刻细节为代价保留最新状态。

Conclusion: 递归VLM是追踪视频中未观测物体状态的一种有潜力的方案；串行计算深度是此类任务的关键瓶颈，时间维度上的循环可为固定深度模型提供可扩展的状态转移能力。

Abstract: Tracking a hidden object through a video is effortless for humans, yet it remains a bottleneck for vision language models (VLMs). In the shell game, where a ball is hidden under one of several identical cups that are then swapped, VLMs that answer directly stay near chance, thinking models fail on long games, and fine-tuning learns only the lengths it has seen. We argue that the limit is serial computation: each swap must be composed with the last result, and a VLM of fixed depth has only a fixed number of state transitions for it. To address this, we propose RecurrentVLM, which makes every attention layer of a pretrained VLM recur over time by computing the keys and values of finished frame groups from the layer's own output. With a single answer token, RecurrentVLM reaches 100% on VET-Bench and 99.6% on MET-Bench, and 99% on 50-swap games after training on at most 30 swaps. We also observe several interesting phenomena: length generalization emerges suddenly at an aha moment; without recurrence, each swap costs an attention layer; the ball's state forms in the first four attention layers; and recurrence keeps the latest state at the cost of details of earlier moments. These results suggest that recurrent VLMs are a potential solution for tracking the states of unobserved objects in video. Code will be released.

</details>


### [92] [Driver Gaze Target Identification via Gaze Estimation and Structured Driving Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25771)
*Xinze Cao,Ke Wang,Deyong Guan,Yuanquan Li,Ke Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出模块化框架，从车内驾驶员图像与前视道路图像同步识别物体/区域级注视目标，实验室Top-1达96.27%，LBW上任务相关物体79.92%、道路类85.58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注视区域分类与连续注意力定位只能提供有限信息，无法确定被注视场景目标的身份；智能车辆视觉注意力监测需要物体级信息。

Method: 四组件框架：(1) MFGaze——MAE预训练ViT结合头姿经FiLM特征级线性调制进行条件化；(2) 全景场景理解构建结构化候选目标集；(3) 多项式岭回归经个体化校准将注视特征映射到2D场景图像坐标；(4) 熵驱动目标推断，自适应融合边界距离几何与类别级语义先验。评测使用三个公开注视数据集、Cityscapes实验室实验及LBW数据集。

Result: 实验室整体Top-1目标识别准确率96.27%；LBW经序列特定校准后任务相关物体Top-1为79.92%、道路类85.58%。MFGaze平均角误差最低：MPIIFaceGaze 4.06°、ETH-XGaze 4.23°、Gaze360 10.52°，跨数据集泛化强；敏感性分析显示超参数扰动下性能稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为驾驶员监测中物体级注视信息的来源。

Abstract: Identifying a driver's gaze target provides object-level information for visual attention monitoring in intelligent vehicles. Gaze-zone classification and continuous attention localization provide limited information about the identity of the attended scene target. This paper proposes a modular framework that identifies object- or region-level gaze targets from synchronized in-cabin driver images and forward-view road images. The framework comprises four components: MFGaze, which combines a Vision Transformer pretrained with a masked autoencoder (MAE) and head-pose conditioning through feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM); panoptic scene understanding, which constructs a structured candidate set; polynomial ridge regression, which maps gaze features to 2D scene-image coordinates through individualized calibration; and entropy-driven target inference, which adaptively fuses boundary-distance geometry with class-level semantic priors. Experiments evaluate the framework using three public gaze datasets, a laboratory test using Cityscapes road scenes and self-collected gaze data, and the Look Both Ways (LBW) dataset. The complete framework achieves an overall Top-1 gaze target identification accuracy of 96.27% in the laboratory experiment. After sequence-specific calibration, it achieves Top-1 accuracies of 79.92% for task-relevant objects and 85.58% for the road class on LBW. MFGaze achieves the lowest mean angular errors among the compared methods, with 4.06$^\circ$, 4.23$^\circ$, and 10.52$^\circ$ on MPIIFaceGaze, ETH-XGaze, and Gaze360, respectively, and demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization. Sensitivity analyses show stable performance under hyperparameter perturbations. These results support the framework as a source of object-level gaze information for driver monitoring.

</details>


### [93] [Verifying Before Enhancing: Target Evidence Chain Verification for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25724)
*Jiayi Zuo,Songwei Pei,Shuhuai Wang,Yifei Wu,Shangguang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VBE-UNet（目标证据链验证网络），以互补证据线索评估候选目标的可靠性，超越单纯依赖响应强度，提升红外小目标检测精度并降低虚警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标尺寸小、纹理弱、边界模糊，与复杂背景难以区分；且类目标背景结构在多尺度上产生显著响应，仅凭响应强度不足以可靠判别目标。

Method: 构建证据链验证框架：反事实背景解释模块对局部目标证据建模；联合证据验证整合跨尺度支持与基于扰动的贡献验证以指导特征重组；由此得到的可靠性估计用于选择性特征精炼，增强目标相关响应、抑制无支撑的背景激活。

Result: 在IRSTD-1K、NUDT-SIRST与NUAA-SIRST数据集上，检测精度优于SOTA方法且虚警率更低；消融实验与证据可靠性分析验证了验证框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 以证据链验证替代单点响应强度判据，可有效区分真实目标与类目标背景干扰，为红外小目标检测提供可靠的候选判别范式。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection is essential for early warning, surveillance, remote sensing, and space-based target perception. However, infrared small targets are typically tiny, weakly textured, and boundary-blurred, making them difficult to distinguish from complex backgrounds. Moreover, target-like background structures can produce salient responses across multiple scales, making response strength alone insufficient for reliable target discrimination. To address this challenge, we propose VBE-UNet, a Target Evidence Chain Verification Network that assesses candidate reliability beyond response magnitude through complementary evidence cues. Specifically, counterfactual background explanation models local target evidence, while joint evidence verification integrates cross-scale support and perturbation-based contribution verification to guide feature recomposition. The resulting reliability estimates guide selective feature refinement, promoting target-relevant responses while reducing unsupported background activations. Extensive experiments on IRSTD-1K, NUDT-SIRST, and NUAA-SIRST demonstrate that VBE-UNet achieves superior detection accuracy and lower false-alarm rates compared with state-of-the-art methods. Ablation studies and evidence reliability analysis further validate the effectiveness of the proposed verification framework.

</details>


### [94] [TRACE: Attribute Execution for Sequential Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25648)
*Xiyang Zhang,Yuanhe Tian,Hongzhi Wang,Yan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE 将“持续生效的属性”与其视觉载体分离，在完整请求校验下优先执行局部外观操作，实现连续图像编辑中的颜色-材质联合恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续图像编辑需在满足新指令的同时保留仍生效的旧要求，避免用户反复重述或修补。难点在于：检索到的参考图可能在已过时表面上带有有效颜色，而当前图像可能缺少新请求的材质；仅靠参考选择或精确物体掩码都无法说明如何协调占据同一区域的属性。

Method: 提出 TRACE：1) active-goal binding，识别应持续保留的目标；2) carrier selection，为属性提供合适的当前像素或新生成像素作为视觉载体；3) tonal calibration，抑制高光与阴影附近的偏色。整体在完整请求检查下优先采用局部外观操作。

Result: 在冻结编辑器、独立视觉阅读器、新源图像以及完整连续编辑基准上均取得验证，支持颜色-材质的联合恢复，并显示局部执行能保持周围场景不变。

Conclusion: 将属性与载体解耦并以局部操作为主的执行策略，可有效解决连续编辑中属性冲突与旧要求保留问题。

Abstract: Given an image and successive user instructions, sequential image editing should return an updated image that satisfies each new request and preserves earlier requirements that remain active. This lets users develop a result without repeatedly restating or repairing their decisions. The difficulty is that a retrieved image can contain a valid color on an obsolete surface, while the current image may lack the newly requested material. Neither reference selection nor a precise object mask alone specifies how to reconcile attributes occupying the same region. We present TRACE, which separates the continuing attribute from its visual carrier and prioritizes local appearance operations under complete-request checks. Active-goal binding identifies what should persist, carrier selection supplies suitable current or newly generated pixels, and tonal calibration reduces tinting near highlights and shadows. Experiments across frozen editors, independent visual readers, new source images, and complete sequential benchmarks support joint color-material recovery and show how local execution preserves the surrounding scene.

</details>


### [95] [RelGeo: Pair-Conditioned Relational Calibration for Frozen Diffusion Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25628)
*Zixuan Dai,Zhiliang Zhang,Daosong Hu,Tang Zheng,Xinkai Liu,Tianyu Lei,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RelGeo，一个成对条件化的关系校准框架，将互惠扩散亲和与RoMa双向输运等异构关系编码为共享源-目标坐标，在无需额外语义对应训练的前提下增强冻结扩散描述子的余弦匹配，平均提升5.51个PCK@0.10点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结扩散描述子虽能召回高质量语义对应候选，但余弦最近邻匹配常选错位置；在DIFT-SD2.1、DiTF-FLUX于SPair-71k、AP-10K、SOCO(CC/SOC/Cross-SOC)上，41-69%的Top-1错误在原始Top-5空间峰中仍存在满足PCK@0.10的有效匹配，说明瓶颈在于候选选择而非候选生成。此外，描述子排序与当前成对关系证据可能偏好不同候选，二者具有部分互补性。

Method: RelGeo将异构关系表达为共享的源-目标坐标：谱关系坐标(SRC)编码互惠扩散Query/Key亲和，形变恒等坐标(WIC)编码可靠性加权的RoMa双向输运端点；以二者内积增广冻结描述子相似度，同时保持全域余弦最近邻匹配，无需额外语义对应训练。

Result: 全部十项评测均获提升，平均+5.51 PCK@0.10；固定候选重排序与成对错位对照实验验证了选择质量的改善及对当前成对关系对齐的依赖性。

Conclusion: 语义对应的关键瓶颈在于候选选择而非候选生成；引入当前成对关系证据进行校准可有效修正冻结描述子匹配，且互补关系证据的融合具有可验证的必要性。

Abstract: Frozen diffusion descriptors retrieve strong semantic correspondence candidates, yet cosine nearest-neighbor matching can select incorrect locations despite valid high-ranking alternatives. Across DIFT-SD2.1 and DiTF-FLUX on SPair-71k, AP-10K, and SOCO (CC, SOC, Cross-SOC), 41-69% of Top-1 errors retain a PCK@0.10-valid match among the original Top-5 spatial peaks, revealing a persistent candidate-selection bottleneck. We find that descriptor rankings and current-pair relational evidence can favor different candidates: reciprocal diffusion Query/Key associations and pretrained RoMa transport exhibit distinct, partly complementary preferences. To incorporate this evidence into matching decisions, we introduce RelGeo, a Pair-Conditioned Relational Calibration framework expressing heterogeneous relations as shared source-target coordinates. Spectral Relational Coordinates (SRC) encode reciprocal diffusion affinities, while Warp Identity Coordinates (WIC) encode reliability-weighted bidirectional transport endpoints. Their inner products augment frozen descriptor similarity while preserving full-domain cosine nearest-neighbor matching without additional semantic-correspondence training. RelGeo improves all ten evaluations by an average of 5.51 PCK@0.10 points; fixed-candidate reranking and pair-misalignment controls corroborate improved selection and dependence on current-pair relational alignment.

</details>


### [96] [LoopedWAM: Looped World Action Models for Elastic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25595)
*Anh Dao,Xinxin Liu,Hang Yu,Jeffrey A Chan Santiago,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopedWAM 通过在每个专家内部循环复用共享的六层 transformer 核心，以 0.58B 参数实现 30 层等效深度，并在视频/动作专家上采用非对称深度与弹性自蒸馏，兼顾精度与部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）将生成式视频建模与机器人动作预测耦合，但通常依赖大型视频与动作 transformer，导致部署效率受限。作者希望在显著压缩模型规模与显存占用的同时，保持竞争性性能，并支持多精度—时延运行点。

Method: 提出 LoopedWAM：每个专家仅存储 6 个 block，通过循环复用执行 30 层等效计算（每专家仅 12 block 存储）。针对视频与动作推理成本不对称（视觉特征每次查询只算一次，动作在每个采样步重复计算），允许两专家采用不同深度。进一步提出弹性自蒸馏，训练单一弹性 checkpoint LoopedWAM-E，覆盖不同动作深度与采样步数预算。无机器人预训练，策略参数 0.58B。

Result: LIBERO 96.1%、RoboTwin 2.0 91.9%、RoboCasaGR1 52.2%；匹配推理条件下峰值 GPU 显存比 Fast-WAM 少 7.4×。LIBERO 上 LoopedWAM-E 在 H100 上 27.6 ms 达 96.0%、24.7 ms 达 95.2%，单 checkpoint 提供多运行点；2 采样步 + 4 次动作循环时，三基准上与 LoopedWAM 差距均在 0.5 点内。消融显示权重共享的深度复用可保持竞争性能，而激进降低动作深度需显式的预算感知训练。真实 AgileX PiPER 机械臂四任务 80 次试验成功率 71.3%。

Conclusion: 循环权重共享与非对称专家深度是压缩 WAM 的有效手段，配合弹性自蒸馏可在单一 checkpoint 内实现精度—时延的可调权衡，并具备真机可行性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) couple generative video modeling with robotic action prediction but typically require large video and action transformers, limiting deployment efficiency. We present LoopedWAM, a compact WAM that recurrently applies a shared six-block core in each expert, executing 30 transformer layers while storing only 12 blocks per expert. To account for their asymmetric inference costs, the video and action experts can operate at different depths: visual features are computed once per query, whereas action computation repeats at every sampling step. We further propose elastic self-distillation, which trains a single elastic checkpoint, LoopedWAM-E, across action-depth and sampling-step budgets. With only 0.58B policy parameters and no robot pretraining, LoopedWAM achieves 96.1% success on LIBERO, 91.9% on RoboTwin 2.0, and 52.2% on RoboCasaGR1, while using $7.4\times$ less peak GPU memory than Fast-WAM under matched inference measurements. On LIBERO, LoopedWAM-E achieves 96.0% success in 27.6 ms or 95.2% in 24.7 ms on an H100, providing multiple accuracy-latency operating points from a single checkpoint; with two sampling steps and four action loops, it stays within 0.5 points of LoopedWAM on all three benchmarks. Ablations demonstrate that weight-shared depth preserves competitive performance, while aggressive action-depth reduction requires explicit budget-aware training. On a real AgileX PiPER arm, LoopedWAM succeeds in 71.3% of 80 trials across four tasks.

</details>


### [97] [Support-Guided Triadic Decoding for Hallucination Mitigation in Multimodal Dialogue with LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25535)
*Yiheng Li,Wenhao Wang,Xingyi Li,Zhilin Tang,Zichang Tan,Zhen Lei,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架SGTD，通过选择性控制不可靠历史的影响并融合互补预测，在多模态多轮对话中抑制幻觉历史传播同时保留有用上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态多轮对话中历史起双重作用：真实回答提供有用上下文，幻觉回答会跨轮传播错误。现有免训练方法全局调整历史影响，难以在抑制误导信息的同时不削弱有用上下文。

Method: 包含四部分：1) 历史答案支持度估计，度量历史回答相对同类型候选的视觉支持度，识别可能误导后续生成的低支持内容；2) 支持靶向注意力干预，削弱该内容影响而保留周边对话，得到支持精炼预测；3) 三元解码，将该预测与全历史预测、无答案视觉证据融合；4) 支持自适应合理性约束，利用视觉一致性与历史支持过滤不合理token。

Result: 在MMHalSnowball与VisDiaHalBench上，跨代表性LVLM，SGTD在幻觉历史条件下持续取得最高准确率，同时保留真实上下文的收益。

Conclusion: 选择性而非全局地调控历史影响，可在免训练条件下有效缓解多模态多轮对话的幻觉传播，兼顾鲁棒性与上下文利用。

Abstract: In multimodal multi-turn dialogue, dialogue history plays a dual role: reliable responses provide useful context for subsequent turns, whereas hallucinated responses can propagate errors across turns. Existing training-free methods globally adjust the effect of dialogue history on generation, making it difficult to suppress misleading information without weakening useful context. To address this trade-off, we introduce Support-Guided Triadic Decoding (SGTD), a training-free framework that selectively controls the influence of unreliable history and fuses complementary predictions during decoding. Specifically, Historical Answer Support Estimation measures the visual support of historical responses against type-matched alternatives and identifies low-support content that may mislead later generation. Support-Targeted Attention Intervention then weakens the influence of this content while preserving the surrounding dialogue, producing a support-refined prediction. Triadic Decoding combines this prediction with the full-history prediction and answer-free visual evidence, while the Support-Adaptive Plausibility Constraint filters implausible tokens using visual agreement and historical support. Experiments on MMHalSnowball and VisDiaHalBench across representative LVLMs show that SGTD consistently achieves the highest accuracy under hallucinated histories while preserving the benefits of factual context.

</details>


### [98] [Multi-View Relighting with Mergeable Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25523)
*Yinan Wang,Rohan Kadkol,Hyunjin Yoo,Jean-François Lalonde,James J. Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MergeableRF：通过合并模型参数实现辐射场重光照，将各基光下训练的外观网络参数按目标环境图的基光系数加权合并，单次渲染即得重光照结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光传输具有线性性：混合光照下的图像等于各基光下图像的加权组合。据此希望直接合并基光下辐射场的参数来重光照，避免材质估计、额外重光照网络或针对目标光照的逐场景优化，并避免图像空间混合带来的多次渲染。

Method: 联合共享几何与每个基光独立的 appearance network，网络从同一初始化出发；引入 merge-aware training，使参数组合能复现对应的图像组合；给定目标 HDR 环境图，计算其基光系数并据此把多个外观网络合并为单一重光照模型；方法兼容 NeRF 与 3DGS。

Result: 在合成 Neural Gaffer 物体与真实 OLATverse 采集上，重光照质量与在相同多光照观测上训练的方法相当；每视角仅需一次渲染。

Conclusion: 通过 merge-aware 训练可将参数可合并性显式注入辐射场表示，实现无需材质估计与目标优化的高效重光照，且对 NeRF/3DGS 通用。

Abstract: We propose MergeableRF, a method for radiance field relighting through model parameter merging. Given multi-view images of a scene under known basis lights, our goal is to relight the scene by directly combining the parameters of radiance fields trained under these lights. This is motivated by the linearity of light transport: the image under a mixture of lights is the same weighted combination of the images under each light. However, combining the parameters of independently trained radiance fields does not produce the corresponding combination of images. To address this, we represent a scene with shared geometry and a separate appearance network for each basis light. We start these networks from the same initialization and introduce merge-aware training, which teaches their parameter combinations to reproduce the corresponding combinations of images. Given a target HDR environment map, we compute its basis-light coefficients and use them to merge the appearance networks into a single relit model. Our method supports both Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), without material estimation, additional relighting networks, or target-specific optimization. Unlike image-space blending, it requires only one rendering pass per viewpoint. Experiments on synthetic Neural Gaffer objects and real OLATverse captures demonstrate relighting quality competitive with methods trained on the same multi-light observations.

</details>


### [99] [PRIO: Pre-trained Representation for Interactive Objects in 4D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25500)
*Shixun Huang,Lifu Wei,Yue Chang,Chen Geng,Heng Zhang,Jinhyung Ahn,Caoliwen Wang,Siyuan Chen,Donglai Xiang,Jiajun Wu,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRIO 是一种预训练的交互式物体表示，能从静态几何单次前向预测物体特定的构型空间，作为拉格朗日力学的广义坐标，使物体在交互式速率下产生物理一致的运动与形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 世界虽可大规模采集、重建与生成，但其中物体是静止的——枕头不会被压缩、门不会摆动，缺乏对物体如何运动、形变并实时响应用户动作的表示。构建交互式 4D 世界模型需要这类表示。

Method: 给定静态几何，PRIO 通过单次前向传播预测物体特定的构型空间（configuration space），为拉格朗日力学提供广义坐标；构型在这些坐标下是线性的，因此动力学所需量可预计算，后续动力学仿真以交互速率运行、无需额外网络推理。模型在多样化 4D 轨迹上预训练，学习可泛化到未见形状的构型先验，支持网格、点云与 3D 高斯表示。

Result: PRIO 在真实到仿真（real-to-sim）工作流与机器人操作中展示了应用；相比现有 4D 生成方法，其动力学生成更快，且更忠实地遵循用户动作。

Conclusion: 通过学习物体特定的广义坐标构型空间并结合拉格朗日动力学，PRIO 为重建与生成场景中的物体提供了实时、可交互、可泛化的物理运动表示。

Abstract: 3D worlds can now be captured, reconstructed, and generated at scale, yet the objects in them stay still: a pressed pillow does not compress, and a pulled door does not swing. Bringing these objects into interactive 4D world models requires a representation of how they can move, deform, and respond physically to user actions in real time. We present PRIO, a pre-trained representation for interactive objects. Given static geometry, PRIO predicts an object-specific configuration space in a single forward pass. This space provides generalized coordinates for Lagrangian mechanics, allowing physical laws and user actions to govern motion. Configurations are linear in these coordinates, allowing the quantities needed by the dynamics to be precomputed. Dynamics then run at interactive rates without further network inference. Pre-trained on diverse 4D trajectories, PRIO learns priors over object configurations that generalize to unseen shapes. It supports meshes, point clouds, and 3D Gaussians, bringing objects in reconstructed and generated scenes into interaction. We demonstrate its use in real-to-sim workflows and robot manipulation. In addition, compared with existing 4D generation methods, PRIO generates dynamics faster and follows user actions more faithfully.

</details>


### [100] [Calibrate in Context: Efficient Data-Free Quantization from Small Synthetic Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25711)
*Haowen Hua,Sen Lin,Yaokun Huang,Zeyi Shao,Hao Zhang,Wei Sui,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出功能网格校准（FGC）与渐进反演，实现小规模合成图像池的低比特量化恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模合成数据池下的低比特（W4A4/W5A5）恢复精度不足：局部响应误差小的量化网格未必对学生块的上下文误差最优，且图像合成质量有限。

Method: 两方面改进：(1) 图像合成端采用渐进反演，用逐步加深的教师前缀更新同一批图像；(2) 学生准备端提出功能网格校准（FGC），在学生当前块输入构建的全精度响应下选择激活尺度与整数偏移，权重固定、仅在消费算子处评估候选，以近局部打分成本引入块上下文。(3) 序列化评测九种骨干、18种W4A4/W5A5设置。

Result: 1,000张图像时全部18个W4A4/W5A5设置均超过MimiQ；256张图像时DeiT-S/ViT-S在W4A4下分别达68.20%/66.90% ImageNet top-1，单张RTX 5090耗时26–27分钟；相较增强版1K图像MimiQ提升13.03与24.36个百分点，准备成本更低、恢复更新次数相同；ImageNetV2上优势保持。

Conclusion: 将块上下文纳入校准、并渐进提升合成图像质量，可在极小数据预算与低算力下实现高精度低比特恢复。

Abstract: We enable accurate low-bit recovery from small synthetic pools by improving both image synthesis and student preparation. A grid with lower local response error can incur greater error in the student's block context. Functional Grid Calibration (FGC) selects activation scales and integer offsets against full-precision responses constructed from the student's current block input. With weights fixed during calibration, FGC evaluates candidates only at their consuming operators, bringing block context to calibration at nearly local-scoring cost. For DeiT and ViT, progressive inversion updates the same images through increasingly deep teacher prefixes. With 1,000 images, the complete pipeline exceeds MimiQ's reported accuracy in all 18 W4A4/W5A5 settings across nine backbones. With 256 images, DeiT-S and ViT-S reach 68.20% and 66.90% ImageNet top-1 accuracy at W4A4 in 26-27 minutes on one RTX 5090. The pipeline outperforms strengthened 1K-image MimiQ by 13.03 and 24.36 percentage points at lower preparation cost and the same recovery-update count. The same low-budget checkpoints retain their advantage on ImageNetV2.

</details>


### [101] [Fixing the Twin, Not the Detector: Sim-to-Real Roadside LiDAR Detection from Digital-Twin Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26035)
*KuanLin Chen,Feng-jui Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一套纯合成数据训练的路侧LiDAR检测pipeline，通过对齐数字孪生与真实场景的系统性差异（坐标、点密度、框约定）、合成缺失行人/骑行者与街道杂波、按真实无标注帧的逐格点密度重采样、以及无标签后处理，在UrbanTwin Sim2Real挑战赛LUMPI赛道获第一（0.5541），V2X-Real赛道第五（0.8102）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实路侧LiDAR检测器标注成本高昂，数字孪生合成数据有望替代人工标注，但孪生与真实站点之间存在传感器差异、目标差异与标注约定差异，导致sim-to-real性能下降。作者想验证：这类问题的收益主要来源是检测器本身，还是孪生-真实差异的校正。

Method: 所有检测器均为仅在合成数据上训练的CenterPoint，单张8GB笔记本GPU训练。核心步骤：1）对齐孪生坐标、点密度与包围框约定到测试传感器；2）补全孪生缺失的行人与骑行者，并加入街道杂波作为无标注负样本；3）按真实尺寸与真实点密度重建孪生目标；4）非学习的重采样至无标注真实帧的逐格（per-cell）点密度；5）无标签后处理利用固定路侧传感器的先验——静态结构逐帧复现、真实目标具有特征性点统计。

Result: 逐格点密度重采样使LUMPI真实度从0.589提升到0.947，接近真实帧之间的0.949；竞赛成绩为LUMPI赛道第一（0.5541）、V2X-Real赛道第五（0.8102）。逐组件并列报告验证集与测试集增益，发现系统性sim-to-real差异校正的增益可迁移到测试集，通常保留验证增益的1/4至3/4，而依赖少量验证目标的调参选择则无法迁移。

Conclusion: sim-to-real路侧LiDAR检测的主要增益来自孪生与真实站点差异的校正，而非检测器结构；系统性差异校正具有良好可迁移性，可据验证集增益外推测试集表现，而验证集过拟合型选择不可靠。

Abstract: Roadside LiDAR detectors are expensive to train on real labels, and digital twins promise to replace these labels, but a detector trained on a twin meets a different sensor, different objects and different annotation conventions on the real site. We describe the pipeline that placed first in the LUMPI track of the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge (IEEE BigData 2026 Cup, 0.5541 combined score) and fifth on its V2X-Real track (0.8102). Every detector is a CenterPoint model trained only on synthetic data, on a single 8 GB laptop GPU. Most of the gain came from correcting differences between the twin and the real site rather than from the detector. We align the twin's coordinates, point density and box convention with the test sensor, generate the pedestrians and riders that the twins lack, plus street clutter as unlabeled negatives, and rebuild twin objects at real size and real point density. A non-learned resampling to the per-cell point density of unlabeled real frames lifts LUMPI realism from 0.589 to 0.947, against 0.949 between real frames. Label-free post-processing then exploits what a fixed roadside sensor guarantees: static structures recur in every frame, and real objects have characteristic point statistics. Reporting the validation and test gain of each component side by side, we find that corrections of systematic sim-to-real differences transfer to the test set, typically keeping a quarter to three quarters of their validation gain, while choices that fit the few objects of a validation set do not.

</details>


### [102] [Why Flat Drafts Stay Flat: Region-Mean Latents Govern Texture in Flow-Based Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25930)
*Zihan Gu,Jiangcan Jia,Hua Zhang,Lulu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文揭示了预训练流模型在平面色块草图上仍保持"扁平"外观的机理：VAE潜空间中区域均值偏移沿共享通道方向，并据此提出免训练渲染与LoRA适配等干预方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面色块草图只指定布局与区域色彩，内部细节留空；但预训练流式生成器即便在照片级提示下仍保留扁平外观，这一持久行为的成因尚不清楚，作者旨在追溯其跨尺度机制并加以利用。

Method: 在VAE潜空间分析像素变化与区域均值的关系；在FLUX.1-dev与Qwen-Image中做受控场交换实验（叠加照片导出的区域均值场 vs. 互补场）；用二阶编码展开解释编码器曲率如何将零均值扰动转为均值偏移；用可解析求解的条件高斯流说明区域噪声平均在高信噪比下带来近单位端点敏感度。干预手段包括：编码前像素抖动、均值场缩放、早期边界保持的免训练渲染；配对LoRA加草稿条件；显式区域均值引导。

Result: 扁平化会系统性移动潜均值，偏移集中于各骨干网络的共享通道方向；给扁平潜变量加上照片导出的区域均值场可恢复纹理，而仅加互补场基本保持扁平；配对LoRA提升内容恢复并在标准速度目标下产生照片式区域均值；区域均值引导在适配后仍有效，可连续控制纹理并改善色彩保真度。

Conclusion: 编码偏移与生成持久行为之间存在因果联系；所提干预在纹理、空间保真与语义恢复三个维度上得到评估，为草图上色/渲染提供可控且可解释的路径。

Abstract: Flat color-block drafts specify layout and regional color while leaving interior detail unspecified, yet pretrained flow-based generators can preserve their flat appearance even under photorealistic prompts. We trace this behavior to a cross-scale mechanism linking fine pixel variation to regional means in variational autoencoder (VAE) latent space. Flattening photo regions systematically shifts their latent means, with offsets concentrated along a shared channel direction within each backbone. Controlled field exchanges in FLUX.1-dev and Qwen-Image establish the effect of this displacement on texture generation: adding the photo-derived regional mean field to a flat latent restores texture, whereas adding its complementary field alone largely preserves flatness. A second-order encoding expansion explains how encoder curvature can convert zero-mean perturbations into mean shifts. An analytically solvable conditional Gaussian flow shows how regional noise averaging enables near-unit endpoint sensitivity at high signal-to-noise ratios. This mechanism motivates training-free rendering through pixel dither before encoding, mean-field scaling, and early boundary holding. Paired low-rank adaptation (LoRA) with draft conditioning improves content recovery and produces photo-like regional means under the standard velocity objective. Explicit regional mean guidance remains effective after adaptation, continuously controlling texture and improving color fidelity. These findings connect encoding shifts to persistent generation behavior and support interventions evaluated for texture, spatial fidelity, and semantic recovery.

</details>


### [103] [Does It Depend on the Split? Frozen Satellite Features vs. Fine-Tuned CNNs for Tree Species Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25902)
*Dharani Deivasihamani,Nandakishore Puttashamachar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在TreeSatAI 20类树种分类任务上，官方划分下微调ResNet-50明显优于冻结DINOv3（macro F1 0.764 vs 0.685）；但官方测试集存在28.8%空间重叠、最近邻标签复制准确率83.6%的泄漏。改用2 km网格+200 m缓冲的空间划分后，两者差异不显著（0.648 vs 0.640，95% CI [-0.054, 0.016]），各标签预算下学习曲线重叠；仅在5个稀有类上冻结模型均值更高（0.566 vs 0.445，CI含零）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "自监督基础模型比监督训练需要更少标签"是常见论断，但作者质疑其在遥感林业任务上的可靠性，并怀疑官方数据集划分存在空间泄漏会夸大监督微调的优势，因此需要在地理分离的评估协议下重新检验该论断。

Method: 任务为0.2 m航拍影像上20类树种分类（TreeSatAI）。对比对象：冻结的DINOv3（4.93亿卫星影像预训练，仅训练线性分类头）与端到端微调的ImageNet ResNet-50。先在官方split上评估，再构建空间划分（hold out整块2 km方格并设200 m缓冲）重新评估，覆盖343–33,126个训练patch的标签预算，并给出配对95%置信区间、稀有类分析与特征诊断；最后在验证集上选择输入尺寸与层（224 px、block 16）。

Result: 官方split：微调0.764 vs 冻结0.685；其中28.8%测试patch与训练/验证patch重叠，最近邻标签复制在其中83.6%正确。空间split：0.648 vs 0.640，配对95% CI [-0.054, 0.016]，不可分辨；全部标签预算下学习曲线重叠；5个最稀有类冻结模型均值F1 0.566 vs 0.445（CI [-0.05, 0.23]）。诊断显示冻结特征主要缺失近缘种（如两种本土栎类）的区分能力。调参后冻结模型在空间split升至0.662。

Conclusion: 官方划分下的"决定性"结论在地理分离评估下变为不确定，说明空间泄漏会显著影响此类比较的推断。冻结基础模型在小样本稀有类上可能具相对优势，但受限于对近缘种的判别力；输入尺寸与层的选择可带来可观提升。作者公开空间划分与代码。

Abstract: Self-supervised foundation models are often said to need fewer labels than supervised training. We test this on a forestry task: classifying 20 tree species classes in 0.2 m aerial images from the TreeSatAI dataset. We compare a frozen DINOv3 model pretrained on 493 million satellite images, with only a linear classifier trained on top, against an ImageNet ResNet-50 that is fine-tuned end to end. On the official split, fine-tuning wins clearly (macro F1 0.764 vs. 0.685). However, 28.8% of the official test patches overlap a training or validation patch, and copying the label of the nearest such patch is correct for 83.6% of them. We therefore build a spatial split that holds out whole 2 km squares with a 200 m buffer. On this split the comparison becomes inconclusive (0.648 vs. 0.640, paired 95% CI of the difference [-0.054, 0.016]), and the learning curves of the two models overlap at every label budget from 343 to 33,126 training patches. On the five rarest classes, the frozen model achieves a higher mean F1 (0.566 vs. 0.445), although the confidence interval for this difference includes zero ([-0.05, 0.23]). Diagnostics show that the frozen features mainly miss differences between closely related species, such as the two native oaks. Choosing the input size and layer on validation data (224 px, block 16) raises the frozen model to 0.662 on the spatial split. A comparison that looks decisive on the official split is thus inconclusive under geographically separated evaluation. We release the spatial split and our code.

</details>


### [104] [Thinking in Streams: Proactive Spatial Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25865)
*Junyoung Hong,Minsu Kim,Jaehyun Kim,Jeonghwan Cho,Jiwoo Lee,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究流式视频中的前瞻性空间推理：模型预先获得查询，需判断空间条件何时成立并及时作答。提出ProSpace-Bench（9项任务，涵盖物体/相机/场景推理）与ProSpace-Train训练集，并设计ProSpace-VLM，通过将因果估计的显式几何编码注入VLM中间层、以KV cache作空间记忆并采用空间token选择实现高效流式处理。实验显示VLM与人类存在显著差距，ProSpace-VLM在及时响应与正确弃答上优于微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频中判断空间条件是否成立，需要在视角不断变化的情况下持续维护并更新空间知识；现有VLM缺乏由此触发的"何时响应"能力，因此需要前瞻性空间推理这一新设定与相应基准。

Method: 构建ProSpace-Bench（物体、相机、场景三类共九项任务）与ProSpace-Train监督训练数据；提出ProSpace-VLM：从流入帧因果地估计几何编码并注入VLM中间层，复用KV cache作为空间记忆，并通过空间token选择实现高效流式推理。

Result: 实验表明VLM与人类在及时空间判断上存在巨大差距；ProSpace-VLM相比微调基线提升了及时响应率和正确弃答率，分析揭示其如何在视角变化中表征已观测的空间信息。

Conclusion: 前瞻性空间推理是当前VLM的明显短板；将显式几何编码注入中间层并结合KV cache空间记忆与token选择，是提升流式空间时序判断能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Recognizing when a requested spatial condition holds in streaming video can require maintaining and updating spatial knowledge across changing viewpoints.
