We study this capability through proactive spatial reasoning, where a model receives a query in advance and determines whether and when to respond as the stream unfolds. We introduce ProSpace-Bench, comprising nine tasks across object, camera, and scene reasoning, and ProSpace-Train for supervised training. To support spatial decisions, we propose ProSpace-VLM, which injects explicit geometric encodings estimated causally from incoming frames into intermediate VLM layers, using the existing KV cache as spatial memory and spatial token selection for efficient streaming. Experiments reveal a substantial gap between VLMs and humans, while ProSpace-VLM improves timely responses and correct abstention over fine-tuned baselines. Analyses examine how ProSpace-VLM represents previously observed spatial information across changing viewpoints.

</details>


### [105] [BADAS-3.0: Real-Time Driving Perception and Planning from One Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26294)
*Roni Goldshmidt,Hamish Scott,Lorenzo Niccolini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 仅用单相机与实时延迟，1B参数视频编码器BADAS-3.0的性能边界研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶模型常依赖多相机与大算力，且评测多为常规驾驶场景，缺乏对"单相机+实时延迟"约束下性能极限与安全性的系统考察。

Method: BADAS-3.0：1B参数视频编码器加四个小预测头；输入单前视相机2秒视频，<50ms内输出当前速度、刚行驶的度量路径、碰撞风险分数及6.4秒内6条候选未来轨迹；并新增两个开环安全基准（真实行车记录仪碰撞/险情、Euro NCAP场景）。

Result: 在给定车速下，跨五个轨迹基准的minADE₆比34B的Alpamayo 2 Super低44%，后者用六相机且慢100倍以上；增益源自双方均未训练过的相机配置，在共享配置上二者持平；两个安全基准上通过率高于所有已发布规划器。

Conclusion: 单相机、实时延迟的紧凑模型可实现强轨迹与安全表现，但跨相机配置的对比提示基准公平性需谨慎；安全性需专用基准评估。

Abstract: We ask how far a driving model can get with one camera and real-time latency. BADAS-3.0 is a 1B-parameter video encoder with four small heads. From two seconds of one forward camera, it predicts the current speed, the metric path just driven, a collision-risk score and six candidate futures over 6.4 seconds, in under 50 ms. Given the car's current speed, BADAS-3.0 has 44% lower minADE$_6$ over five trajectory benchmarks than the 34B Alpamayo 2 Super, which reads six cameras and runs over 100x slower. The gain comes from camera rigs that neither model was trained on. On the rig they share, the two are on par. Existing benchmarks are mostly nominal driving, so we add two open-loop safety benchmarks, one of real dashcam collisions and near-misses and one of Euro NCAP scenarios. On both, BADAS-3.0 has a higher pass rate than every published planner we tested.

</details>


### [106] [MultiBinder: Preventing Multi-Subject Identity Leakage in Personalized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26164)
*Wei Li,Yiheng Zhang,Zhaofan Qiu,Ting Yao,Xiaoyan Sun,Tao Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MultiBinder，采用"先绑定后叙事"范式，通过跨模态实体绑定与叙事感知主体排布两阶段条件化，解决多主体个性化视频生成中的身份泄漏与语义纠缠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化视频生成存在可扩展性瓶颈：定制主体数量增加时，现有方法出现严重身份泄漏与语义纠缠，生成视频属性混杂、叙事不连贯。作者将其归因于扩散模型在融合前缺乏文本实体短语与对应视觉模式之间的显式绑定。

Method: 两阶段条件化：(1) 跨模态实体绑定，设计实体感知Q-Former从每个主体参考图提取身份中心特征，并与专属实体短语紧对齐，得到解耦的"身份token"；(2) 由MLLM担任"叙事导演"的叙事感知主体排布，从文本提示中捕获各主体动作，合成"谁在做什么"的情境化主体特征并保留角色特异区分。随后经专用条件通路注入视频扩散模型，实现精确属性分配。

Result: 在通用基准与新建多主体基准上，MultiBinder在多主体身份保真度上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 显式"绑定—叙事"先于融合的条件化设计可有效缓解多主体视频生成的身份泄漏与语义纠缠，为可扩展的个性化视频生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Personalized video generation faces a significant scalability challenge: as the number of custom subjects increases, existing methods suffer from severe identity leakage and semantic entanglement, resulting in videos with blended attributes and incoherent narratives. We identify that this stems from a lack of explicit binding between textual entity phrases and their corresponding visual patterns prior to fusion in diffusion models. To address this, we propose MultiBinder, a novel framework built on a ``bind-then-narrate'' paradigm. Our approach introduces a two-stage conditioning process before video diffusion. First, Cross-modal Entity Binding devises an entity-aware Q-Former to extract identity-centric features from each subject's reference image and tightly align them with its unique entity phrase, yielding disentangled ``identity tokens.'' Second, a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) acts as a ``narrative director'' in the Narrative-Aware Subject Arrangement stage, capturing the action of each subject from the text prompt and synthesizes contextualized subject features that encapsulate ``who is doing what'' and preserve role-specific distinctions. These features are then injected into a video diffusion model via dedicated conditioning pathways, ensuring precise attribute allocation. Extensive experiments on both general and newly curated multi-subject benchmarks show that MultiBinder significantly outperforms existing methods in multi-subject identity fidelity.

</details>


### [107] [When Foundation Models Disagree: Representational Heterogeneity for Calibrated Uncertainty in Whole-Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26159)
*Yekai Shen,Yuqian Wang,Zishun Liao,Rongsheng Wang,Wenting Chen,S. Kevin Zhou,Qingsong Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CALIFUSE，将不确定性估计与多病理基础模型表征融合相结合，以表征异质性作为置信度线索，提升WSI分类的校准与误分类检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计器仅在单一特征空间内运作，忽略了基础模型表征选择本身引入的不确定性，导致预测置信度难以信任。

Method: CALIFUSE面向校准而非精度进行融合：冻结各基础模型编码器，为每个FM单独训练任务特定的nnMIL分类器；推理时融合滑片级决策共识与chunk级证据一致性（以Jensen-Shannon散度量化），无需额外训练融合模块。

Result: 在七个WSI分类任务及多种FM组合下，相较最强单编码器基线与朴素集成平均，CALIFUSE在校准、误分类检测和选择性预测上均更优，同时保持有竞争力的平衡准确率。

Conclusion: 将多基础模型的表征异质性显式用作置信度信号，是一种无需额外融合训练的、有效的WSI不确定性估计方案。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models supply strong representations for whole-slide image classification, yet the confidence attached to their predictions remains hard to trust. Prevailing uncertainty estimators act within a single feature space and thereby overlook the uncertainty that the choice of foundation-model representation itself induces. This work introduces CALIFUSE, a calibration-oriented framework that fuses several pathology foundation models for the specific purpose of uncertainty estimation. Departing from accuracy-driven fusion, CALIFUSE reads representational heterogeneity across encoders as an explicit confidence cue. Slide-level decision consensus is coupled with chunk-level evidence agreement quantified through Jensen-Shannon divergence, so that both prediction stability and fine-grained evidence consistency inform the estimate. Foundation-model encoders stay frozen. A task-specific nnMIL classifier is still trained for each FM; CALIFUSE then estimates confidence at inference without training an extra fusion module. Evaluated on seven WSI classification tasks under several foundation-model combinations, the framework sharpens calibration, misclassification detection, and selective prediction relative to both the strongest single-encoder baseline and naive ensemble averaging, all while preserving competitive balanced accuracy.

</details>


### [108] [ScaleWorld: High-Resolution World Generation through Shared Prediction and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26615)
*Yansong Qu,Wesker Zheng,Quanjian Song,Zhiyuan Zhang,Xiao Cao,Shengchuan Zhang,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScaleWorld：单一共享骨干因果视频世界模型，统一低分辨率预测与高分辨率精修，无需独立精修模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率视频世界模型能呈现精细场景结构与纹理，但直接生成高分辨率视频计算与显存开销巨大。

Method: 单一共享骨干对每个视频chunk先完成低分辨率预测，再以其为条件做一步高分辨率精修；流式生成中维护两套因果状态（长低分辨率历史用于预测、短高分辨率历史用于精修）；针对蒸馏中的任务干扰，提出任务非对称在线策略蒸馏(TA-OPD)，通过各自采样与历史策略下的局部速度匹配，将预测与精修行为从专家教师迁移至共享学生。

Result: 大量实验表明，ScaleWorld 实现高质量世界生成，并具备更优的计算与显存效率。

Conclusion: 以“自预测—自精修”的共享骨干范式替代独立精修模型，在保证高分辨率生成质量的同时显著降低流式生成成本。

Abstract: High-resolution generation enables video world models to render fine-grained scene structures and textures, but directly generating high-resolution video incurs substantial computational and memory costs. We present ScaleWorld, a causal video model that unifies low-resolution world prediction and high-resolution refinement within a single shared backbone. By refining its own predictions, ScaleWorld produces high-resolution world videos without requiring a separate refinement model. For each video chunk, the shared backbone first completes a low-resolution prediction and then performs one-step high-resolution refinement conditioned on that prediction. To support efficient streaming generation, we maintain separate causal states with a long low-resolution history for prediction and a short high-resolution history for refinement. To address task interference during distillation, we separate specialist optimization from capability consolidation and introduce task-asymmetric on-policy distillation (TA-OPD). This procedure transfers prediction and refinement behaviors from specialized teachers into a shared student through local velocity matching under their respective sampling and history policies. {Extensive experiments demonstrate that ScaleWorld achieves high-quality world generation with improved computational and memory efficiency.

</details>


### [109] [Deterministic Temporal Embedding for Calcium Imaging Trace Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26522)
*Qi Gao,Julius Beichert,Miriam Ratliff,Karl Rohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种确定性时序嵌入框架，将像素级荧光迹映射到低维表示，使时间相干的源形成空间连贯区域，从而在无细胞形状先验下可靠提取钙信号迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙成像中可靠提取钙依赖荧光迹需先识别反映同一底层细胞活动的像素；现有方法常依赖显式细胞形状先验或随机负采样，对不规则、空间相邻源不够稳健。

Method: 以模糊邻域图与谱初始化出发，优化类平衡交叉熵目标；采用精确稀疏吸引项，并用确定性粒子-网格（particle-mesh）分解处理稠密排斥相互作用并辅以稀疏边补偿，避免随机负采样；将所得隐表示空间解码为源支撑以提取迹，不施加显式形状先验。

Result: 在合成数据与在体双光子钙成像数据上，稳健恢复不规则及空间相邻源，嵌入优化高效且确定。

Conclusion: 该确定性时序嵌入框架可在无形状先验条件下实现可靠的源分离与钙迹提取，兼具稳健性与计算效率。

Abstract: Reliable extraction of calcium-dependent fluorescence traces in calcium imaging sequences requires identifying pixels whose fluorescence intensity reflects the same underlying cellular activity. We present a deterministic temporal embedding framework that maps pixel-wise fluorescence traces to a low-dimensional representation in which temporally coherent sources form spatially coherent regions. Starting from a fuzzy-neighborhood graph and spectral initialization, we optimize a class-balanced cross-entropy objective using exact sparse attraction and a deterministic particle-mesh decomposition of the dense repulsive interaction with sparse edge compensation, avoiding stochastic negative sampling. The resulting latent representation is spatially decoded into reliable source supports for trace extraction without imposing an explicit cellular shape prior. Experiments on synthetic and in vivo two-photon calcium imaging data demonstrate robust recovery of irregular and spatially touching sources together with efficient deterministic embedding optimization.

</details>


### [110] [HumanCOIN: A 12,500-Hour Dataset for Human Intent in Egocentric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26434)
*Shihan Wu,You Liu,Chunyu He,Minghui Qin,Longhan Jiao,Jinbang Guo,Shaoxuan Xie,Kaixi Tian,Wenjie Chen,Jingrui Pang,Yuanhang Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Miao Liu,Tengfei Pan,Yance Jiao,Yunyan Zhang,Yihang Li,Yongjian Shen,Li Liu,Kai Zhu,Chen Fang,Jianxing Chen,Sihan Liu,Xiaofeng Wang,Jie Zhao,Jing Li,Yueqi Wang,Yichen Tang,Wei Li,Zhexuan Yang,Yue Cao,Ji Zhao,Kai Wang,Dongsheng Jiang,Yongwei Wang,Wei Mu,Jiangmiao Pang,Maoqing Yao,Wenchao Ding,Zheng Zhu,Zhongpu Xia,Dalong Du,Jianwei Niu,Dahui Gao,Zhiwei Yu,Bo Lei,Huan Yang,Yonghua Lin,Tiejun Huang,Zhongyuan Wang,Guocai Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HumanCOIN，首个围绕"人类意图"构建的第一人称操作数据集，用三层代理信号（注视、表面肌电、头皮脑电）把决策到执行的意图通路完整记录下来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称操作数据集把"观测-动作对"当作原子单元，将动作的生成与执行压缩进策略网络权重，使动作背后的意图状态不可观测；而人类运动控制研究表明意图在观测与动作之间起中介作用，并沿"决策→效应"通路留下可观测痕迹（注视先于手部到达、接触前肌电负荷上升、运动指令前皮层活动先现）。

Method: 构建大规模第一人称操作数据集HumanCOIN，覆盖38类场景、12,500小时，设三层意图代理：L1注意力层（10,000 h，头带相机视频推断注视，暴露下一关注/抓取目标）；L2用力层（2,000 h，臂带表面EMG，反映执行完成前的负荷预期）；L3认知层（500 h，头皮EEG，早期读出任务决策与监控）。高层继承低层全部代理；另提供多视角RGB、深度图、点云、6-DoF头部与腕部位姿、步骤级动作与物体标注，L3受控设置下直接测量注视、手部位姿与手指运动学，并随数据集发布时钟与标定数据以便重新对齐任意读出率。

Result: 形成从视觉定向到皮层准备的三站点意图通路记录，使"观测-动作对"背后的过程可在数据集规模上被建模；数据规模与层级体系达成12,500小时、38类场景、三层代理的覆盖。

Conclusion: 将数据集设计范式从"观测-动作对"推进到"意图中介通路"，为意图感知的策略学习与人类运动控制建模提供可扩展的数据基础。

Abstract: First-person manipulation datasets treat the observation-action pair as the atomic unit and compress the generation and execution of an action into a policy network's weights, rendering the intent state behind the action unobservable. Human motor control research shows that intent mediates between observation and action and leaves observable traces along the intent pathway from decision to effect: gaze reaches the target before the hand, muscle load builds before contact, and cortical activity precedes the motor command. To record this pathway at scale, we present HumanCOIN, a first-person manipulation dataset built around human intent: 12,500 hours across 38 scene classes with three tiers of proxies. At the output end, the L1 attention tier (10,000 h) infers gaze from headband camera video, exposing the operator's next object of attention and reach before the hand moves. Midway, the L2 exertion tier (2,000 h) records surface EMG from armbands, making load expectations readable before execution is complete. At the origin, the L3 cognition tier (500 h) records scalp EEG, giving early readouts of task decisions and monitoring. Each tier inherits all proxies from the tiers below. The dataset further provides multi-view RGB images, depth maps and point clouds, 6-DoF head and wrist pose, and step-level action and object annotations. The controlled L3 setting additionally measures gaze, hand pose and finger kinematics directly. Clock and calibration data are released with the dataset so that any read rate can be re-aligned. The three tiers cover three stations, from visual orienting to cortical preparation, making the process behind visual-action pairs available for modeling at dataset scale. Project page: https://flagopen.github.io/HumanCOIN.

</details>


### [111] [Training Vision Models for LLM-Driven Weight-Space Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26408)
*Timo Ropinski,Sebastian Kranz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向视觉模型的LLM权重空间编程基准，并在Waterbirds、CIFAR-100、PACS和Entity-30上评测5个LLM对卷积网络与ViT的重新标注和纠错编辑，报告2600余次编辑过程，提出Edit-Ready Training (ERT)以提升编辑成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明LLM可在一定设定下直接修改神经网络权重（权重空间编程），但专门训练这些被编辑网络是否能促进该过程成功尚不清楚。

Method: 构建LLM驱动的视觉模型权重空间编程基准：5个LLM在Waterbirds、CIFAR-100、PACS、Entity-30上对卷积网络与视觉Transformer执行重标注与纠错编辑；每次编辑在留出图像上评分（成功率、副作用、精度损失）；对比标准训练、标签平滑、基于梯度的可编辑训练及提出的Edit-Ready Training（ERT，预演LLM所执行的直接闭式权重编辑）。

Result: 标准网络难以编辑时，为编辑做准备可显著提升LLM编辑成功率；ERT在全部基线中为GPT-6 Astra和Claude Opus 5.5取得最高平均得分。

Conclusion: 网络的可编辑性可通过训练设计得到改善，ERT为LLM权重空间编程提供了有效的准备方案；作者将公开LLM测试框架、任务、编辑轨迹与评分。

Abstract: It has been demonstrated that, in certain settings, large language models can perform weight-space programming to directly modify the weights of neural networks. However, it is unknown how dedicated training of these networks can contribute to the successes of weight-space programming. We present a benchmark of LLM-driven weight-space programming on vision models, in which five LLMs edit convolutional networks and vision transformers on relabeling and error-correction tasks on Waterbirds, CIFAR-100, PACS and Entity-30. We report more than 2,600 such edit processes, whereby each edit is scored on held-out images for success, side effects and accuracy loss. We compare standard training with label smoothing, gradient-based editable training, and our Edit-Ready Training (ERT), a novel method that rehearses the direct, closed-form weight edits LLMs perform. We show that preparing networks for editing raises the success of LLM edits substantially where standard networks are hard to edit. ERT further achieves the highest mean score for GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5 across all evaluated baselines. We will release our LLM testing framework alongside all tasks, editing trajectories and scores. Project page: https://viscom.uni-ulm.de/wsp

</details>


### [112] [GateTrace: Where Do Crowd Crossings Go Missing Under Occlusion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26388)
*Ao He,Qianchen Yu,Vivian W. H. Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GateTrace诊断工具，用于定位行人计数中被漏检的"穿越"事件，并在MOT20上量化低可见度条件下跟踪系统恢复率的显著下降及其丢失阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型活动中的行人计数只给出总数，掩盖了哪些穿越被漏检。体育场实测显示，清晰可见的穿越约有80%被记录，而严重遮挡的穿越记录不足三分之一。由于现场视频缺乏逐帧标注轨迹，作者转向MOT20（含行人轨迹与可见度标注）进行可复现的漏检诊断。

Method: 提出GateTrace：在固定规则下从标注中导出参考穿越事件；按身份、方向、时间和位置匹配系统上报结果；利用保存的检测框与计数决策追溯漏检来源。在共享检测器下比较ByteTrack、OC-SORT、BoT-SORT，并对MOT20调优的BASE输出进行后验分析；另做消融，去除BASE的后期校验环节以测量精度代价。

Result: 开发序列低可见度参考穿越的恢复率为38.9–46.7%，高可见度则为98.1–99.0%；该差距在另外两条序列的预设协议下重现。MOT20调优BASE的已发布输出在两组序列低可见度下恢复63–78%。在四个系统和两组序列中，47–79%的低可见度漏检源于：附近无与目标关联的输出，或保存轨迹中不存在可能的穿越。去除BASE后期校验后，开发序列上多数漏检仍未解决，且相对固定参考列表的精度从约96%降至77%。这些漏检也出现在两名人工标注者复核视频后仍确认的采样参考穿越中。

Conclusion: GateTrace能够定位穿越在哪一环节丢失，并量化修改后期校验规则带来的恢复率与精度代价；该工作将公开代码与逐穿越记录，为行人计数系统的漏检审计提供可复现框架。

Abstract: At large events, pedestrian tracking supports entrance and passageway counts, but totals hide which crossings were missed. In a stadium study, the system recorded about four in five clearly visible crossings but fewer than one in three mostly obscured crossings. The videos lacked trajectories annotated across frames, so we turned to MOT20, which provides pedestrian trajectories and visibility. We introduce GateTrace to locate missed crossings and assess changes to counting rules. It derives reference crossings from annotations under fixed rules, matches reports by person, direction, time and position, and traces misses through saved boxes and counting decisions. With a shared detector, ByteTrack, OC-SORT and BoT-SORT recover 38.9-46.7% of low-visibility reference crossings on development sequences, versus 98.1-99.0% at high visibility. The gap recurs on two further sequences under a protocol fixed in advance. Published outputs of MOT20-tuned BASE recover 63-78% at low visibility across both sequence groups. Losses stop at different stages across systems. Across four systems and both groups, 47-79% of low-visibility misses have no nearby output linked to the person or no possible crossing in the saved tracks. Removing later checks from BASE on development sequences leaves most misses unresolved and lowers precision against the fixed reference list from about 96% to 77%. Misses also remain among sampled reference crossings that two human annotators supported after checking the videos. GateTrace shows where crossings are lost and measures the recovery and precision costs of changing later checks. We will release the code and per-crossing records.

</details>


### [113] [CryoGaussian: Heterogeneous Cryo-EM Reconstruction Made Explicit with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26128)
*Ziwen Wang,Jiajun Fan,Chaoran Cheng,Ruihan Guo,Yanru Qu,Juexiao Zhou,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CryoGaussian，一种基于高斯泼溅（Gaussian splatting）的异质性冷冻电镜三维重建方法，通过可微光栅化器将高斯密度表示直接拟合到粒子图像，实现了对组分异质性与连续构象变化的SOTA重建精度，并能解析稀有瞬态结构与连续构象运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单颗粒冷冻电镜（cryo-EM）虽能从含噪粒子图像中解析大分子结构，但稀有、瞬态的组分状态以及细微、连续的构象变化难以被现有方法分辨，因此需要一种能同时刻画组分异质性与连续构象变异的重建框架。

Method: CryoGaussian 采用显式高斯密度表示，通过可微光栅化器渲染经对比度传递函数（CTF）调制的正交线积分，将高斯直接拟合到粒子图像。高斯以随机方式初始化，其数量由估计的分子密度空间支撑确定，最小尺寸以粒子图像的奈奎斯特频率为下界。为刻画组分与构象变异，方法学习粒子图像级与逐高斯潜在变量，共同调制每个高斯的基础位置、幅值与尺度；随后在推断出的潜变量条件下拟合高斯，重建每个结构状态。此外针对加性 cryo-EM 投影对光栅化器进行优化，训练加速 12.8–28.0 倍。

Result: 在 CryoBench 上，CryoGaussian 在组分异质性与构象异质性两方面均取得 state-of-the-art 重建精度。在实验数据上，它解析出 50S 核糖体亚基的一个稀有组分状态（class C4，仅占 1.3% 粒子），其中 rRNA 螺旋 68 在中心突起缺失的情况下就位；同时刻画了预催化剪接体中 SF3b 与解旋酶亚复合体的连续构象运动。

Conclusion: CryoGaussian 以高斯泼溅表示结合潜变量调制，为异质性 cryo-EM 重建提供了高精度、可解释的显式密度框架，既能捕捉稀有的瞬态组分状态，也能刻画连续的构象变化，并通过光栅化器优化显著提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Single-particle cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) determines macromolecular structures from noisy particle images. However, rare, transient compositional states and subtle, continuous conformational variation are difficult to resolve. We present CryoGaussian, a Gaussian splatting method for heterogeneous cryo-EM reconstruction that fits an explicit Gaussian density representation directly to the particle images through a differentiable rasterizer rendering orthographic line integrals modulated by the contrast transfer function. The Gaussians are initialized randomly, with their number derived from the estimated spatial support of the molecular density and their minimum size bounded by the Nyquist frequency of the particle images. To capture the compositional and conformational variation across particle images, we learn particle-image and per-Gaussian latents that jointly modulate the base position, amplitude, and scale of each Gaussian. Each structural state is then reconstructed by fitting the Gaussians conditioned on its inferred latent. On CryoBench, CryoGaussian achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction accuracy for both compositional and conformational heterogeneity. On experimental data, it resolves a rare compositional state of the 50S ribosomal subunit (class C4, 1.3% of particles) with rRNA helix 68 docked in the absence of the central protuberance, and continuous conformational motion of the SF3b and helicase subcomplexes of the pre-catalytic spliceosome. We further optimize the rasterizer for additive cryo-EM projection, reducing training time by 12.8-28.0$\times$.

</details>


### [114] [Less Than Meets the Eye: Action-Set Identifiability and Principled Abstention for 3D Physical Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26056)
*Reshad Ul Karim,Abrar Samin,Sammam Mahdi,Syeda Maliha Tabassum,Md Rashad Al Hasan Rony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Intervene3D框架，通过有界相机运动与最大最小可分离性来选择运动、区分镜面/显示屏/走廊等深度歧义假设，并在无法分离时弃答，配合充分性检验拒绝无假设可解释的观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一图像可能对应走廊、镜面反射或显示屏显示等多种物理成因，其真实表面深度不同，导致单张图像下深度估计存在本质歧义；需要一种能主动干预、利用有限相机运动区分假设并明确弃答的机制。

Method: 输入图像与初始几何估计，对每个假设与候选运动预测视图，以最大最小可分离性准则选择运动；仅在可用运动能分离仍有争议的假设时报告机制与首个物理表面，否则弃答；另设充分性检验拒绝任何假设都无法解释的观测；真实显示实验中用冻结深度网络，第二张图像仅提供光度证据而不输入深度网络。

Result: 匹配合成基准（精确路标）上：关闭弃答时对76.4%不可分辨场景给出自信答案，开启弃答后精确与噪声路标下均无自信答案；回答场景100%准确时，最大最小选择回答61.0%场景，求和选择仅43.1%；充分性检验将强曲率镜面场景错误答案从56.8%降至12.4%。真实显示实验中，单次执行运动使12个网络汇总中位表面误差降低31.5%；这些网络在33.4%的错觉基准图像上注释面板内误差大于外部，在36.9%的Mirror3D帧上把首个物理表面置于玻璃之后。

Conclusion: 有界相机运动结合可分离性选择与弃答可有效消解深度歧义并保证回答可靠性，同时揭示现有单目深度网络在镜面/显示等歧义场景中存在系统性深度错误。

Abstract: A corridor, its mirror reflection, and its image on a display can look identical, yet their physical surfaces lie at different depths. We test whether bounded camera motions distinguish these explanations. Intervene3D takes an image and an initial geometry estimate, predicts the view under each hypothesis and candidate motion, and selects a motion by maximin separability. It reports the mechanism and first physical surface only if the available motions separate the hypotheses still in contention. Otherwise it abstains. An adequacy test also rejects observations that no hypothesis explains. On a matched synthetic benchmark with exact landmarks, disabling abstention gives confident answers on 76.4% of unresolvable scenes. With abstention, none receives a confident answer under exact or noisy landmarks. At 100% accuracy on answered scenes, maximin selection answers 61.0% of scenes and summed selection 43.1%. The adequacy test lowers wrong answers on strongly curved mirror scenes from 56.8% to 12.4%. Real display experiments compare two hypotheses using one frozen depth-network estimate. The second image supplies photometric evidence and is never passed through the depth network. One executed motion reduces the pooled median surface error of twelve networks by 31.5%. These networks have larger error inside the annotated panel than outside on 33.4% of illusion-benchmark images. They place the first physical surface behind the glass on 36.9% of Mirror3D frames.

</details>


### [115] [Depth Pro 2: Sharp and Temporally Consistent Metric Monocular Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26603)
*Peiyun Hu,Marko Mihajlovic,Lyndon Duong,Lahav Lipson,Wei Dong,Amaël Delaunoy,Stephan R. Richter,Vladlen Koltun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Depth Pro 2 提出单一可扩展网络，同时处理图像与视频，在原生分辨率下预测稠密度量深度，兼顾度量精度、边界锐度与时间一致性，并发布覆盖多档运行时的模型族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度估计方法存在取舍：多帧方法偏向时间一致性，单帧方法偏向边界锐度，难以同时满足二者。

Method: 训练一个统一的、可扩展的网络，输入同时支持图像与视频，直接输出原生分辨率的稠密度量深度图，从而在单一框架内融合多帧的时间信息与单帧的高分辨率细节。

Result: 所生成深度图同时具备度量准确、边界锐利、时间一致三项特性，在上述各维度上均优于先前工作；并发布在度量精度、锐度、时间一致性与运行时上定义新帕累托前沿的模型族。

Conclusion: 统一的图像-视频联合训练网络可打破时间一致性与边界锐度之间的权衡，为稠密度量深度估计提供新的性能-效率前沿；代码与权重已公开。

Abstract: We present Depth Pro 2, an approach to estimating dense metric depth for images and video at full resolution. By analyzing prior methods for monocular depth estimation, we find that they commonly trade off temporal consistency and boundary sharpness: multi-frame methods favor consistency, single-frame methods favor sharpness. To overcome this trade-off, we train a single, scalable network that processes both images and video and predicts depth maps at native resolution. Depth Pro 2 produces depth maps that are metrically accurate, sharp, and temporally consistent all at once, outperforming prior work in all of these dimensions. We release a family of models that define a new Pareto frontier in terms of metric accuracy, sharpness, temporal consistency, and runtime. We release code and weights at https://apple-aiml-research.github.io/ml-depth-pro-2/

</details>


### [116] [Multi-institutional grading of Barrett's esophagus dysplasia without stain normalization using a pathology foundation model and multiple instance learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26562)
*Amirali Khosravi,Jaidip M. Jagtap,D. Chamil Codipilly,Catherine E. Hagen,Jason T. Lewis,Rickey E. Carter,Shahriar Faghani,Vani J. A. Konda,Fouad Otaki,Shajan Peter,Nicholas Shaheen,Sachin Wani,Prasad G. Iyer,Bradley J. Erickson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究基于基础模型的深度学习系统，利用四家机构969张H&E全切片图像，对Barrett食管异型增生进行三级分级并检测二分类异型增生，在独立测试集上取得0.96的宏平均AUC，且跨机构表现一致、无需染色归一化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barrett食管异型增生的组织学分级存在显著的观察者间变异性，尤其是低级别异型增生，给临床决策带来困难；既往系统依赖机构特定的染色与预处理流程，制约了跨中心部署。

Method: 回顾性多机构诊断准确性研究。使用四家机构共969张H&E染色全切片图像；先以专家标注训练图块级分类器定位诊断相关区域，再采用属性感知的多示例学习（MIL）进行切片级分类，分为NDBE、低级别异型增生、高级别异型增生三类。测试集为独立留出的130例。

Result: 测试集宏平均AUC为0.96（95%CI 0.93–0.98）。二分类异型增生检测灵敏度98%（80/82）、特异度81%（39/48），阴性似然比0.030，与专家共识的二次加权Kappa为0.83。无高级别异型增生被误判为NDBE。各机构间性能一致，且未使用染色归一化。

Conclusion: 染色鲁棒的基础模型表征可使系统跨机构一致部署，有望免除既往系统所依赖的站点特异性预处理，从而支持泛化性更强的Barrett食管异型增生辅助分级。但研究为回顾性设计，样本量与单一测试集规模有限，仍需前瞻性多中心验证。

Abstract: Barrett's esophagus dysplasia grading suffers substantial inter-observer variability, particularly for low-grade dysplasia, complicating clinical decision-making. We conducted a retrospective multi-institutional diagnostic accuracy study of a foundation model-based deep learning system for dysplasia grading, using 969 H\&E-stained whole slide images from four institutions. Patch-level classifiers trained on expert annotations identified diagnostically relevant regions, followed by attribute-aware multiple instance learning for slide-level classification as non-dysplastic Barrett's esophagus (NDBE), low-grade dysplasia, or high-grade dysplasia. On a held-out test set (n = 130), macro-averaged AUC was 0.96 (95\% CI 0.93--0.98). For binary dysplasia detection, sensitivity was 98\% (80/82) and specificity 81\% (39/48), with a negative likelihood ratio of 0.030 and quadratic-weighted \kap{} of 0.83 against expert consensus. No high-grade dysplasia case was misclassified as NDBE. Performance was consistent across institutions without stain normalization, indicating that stain-robust foundation-model representations may remove the site-specific preprocessing that has constrained prior systems to institution-specific deployment.

</details>


### [117] [Risk-Controlled Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26438)
*Zixuan Liu,Hashem Haghbayan,Zizhan Zheng,Siavash Khajavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出R2-VLA：一个风险控制的检索增强VLA框架，通过学习不确定性预测器并结合任务级风险控制校准阈值，在满足用户指定任务成功率的前提下最小化专家干预，实现可靠的测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强VLA虽能利用专家演示作为额外上下文进行免参数更新的测试时自适应，但即使检索到的演示与当前任务相关，VLA仍可能无法有效利用这些信息而导致任务失败。因此需要判断何时可自主执行、何时需专家介入。

Method: 1) 学习不确定性预测器，基于检索到的演示估计VLA预测动作的不确定性，据此决定自主行动或请求专家协助；2) 引入任务级风险控制流程，校准不确定性阈值以满足用户指定的任务失败风险要求；3) 持续将专家纠正写入检索记忆以提升后续自适应，同时保持既定的任务级风险保证。

Result: 在三个VLA骨干网络和四个LIBERO基准套件上的实验表明，R2-VLA将平均任务成功率提升9.2–11.2个百分点，并以更少的专家请求达到目标成功率。

Conclusion: 将检索演示的有用性与任务级风险控制相连接，可有效实现成功的机器人操作，在保证任务成功率要求的同时减少专家干预成本。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) models enable efficient test-time adaptation by leveraging expert demonstrations as additional context without updating model parameters. However, even when retrieved demonstrations are relevant to the current task, the VLA may fail to effectively utilize the retrieved information, ultimately leading to task failure. To address this challenge, we propose R2-VLA, a risk-controlled retrieval-augmented VLA framework that enables reliable test-time adaptation by satisfying a user-specified task success rate requirement with minimal expert assistance. Specifically, R2-VLA learns an uncertainty predictor that estimates the uncertainty of the VLA's predicted actions given the retrieved demonstrations, guiding when to act autonomously or request expert assistance. We further introduce a task-level risk-control procedure that calibrates the uncertainty threshold to satisfy a user-specified task-failure risk requirement. Moreover, R2-VLA continually incorporates expert corrections into its retrieval memory to improve future adaptation while preserving the prescribed task-level risk guarantee. Experiments across three VLA backbones and four LIBERO benchmark suites demonstrate that R2-VLA improves average task success by 9.2--11.2 percentage points, achieving the desired target with fewer expert requests. These results highlight the effectiveness of connecting the usefulness of retrieved demonstrations with task-level risk control to enable successful robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [118] [WeMM-Video: Pushing the Frontiers of Open-Source Omni-Modal Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26409)
*Changli Tang,Jie Yang,Tianyi Wang,Lei Li,Daoze Zhang,Zijie Xin,Zhan Liu,Li Li,Jinchang Xu,Fengyun Rao,Chao Zhang,Jing Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeMM-Video 是一系列中英双语全模态基础模型（2B/4B/9B/35B-A3B），以音视频联合感知与推理为核心，统一文本、图像、音频、视频，支持非思考与思考双模式，并在 120+ 基准上取得开源最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带音频的视频理解是多模态智能的核心难题；现有开源模型在全模态视频理解与推理上仍落后于闭源模型，缺乏兼顾通用能力与显式推理的统一方案。

Method: 构建 2B/4B/9B/35B-A3B 四档中英双语全模态模型；在文本/图像/音频/视频上统一建模，采用混合思考范式（instruct 非思考模式 + 逐步推理思考模式）；基于约 1.2 万亿 token 大规模多模态语料进行渐进式预训练，并通过多教师 OPD、抗重复 MrDPO、专家 RL 的多阶段后训练流水线优化。

Result: 在 120+ 基准上评估：全模态视频理解与推理表现领先，通用音频、无声视频、图像与文本任务保持高竞争力；旗舰 WeMM-Video-35B-A3B 刷新开源 SOTA，显著超过 Qwen3.5-Omni-Flash 与 Seed2.0-Mini-0428，并缩小与 Gemini-3.0-Flash 的差距；9B 版本超越更大的 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct。

Conclusion: WeMM-Video 以规模化数据、渐进预训练与多阶段后训练实现开源领先的全模态视频理解与推理能力，并公开模型家族及评估与复现流水线，推动开源社区研究。

Abstract: Video understanding, particularly with accompanying audio, remains a central challenge in multimodal intelligence. In this report, we introduce \textbf{WeMM-Video}, a family of Chinese-English bilingual omni-modal foundation models spanning 2B, 4B, 9B, and 35B-A3B variants. Centering on joint audio-visual perception and reasoning, WeMM-Video unifies text, image, audio, and video processing within a hybrid-thinking paradigm, seamlessly supporting both a non-thinking instruct mode for fast, direct responses and a thinking mode for explicit step-by-step reasoning. The models are trained on a large-scale multimodal corpus of approximately 1.2 trillion tokens through progressive pre-training and a multi-stage post-training pipeline incorporating multi-teacher OPD, anti-repetition MrDPO, and specialist RL. Evaluated across more than 120 benchmarks, WeMM-Video demonstrates superior performance in omni-modal video understanding and reasoning, while maintaining highly competitive capabilities across general audio, silent video, image, and text tasks. Specifically, in omni-modal video evaluations, our flagship WeMM-Video-35B-A3B establishes a new open-source state of the art, substantially outperforming proprietary models such as Qwen3.5-Omni-Flash and Seed2.0-Mini-0428 while narrowing the gap with the strong Gemini-3.0-Flash, and our compact WeMM-Video-9B surpasses the larger open-source Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct. We publicly release the WeMM-Video family along with our evaluation and reproduction pipeline to foster further research and development in the open-source community.

</details>


### [119] [Point Dynamics as Proxy for Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25946)
*Dong An,Tianyu Liu,Haoran Ding,Wen Yang,Anqing Duan,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出将机器人世界模型中的"动力学预测"与"视觉渲染"解耦，以3D点动力学作为二者之间的中介表示，从而提升物体运动与动作的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人世界模型虽能紧跟给定动作，但被操作物体常出现塌陷或运动与动作不一致。作者归因于条件信号不足：动作或机器人渲染只指明"机器人去哪"，未指明"场景如何响应"，导致单一视频模型需在渲染外观的同时隐式推断交互动力学。

Method: 将首帧提升为3D点，用学习到的点动力学模型模拟这些点对机器人运动的响应，再将模拟后的点渲染回与像素对齐的视频，供任意视频模型读取，实现动力学预测与视觉渲染的分离。

Result: 在DROID数据集上用两种不同架构的视频渲染器做受控实验，点动力学代理使物体运动误差分别降低8.6%和6.2%，且不损害图像质量；在真实双臂机器人上降低8.1%。视频随仿真变化：动力学越准确则物体运动越准确，编辑点可引导视频生成。

Conclusion: 动力学建模的进步可转化为更忠实的机器人世界模型，3D点动力学作为显式中介是有效且可迁移的方案。

Abstract: Robotic world models predict future video frames from the current observation and a sequence of robot actions. While recent models follow the given actions closely, manipulated objects often collapse or move inconsistently with the action. We attribute this to the conditioning: an action, or a rendering of the robot, specifies where the robot goes but not how the scene responds, leaving a single video model to infer the interaction dynamics implicitly while also rendering appearance. We therefore separate dynamics prediction from visual rendering, with 3D point dynamics as the proxy between them. Specifically, we lift the first frame into 3D points, simulate how they respond to the robot's motion with a learned point dynamics model, and render the simulated points back into pixel-aligned videos that any video model can read. In controlled experiments on the DROID dataset with two video renderers of different architectures, this point dynamics proxy reduces object motion error by 8.6% and 6.2% without degrading image quality, and by 8.1% on a real bimanual robot. Moreover, the video follows the simulation: more accurate dynamics yield more accurate object motion, and editing the points steers the video. This suggests that advances in dynamics modeling can translate into more faithful robotic world models.

</details>


### [120] [One-to-Many: Diverse and Bidirectional Image-to-Image Translation via Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26058)
*Sho Shimotsumagari,Seiichi Uchida,Shumpei Takezaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出O2MFlow：以潜变量为条件的Rectified Flow模型，在单一传输框架内同时实现一对多前向生成与反向翻译（target→source）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对图像翻译任务中，同一源图像往往存在多种合理的目​​标外观，但训练数据仅提供单一目标。现有方法难以在保证可逆性的同时生成多样输出。

Method: 将RF动态以"外观潜变量"为条件，使传输变成一族由潜变量索引的双射：固定潜变量时，映射可由时间反向积分求逆；采样潜变量则对同一输入产生多样输出。并将可复用外观变化形式化为Shared Latent Transport，用KL正则直接控制训练与生成间的源—潜变量分布差距。

Result: 在三个基准上，O2MFlow呈现随数据集变化的前向多样性—保真度权衡；在评估方法中取得最低的反向LPIPS-Dist，并在两个基准上取得最低的反向FID。

Conclusion: 以潜变量条件化的Rectified Flow可统一一对多生成与可逆反向翻译，KL正则对分布对齐具有关键控制作用，方法在反向翻译质量上具竞争力。

Abstract: Many paired image-to-image translation tasks admit multiple valid target appearances, while training provides only one target per source. We propose One-to-Many Flow (O2MFlow), a latent-conditioned Rectified Flow (RF) model for one-to-many generation and backward translation (target$\rightarrow$source) within a single transport framework. Conditioning the RF dynamics on an appearance latent turns transport into a family of latent-indexed bijections: a fixed latent gives a mapping that is inverted by time-reversed integration, and sampled latents produce diverse outputs for the same input. We formalize reusable appearance variations as Shared Latent Transport and show that Kullback--Leibler (KL) regularization directly controls the source--latent distribution gap between training and generation. Across three benchmarks, O2MFlow offers dataset-dependent forward diversity--fidelity trade-offs and the lowest backward LPIPS-Dist among evaluated methods, with the lowest backward FID on two benchmarks.

</details>


### [121] [Contactless Estimation of Respiratory Frequency Using 3D-CNN on Thermal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26367)
*Federica Gioia,Filippo Pura,Alberto Greco,Dario Piga,Arcangelo Merla,Marco Forgione*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于3D-CNN的深度学习方法，可直接从原始热成像视频估计呼吸率，无需复杂预处理与ROI跟踪，验证集R²约为0.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 呼吸率等生理参数监测对多种病理状态的诊断与管理至关重要。热成像作为非接触式替代方案具有潜力，但现有方法通常依赖大量预处理以及人工或半自动的感兴趣区域（ROI）跟踪，限制了实际应用可行性。

Method: 构建3D卷积神经网络（3D-CNN）直接作用于原始热视频数据；针对小样本问题，采用数据增强与来自合成数据集的迁移学习进行训练。

Result: 在预处理视频与原始热视频上，验证集R²均约为0.61，表明模型可直接从原始数据中获得与预处理输入相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法简化了工作流程，提升了热成像在远程医疗、汽车驾驶员监测等真实场景中的应用可行性；但R²约0.61的精度仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Monitoring physiological parameters such as respiratory rate ($f_R$) is essential for diagnosing and managing various pathological conditions. Thermal imaging offers a promising contactless alternative to traditional methods, which often rely on partially invasive sensors or obtrusive wearable systems. However, existing approaches for $f_R$ estimation from thermal signals typically require extensive pre-processing and manual or semi-automatic region-of-interest (ROI) tracking, limiting their practical applicability. This study proposes a deep learning-based method for estimating $f_R$ directly from thermal videos, eliminating the need for complex pre-processing and ROI tracking. A 3D Convolutional Neural Network (3D-CNN) is developed to operate on raw thermal video data. To address challenges related to small datasets, the model is trained using data augmentation and transfer learning from synthetic datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves a validation $R^2$ score of approximately 0.61 on both pre-processed and raw thermal videos. By simplifying the workflow, this method holds promise for enhancing the feasibility of thermal imaging in real-world applications, such as remote healthcare and driver monitoring in automotive applications.

</details>


### [122] [FirstCross: Convex Single-Photon 3D Reconstruction from Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26321)
*Chi-Wei Lee,Cheng-Han Chiang,Yi-Chun Chang,An-Kai Chen,Po-Chih Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FirstCross 提出一种无网络、凸优化的单光子激光雷达三维重建方法，避免逐场景非凸神经优化，将前向首次穿越的泊松似然分解为两个可解凸项，在体素网格上求解，实现远超神经方法的速度与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有领先神经方法（如 Transientangelo）需一小时以上的逐场景非凸优化，且精度随光子通量下降而退化，限制了少视角、少光子场景的实用性与低曝光采集。

Method: 针对实心物体，射线在给定信号/背景率下的泊松似然仅通过"首次穿越"依赖场景。将该似然分解为两项：第一项惩罚位于已探测光子之前的表面，并具有紧凸闭包——用运行最大值透射率替代 NeRF 的乘积透射率的体渲染；第二项处理无表面可解释的光子，在假设每个已探测表面点后物体足够厚的条件下给出凸上界。整体在体素网格上求解一段短序列凸问题，无需神经网络。

Result: 在 Transientangelo 自有真实数据协议下，FirstCross 在每个测试场景、视角数与通量水平上深度误差均更低；三视角且光子数比协议最低通量再少100倍时，平均物体深度误差仍低于 Transientangelo 使用全部光子的一半。基于其检测表面点，最佳经典融合的误差是其三视角误差的1.3–1.8倍；真实房间级采集用1%光子时，TransientNeRF 误差为其2.8–7.7倍。

Conclusion: FirstCross 使少视角单光子扫描仪可将曝光降至1%，并在单块 GPU 上约一分钟完成物体重建，兼具高精度与高效率。

Abstract: Single-photon lidar records the arrival time of each returning photon, enabling 3D reconstruction from few views and scarce photons. However, leading neural methods such as Transientangelo require over an hour of non-convex per-scene optimization, and their accuracy degrades as photon flux decreases. We propose FirstCross, a network-free approach that instead solves a short sequence of convex problems on a voxel grid. For a solid object, the Poisson likelihood of a ray with given signal and background rates depends on the scene solely through its first crossing, the point where the ray first enters the object. Decomposing this likelihood yields two tractable terms. The first penalizes surfaces in front of detected photons and admits a tight convex closure, namely volume rendering with a running-maximum transmittance in place of NeRF's product transmittance. The second accounts for photons that no surface explains and admits a convex upper bound once the object is assumed to be thick behind each detected surface point. Compared with Transientangelo under its own real-data protocol, FirstCross achieves lower depth error across every test scene, view count, and flux level. With three views and 100 times fewer photons than at the protocol's lowest flux, our mean object depth error stays under half of Transientangelo's with all photons. Given our detected surface points, the best classical fusion has 1.3 to 1.8 times our three-view error across flux levels. On real room-scale captures with 1% of their photons, TransientNeRF has 2.8 to 7.7 times our error. FirstCross lets few-view single-photon scanners cut exposure to 1% and reconstruct an object in about a minute on one GPU.

</details>


### [123] [C-LiTE: A Lightweight Foundation Model for Cellular feature learning in Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26316)
*Esha Sadia Nasir,Jiaqi Lv,Gozde N. Gunesli,Shan E Ahmed Raza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量细胞级病理基础模型 C-LiTE（2200 万参数），采用原分辨率 4×4 patch 嵌入处理 64×64 细胞裁剪，并用伪逆变换迁移 ViT-5-S 预训练权重；在 6000 万细胞裁剪数据上自监督预训练，冻结特征下 7 个数据集平均 macro-F1 达 0.670。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞形态是肿瘤分级、核分裂评估、免疫表型分析的基础，但多数病理基础模型在组织块级别训练，仅隐式建模细胞特征；已有细胞级模型或局限于特定细胞群，或依赖数亿参数的昂贵架构。此外，ViT 直接用于小尺寸细胞裁剪面临架构难题：上采样增加算力但不增加形态信息，还可能引入插值伪影。

Method: 1) 设计 2200 万参数轻量 ViT 式模型 C-LiTE，采用面向细胞输入的原分辨率 tokenisation：输入 64×64 px 细胞中心裁剪，用 4×4 patch embedding 生成 16×16 token 网格，无需上采样；2) 用基于伪逆的变换将预训练 ViT-5-S 的 patch embedding 权重适配到更细 patch 分辨率，实现权重初始化以迁移大规模视觉知识；3) 从 TCGA 构建 6000 万个细胞中心裁剪的预训练集，跨 19 种癌型、16 种组织类型均衡采样，进行自监督预训练。

Result: 在覆盖核分裂象识别、细胞核与细胞类型分类、免疫细胞表型分析的 7 个公开数据集上，冻结特征评估取得平均 macro-F1 = 0.670，接近 CytoFormer 的 0.682，而参数量约少 30 倍。

Conclusion: 原分辨率 tokenisation 与伪逆权重迁移可在极小参数规模下实现有竞争力的细胞级表征；所构建的 6000 万细胞裁剪数据集为目前最大公开的 H&E 细胞级自监督预训练集，为高效细胞级病理分析提供可行基线。

Abstract: Characterising individual cells is fundamental to computational pathology, as cellular morphology informs tasks such as tumour grading, mitotic assessment and immune profiling. However, most pathology foundation models are trained on tissue patches and capture cellular features only implicitly, while existing cell-level models are either restricted to specific cell populations or rely on computationally expensive architectures with hundreds of millions of parameters. Applying ViTs directly to small cellular crops also presents an architectural challenge: upsampling increases computational cost without adding morphological information and may introduce interpolation artefacts. To address these limitations, we introduce C-LiTE, a lightweight foundation model for cellular representation learning in H&E-stained histopathology, with only 22 million parameters. C-LiTE employs a native-resolution tokenisation strategy tailored to cellular inputs, processing 64 x 64 px cell-centred crops using a 4 x 4 patch embedding to produce a 16 x 16 token grid without image upsampling. To transfer visual knowledge from large-scale pre-training, we adapt the patch embedding weights of a pre-trained ViT-5-S using a pseudoinverse-based transformation, enabling weight initialisation at the finer patch resolution. We further assemble a pre-training dataset of 60 million cell-centred crops from TCGA, balanced across 19 cancer types and 16 tissue types. To our knowledge, this represents the largest public dataset for self-supervised pre-training on H&E images at the cellular level. We evaluate C-LiTE on seven public datasets spanning mitotic figure recognition, nucleus and cell-type classification, and immune cell phenotyping. Under frozen-feature evaluation, C-LiTE achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.670, approaching CytoFormer (0.682) with approximately 30x fewer parameters.

</details>


### [124] [A version of the Cloude/van Zyl decomposition for full/quad pol synthetic aperture radar data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26195)
*Allan Aasbjerg Nielsen,Paul Jacques Connetable,Henning Skriver,Knut Conradsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出适用于全/四极化数据的Cloude/van Zyl分解新版本，并在Vejers X波段F-SAR数据上与原版对比，新版本给出更低熵、主散射机制更聚焦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原Cloude/van Zyl分解仅面向反射对称极化SAR数据，需将其扩展到全/四极化数据情形，并厘清其散射机制解释方式。

Method: 基于Cloude/van Zyl分解构造全/四极化版本，并与反射对称版本一同在丹麦Vejers的X波段F-SAR数据上验证；借助Cloude/Pottier的ᾱ角辅助解释散射机制。

Result: 两版本差异主要出现在极亮的人造目标处；新版本熵更低，对更显著的主散射机制具有更好聚焦。ᾱ角便于解释新版本的散射机制。

Conclusion: 全/四极化版Cloude/van Zyl分解可行，可在强人造目标上获得更低熵和更清晰的主散射机制刻画，ᾱ角为其机制解释提供支撑。

Abstract: Based on the Cloude/van Zyl decomposition of reflection symmetric polarimetric SAR data we present a version for full/quad polarisation data. We demonstrate both versions on X-band F-SAR data over Vejers, Denmark. Differences between the original and our version of the decomposition occur especially for very bright man-made targets. These differences come with lower entropy for our version giving an improved focus on a more pronounced dominant scattering mechanism for these objects. Interpretation of the scattering mechanism related to the full/quad pol version of the decomposition is facilitated by means of the Cloude/Pottier $\barα$ angle.

</details>


### [125] [Backbone Matters: Benchmarking Encoder-Decoder Networks for Semantic Segmentation of Driving Scenes on BDD100K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26191)
*Ehsan Rassekh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在BDD100K上用统一协议系统比较4种解码器（U-Net、FPN、LinkNet、PSPNet）与6个家族13个骨干网络组合的52个分割模型，发现骨干选择与解码器强耦合，FPN+EfficientNet-B4取得最佳62.6% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割是自动驾驶场景理解的核心，现有网络多为编码器-解码器结构，但骨干网络（backbone）的选择往往缺乏系统性对比依据，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在BDD100K驾驶数据集上开展受控消融式对比：4个解码器 × 13个骨干（来自ResNet、VGG、DenseNet、Inception、MobileNet、EfficientNet六个家族），共52个模型，全部采用同一训练协议，以mIoU、精度和损失评价。

Result: 最优模型为FPN解码器+EfficientNet-B4，达到62.6% mIoU、89.8%精度、损失0.092；52个模型中仅2个（均为FPN+EfficientNet）超过BDD100K基准报告的56.9% mIoU。骨干效应高度依赖解码器：同一EfficientNet-B4在PSPNET下仅42.6% mIoU、在FPN下为62.6%；最优与最差骨干的差距从12.0个百分点（U-Net）到42.5个百分点（FPN）不等。

Conclusion: 骨干网络与解码器之间存在显著交互作用，不能脱离解码器单独评价骨干优劣；该研究明确了结论的适用范围并讨论了局限性，为自动驾驶分割模型选型提供了实证参考。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation gives an autonomous vehicle a dense, pixel-level description of its surroundings and is a core part of scene understanding. Most segmentation networks consist of an encoder (the backbone) and a decoder, yet the backbone is often chosen without a systematic comparison. This work studies this choice on the BDD100K driving dataset. Four widely used decoders (U-Net, FPN, LinkNet and PSPNet) are combined with 13 backbones from six families (ResNet, VGG, DenseNet, Inception, MobileNet and EfficientNet), and all 52 models are trained with one common protocol. The best model, an FPN decoder with an EfficientNet-B4 backbone, reaches 62.6% mean intersection over union (mIoU), 89.8% accuracy and a loss of 0.092. Only two of the 52 models, both FPN with EfficientNet, exceed the 56.9% mIoU reported with the BDD100K benchmark. The effect of the backbone depends strongly on the decoder: the same EfficientNet-B4 gives 42.6% mIoU with PSPNet but 62.6% with FPN, and the gap between the best and the worst backbone ranges from 12.0 points (U-Net) to 42.5 points (FPN). The results and the scope of this study are discussed.

</details>


### [126] [RegionAgent: Two-Stage Preference Optimization for Regional Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26179)
*Weiyu Zhou,Jiacong Tang,Tao Hu,Axi Niu,Jinqiu Sun,Peng Wu,Wei Dong,Qingsen Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RegionAgent框架，通过两阶段偏好学习训练VLM，在每次迭代为不同退化区域生成定制化复原动作，实现非均匀复合退化的自适应图像复原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法采用全局统一操作，忽略区域差异，导致局部复原不完整甚至破坏图像内容；此外，同一区域可能需要连续多次复原动作，前序动作会改变残余退化特征或引入伪影，干扰后续复原。

Method: 以VLM为决策器，逐迭代生成区域特定的复原动作。第一阶段用步级DPO，基于即时复原反馈构建偏好，仅监督动作对中的差异部分；第二阶段用轨迹级DPO，基于完整轨迹评分构建偏好，仅监督轨迹对中唯一不同的动作。并构建含10K动作对与20K轨迹对的区域复原偏好数据集用于两阶段训练。

Result: 在多个数据集上，混合退化与单一退化场景均表现强复原性能；消融实验验证两阶段偏好学习对复原质量的有效性。

Conclusion: 区域自适应、基于偏好学习的VLM决策框架能有效应对非均匀复合退化，步级与轨迹级偏好学习的组合兼顾了即时效果与长期轨迹收益。

Abstract: Images under non-uniform coupled degradations require diverse restoration capabilities for different degraded regions. Existing methods employ globally uniform operations, potentially overlooking regional differences and causing incomplete restoration or even damage to image content. Moreover, a degraded region may require successive restoration actions, and earlier actions may alter residual degradation characteristics or introduce artifacts, potentially hindering subsequent restoration. To address these challenges, we propose RegionAgent, an adaptive restoration framework that trains a Vision-Language Model (VLM) through two-stage preference learning to generate a region-specific restoration action at each iteration. Specifically, we first train the VLM to select an action suited to the current region through step-level Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), using preferences derived from immediate restoration feedback to supervise only the differing component in each action pair. To account for the effects of current actions on subsequent restoration, we then apply trajectory-level DPO, using preferences derived from complete trajectory scores to supervise the sole differing action in each trajectory pair. Furthermore, as conventional image-pair datasets do not directly provide preferences over regional restoration decisions, we construct a regional restoration preference dataset comprising 10K action pairs and 20K trajectory pairs for two-stage VLM training. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate strong restoration performance under both mixed and single degradations. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of two-stage preference learning in improving restoration quality.

</details>


### [127] [StreamEgo: Streaming Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26165)
*Tianyi Xu,Boyang Lyu,Yunkai Tang,Kairan Ding,Jiahang Li,Weiyu Guo,Yandong Guo,Shuchen Weng,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamEgo：面向无界、可泛化外中心到内中心（Exo2Ego）视频生成的实时流式框架，单张H100上达24.3 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Exo2Ego视频生成可将第三人称录像转为第一人称视角，服务AR/VR与具身机器人示教合成；扩展到流式场景可支持实时应用。现有方法存在操作区域质量退化、过拟合、仅限定长片段且延迟过高的问题。

Method: 提出流式双分支先验，为外观转换与骨骼运动学提供像素对齐的引导以刻画复杂操作动态；引入区域感知损失课程，提升泛化性并稳定多目标扩散训练；将模型蒸馏为分块自回归模型，并辅以区域感知破坏机制与首块课程。

Result: 实现无界、可泛化的高质量实时流式Exo2Ego生成，单张H100 GPU上达24.3 FPS。

Conclusion: StreamEgo通过流式双分支先验、区域感知损失课程与分块自回归蒸馏，缓解操作区域退化、过拟合与定长高延迟问题，使Exo2Ego生成可实时流式部署。

Abstract: Exocentric-to-egocentric (Exo2Ego) video generation transforms third-person recordings into first-person viewpoints, unlocking immersive AR/VR experiences and scalable demonstration synthesis for embodied robotics. Extending it to a streaming setting is expected to further enable live applications. However, existing methods suffer from degraded manipulation regions, overfitting and confinement to fixed-length clips with prohibitive latency. To address these limitations, we present StreamEgo, a real-time framework for unbounded, generalizable Exo2Ego video generation. To resolve intricate manipulation dynamics while supporting the streaming pipeline, a streaming dual-branch prior provides pixel-aligned guidance for translating appearance and skeletal kinematics. We also introduce a region-aware loss curriculum to improve generalization and stabilize multi-objective diffusion training. Furthermore, we distill the model into a chunk-wise autoregressive model, augmented with a region-aware corruption mechanism and a first-chunk curriculum, enabling high-quality, generalizable real-time streaming Exo2Ego generation at 24.3 FPS on a single H100 GPU.

</details>


### [128] [FlexVSR: Continuous-Scale Super-Resolution for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26178)
*Ji Wang,Haonan An,Jiasheng Zhang,Renlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexVSR：一种解耦框架，仅需少数尺度监督即可实现连续尺度的实时流式生成式视频超分；核心是轻量潜在适配器 ReLSA，在不更新 DiT 的前提下完成尺度感知适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时流式视频生成需适配多种源分辨率、目标显示与算力预算，但固定尺度的生成式 VSR 难以连续扩展：从较大固定尺度下采样存在冗余开销，重新优化 DiT 代价高且破坏共享生成先验。

Method: 提出 FlexVSR 解耦框架。核心洞察是将固定尺度潜在表征朴素重采样到新目标网格会引入尺度相关的输入失配，可通过参数高效、尺度感知的潜在接口适配来缓解。具体设计 ReLSA（Refined Latent Scale Adaptation）：在潜在表征进入 DiT 前构建并精炼目标网格潜在，冻结共享 VSR 先验；仅 0.274M 可训练参数，适配成本 7.3 H100 GPU 小时，不引入额外时序依赖，保持原生因果流式协议。

Result: 实验显示重建与时序质量具竞争力或更优；延迟低于多数原生尺度流式基线与离线方法；对未见连续尺度具备有效泛化能力。

Conclusion: FlexVSR 以轻量尺度感知适配实现连续尺度流式生成式 VSR，弥补了实时流式场景中连续尺度缺失的空白，兼顾效率、质量与泛化。

Abstract: Real-time streaming video generation has gained rapid momentum in live content creation and interactive media. Deploying such systems in practice requires flexible scaling factors to accommodate diverse source resolutions, target displays, and computational budgets. However, extending fixed-scale generative video super-resolution (VSR) to varying target scales poses two challenges: downsampling from a larger fixed scale incurs redundant cost, while re-optimizing the diffusion transformer (DiT) is costly and alters the shared generative prior. To address both, we propose FlexVSR, a decoupled framework for continuous-scale streaming generative VSR with supervision at only a few scales. Our key insight is that naively resampling fixed-scale latents onto a new target grid induces scale-dependent input mismatch, which can be effectively addressed through parameter-efficient, scale-aware adaptation of the latent interface without updating the DiT. To this end, FlexVSR introduces ReLSA (Refined Latent Scale Adaptation), a lightweight latent adapter that constructs and refines target-grid latents before they enter the DiT, enabling a shared frozen VSR prior to generalize across continuous scales with only 0.274M trainable parameters and an adaptation cost of 7.3 H100 GPU-hours, while introducing no additional temporal dependencies and preserving the native causal streaming protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive or superior reconstruction and temporal quality, lower latency than most native-scale streaming baselines and offline methods, and effective generalization to unseen continuous scales, filling the continuous-scale gap in real-time streaming generative VSR.

</details>


### [129] [Streaming-AAA: Streaming AAA Game Video Generation at High Resolution, High Frame Rate, and Low Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26040)
*Zhihao Hu,Jianqi Ma,Jun Zhang,Jiayang Bai,Haifeng Shi,Yanxiang Zhuang,Zhongqian Sun,Weidong Zhang,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Streaming-AAA，一个可控无限生成 AAA 级游戏视频的新范式，在单张 NVIDIA A800 上以 1080p、43 FPS、198 ms 端到端交互延迟生成，并支持长时间稳定游玩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控流式视频生成方法难以同时实现高视觉保真度、高帧率与低交互延迟，导致响应性差、用户体验退化，距离实用化游戏模拟仍有较大差距。

Method: 三项关键设计：(1) 重新设计 VAE 架构以实现高效高分辨率解码，支持 1080p 实时合成；(2) 采用 diffusion forcing 预训练扩散模型并显著延长时序训练视野，以捕获长期依赖并维持持久记忆；(3) 进行后训练蒸馏与记忆压缩，在保持视觉质量与时序一致性的同时提升推理效率。

Result: 在单张 NVIDIA A800 上实现 1080p、43 FPS、198 ms 端到端延迟，并支持长时程生成且无严重时序退化或崩溃；相比已有可控视频生成方法，在视觉保真度、帧率、延迟和长期稳定性上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: Streaming-AAA 显著提升了交互式 AAA 游戏视频生成的可玩性，为可控流式视频生成走向实用化游戏模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Controllable streaming video generation offers a promising route toward interactive game simulation, yet existing methods remain far from practical gameplay. They often fail to jointly achieve high visual fidelity, high frame rates, and low interaction latency, resulting in poor responsiveness and degraded user experience. We present Streaming-AAA, a new paradigm for controllable infinite generation of AAA-quality game videos. Our system generates 1080p gameplay videos at 43 FPS with 198 ms end-to-end latency interaction on merely NVIDIA A800 GPUs, while supporting long-horizon play without severe temporal degradation or collapse. Streaming-AAA is built upon three key designs. First, we redesign the VAE architecture for efficient high-resolution decoding, enabling real-time synthesis at 1080p. Second, we pretrain the diffusion model using diffusion forcing and significantly extend the temporal training horizon, allowing the model to capture long-term dependencies and maintain persistent memory. Third, we perform post-training distillation and memory compression to improve inference efficiency while retaining visual quality and temporal consistency. Experiments show that Streaming-AAA substantially improves visual fidelity, frame rate, latency, and long-term stability over prior controllable video generation approaches, enabling a significantly more playable experience for interactive AAA game video generation.

</details>


### [130] [TwinD: Generalizing Deepfake Detection from Blending to Entire Face Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25935)
*Andrii Yermakov,Jan Cech,Mario Fritz,Jiri Matas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TwinD 通过在 FaceForensics++ 假脸与两类"孪生"样本（重建孪生、斑块孪生）上训练，为换脸拼接伪迹与整脸合成伪迹提供互补取证线索，在混合与整脸合成（EFS）基准上均达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部深度伪造检测器多针对"混合型造假"（生成人脸粘贴回真实帧）训练，而新一代生成器可合成整张人脸、不留混合痕迹；在19个数据集基准上，最优既有检测器在10个混合数据集上平均 AUROC 达96.2%，但在5个整脸合成数据集上均不超过78.1%，AIGC 检测器也无法弥补该差距。

Method: 提出 TwinD：训练数据为 FaceForensics++ 假脸加两类由真实帧构造的孪生样本。①重建孪生：真实帧经自编码器重建，并将真实像素随机回填至某一区域，用于捕捉解码器的神经痕迹；②斑块孪生：将真实帧的篡改副本与原帧混合，用于捕捉混合伪迹，无需生成器、也无需人脸形状掩码。两者构成互补的取证线索以提升对未见操纵的泛化。

Result: TwinD 平均 AUROC 达96.3%，较此前最优方法提升8.9个百分点，其中混合数据集98.1%、EFS 数据集95.6%；仅使用孪生样本、不借助深度伪造数据集训练时，单模型在三个随机种子下平均仍达94.3%。

Conclusion: 以自监督式"孪生"构造替代对特定伪迹的依赖，可同时覆盖混合与整脸合成两类伪造，显著提升跨数据集泛化能力；代码、训练模型与训练数据已开源以便复现。

Abstract: Facial deepfake detectors are usually trained on blending fakes, where a generated face is pasted back into a real frame. Recent generators synthesize the entire face and leave no traces of blending. On a benchmark of 19 datasets released from 2019 to 2026, the best prior facial deepfake detector reaches a mean AUROC of 96.2% on ten blending datasets, but none exceeds 78.1% on five datasets of entire face synthesis (EFS). AI-generated content (AIGC) detectors do not close this gap. We propose TwinD, a detector trained on FaceForensics++ fakes and two kinds of twins: fakes made from a real frame. A reconstruction twin is a real frame reconstructed by an autoencoder, with the real pixels put back in a random region. It targets the neural traces of a decoder. A blob twin blends an altered copy of a real frame with the frame itself, targeting blending artifacts. It needs no generator and no face-shaped mask. Together, these twins provide two complementary forensic cues that generalize to unseen manipulations. TwinDreaches a mean AUROC of 96.3%, 8.9 points above the prior state-of-the-art, with 98.1% on the blending datasets and 95.6% on the EFS datasets. Trained on twins alone, without a deepfake dataset, single models still reach 94.3% on average over three seeds. For reproducibility, the code, trained model, and training data are at: https://github.com/yermandy/TwinD

</details>


### [131] [EgoWitness: Benchmarking Instance-Level Spatial Memory and Reasoning in Egocentric Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25849)
*Zhengru Fang,Zehao Liu,Mingyu Tsoi,Yaoyu Zhu,Junlong Li,Fengmao Lv,Sijie Cheng,Yuanliang Sun,Fang Yang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EgoWitness 基准（约324小时第一视角长视频、8,453道人工校验问答，测试实例级空间记忆），并给出 EgoClue 空间记忆智能体，将4B控制器准确率从23.64%提升至72.65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频基准聚焦事件、物体类别或相对位置，多模态模型在实例级空间记忆（"何时何地见过某物体、谁见过"）上几乎未被评测，因此需要专门的基准与更强的解法。

Method: 构建 EgoWitness：覆盖周级与短时程第一视角录制，7类能力、超100万个经审核掩码（34个注册地标、21类可移动物体），通过房间时间线建立关联；并提出 EgoClue 智能体，流程为检索候选区间→验证实例身份→用五个工具计算时空关系，采用 SFT + GRPO 两阶段训练4B控制器。

Result: 12个直接推理VLM中，开源权重模型最高仅46.97%，在"谁最后看到该物体"上均不超过35%；EgoClue 在1,320道留出问题上把4B控制器从23.64%提升至72.65%；证据链合成在周级问题上达74.85%，在训练未见环境中达80.56%。

Conclusion: 实例级空间记忆是当前多模态模型的显著短板，而"检索—身份验证—工具化时空推理"的智能体范式配合两阶段强化学习可大幅弥补该差距并具备跨环境泛化能力。

Abstract: Recalling where and when an object was last seen and who saw it requires recognizing it across days and people. Existing long-video benchmarks focus on events, object categories, or relative locations, leaving instance-level spatial memory largely untested in multimodal models. To address this gap, we present EgoWitness, a benchmark for instance-level spatial memory and reasoning in egocentric video. Its 8,453 human-verified questions span approximately 324 hours of week-long and short-horizon recordings and test seven abilities, from recognizing an object or room to recalling who saw it and reasoning about room visits. Over one million reviewed masks of 34 registered landmarks and 21 movable-object labels are linked through room timelines. Among twelve direct vision-language models (VLMs), open-weight models reach at most 46.97% accuracy, and none exceeds 35% on identifying who saw an object last. We therefore propose EgoClue, a spatial-memory agent that retrieves candidate intervals, verifies instance identity, and computes spatial and temporal relations with five tools. Two-stage training with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) raises its 4B controller from 23.64% to 72.65% accuracy on 1,320 held-out questions. Evidence-trace synthesis matches the best direct VLM at 74.85% on week-long questions and achieves 80.56% in environments excluded from training.

</details>


### [132] [Online Temporal Rotations for 2 Bit KV-Cache Quantization in Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26334)
*Ryan Lucas,Petros Prastakos,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRQ，对KV缓存中时间相邻帧做自适应正交旋转后再量化，轻量参数化(2比特平面旋转树+固定Hadamard)，在线拟合且无需梯度，2比特下VBench指标超越QVG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer在去噪步骤间复用KV缓存，长视频生成时成为瓶颈；作者发现时间上相距较远的缓存向量量化范围大、误差高，而时间相邻token的KV缓存存在逐通道的持续模式，且该模式随视频而异，因此需要针对每个视频自适应设计的旋转。

Method: TRQ：在量化前对缓存中时间相邻的帧进行旋转。对每个时间相邻缓存向量块，拟合一个正交变换，使该块逐通道均值映射为近似跨通道恒定的向量（mean-flattening目标）。旋转参数化为2×2平面旋转树（每个仅存2比特），后接所有块共享的固定Hadamard变换；无梯度、不修改模型权重，并证明简单贪心拟合对该目标全局最优；推理时对新视频在线拟合，并发布融合Triton内核。

Result: 2比特精度、压缩率匹配条件下，TRQ将VBench主体一致性从QVG的90.38提升至92.02（LongCat-Video-13B），从86.33提升至87.61（HY-WorldPlay-8B），其他指标亦有改善。

Conclusion: 利用时间相邻KV缓存的逐通道持续模式，视频自适应旋转量化可显著降低长视频生成中KV缓存的量化误差，且以极轻量的旋转参数和高效在线拟合实现，优于现有SOTA方法。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers reuse key--value (KV) caches across denoising steps, which quickly becomes a bottleneck for long video generation. We find that quantizing temporally distant cache vectors leads to a large range and high quantization error, but that nearby video tokens in the KV-cache share a persistent pattern per channel. These patterns also vary across videos, motivating rotations that are adapted to each video's unique cache. We propose Temporal Rotation Quantization (TRQ), a method that rotates frames in the cache that are adjacent in time before quantization. For each block of temporally adjacent cache vectors, TRQ fits an orthogonal transform that maps the block's per-channel means to a vector that is close to a constant across channels. To keep the quantization format lightweight, we parameterize this rotation as a tree of $2 \times 2$ planar rotations, each stored with only two bits, followed by a fixed Hadamard transform shared across all blocks. The rotations are fit without gradients or changes to model weights, and we prove that a simple greedy fit is globally optimal for our 'mean-flattening' objective. The transforms are fit online for each new video, and we release fused Triton kernels to make this process efficient at inference. At 2-bit precision and matched compression, TRQ improves VBench subject consistency over the state-of-the-art method QVG from 90.38 to 92.02 on LongCat-Video-13B and from 86.33 to 87.61 on HY-WorldPlay-8B, as well as improving other metrics.

</details>


### [133] [Tracking Objects Through Assembly and Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26269)
*Cheryl Lam,Yihong Sun,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无提示（prompt-free）的物体变换追踪新范式，并给出零样本系统 Atlas 与 OmniST 基准，在装配与分解事件上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追踪物体在变换过程中的身份与轨迹是理解真实世界动态的关键。现有工作虽已将传统物体追踪扩展到状态变化检测，但需给定初始物体提示，且只能检测该物体的分解（decomposition）事件，无法处理以多个物体为起点的装配（assembly）事件。

Method: 核心洞见是“装配即分解的逆过程”。据此提出 Atlas：无需任何提示、零样本运行，构建前向与反向两类时空划分（spatiotemporal partitions），并对二者联合推理，从而恢复物体轨迹及其变换关系。同时构建 OmniST 基准，对物体及两类变换事件提供密集标注。

Result: 在 OmniST 上，Atlas 恢复的状态变化数量是先前方法的两倍以上；在仅评估分解事件的既有基准上仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 将追踪任务重构为无提示、双向（分解+装配）的时空划分联合推理问题，可统一处理两类变换事件，验证了“装配为分解之逆”这一洞见的有效性。

Abstract: Tracking objects through transformations is essential for understanding real-world dynamics. Recent work has extended traditional object tracking to detect state changes. However, existing methods require an initial object prompt and detect only its decompositions, missing assembly events, which begin with multiple objects rather than one. We therefore introduce a prompt-free formulation of this task, where the goal is to recover all object tracks and detect both decomposition and assembly events given only a video. Our key insight is that assembly is decomposition in reverse. Building on this insight, we present Atlas, a zero-shot system that constructs and jointly reasons over forward and reverse spatiotemporal partitions to recover object tracks and their transformations. We further introduce OmniST, a benchmark with dense annotations of objects and both transformation types. On OmniST, Atlas recovers more than twice as many state changes as prior methods while remaining competitive on existing benchmarks that only evaluate decomposition.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Covers and Titles: Evidence-First Ranking for Robust Cold-Start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26249)
*Fuyi Wang,Yuxin Cao,Yifan Liao,Renyang Liu,Lin Li,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出可实现的封面包装攻击REAP（经H.264转码、缩放、裁剪、JPEG模拟平台信道），使冷启动视频排名百分位提升32点；并观察到仅看视频主体的塔对冷启动排序最优，据此提出证据优先排序EFR，通过支撑门控残差（界γ）与排名百分位融合实现可证明防御，攻击增益降81%，冷启动Recall@100从0.1133升至0.170，合并NDCG@10下降不超过4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对内容感知推荐系统的攻击只扰动像素或特征且忽略平台重编码，防御则针对范数有界扰动的训练，既不匹配真实编辑也无法保持推荐质量；因此需要一个真实可实现的攻击来揭示冷启动包装编辑的风险，并设计与之匹配的防御。

Method: (1) REAP：构建模拟平台信道（H.264转码、重缩放、裁剪、JPEG）的包装编辑攻击；(2) 实证发现冷启动排序无需包装信息，仅看视频主体的塔表现最佳；(3) EFR：以视频主体为主评分冷视频，包装仅通过受γ约束、由支撑度门控的残差进入，并用排名百分位融合冷/热排名，提供对任意包装编辑的精确证书。

Result: REAP使冷视频在无关受众中的排名百分位提升32.0点；EFR在MicroLens-100K上将攻击增益削减81%，冷启动Recall@100由0.1133提升至0.170，同时合并NDCG@10与无防御系统相差在4%以内。

Conclusion: 以内容包装为核心的冷启动推荐易受可实现编辑攻击，而证据优先的排序设计可在保持推荐质量的同时提供形式化鲁棒性保证，实现攻防一致、可验证的冷启动推荐。

Abstract: Short-video platforms rank new uploads before anyone has watched them, using content towers that read a video's cover, title and frames. Existing attacks on content-aware recommenders perturb pixels or features and ignore platform re-encoding, and existing defenses train against norm-bounded perturbations that neither match real edits nor preserve recommendation quality. We show with REAP, a realizable attack through {a simulated platform channel (H.264 transcoding, rescaling, cropping and JPEG)}, that packaging edits raise a cold video by 32.0 rank-percentile points in unrelated audiences. We then observe that cold-start ranking does not need the packaging at all: a tower that sees only the video body ranks cold items best. Building on this, we propose Evidence-First Ranking (EFR), which scores cold videos from their body, admits packaging only through a support-gated residual bounded by $γ$, and merges cold and warm rankings by rank percentile, yielding an exact certificate against any packaging edit. On MicroLens-100K, EFR cuts the attack gain by 81% and raises cold-start Recall@100 from 0.1133 to 0.170, while keeping the merged NDCG@10 within 4% of the undefended system.

</details>


### [135] [Attention Drift Regularization with Adaptive Initialization for Domain-Robust Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26234)
*Shivangi Rai,Vinod K Kurmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DGA-CAFC框架，针对无源域自适应（SFDA）中的注意力漂移与域差无感初始化两大失效模式，分别以反事实注意力特征一致性（CAFC）与域差感知源模型选择（DGASS）加以解决，并在SFDA与域泛化（DG）基准上一致优于近期基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFDA需在无源数据下将预训练源模型适配到无标签目标域，但存在两个关键失效模式：(1) 注意力漂移——分布偏移下源域学到的注意力图偏向虚假/背景特征，破坏特征表示稳定性；(2) 域差无感初始化——对所有目标域统一使用同一固定源模型，忽视域差严重程度，在大偏移下过早过拟合于噪声伪标签。

Method: DGA-CAFC由两模块构成。CAFC（反事实注意力特征一致性）：最小化“学习注意力下的表示”与“反事实启发的归一化扰动下的表示”之间的差异，以提升特征稳定性，促使学到不依赖虚假注意力漂移的域鲁棒特征。DGASS（域差感知源模型选择）：以目标预测熵估计域差严重度，并选择置信度水平与该估计域差最匹配的源模型变体，从而减少负迁移、稳定伪标签质量。此外，CAFC可作为轻量正则项迁移至标准域泛化目标，且无需目标数据。

Result: 在SFDA与DG多类基准上的大量实验表明，该方法相较近期基线取得一致提升，验证了框架的有效性与通用性（含向DG的无目标数据迁移）。

Conclusion: 通过同时约束注意力漂移与依赖域差的源模型初始化，DGA-CAFC提升了SFDA的稳定性与伪标签质量，并具备跨任务的通用性（可作DG轻量正则）。

Abstract: Source-free domain adaptation (SFDA) adapts a pretrained source model to unlabeled target domains without source data access, yet two critical failure modes remain unaddressed. First, attention drift: source-learned attention maps shift toward spurious or background features under distribution shift, destabilizing feature representations. Second, domain-gap-agnostic initialization: applying a single fixed source model uniformly across all target domains ignores domain gap severity, causing premature overfitting to noisy pseudo-labels under large shifts. We propose DGA-CAFC to address both failures. The Counterfactual Attention-based Feature Consistency (CAFC) module enforces feature stability by minimizing the discrepancy between representations computed under learned attention and those under counterfactual-inspired normalized perturbations, thereby promoting domain-robust features independent of spurious attention shifts. The Domain-Gap Aware Source Model Selection (DGASS) module estimates domain gap severity via target prediction entropy and selects the source model variant whose confidence level best matches the estimated gap, reducing negative transfer and stabilizing pseudo-label quality. Crucially, CAFC transfers beyond SFDA: applied as a lightweight regularizer to standard domain generalization objectives without target data. Extensive experiments across SFDA and DG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently improves over recent baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness and generality of the proposed framework. Project page is available at https://visdomlab.github.io/DGA_CAFC

</details>


### [136] [RL Post-Training in Diffusion Transformers Is Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26627)
*Tuna Han Salih Meral,Kaan Oktay,Alec Helbling,Atakan Kara,Duen Horng Chau,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 仅用一组与输入无关的固定转向向量（加在冻结基座模型各block输出上），即可复现RL后训练带来的大部分奖励增益，表明RL的收益主要来自恒定的输出偏移而非复杂输入依赖变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练能显著提升扩散Transformer的视觉质量、文字渲染与组合准确性；由于这些能力本质上是提示依赖的，直觉上认为提升它们需学习复杂的输入依赖变换。本文希望隔离验证：这些增益中有多少可由一个输入无关的恒定偏移解释。

Method: 在冻结基座模型的每个block输出上叠加一组固定的转向向量（对SD3.5-M仅0.29M参数，比RL调优的LoRA的18.8M少65倍），以匹配公开RL检查点的速度预测为目标进行拟合，目标函数中不包含奖励；此外还以零初始化、在线RL方式直接训练这些向量。

Result: 在六个RL调优图像模型上复现了90.0–96.3%的奖励增益，在两个RL调优视频模型上分别为89.8%和111.9%；零初始化加在线RL训练的向量在SD3.5-M的文字渲染（OCR）和人类偏好（PickScore）上单独超过基于权重更新的RL。

Conclusion: 策略梯度式RL后训练的大部分收益可由一个恒定偏移实现：无需新的输入依赖变换，它只是把预训练生成器引导向其本已可计算的分布。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training substantially improves the visual quality, text rendering, and compositional accuracy of diffusion transformers. Because capabilities such as rendering requested text or composing multiple objects are inherently prompt-dependent, a natural hypothesis is that improving them requires learning complex, input-dependent transformations. To isolate how much of these gains an input-independent shift explains, we probe each RL-tuned model with a deliberately simple parameterization: a fixed set of steering vectors added to the block outputs of the frozen base model. On SD3.5-M, these vectors hold 65x fewer parameters than the RL-tuned LoRA (0.29M against 18.8M). We fit them to match the velocity predictions of public RL-tuned checkpoints, with no reward in the objective. Although the vectors are identical for every prompt, token, and sampling seed, they recover 90.0-96.3% of the reward gains of six RL-tuned image models, and 89.8% and 111.9% on two RL-tuned video models. Initialized at zero and trained with online RL, the vectors alone exceed the reward gains of weight-based RL on text rendering (OCR) and human preference (PickScore) on SD3.5-M. A constant shift thus suffices for most of the gain of policy-gradient RL post-training: without new input-dependent transformations, it steers the pretrained generator toward what it can already compute.

</details>


### [137] [One Prior, Two Dynamics: Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Self-Supervised Latent Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26617)
*Yuxin Jiang,Wenqiang Sun,Jiaqi Li,Yuchao Gu,Lvmin Zhang,Ivor Tsang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: O²LaF 从同一个预训练视频 DiT 初始化逆动力学与正动力学两个模型，通过控制信息访问与紧凑瓶颈端到端联合优化，在无标注视频上学习可迁移的潜在动作，取得少样本动作探测与零样本动作迁移的 SOTA，并可推广到 V-JEPA2 等预测表征先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频只呈现世界变化，却很少显式给出导致变化的行为，潜在动作学习虽能从视觉过渡中恢复监督信号，但通常几乎从零学习潜在动力学，未充分利用预训练视频模型的先验。

Method: 核心原则为“一个先验、两种动力学”：以同一预训练视频 DiT 初始化两个模型，其一为带可学习动作查询（action queries）的前馈过渡编码器，承担逆动力学；其二为因果的、动作条件化的视频生成器，承担正动力学。通过限制各模型的信息访问，并通过紧凑瓶颈对两种动力学进行端到端联合优化。框架亦扩展至 V-JEPA2 等预测表征先验。

Result: 在导航与机器人操作任务上，O²LaF 在少样本动作探测上刷新 SOTA，并在跨视觉语境与跨具身（embodiment）的零样本动作迁移上表现更强；使用 V-JEPA2 预测表征先验亦可取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 预训练视频模型可被重新利用以同时发现可迁移动作并预测其后果，为潜在动作学习提供了一种复用生成式或预测式视频先验的更广义范式。

Abstract: Videos reveal how the world changes, but rarely the actions that cause those changes. Latent action learning recovers such supervision from visual transitions, yet often learns the underlying dynamics largely from scratch. We ask whether a pretrained video generator can instead provide the prior for both inverse and forward dynamics. We introduce O$^{2}$LaF, built on a simple principle: one prior, two dynamics. We initialize both models from the same pretrained video DiT and adapt them to their distinct roles: a feed-forward transition encoder with learnable action queries for inverse dynamics, and a causal, action-conditioned video generator for forward dynamics. By controlling information access and optimizing both dynamics end-to-end through a compact bottleneck, O$^{2}$LaF learns transferable actions from unlabeled videos. Across navigation and robotic manipulation, O$^{2}$LaF sets a new state of the art in few-shot action probing and enables stronger zero-shot action transfer across visual contexts and embodiments. Beyond generative video priors, we further extend our framework to predictive representation priors such as V-JEPA2, achieving competitive performance. Our findings suggest a broader paradigm for latent-action learning: repurposing pretrained video models to discover transferable actions and predict their consequences.

</details>


### [138] [SGAR: Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction via Sparse Geometry-Guided Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26493)
*Aoxiang Fan,Maria Pilligua,Corentin Dumery,Oliver Heinimann,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SGAR 用几何相似性引导的稀疏注意力替代密集全局注意力，使前馈三维重建在多视图场景下更快且更准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建模型采用密集全局注意力关联所有视图 patch，其计算代价随视图数呈二次增长，难以扩展到大规模重建场景。作者从经典 SfM 视角指出：三维结构来源于二维区域的几何匹配，因此大部分密集注意力计算是冗余的。

Method: 提出 Sparse Geometric Attention Reconstruction（SGAR）：每个 patch token 仅与来自其他视图的一小部分 patch token 交互，这些 token 由轻量的可学习 key 选择模块挑选，该模块被训练用于预测 token 间的跨视图三维邻近性，从而以几何相似性引导的稀疏注意力取代密集全局注意力。SGAR 为通用方法，可通过微调应用于不同前馈模型。

Result: SGAR 在现有密集与稀疏方法中速度最快，同时是稀疏方法中最准确的；在 1000 视图下比密集注意力快达 12 倍，且在现有稀疏方法失效时仍保持稳定的位姿精度；在相同时间预算下重建出更完整的几何。

Conclusion: 基于 SfM 几何匹配先验的稀疏注意力可在保证甚至提升精度的同时显著降低计算开销，是扩展前馈三维重建至大规模视图集合的有效且通用途径。

Abstract: Feed-forward models have been very successful in 3D reconstruction with ad hoc attention designs that relate all view patches densely. However, the cost of this dense global attention grows quadratically with the number of views, preventing the model from applying to larger-scale reconstruction settings. In this paper, we argue that most dense attention computations are unnecessary from a classical structure-from-motion perspective: 3D structures stem from geometrically matching regions in 2D. We bring this principle to learning-based feed-forward reconstruction with our Sparse Geometric Attention Reconstruction (SGAR) method. In SGAR, each patch token attends to only a small set of patch tokens from other views, chosen by a lightweight learned key selection module trained to predict cross-view 3D proximity of tokens. Thus, dense global attention is replaced by much more efficient geometric-similarity-guided sparse attention. SGAR is a general method that can be applied to different feed-forward models by fine-tuning. In our experiments, we show that SGAR is the fastest among both state-of-the-art dense and sparse methods and the most accurate sparse one. It runs up to 12-times faster than dense attention at 1,000 views with stable pose accuracy where existing sparse methods fail, and shows the best performance in reconstructing more complete geometries on the same time budget.

</details>


### [139] [SHUSH: Emission-Aware Reward Design for Proactive Video Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26478)
*Shawn Liang,Soamar Homsi,Gourav Datta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出MMDuet2的PAUC奖励只奖励及时性而不惩罚回复数量，导致过度发射；提出针对区间标注的emission-aware F1奖励，通过独占的span–emission匹配同时惩罚误发与漏发，显著减少冗余回复并提升F1与精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动式视频助手不仅要决定"说什么"，还要决定"何时说"。现有强化学习目标（如MMDuet2的PAUC）只奖励及时、正确的响应，却不对回复数量计价，从而鼓励不必要或重复的发射。作者需要一种能对发射行为"计价"的训练与评测目标。

Method: 1) 分析性证明在PAUC奖励下，额外响应被弱惩罚或视为免费，系统性诱发过度发射；2) 提出面向区间值标注的emission-aware F1奖励，采用独占的span–emission匹配（exclusive span–emission matching）对误发（false emissions）与漏span（missed spans）计价；3) 在MMDuet2训练配方下，于OmniPro视觉子集与ProactiveVideoQA上验证，并给出更保守的变体。

Result: 在OmniPro视觉子集上，新策略回复频率比MMDuet2低2.5倍，同时timing F1由0.259升至0.396，content-aware F1由0.103升至0.154；更保守变体将回复量削减82.5%，精确率从0.161升至0.418。在ProactiveVideoQA上，同一奖励在基线几乎沉默处补充了回复；而PAUC虽有近垫底的F1，却把重度过度发射的策略排到第二，说明其对响应数量不敏感。

Conclusion: 主动式助手应在训练与评测中采用对发射"计价"的目标；现有仅优化及时性的PAUC既会诱发过度发射，也无法正确排序策略质量。

Abstract: Proactive video assistants must decide not only what to say, but also when to speak. Existing reinforcement-learning objectives can reward timely, correct responses without accounting for response volume, thereby encouraging unnecessary or repeated emissions. We identify this failure mode in MMDuet2, whose proactive area-under-the-curve (PAUC) reward promotes timely responses but does not price the number of emissions. We show analytically that this makes additional responses weakly penalized or effectively free, systematically encouraging over-emission. We then propose an emission-aware F1 reward for interval-valued annotations, using exclusive span--emission matching to price false emissions and missed spans. Under MMDuet2's training recipe, our policy on the visual subset of OmniPro replies 2.5x less often than MMDuet2 while raising timing F1 from 0.259 to 0.396 and content-aware F1 from 0.103 to 0.154; a more conservative variant cuts replies by 82.5% and raises precision from 0.161 to 0.418. On ProactiveVideoQA, the same reward adds replies where the baseline is nearly silent, and PAUC ranks a heavily over-emitting policy second despite near-last F1, because it is insensitive to response count. Our results indicate that proactive assistants should be trained and evaluated with objectives that price emissions.

</details>


### [140] [Asuka-FM: A System-Architectural Co-Design Framework for Efficient Text-to-Image Pretraining on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26437)
*Víctor Rodrigo González,Pablo M. Olmos,Ignacio Peis,Alejandro Lancho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Asuka-FM是一个端到端系统与架构框架，证明约2.06亿参数的双流DiT可在单张消费级GPU（RTX 4090, 24GB）上从零预训练，约160 GPU小时、成本低于55美元，并在256px/512px开放域基准上取得GenEval 0.588/0.609、DPG 78.96/78.51的帕累托最优效率表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿T2I基础模型依赖数十亿参数DiT与大规模集群预训练，学术实验室难以复现。单卡从零训练DiT存在三大瓶颈：注意力二次序列开销、变长宽比分桶引发的JIT编译图断裂、以及冻结VAE带来的自编码器吞吐墙。
