Method: 四项核心设计：(i) 无AdaLN的双流DiT骨干以减少参数；(ii) 通过稀疏-稠密残差融合（SPRINT）进行结构化序列稀疏化，降低注意力FLOPs超50%；(iii) 连续长宽比归一化RoPE结合移位方形裁剪训练，保证静态张量形状并实现零样本虚拟相机控制；(iv) 像素适配阶段使用千万参数级Patch Detailer Head，以速度回归（v-prediction）训练。

Result: 在无基准特定微调的开放域评测中，256px下GenEval 0.588、DPG 78.96；512px下GenEval 0.609、DPG 78.51。

Conclusion: 低算力、低成本的单卡双流DiT可达到有竞争力的生成质量，为"民主化"基础模型训练确立了新的效率帕累托前沿。

Abstract: Frontier text-to-image (T2I) foundation models predominantly rely on multi-billion parameter Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) pretrained across massive compute clusters, creating an accessibility barrier for academic laboratories. When training DiTs from scratch on single consumer GPUs (e.g., an NVIDIA RTX 4090 with 24\,GB VRAM), practitioners encounter three primary bottlenecks: quadratic attention sequence costs, Just-In-Time (JIT) compilation graph breaks induced by variable aspect-ratio bucketing, and an autoencoder throughput wall where frozen Variational Autoencoder (VAE) operations dominate step latency. In this work, we introduce \textbf{Asuka-FM}, an end-to-end system and architectural framework demonstrating that competitive dual-stream DiTs ($\sim$206M parameters) can be pretrained from scratch on a single consumer GPU in $\sim$160 GPU hours for under \$55. Asuka-FM integrates four core designs: (i) an AdaLN-free dual-stream DiT backbone reducing parameter count; (ii) structured sequence sparsification via Sparse-Dense Residual Fusion (SPRINT) to cut attention FLOPs by over 50\%; (iii) continuous aspect-normalized Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) coupled with shifted square crop training, guaranteeing static tensor shapes while unlocking zero-shot virtual camera control; and (iv) a pixel adaptation phase utilizing a lightweight (10M parameter) Patch Detailer Head operating under velocity regression ($v$-prediction). Evaluated on open-domain benchmarks without benchmark-specific fine-tuning, Asuka-FM achieves GenEval 0.588 / DPG 78.96 scores at 256px and GenEval 0.609 / DPG 78.51 scores at 512px, setting a new Pareto-optimal efficiency standard for democratic foundation modeling.

</details>


### [141] [FFGaussType: Feed-Forward Conversion of Glyph and SVG Geometry into Compact Gaussian Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26431)
*Haoan Feng,Tianfu Wang,Peimeng Guan,Dae Yeol Lee,Guan-ming Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FFGaussType 通过一次前向传播将挤出的字形与 SVG 轮廓转换为高斯资产，实现可扩展的 3D 文本与图标内容创建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式 3D 场景中文本与图标可置于场景内部并遵循遮挡、透视与光照，但在高斯场景中每个新字形/图标都需单独迭代优化，严重限制内容创建的规模化。

Method: 将挤出的字形与 SVG 轮廓转为高斯资产：在已知表面上采样锚点，点编码器将锚点映射到单一特征平面，由所有资产共享的解码器预测携带表面法线的神经高斯；几何转换与材质/光照选择解耦，特征平面支持图像与视频编码以实现紧凑传输。同时构建 27,221 个字形与 SVG 图标配对数据集（含锚点及标定的法线、轮廓渲染），并按字体族划分留出评估。

Result: 基于准备好的锚点，每资产转换耗时 9.1 ms，计算与内存开销远低于现有基线；在未见字体族与测试图标上达到 1.62° 法线误差与 0.985 轮廓 IoU，为所比较的前馈转换器中最佳，可媲美需数千步逐资产拟合的保真度。

Conclusion: 前馈式高斯化转换可高效、高质量地生成 3D 文本与图标资产，替代逐资产迭代优化；代码与数据集将于论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Immersive 3D content allows text and graphical elements, such as subtitles, annotations, and icons, to be placed inside a scene rather than displayed as flat overlays. Placed this way, they respect occlusion and perspective and take on user-assigned materials and lighting while preserving their original shapes. In Gaussian scenes, however, each new glyph or icon requires its own iterative optimization, which limits scalable content creation. We present FFGaussType, which converts extruded glyph and SVG outlines into Gaussian assets in a single forward pass. A point encoder maps anchors sampled on the known surface to a single feature plane, from which a decoder shared by all assets predicts neural Gaussians carrying surface normals. This separates geometric conversion from material and lighting choices, and the feature plane supports image and video coding for compact delivery. We construct a paired dataset of 27,221 glyph and SVG icon assets with anchors and calibrated normal and silhouette renderings, holding out font families for evaluation. Conversion from prepared anchors takes 9.1 ms per asset, at a fraction of the compute and memory of prior baselines. On unseen font families and test icons, FFGaussType achieves $1.62^\circ$ normal error and $0.985$ silhouette IoU, the best among the compared feed-forward converters, matching the fidelity that per-asset fitting needs thousands of steps to reach. The code and dataset will be released after publication.

</details>


### [142] [ViDecide: Teaching Video Diffusion Models to Reason and Plan Step by Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26389)
*Kaiwen Zhang,Yiming Gao,Zeqi Xiao,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出ViDecide，一个以决策为中心的框架，将视频生成训练从"固定长度片段"转变为"因果状态转移+停止决策"的决策链，并提出Corrective Fine-tuning (CFT)以实现稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进视频生成模型虽能合成高度逼真视频，但在多步推理与规划中仍频繁违反物理与逻辑约束，即使使用大规模推理数据或现有diffusion RL方法训练后依然如此。作者认为根源在于传统视频训练范式把"解"当作固定长度片段，而非一系列决策。

Method: 将视频扩散模型训练为因果预测状态转移并决策何时停止，配合以决策为中心的训练调度；进一步提出Corrective Fine-tuning (CFT)，通过将失败决策的噪声映射到模型自身成功延续的噪声上，从而纠正错误，无需策略梯度优化即可稳定提升。

Result: 在抽象游戏、规划任务及新提出的多步机器人规划基准上，ViDecide取得平均83.8%严格成功率，远高于标准微调的23.3%和最强先前方法的67.8%，并能泛化到更大规模谜题与未见布局。

Conclusion: 该框架释放了视频生成模型作为视觉世界中可靠推理器与规划器的潜力。

Abstract: Current leading video generation models can synthesize highly realistic videos, while still frequently violating physical and logical constraints in multi-step reasoning and planning, even after training with large-scale reasoning data or existing diffusion RL methods. We attribute these failures to the conventional video training paradigm, which treats a solution as a fixed-length clip rather than a chain of decisions. We present ViDecide, a decision-centric framework that trains video diffusion models to predict state transitions causally and decide when to stop, with a decision-focused training schedule. We further propose Corrective Fine-tuning (CFT), which corrects failed decisions by mapping their noise to the model's own successful continuations, achieving stable improvement without policy-gradient optimization. Across abstract games, planning tasks, and a new benchmark of multi-step robot planning, ViDecide achieves an average strict success rate of 83.8%, far above 23.3% for standard fine-tuning and 67.8% for the strongest prior method, and generalizes to larger puzzles and unseen layouts. Our framework therefore unlocks greater potential for video generation models as reliable reasoners and planners in the visual world. Project page: https://kevin-thu.github.io/ViDecide.

</details>


### [143] [EgoCaptioner: Towards Comprehensive and Fine-Grained Egocentric Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26380)
*Yuqi Tang,Yang Shi,Yuran Wang,Tengfei Liu,Rundong Wang,Zongyang Qiu,Qixun Wang,Zhuoran Zhang,Xuanyu Zhu,Yifan Xu,Yiyan Ji,Shujie Li,Shixuan Liu,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoCaptioner：以"视频级概览+带时间戳的步骤化叙述"为字幕格式，配合粗到细标注管线（5万样本、八个第一视角视频源）与GRPO多奖励优化，在第一视角字幕生成、字幕条件QA与时间对齐上取得开源模型领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角视频字幕要求细粒度细节与准确时序，但现有多模态大模型常漏检短暂动作、混淆动作顺序、误判佩戴者自身运动；同时现有字幕格式难以兼顾细节叙述与精确时间标记。

Method: 提出混合格式字幕：视频级概览（场景、参与者、佩戴者整体活动）+带时间戳的步骤化叙述（叙述颗粒度随感知需求自适应）。构建粗到细标注管线：先确定事件结构再扩展步骤描述，从八个第一视角视频源生成50,000条训练样本；在传感器可用时用姿态导出的运动事实补充字幕参考以增强事实监督可靠性。设计互补奖励（事实准确性与覆盖度、步骤级时间对齐、惩罚碎片化与冗余叙述），通过GRPO引导字幕精炼。

Result: 在五个第一视角基准上，字幕质量、字幕条件QA与时间对齐均在被评估开源模型中领先；在PhysCapBench上超过Gemini 3.1 Pro，在字幕到QA协议下Minerva-Ego上超过所有被评估的闭源基线。消融显示事实奖励与时间奖励作用互补，粗到细标注的监督效果优于单遍标注。

Conclusion: 结构化（概览+时间戳步骤）字幕格式与粗到细标注、多奖励GRPO训练相结合，可有效缓解第一视角字幕的时序与细节缺陷，并以较小数据规模取得强竞争力表现。

Abstract: Egocentric video captioning requires fine-grained detail and accurate timing, yet current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often miss brief actions, confuse their order, or misinterpret the wearer's motion. Existing caption formats also offer limited support for combining detailed narration with precise timing. We present EgoCaptioner, which pairs a video-level overview of the setting, participants, and the wearer's overall activity with timed steps whose narration adapts to varying perceptual demands and marks key moments at specific timestamps. We develop a coarse-to-fine annotation pipeline that establishes event structure before expanding step descriptions, yielding 50,000 training examples from eight egocentric video sources. Complementary rewards assess factual accuracy and coverage, measure step-level temporal alignment, and penalize fragmented or redundant narration, guiding caption refinement through Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). To improve the reliability of factual supervision, we supplement caption references with pose-derived motion facts when sensor data are available. Across five egocentric benchmarks, EgoCaptioner achieves leading performance among evaluated open-source models in caption quality, caption-conditioned question answering, and temporal alignment. It also surpasses Gemini 3.1 Pro on PhysCapBench and all evaluated proprietary baselines on Minerva-Ego under the caption-to-QA protocol. Ablations support the complementary roles of factual and temporal rewards and show that coarse-to-fine annotation provides more effective supervision than single-pass annotation.

</details>


### [144] [Learning Reconstruction Errors as Spatiotemporal Evidence for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26116)
*Jiahua Jiang,Jinglai Li,Jianru Zhang,Jin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种检测AI生成视频的方法，将重建误差作为统一的时空证据来源：联合优化重建器与分类器以获得更具判别性的重建误差，并用3D卷积直接从连续重建误差中提取时空证据，无需额外的原始视频特征流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频的泛滥带来虚假信息、欺诈和社会风险，亟需检测手段。基于重建误差的方法在AI生成图像检测上表现良好，但扩展到视频时存在两个问题：(i) 固定的重建器未针对检测目标优化，判别信号弱；(ii) 常需引入额外的原始视频特征流建模时序依赖，增加架构开销。

Method: 第一，联合优化重建器与下游分类器，使重建误差对检测AI生成视频更具信息量；第二，论证连续重建误差本身即蕴含时序证据，因此仅在其上施加3D卷积即可捕获时空证据，从而省去原始视频特征流。

Result: 在超过30种现有AI视频生成器上进行大量实验，与基线方法相比取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 重建误差可作为检测AI生成视频的统一时空证据来源；端到端联合优化与基于重建误差的3D卷积是有效且轻量的设计，可在不引入原始视频流的情况下实现有竞争力的检测性能。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-generated videos has raised growing concerns about misinformation, fraud, and potential societal risks, creating an urgent need for detection. Reconstruction-error-based methods have shown strong empirical performance for AI-generated image detection by exploiting discrepancies between an input and its reconstructed version. Their extension to video detection, however, faces two challenges: (i) a fixed reconstructor is not optimized for the detection objective and may yield weak discriminative signals; and (ii) an additional raw-video feature stream is often introduced to model temporal dependencies, increasing architectural overhead. To address these challenges, we develop a detector that learns and exploits reconstruction errors as a unified source of spatiotemporal evidence. First, we jointly optimize a reconstructor and the downstream classifier to produce reconstruction errors that are more informative for detecting AI-generated videos. Second, we show that consecutive reconstruction errors alone carry temporal evidence, and thus a 3D convolution over them captures spatiotemporal evidence without a raw-video stream. Extensive experiments on over 30 existing AI-video generators demonstrate competitive performance against baselines.

</details>


### [145] [Domain-Aware Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy Spacecraft Pose Estimation under Sim-to-Real Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26065)
*Nidhal Eddine Chenni,Arunkumar Rathinam,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在SPEED+数据集上，合成图像标定的共形姿态估计在真实图像上失去覆盖率保证且产生大量"静默失败"；作者改用几何保持的外观翻译后的合成图像进行标定，从而恢复覆盖率并将静默失败率从最高43.5%降至不超过3.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主交会与近距操作既需要精确的目标航天器位姿估计，也需要判断该估计何时可信。共形预测可给出带形式化保证的位姿不确定集，但其覆盖率保证要求标定数据与测试数据同分布；而在轨感知中，带标签的标定图像只能来自合成渲染，实际运行图像为真实图像，构成分布偏移。

Method: 在SPEED+数据集上验证合成图像标定的共形姿态估计在真实图像上的失效情况；随后采用外观翻译模型把合成图像转换为真实图像外观，由于翻译只改变外观而保持几何结构，合成位姿标签仍然有效，且无需任何带标签的真实图像，再用翻译后的图像完成共形标定。

Result: 直接使用合成图像标定时，覆盖率保证在真实图像上失效，且多数失败为静默失败——预测位姿集看似合理却排除了真实位姿。采用两种翻译模型进行标定后，在误覆盖率0.1及以上时覆盖率恢复到规定水平，静默失败率由最高43.5%降至最多3.3%。

Conclusion: 几何保持的外观翻译可作为一种无需真实标签的域适配式共形标定手段，有效修复合成到真实分布偏移下的覆盖率失效，使共形姿态估计更接近在空间任务中可信部署。

Abstract: Autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations require not only an accurate estimate of the pose of the target spacecraft, but also a reliable indication of when this estimate can be trusted. Conformal prediction provides such an indication with a formal guarantee, by producing pose uncertainty sets that contain the true pose with a specified probability. Such a guarantee, however, assumes that calibration and test data follow the same distribution, an assumption that is violated in orbital perception, where labelled calibration images are available only from synthetic renderings while operational images are real. In this work, we show on the SPEED+ dataset that conformal pose estimation calibrated on synthetic images loses its coverage guarantee on real images, and that many of the resulting failures are silent: the predicted pose set appears valid but excludes the true pose. To address this, we calibrate on synthetic images translated to the appearance of real images. Since translation alters appearance while preserving geometry, the synthetic pose labels remain valid and no labelled real images are required. Experiments with two translation models show that this calibration restores coverage above the specified level for miscoverage levels of 0.1 and above, and reduces silent failures from up to 43.5% to at most 3.3% of the test images, bringing conformal pose estimation a step closer to trustworthy deployment in space.

</details>


### [146] [Fast Self-Supervised Generative CT Denoising via Few-Step Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26370)
*Xingyi Yao,Zhengtao Ma,Zhennong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 N2N-iMF，将 Noise2Noise 与改进的 Mean Flow 结合，用少步采样生成并平均多个独立噪声输出以实现自监督 CT 去噪，在薄层脑部与低剂量腹部 CT 上以更少网络评估次数和更低时延超过 DDIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量与薄层 CT 噪声大、掩盖解剖细节，而干净参考图像难以获得；Noise2Noise 可自监督训练但直接回归易过度平滑，多独立样本平均可缓解模糊，然而多步扩散采样重复开销大。

Method: N2N-iMF：模型学习以配对噪声图像为条件生成另一幅噪声图像，推理时独立采样多个输出并平均以抑噪；借助 Improved Mean Flow 实现少步采样，无需蒸馏，且平均样本数在推理阶段可调。评估涵盖四种采样方法、1–20 平均次数，并在真实光子计数 CT 脑扫描上验证。

Result: 每条件输入 10 个样本、每样本 3 次网络评估下，脑 CT MAE 2.60 HU、腹部 CT 14.02 HU，优于 50 次评估的 DDIM（2.91 与 14.53 HU）；同 GPU 下单输出生成时间由 7.29 s 降至 0.43 s；8 例 PCCT 上灰白质 CNR 达 1.341，N2N 为 0.916，DDIM 为 1.176。

Conclusion: N2N-iMF 在不使用蒸馏的前提下加速自监督生成式 CT 去噪，并在推理时保持平均输出数可调，兼顾图像质量与采样效率。

Abstract: Low-dose and thin-slice computed tomography (CT) images suffer from elevated noise that obscures anatomical detail, while clean references for supervised denoising are often unavailable. Although Noise2Noise permits self-supervised training from noisy pairs, direct regression tends to oversmooth fine structure. Averaging multiple independently generated samples mitigates this blur, though repeating multi-step diffusion sampling is costly. We introduce Noise2Noise with Improved Mean Flow (N2N-iMF) to accelerate this process via few-step sampling. N2N-iMF learns to generate one noisy image conditioned on its paired counterpart; at inference, independent outputs are sampled and averaged to suppress noise. We evaluated image quality and sampling efficiency on simulated thin-slice brain and low-dose abdominal CT across four sampling methods and averaging counts from 1 to 20, and validated the approach on real-world photon-counting CT (PCCT) brain scans. With ten samples per conditioning input and three network evaluations per sample, N2N-iMF achieved a mean absolute error of 2.60 HU on brain CT and 14.02 HU on abdominal CT, outperforming 50-evaluation denoising diffusion implicit model (DDIM) sampling (2.91 and 14.53 HU). On the same GPU, per-output generation time dropped from 7.29 to 0.43 s. Across eight PCCT cases, N2N-iMF reached a mean gray-white matter contrast-to-noise ratio of 1.341, versus 0.916 for Noise2Noise and 1.176 for DDIM. N2N-iMF accelerates self-supervised generative CT denoising without distillation while keeping the number of averaged outputs adjustable at inference.

</details>


### [147] [OffBeat: Multicoset Sampling for 4D Capture Beyond the Camera Frame Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26347)
*Chi-Wei Lee,An-Kai Chen,Cheng-Han Chiang,Yi-Chun Chang,Po-Chih Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OffBeat 将交错触发多相机装置建模为空间分布的多余采样器（multicoset sampler），从标定推导同步发射组与发射顺序，并给出原语寿命下界与解析平滑盒核，在 N3DV 模拟交错采集上超越 4DSloMo 与同步方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步多相机装置在同一时间戳记录所有视角，丢失帧间运动；交错触发可将同样帧数分散到更多时间戳，但 4DSloMo 在 N3DV 上的逐行顺序触发使整行等效为一个运动相机，深度与运动相互混淆。论文旨在消除这种歧义并提升动态区域重建质量。

Method: 将交错装置视为空间分布的多余采样器（同一采样网格的平移副本）；仅由标定推导同时发射的相机分组及发射顺序，使每个时间戳仅由一组记录。分析短寿命原语导致组间不一致误差（如颜色偏移）引发闪烁；证明原语不透明度对时间加权等权于各组当且仅当其持续时间不少于一个相机帧间隔。在 Spacetime Gaussians 上用最短寿命近似该界，并提出闭式平滑盒核精确满足该界。

Result: N3DV 模拟交错采集中，OffBeat 在全部测试帧上优于含视频扩散的完整 4DSloMo 流水线，动态区域平均 PSNR 提升 3.04 dB；在首末同步帧之间，比各场景最佳同步方法（含或不含插帧）平均提升 1.98 dB，而同步采集在其自身帧上更优。PanopticSports 上该帧区间内 PSNR 与带插帧的同步采集相当。每个 N3DV 场景上，以本文调度替换 4DSloMo 调度均提升动态区域 PSNR（Spacetime Gaussians、无扩散 4DSloMo 及本文重建）。

Conclusion: 交错触发的时序设计（组划分与顺序）而非仅帧数决定 4D 重建质量；仅改变各相机发射时机即可在相同帧数下跟踪更快运动。

Abstract: For 4D reconstruction, a synchronized multi-camera rig records all views at the same timestamps and misses the motion between them. Staggered triggers spread the same frames over more timestamps. However, when the cameras along each row fire one after another, as in 4DSloMo's schedule on the N3DV rig, the row acts as one moving camera that confounds depth with motion. We introduce OffBeat, which models a staggered rig as a spatially distributed multicoset sampler: shifted copies of one sampling grid. From the calibration alone, OffBeat derives camera groups that fire together and their firing order. Since one group records each timestamp, a short-lived primitive can fit an error its cameras share, such as a color offset, and flicker in novel views. We show that a primitive's opacity over time weights all groups equally, for every number of groups, only if it lasts at least one camera frame interval. On Spacetime Gaussians, a minimum lifetime approximates this bound, and a closed-form smoothed box of that length satisfies it exactly, with less flicker than a Gaussian in its place at nearly equal PSNR. In simulated staggered capture on N3DV, OffBeat outperforms the full 4DSloMo pipeline with video diffusion on every test frame, by 3.04 dB in mean dynamic-region PSNR. Between the first and last synchronized frame, it outperforms the best synchronized method of each scene, with or without frame interpolation, by 1.98 dB in mean dynamic-region PSNR, while synchronized capture is better at its own frames. On PanopticSports, in this frame range, OffBeat is comparable in PSNR to synchronized capture with interpolation. On every N3DV scene, replacing 4DSloMo's schedule with ours raises the dynamic-region PSNR of Spacetime Gaussians, 4DSloMo without diffusion, and our reconstruction. Multi-camera rigs could follow faster motion at the same frame count by changing only when each camera fires.

</details>


### [148] [ResVision: Spectrum-Matched Resizing and Context-Preserving Inspection for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26318)
*Guojian Zhan,Jiahao Xu,Yahui Liu,Yao Hu,Chuan Mu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ResVision通过谱匹配缩放(SMR)与上下文保持式检视(CPI)解决agentic视觉推理中的三个瓶颈,在4个前沿VLM、29个基准上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic视觉推理通过选择性放大证据区域来增强视觉语言模型,且不引入新场景内容,但其收益依赖两点:细粒度结构是否仍可获取、局部观测是否仍锚定于完整场景。现有方法存在三个反复出现的瓶颈:高频细节在视觉token形成过程中被削弱;空间分散的证据虽被编码,但语言模型难以检索;孤立裁剪图因尺度与位置失配而削弱整体—细节对应关系。

Method: 提出ResVision,含两个互补组件:(1) Spectrum-Matched Resizing (SMR),估计图像复杂度并选择自适应全局采样尺度,对简单输入避免不必要放大,对细节丰富图像分配更多视觉token;(2) Context-Preserving Inspection (CPI),每次检视裁剪图时同时返回完整工作视图,在工具轮次间同时保留局部细节与全局上下文。

Result: 在4个前沿VLM与覆盖六大视觉推理家族的29个基准上评测Direct与Agentic变体。完整ResVision(Agentic Base + SMR + CPI)对每个模型均为最优,较Direct Base提升3.36–4.94分,较Agentic Base提升1.87–2.94分。

Conclusion: 实验表明自适应视觉采样与上下文保持式检视分别针对多模态推理中不同且互补的约束,二者结合可稳定提升agentic视觉推理性能。

Abstract: Agentic visual reasoning improves vision-language models by selectively enlarging evidence without adding new scene content, but the benefit depends on whether fine structure remains accessible and whether local observations stay grounded in the full scene. We identify three recurring bottlenecks: high-frequency detail can be attenuated during visual-token formation; spatially distributed evidence can remain encoded yet difficult for the language model to retrieve; and isolated crops can weaken whole-detail correspondence through scale and positional mismatch. ResVision addresses these limitations with two complementary components. Spectrum-Matched Resizing (SMR) estimates image complexity and selects an adaptive global sampling scale, avoiding unnecessary enlargement on simple inputs while allocating more visual tokens to detail-rich images. Context-Preserving Inspection (CPI) returns each inspected crop together with the complete working view, preserving local detail and global context across tool turns. We evaluate Direct and Agentic variants on four frontier VLMs and 29 benchmarks spanning six visual-reasoning families. Complete ResVision (Agentic Base plus SMR plus CPI) is best for every model, improving by 3.36 to 4.94 points over Direct Base and 1.87 to 2.94 points over Agentic Base. These results show that adaptive visual sampling and context-preserving inspection address distinct, complementary constraints in multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [149] [MixReact: Physics-Based Reactive Humanoid Control from Multimodal Cues via a Discrete-Continuous Mixture of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26218)
*Bin Li,Xing Zhu,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MixReact，首个面向文本、音频、视频三类线索的统一物理仿真人形反应控制框架，用DisCo-MoT做状态预测、HAT做物理执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一线索驱动反应生成方法支持的线索模态有限，且仅做纯运动学生成，结果易出现物理伪影，缺乏语义合理且物理可执行的行为。

Method: 提出DisCo-MoT：在MoT架构内以单向混合注意力耦合离散掩码建模分支与连续自回归扩散分支进行状态预测；再用History-aware Action Transformer（HAT）将预测的下一状态与执行历史映射为可执行动作，实现闭环物理控制。

Result: 在文本、音频、视频三种模态上均较基线在保真度、多样性和物理一致性上取得一致提升；消融实验验证各组件互补贡献。

Conclusion: 离散上下文引导可缓解一对多反应生成中的均值化坍塌，连续状态空间直接预测绕过离散表示的解码瓶颈，二者结合实现多模态线索驱动的物理一致人形反应生成。

Abstract: Humanoid agents in everyday environments are expected to react to diverse cues as humans do, with semantically appropriate and physically executable behaviors. Existing cue-driven reaction generation methods support only a narrow range of cue modalities and are limited to purely kinematic generation, leaving the resulting reactions susceptible to physical artifacts. To this end, we present MixReact, the first unified framework for physics-based reactive humanoid control from text, audio, and video cues, integrating a Discrete--Continuous Mixture of Transformers (DisCo-MoT) for state prediction with a History-aware Action Transformer (HAT) for physical execution. DisCo-MoT couples a discrete masked-modeling branch with a continuous autoregressive diffusion branch via one-way mixture attention within a MoT architecture. Guidance from discrete context mitigates collapse into averaged behaviors in one-to-many reaction generation, while direct prediction in continuous state space bypasses the decoding bottleneck of discrete representations. HAT then maps the predicted next state and observed execution history to executable actions, enabling robust closed-loop control in physics-based simulation. Experiments across all three modalities demonstrate consistent improvements over the baselines in fidelity, diversity, and physical consistency, while ablations validate the complementary contributions of the components. Our code will be released to facilitate future research. Project page: https://binlee26.github.io/MixReact_Page/

</details>


### [150] [Can World Models Predict Rice Growth? Toward Agricultural World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26145)
*Wenkai Ji,Zeteng Li,Yinkun Guo,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Zhenong Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"农业世界模型"任务：仅凭单张照片、品种与相机视角，预测同一植株在数天至数月后的形态；提出AgroWM，先生成"生长脚本"再渲染，显著优于通用图像生成器与强域内基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型仅能想象未来数秒的物理演化，而种植者需要预判作物数周至数月后的发育（如一个月后是否抽穗、收获时形态如何）。初步分析显示，最先进的图像生成器在此任务上失效：植株生长幅度过小，或呈现错误生育期的颜色与形态；作者认为其根源在于模型未显式推断照片中间接揭示的发育状态。

Method: 两阶段解耦设计：推理模块PhenoClock先推断当前生育期、预测未来生育期，并写出含株高、株面积、颜色等数值目标的Growth Script；生成模块Rooted Renderer依据该脚本对原照片进行编辑，在原株上新增器官与颜色。评估上，因像素指标可能偏好"原图复制"，改用目标植株自身后期照片校验性状与发育时序（Stage R@1）。

Result: 水稻上AgroWM的Stage R@1为54.6%，对比GPT Image 2.5的34.8%、Qwen Image 2.1的24.0%、最强域内基线（同骨干但无Growth Script微调）的47.4%；前瞻9–20周仍达46.4%（基线35.7%）。在株型与生育期尺度均不同的玉米上，同一设计在四项指标上同样最优。

Conclusion: 显式"先推理发育、再生成形态"的中间表示（Growth Script）是长时程作物形态预测的关键，且跨作物可迁移；以发育时序与性状为依据的评估比像素指标更贴合任务本质。

Abstract: World models can imagine how a physical scene evolves over the next few seconds, but growers need to know how a crop will develop over weeks to months: will this rice plant have headed a month from now, and how will it look at harvest? We introduce agricultural world modeling, the task of predicting how the same plant will look at a requested horizon of days to months, from one photo of it, its cultivar, and its camera view. In preliminary analysis, even state-of-the-art image generators get this wrong: they often grow the plant too little or give it the color and form of the wrong growth stage. We argue that they do not work out the plant's developmental state, which the photo reveals only indirectly. We therefore propose AgroWM, which reasons about development before it draws growth. Its reasoning module, PhenoClock, works out the current growth stage, predicts the future one, and writes a Growth Script with numeric targets for height, plant area, and color; its generation module, the Rooted Renderer, then edits the photo to follow this script, adding new organs and colors to the photographed plant. Because pixel scores can prefer an unchanged copy of the photo, we instead check each forecast's traits and developmental timing against the plant's own later photos. On rice, 54.6% of the forecasts of AgroWM are matched to the target stage (Stage R@1), against 34.8% for GPT Image 2.5, 24.0% for Qwen Image 2.1, and 47.4% for the strongest domain-specific baseline, the same backbone fine-tuned without Growth Scripts; 9 to 20 weeks ahead, it still reaches 46.4%, against 35.7% for that baseline. On maize, a crop with a different body plan and stage scale, the same design again scores best on all four measures.

</details>


### [151] [Sample-efficient end-to-end Optical Music Recognition through Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25975)
*Eric Ayllon,Francisco J. Castellanos,Antonio Rios-Vila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索将主动学习（Active Learning）用于端到端光学音乐识别（OMR）在低资源目标域上的适配，通过不确定性/多样性采样策略减少人工标注成本，并在谱表级与整页语料上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端 OMR 在未见数据上性能下降，常规做法是用目标域标注样本微调，但需大量人工标注，成本高、耗时长；因此在可标注量有限的低资源场景下需要高效的样本选择方式。

Method: 引入主动学习流程，在低资源条件下从目标域中选择信息量大的样本进行标注；对比基于不确定性（置信度）与基于多样性的采样策略，并分别在谱表级（staff-level）与整页（full-page）两类语料上评测。

Result: 基于置信度的采样效果与基于真实误差的 oracle 选择相当；两类语料各自受益于为其定制的采样策略。

Conclusion: 主动学习可有效降低 OMR 域适配中的标注负担，且不存在单一最优采样策略，应根据谱表级或整页等具体场景选择相应策略。

Abstract: End-to-end approaches to Optical Music Recognition (OMR) have recently shown promising results, but their performance often degrades on unseen data. A common solution is to pretrain and fine-tune models with annotated samples from the target domain, improving adaptation to new distributions. However, this process typically requires extensive manual annotation to yield competitive models, making it costly and time-consuming. In this work, we explore Active Learning to reduce this burden by selecting informative samples for labeling in low-resource scenarios, where the amount of manual annotation that can be performed is limited. We evaluate both uncertainty- and diversity-based sampling strategies on staff-level and full-page corpora. Results show that confidence-based sampling performs comparably to oracle selection (based on true error), and that each scenario benefits from a tailored strategy.

</details>


### [152] [MorphTwin: Enriching Deformable Material Responses from Videos for Morphable and Interactive Physical Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25963)
*Haoqin Hong,Ding Fan,Fubin Dou,Tianyu Huang,Yuzhuo Chen,Zhemeng Zhang,Hanxiao Jiang,Congcong Zhu,Gerhard Petrus Hancke,Jingrun Chen,Rynson WH Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MorphTwin通过丰富局部变形模式实现体积与形状响应的独立控制，在保持标准弹簧力与显式仿真的前提下，提升了从视频重建交互式物理孪生体的准确性与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视频重建交互式物理孪生体需同时准确捕捉物体形变并保持高效仿真。作者观察到：当物理表示限制了物体可形变方式时，仅复现视频中的运动并不能保证对新施加力的响应正确；在弹簧型孪生体中，体积响应与形状响应的耦合正是这一限制的根源。

Method: 提出MorphTwin，通过增加局部变形模式来捕捉并独立控制体积响应与形状响应，同时保留标准弹簧力与显式仿真；采用离线标定将共享的相对响应变化映射为各重建图上的刚度更新，以支持可解释的材质编辑与迁移。

Result: 在合成场景相同逆问题设置下，相较PhysTwin，泊松比平均绝对误差降低75.8%，杨氏模量平均相对误差降低22.1%；构建了覆盖不同刚度物体交互的自我中心立体与RGB-D基准；真实实验表明重建精度提升，未来跟踪误差最多降低14.4%，并保持交互级速度。

Conclusion: 解耦体积与形状响应是提升弹簧型物理孪生体物理保真度的关键，MorphTwin在保持显式仿真效率的同时显著改善材质参数辨识与真实场景跟踪性能。

Abstract: Reconstructing interactive physical twins from video requires accurately capturing how objects deform while maintaining efficient simulation. We observe that reproducing motion in a video does not guarantee correct responses to new forces when the physical representation restricts how an object can deform. In spring-based twins, coupling between volume and shape responses creates such a limitation. We present MorphTwin, which enriches local deformation modes to capture and independently control these responses while retaining standard spring forces and explicit simulation. To support interpretable material editing and transfer, we use offline calibration to map shared relative response changes to stiffness updates on each reconstructed graph. Under identical inverse settings on synthetic scenes, MorphTwin reduces the mean absolute error of Poisson's ratio by 75.8% and the mean relative error of Young's modulus by 22.1% compared to PhysTwin. We introduce an egocentric stereo and RGB-D benchmark covering interactions with objects of varying stiffness. Real-world experiments demonstrate improved reconstruction accuracy and up to 14.4% lower future tracking error than PhysTwin at interactive speeds.

</details>


### [153] [TrajCal: Trajectory-Calibrated Time Schedules for Few-Step Flow Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25847)
*Junchi Zhang,Zihan Gu,Jiangcan Jia,Lulu Zhao,Wenbo Su,Hua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的轨迹校准框架TrajCal，用散射搜索优化少步采样调度，在低评估预算下获得高于各基线的参考保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow matching可生成高质量图像与视频，但迭代采样需反复评估大网络；现有少步方法易使输出失真、与多步参考解的一致性下降。

Method: 训练无关的少步采样校准框架：以散射搜索（scatter search）元启发式为核心，从多样化种子调度出发，对候选调度进行变形与组合，按最终输出保真度选定单一固定调度并复用于新输入；种子可为随机生成或基于轨迹生成。

Result: 图像与视频生成中，低评估预算下参考保真度优于所有对比基线，同时视觉质量具竞争力；随机种子已有效，轨迹种子进一步提升保真度；摄影域校准的调度可跨风格泛化到未见图像风格。

Conclusion: 将元启发式搜索引入少步采样调度校准，可在不重训练的前提下提升参考一致性并具备跨风格迁移能力。

Abstract: Flow matching enables high-quality image and video generation, but its iterative sampling process requires repeated evaluations of large neural networks. Existing few-step methods can distort the output and reduce its agreement with a many-step reference. We propose TrajCal, a training-free calibration framework for few-step sampling. Based on scatter search, a well-established metaheuristic for difficult optimization problems, TrajCal starts with diverse seed schedules, deforms and combines these candidates, and selects one fixed schedule by final output fidelity for reuse on new inputs. For both image and video generation, TrajCal achieves higher reference fidelity than all compared baselines at low evaluation budgets while maintaining competitive visual quality. The search is effective even with randomly generated seeds, while trajectory-based seeds further improve fidelity. For cross-style generalization, photography-calibrated schedules also improve fidelity on unseen image styles.

</details>


### [154] [Coda-DaVinci: Reimagining Video Generation with Code2Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26595)
*Yiyan Ji,Qixun Wang,Yang Shi,Zhuoran Zhang,Yifan Xu,Tengfei Liu,Yuqi Tang,Xiaohan Zhang,Shujie Li,Jiafu Tang,Xuanyu Zhu,Xinyu Liu,Yan He,Yushuo Guan,Zihao Wang,Xianghua Ying,Shixuan Liu,Haotian Wang,Wenjing Yang,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统研究Code2Video中3D代理（proxy）的构建与提示策略，提出任务自适应的多智能体框架Coda-DaVinci。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Code2Video借助智能体编写的3D代理控制视频结构与动态，但如何构建代理与提示以获得最优下游质量尚不清楚，该范式的能力边界亦缺乏系统认识。

Method: 通过受控实验与盲评人体评估，考察代理粒度、物理仿真与提示三个因子；据此设计Coda-DaVinci：在代理引导与直接生成间自适应选择、按实体自适应粒度、协调仿真与代理对齐提示，并用多智能体框架保护场景资产、通过视觉检查与独立审阅驱动修复。

Result: 粗粒度代理利于自然运动，中粒度代理利于一致性；物理仿真与代理对齐提示提升平均质量。Coda-DaVinci在VTR指令遵循、R2M场景一致性上领先，物理合理性优于直接生成，视觉真实感优于Code2Video基线；长视频在事件结构与跨交互一致性上有增益，但转场稳定性与场景设定遵循仍有不足。

Conclusion: 代理粒度等设计存在任务相关权衡，需自适应策略；Coda-DaVinci验证了该思路的有效性，同时揭示转场稳定性等局限为未来方向。

Abstract: Code2Video uses agent-authored 3D proxies to guide video generation, enabling explicit control over structure and dynamics. However, how to construct proxies and prompts for optimal downstream quality remains underexplored, as do the capabilities and limitations of this paradigm. Through controlled experiments and blinded human evaluation, we study proxy granularity, physics simulation, and prompting. The results reveal task-dependent trade-offs: coarse proxies favor natural motion, while medium-grained proxies favor consistency. Physics simulation and proxy-aligned prompting further improve average quality. Guided by these findings, we introduce Coda-DaVinci, a task-adaptive agentic framework for Code2Video generation that selects between proxy-guided and direct generation, adapts modeling granularity to each entity, and coordinates simulation with proxy-grounded prompting. Its multi-agent harness preserves scene assets and guides repairs through visual inspection and independent review. Experiments demonstrate leading instruction following on VTR and scene consistency on R2M, improved physical plausibility over direct generation, and higher visual realism than the compared Code2Video baselines. Long-video evaluations further show gains in event structure and consistency across successive interactions, while revealing limitations in transition stability and adherence to scene settings.

</details>


### [155] [AdaPixFlow: Learning Continuous-Time 3D Dynamics Across Scenes from Sparse Multi-view Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26433)
*Hrishikesh Viswanath,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应像素流(Adaptive Pixel Flows)，从多视角RGB-D观测学习跨场景的连续时间动力学算子，输出3D速度场并积分预测物体在观测间及观测外的位置，无需逐场景优化或预定义运动模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景中的物体3D运动恢复与未观测时刻位置预测是核心问题。现有方法或是针对单一场景优化速度场(不可跨场景迁移)，或是施加预设的物理/运动学假设(泛化受限)，缺少能跨场景泛化的学习式动力学算子。

Method: 将所有相机的观测融合到世界坐标下的共享体素网格，由网络从该网格预测连续时间速度场；算子在整个场景集合上训练以获得可迁移的运动先验，并可从观测状态出发对该场积分，实现观测间与观测序列之外的位置预测。方法无需逐场景优化，也不依赖预设运动模型。

Result: 在留出的PhysInOne场景上，F1较最强对比方法在插值预测上提升2.5%、未来预测提升15%；相较逐场景速度场方法分别提升30%和85%。零样本迁移到Spring-Gaus时，未来预测F1提升12%。

Conclusion: 跨场景学习的连续时间动力学算子可有效替代逐场景优化与人工运动先验，具备良好的泛化与零样本迁移能力，为多视角动态重建中的运动预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Understanding how objects move in dynamic scenes requires recovering their 3D motion from visual observations and predicting their positions at unobserved times. Existing methods either optimize scene-specific velocity fields or impose predefined physical and kinematic assumptions. We instead study learning a continuous-time dynamics operator across scenes, mapping multi-view visual observations to 3D object kinematics. We introduce Adaptive Pixel Flows (\ours{}), which learns this operator from multi-view RGB-D observations without per-scene optimization or predefined motion models. The method fuses observations from all cameras into a shared voxel grid in world coordinates, from which the network predicts a continuous-time velocity field. Because the operator is trained across scenes, it learns motion priors that carry over to unseen scenes. The field can then be integrated from observed states to predict object positions between observations and beyond the observed sequence. On held-out PhysInOne scenes, \ours{} improves F1 over the strongest competing method by 2.5\% for in-between prediction and 15\% for future prediction. Compared with per-scene velocity-field methods, the improvements are 30\% and 85\%, respectively. Applied zero-shot to Spring-Gaus, it improves future-prediction F1 by 12\%.

</details>


### [156] [Better Retrieval, Not Better Binding: Pretraining Exposure in Egocentric Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26120)
*Yuqi Guo,Michael Wray*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ego-ExBind诊断协议，区分自中心视频文本检索中"预训练曝光驱动的检索收益"与"动宾组合理解"，并证明曝光重加权可提升检索但未增强动宾绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型在零样本检索上表现良好，但在组合理解上存在局限，尚不清楚其是否真正理解动词与名词之间的组合关系；同时检索收益可能仅源于预训练语料的曝光/熟悉度而非组合理解。

Method: 构建Ego-ExBind协议：基于EgoVLPv2所用EgoClip语料建立曝光台账（Exposure Ledger），在EPIC-KITCHENS-100多实例检索（MIR）上划分Seen Composition、Unseen Composition、Unseen Atom三种划分；通过受控的名词替换与动词替换负样本探测名词与动词绑定通道；在控制动名词边际频率后分析成对曝光与检索的关系；并在固定训练检查点、仅替换推理阶段曝光输入的条件下测试曝光重加权。

Result: 零样本检索与seen组合内部成对曝光正相关（控制边际频率后仍成立）；更高的成对曝光对应更大的名词替换margin、但动词替换margin略小，呈现不对称性；曝光重加权可提升检索性能，而swap margin几乎不变，且收益依赖于正确的样本级曝光对应关系。

Conclusion: 曝光重加权能在不增强动宾组合理解的情况下提升自中心视频文本检索，说明检索提升可源于预训练熟悉度而非组合理解；Ego-ExBind为评估未来自中心MIR改进是否反映组合理解提供了诊断框架。

Abstract: Video-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on video-text retrieval and are increasingly used for egocentric video understanding. Yet, despite gains in zero-shot retrieval, they have shown limitations in compositional understanding, and it remains unclear whether they understand compositional relationships between verbs and nouns. In this work, we introduce Ego-ExBind, a compositional diagnostic protocol for analyzing pretraining exposure and verb-noun binding in egocentric video-text retrieval. Ego-ExBind consists of an Exposure Ledger built from the EgoClip pretraining corpus used by EgoVLPv2, which defines Seen Composition, Unseen Composition, and Unseen Atom splits for EPIC-KITCHENS-100 multi-instance retrieval (MIR). We further probe noun and verb binding channels through controlled noun-swap and verb-swap negatives. We find that zero-shot retrieval is positively associated with pair exposure within seen compositions, even after controlling for the marginal frequencies of verbs and nouns. However, higher pair exposure is associated with larger noun-swap margins and slightly smaller verb-swap margins. This asymmetry motivates us to test whether exposure reweighting can improve retrieval with little change in the measured swap margins. To isolate the effect of exposure input, we keep the trained checkpoint fixed and replace only the exposure input at inference. We demonstrate that this exposure reweighting can improve video-text retrieval without stronger verb-noun compositional understanding, and that the gain depends on the correct sample-level exposure correspondence. By separating exposure-driven retrieval gains from verb-noun binding, Ego-ExBind provides a diagnostic framework for evaluating whether future improvements in egocentric MIR reflect compositional understanding rather than pretraining familiarity.

</details>


### [157] [HiUni: Harmonizing Understanding and Generation in Shared Visual Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26107)
*Jiahao Hu,Jingdong Du,Yichen Zhang,Yi Song,Zhenyu Zhang,Jiaxin Niu,Ruihang Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Yiqian Zhang,Yong Huang,Zefeng Zhang,Zheyuan Gu,Shuohuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HiUni，通过让理解与生成共享同一个语言对齐ViT和稠密Transformer主干、仅在输出端保留任务特定计算，并以Pixel Diffusion Head与轻量HiGate解决像素空间优化与细粒度空间信息缺失问题，实现高度统一的视觉理解与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态统一模型为适配理解与生成两类任务的差异需求，往往分离视觉表示或主干计算，限制了统一程度；本文探究预训练视觉语言模型能否以大量参数共享的方式直接扩展为统一模型。

Method: HiUni对理解与生成复用同一语言对齐ViT与稠密Transformer主干，任务特定计算仅限输出侧。针对像素空间生成难优化、ViT语义抽象丢失合成所需细粒度空间细节两大挑战，引入面向直接像素空间优化的Pixel Diffusion Head，以及输出阶段附近提供细粒度空间细节的轻量HiGate通路。

Result: HiUni平均理解得分与预训练VLM相近，同时在图像生成与编辑上取得有竞争力表现；对照比较与分析表明单一稠密主干无需任务特定主干计算即可有效支撑两类能力。

Conclusion: 统一多模态建模不必依赖分离的表示或主干计算，共享稠密主干加输出端轻量任务适配是更简洁的统一路径。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models aim to support visual understanding and generation within a single framework. However, prevailing approaches often separate their visual representations or backbone computation to accommodate the different requirements of these two tasks, which limits the degree of unification. In this paper, we present HiUni, which revisits this design choice and explores whether a pretrained vision language model can be directly extended into a unified model with extensive parameter sharing. Specifically, HiUni reuses the same language-aligned ViT and dense Transformer backbone for both capabilities, while confining task-specific computation to the output side. Yet such a design introduces two key challenges. Generation in pixel space is difficult to optimize, and semantic abstraction in pretrained ViT discards fine spatial details required for synthesis. To address these challenges, HiUni introduces a \emph{Pixel Diffusion Head} tailored to direct pixel-space optimization and a lightweight \emph{HiGate} pathway that provides fine-grained spatial details near the output stage. Extensive experiments show that HiUni achieves a similar average understanding score to the pretrained VLM while attaining competitive performance on image generation and editing. Controlled comparisons and analyses further demonstrate that a single dense backbone can effectively support both capabilities without task-specific backbone computation, highlighting a simpler path toward unified multimodal modeling.

</details>


### [158] [Frequency-Synchronized Boundary Coupling for Training-Free Multi-Prompt Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26098)
*Yipan Xu,Shuyong Gao,Jiyuan Fu,Keliang Yin,Jiayu Chen,Qianyu Guo,Yang Liu,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreqSync：一种免训练框架，将多提示长视频生成中的提示切换建模为“频率选择性边界耦合”，通过FS-SCA与RCSS在频域上协调相邻片段，实现平滑过渡与提示对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与Transformer视频架构推动了文生视频进展，但从演化文本生成长而连贯的视频仍困难：从零训练长视频模型需大量算力与大规模多事件数据；免训练多提示生成又需在不破坏时间连续性的前提下激活新提示语义。作者指出已有过渡策略存在“频率无关的边界耦合问题”——耦合过弱导致突变、闪烁、背景跳变，耦合过强或过粗则过度保留源语义，在提示边界处产生鬼影或主体重复。

Method: 提出免训练框架FreqSync，把提示过渡表述为频率选择性边界耦合，包含两个组件：频率同步的源条件注意力（FS-SCA）与残差裁剪的频谱拼接（RCSS）。前者使相邻片段共享稳定的低、中频结构与运动线索；后者在显式残差约束下让高频、提示特定的细节由目标片段主导。

Result: 大量实验表明，FreqSync在过渡平滑度与提示对齐上达到state-of-the-art，同时保持较强的感知视频质量，验证其对连贯多提示长视频生成的有效性。

Conclusion: 以频率选择性的方式处理边界耦合，可在不额外训练的情况下兼顾时间连续性与新提示语义注入，为多提示长视频生成提供有效方案。

Abstract: Text-to-video generation has made remarkable progress with diffusion models and transformer-based video architectures, yet generating coherent long videos from evolving textual descriptions remains challenging. Training long-video models from scratch requires substantial computation and large-scale multi-event data, while training-free multi-prompt generation must activate new prompt semantics without breaking temporal continuity. We identify a frequency-agnostic boundary coupling problem in existing transition strategies: weak coupling causes abrupt changes, flicker, and background jumps, whereas overly strong or coarse coupling may over-preserve source semantics and produce ghosting or duplicated subjects around prompt boundaries. To address this problem, we propose FreqSync, a training-free framework that formulates prompt transitions as frequency-selective boundary coupling. FreqSync combines Frequency-Synchronized Source-Conditioned Attention (FS-SCA) with Residual-Clipped Spectral Stitching (RCSS), enabling adjacent segments to share stable low- and mid-frequency structure and motion cues while keeping high-frequency prompt-specific details target-dominant under explicit residual constraints. Extensive experiments show that FreqSync achieves state-of-the-art transition smoothness and prompt alignment while maintaining strong perceptual video quality, demonstrating its effectiveness for coherent multi-prompt long video generation.

</details>


### [159] [Learning to Think with Images Faithfully via Adaptive Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26199)
*Yu Zhao,Jiaxin Zhang,Rohit Saxena,Fazl Barez,Pasquale Minervini,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出"Thinking with Images"模型虽能提升精度但存在工具使用不忠实问题（调用裁剪工具却不依赖放大证据），将其归因于训练数据，提出基于模型能力演化的数据选择与合成管线，并辅以自适应性奖励，在提升忠实性的同时达到视觉搜索基准SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TwI模型（主动裁剪、放大细粒度区域）虽带来更高精度与可解释性，但分析发现精度提升并不保证忠实的工具使用：模型常调用工具却实际不依赖放大区域，削弱了工具辅助推理的可靠性。作者将这种不忠实行为追溯到训练数据——当问题无需放大即可作答时，模型会忽略返回证据而答对，且随训练进行模型越擅长无工具作答，该问题被进一步放大。

Method: 1) 随模型能力演化调整训练数据，仅在"正确的放大证据仍能帮助模型答对"的问题上训练，以保持缩放全程有用；2) 数据合成管线：定位小物体并围绕其细粒度细节构建可验证问题；3) 自适应性奖励：在缩放不必要时倾向无工具作答。整个过程不直接监督推理过程。

Result: 推理忠实性显著提升；视觉搜索基准达SOTA：V* +11.6%，HR-Bench 4K +7.9%，HR-Bench 8K +9.4%；在多模态幻觉基准上亦优于对比的TwI基线。

Conclusion: 提升TwI忠实性并不以牺牲精度为代价；二者均可从正确的训练数据中习得，无需直接监督推理过程。

Abstract: Thinking with images (TwI) empowers vision-language models (VLMs) to overcome perceptual bottlenecks by actively cropping and zooming into fine-grained visual details, promising both higher accuracy and interpretable, region-grounded reasoning. However, our analysis shows that the improved accuracy of TwI models does not guarantee faithful tool use; they frequently invoke tools without actually relying on the zoomed region, undermining the reliability of tool-assisted reasoning. We trace this unfaithful behaviour to the training data: when questions are answerable without zooming, the model can answer correctly while ignoring the returned evidence, and this problem is exacerbated during training as the model becomes better at answering without tools. To keep zooming useful throughout training, we adapt the training data to the model's evolving capability and train on questions where correct zoomed evidence can still help the model answer correctly. This selection is supported by a data synthesis pipeline that localises small objects and constructs verifiable questions about their fine details, and complemented by an adaptiveness reward that favours tool-free answers when zooming is unnecessary. The experimental results show that our method substantially improves reasoning faithfulness and achieves state-of-the-art performance on visual search benchmarks (+11.6% on V*, +7.9% on HR-Bench 4K, and +9.4% on HR-Bench 8K). On multimodal hallucination benchmarks, our model also outperforms the compared TwI baselines. These results suggest that improving TwI faithfulness does not sacrifice accuracy and that both can be learnt from the right training data without directly supervising the reasoning process.

</details>


### [160] [Posterior Matching Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26629)
*Hidir Yesiltepe,Julius Berner,Arash Vahdat,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Posterior Matching Distillation (PMD)，一种基于分布的扩散蒸馏方法，通过匹配指数重加权后验均值来监督学生模型的整个条件后验，实现零种群损失下恢复教师完整后验，并支持训练时间网格内任意步数采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分布的扩散蒸馏依赖 reverse-KL 目标，其 mode-seeking 偏差会偏向教师的主导模态，牺牲样本多样性，导致学生输出分布与教师分布匹配不完整。

Method: 将 mode-covering 的后验采样与任意步生相结合：在固定噪声状态条件下，匹配学生样本的指数重加权均值与对应教师后验均值；在高斯扰动下，该指数倾斜后验均值可由一次偏移输入上的教师评估得到；证明在开邻域内匹配倾斜均值可唯一确定完整后验，因此仅用一族均值约束即可监督整个条件分布，无需辅助学生 score 网络。

Result: 在 SDXL 与 FLUX.2 [klein] 4B 上，PMD 在两种模型的 4 步方法中取得最佳 FID，同时在提示对齐与感知质量指标上保持有竞争力或领先的表现。

Conclusion: PMD 通过指数倾斜均值匹配构造了一族可唯一刻画后验的约束，兼具模式覆盖性与任意步采样灵活性，在少步扩散蒸馏中实现更优的分布匹配与生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion distillation accelerates image and video generation by compressing a pretrained teacher's iterative sampling process into a few network evaluations. Distribution-based methods achieve this by matching the student's output distribution to the teacher's. This matching is often driven by reverse-KL objectives, whose mode-seeking bias favors dominant teacher modes at the expense of diversity across samples. We introduce Posterior Matching Distillation (PMD), a new member of the distribution-based distillation family that combines mode-covering posterior sampling with any-step generation. At zero population loss, PMD recovers the teacher's full posterior of clean images at each trained noise level. Its stochastic student supports any-step sampling budgets within the trained timestep grid without further training. To learn these posteriors, PMD matches exponentially reweighted averages of student samples conditioned on a fixed noisy state to corresponding teacher posterior means. Under Gaussian corruption, exponentially tilted posterior mean is a single teacher evaluation at a shifted input. We prove that, matching tilted means over an open neighborhood uniquely identifies the full posterior. PMD thus supervises the entire conditional distribution through a family of mean constraints, without any auxiliary student-score network. Experiments on SDXL and FLUX.2 [klein] 4B demonstrate that PMD achieves the best FID among 4-step methods on both models, while maintaining competitive or leading performance across prompt-alignment and perceptual-quality metrics.

</details>


### [161] [AURA: AUdio-dRiven streaming Avatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26540)
*Nathaniel Cohen,Nicolas Dufour,Amélie Royer,Alasdair Newson,Patrick Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AURA 提出一种流式音频驱动说话头视频扩散模型，通过滚动KV缓存逐块因果生成，并用两阶段蒸馏（轨迹对齐+带反向模拟的DMD）将双向教师模型压缩为少步因果学生模型，实现单卡实时生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动视频扩散模型需对多秒视频块进行数十步去噪，且依赖双向模型的多秒音频前瞻，延迟高、无法在线因果生成，不适配语音助手、虚拟形象等流式应用。

Method: 采用滚动KV缓存的逐块流式生成，仅需当前块音频即可开始生成；构建1.3B与5B两种规模。两阶段蒸馏：第一阶段以块级滑动窗口因果注意力掩码、更少扩散步数训练因果学生模型匹配教师轨迹；第二阶段施加带反向模拟的分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）以缩小剩余质量差距。

Result: AURA 1.3B在单GPU上实时输出576p（16Hz播放率下20.7 fps），5B版本720p达9.7 fps；1.3B比其双向教师快23倍，低分辨率下质量与TalkVerse相当；5B在720p匹配TalkVerse教师且快17倍。

Conclusion: 验证了蒸馏式流式因果架构可在保持教师级质量的同时实现数量级加速与低延迟在线生成；开源两模型权重、30K合成视频及预计算教师轨迹以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Current audio-driven video diffusion models can synthesize realistic talking-head videos but generally require dozens of diffusion denoising steps over multi-second video chunks, making them unsuitable for streaming applications such as voice assistants and virtual avatars, where low-latency and online causal generation are required properties. AURA generates video chunk by chunk with a rolling KV cache, requiring only the audio of the current chunk to start generating, rather than a multi-second audio look-ahead like bidirectional models. It comes in two sizes: AURA 1.3B streams 576p video in real time on a single GPU (20.7 fps for a 16 Hz playback rate), making it suited to interactive avatar applications, and AURA 5B streams 720p video at 9.7 fps. Our two-stage distillation pipeline first aligns the causal student with the teacher, by training it to match the teacher's trajectories with fewer diffusion steps and a chunk-wise sliding window causal attention mask, then applies Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) with backward simulation to close the remaining quality gap. On standard talking-head benchmarks, AURA 1.3B runs 23x faster than its own bidirectional teacher and reaches a quality comparable to TalkVerse at a lower resolution, and AURA 5B matches its TalkVerse teacher at 720p while running 17x faster. To support reproducibility and further research on streaming avatar generation, we release the weights of both models along with a dataset of 30K synthetic videos and their precomputed teacher trajectories.

</details>


### [162] [Plan4D: Generative Plannable 4D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26516)
*Hangwei Zhang,Rujing Dang,Ruimin Lin,Jiahong Wu,Xiangxiang Chu,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: plan4d是一个可规划的生成式4D世界模型，能从观测场景与文本动作预测未来RGB视频及稠密4D几何（深度、相机、点云、场景流），通过模型拼接架构取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型偏重生成视频的美学质量，缺乏显式4D建模，物理基础薄弱且时间几何一致性有限；同时带配对动作与稠密4D标注的训练数据稀缺。

Method: 提出模型拼接（model-stitching）架构，将Depth Anything 3与LongCat-Video耦合，并附加辅助DPT头预测场景流，从而利用视频生成器与3D重建器的互补先验，无需大量成对4D标注。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的大量实验表明，plan4d在环境反应的生成式4D建模上达到SOTA，同时显著提升生成未来视频的视觉保真度与几何精度。

Conclusion: plan4d为构建可规划的4D世界模型提供了可行方向，支持在动态3D场景中进行长时程、细粒度动作的规划与生成；项目主页为 https://fogradio.github.io/plan4d-project。

Abstract: Most world models for future prediction prioritize aestetic quality of generated videos, but offer only weak physical grounding and limited geometric consistency over time, due to the lack of explicit 4D modeling. In this paper, we introduce plan4d, a possible next milestone towards building a generative plannable 4D world model that predicts future RGB videos together with dense 4D geometry, including depth, cameras, point clouds, and scene flow from an observed scene context and text-specified actions. Since training data with paired actions and dense 4D annotations is inherently scarce, plan4d leverages the complementary priors of foundational video generators and 3D reconstructors. Specifically, we propose a model-stitching architecture that couples Depth Anything 3 with LongCat-Video and augments it with an auxiliary DPT head for scene-flow prediction. The resulting model allows us to plan and generate complex, long-horizon, and fine-grained actions in a dynamic 3D scenes, while substantially improving both visual fidelity and geometric accuracy of the generated future videos. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that plan4d achieves state-of-the-art performance in generative 4D modeling of environmental reactions. Project Page: https://fogradio.github.io/plan4d-project

</details>


### [163] [Direct3D-V3: Latent-Free Diffusion Transformers in the Native Voxel Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26511)
*Shuang Wu,Jiachen Qian,Youtian Lin,Feihu Zhang,Philip Torr,Yao Yao,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Direct3D-V3 提出无潜空间（latent-free）的扩散Transformer框架，直接在原生SDF体素域上训练与采样3D生成，无需VAE，并借助隐式流形正则、Marching Cubes法向监督与相机参数辅助预测提升几何质量与图文对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原生3D生成方法（vecset、triplane、结构化稀疏体素等）虽架构各异，但共享同一前提：先将几何压缩到潜空间再由生成器建模。这种VAE重建瓶颈限制了局部细节的表达，且训练目标并非原生几何域。

Method: 1) 直接对原始SDF体素网格做扩散建模，用DiT在原生体素域训练与采样，完全舍弃VAE；2) 引入基于相邻体素的隐式流形正则，抑制非水密表面；3) 将预测SDF经Marching Cubes转为网格，用渲染法向损失直接监督预测SDF；4) 以辅助任务预测条件图像的相机参数，强化图像观测到3D形状的映射学习；5) 支持生成式重建：扩散与法向引导细化交替进行。

Result: 该方法消除了重建瓶颈，训练目标位于原生体素域，生成的网格具有显著更丰富的局部细节；同时可扩展至生成式重建，在保持与观测视角对齐的同时由生成先验补全未观测区域。

Conclusion: 几何可直接在原生SDF体素域由生成器建模，无需潜空间压缩；隐式流形正则与法向监督使直接体素表述在几何上可靠，原生SDF输出与相机预测也使框架易于扩展到生成式重建任务。

Abstract: Native 3D generation has largely been built around latent diffusion, with recent advances concentrating on stronger variational autoencoders (VAE) and increasingly elaborate latent representations, from vecset and triplanes to structured sparse voxels. Despite their architectural differences, these methods all share the same premise: geometry is first compressed into a latent space and only then modeled by the generator. We take a different route and propose Direct3D-V3, a latent-free diffusion transformer (DiT) framework that discards the VAE altogether and operates in the native voxel domain, directly modeling raw SDF voxel grids during both training and sampling. To make this direct voxel formulation geometrically reliable, we introduce an implicit manifold regularization over neighboring voxels to suppress non-watertight surfaces, and convert predicted SDF grids into meshes via Marching Cubes so that rendered normal losses can supervise the model directly from its predicted SDF. To improve alignment between the conditioning image and generated geometry, Direct3D-V3 also predicts the camera parameters of the conditioning image as an auxiliary task, encouraging the DiT to learn the mapping from image observations to 3D shape. This latent-free design removes the reconstruction bottleneck, brings training under an objective in the native voxel domain, and yields meshes with substantially richer local detail. The predicted camera and native SDF voxel output further make Direct3D-V3 extensible to generative reconstruction, where diffusion is interleaved with normal-guided refinement to improve alignment with the observed view while the generative prior completes unseen regions. Project page: https://dreamtechai.github.io/Direct3D-V3.

</details>


### [164] [Fixing the Last Mile: Failure-Aware Image Restoration with Region-Specific Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26456)
*Hongyu An,Fanghua Yu,Kainan Yan,Luc Van Gool,Chao Dong,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Failure-Aware Image Restoration (FAIR)，通过在单次前向的复原模型中对"何处易出现局部失败"与"该区域需要何种纠正引导"进行区域约束条件化，把研究重点从全局保真度转向局部感知可接受性，解决图像复原的"最后一公里"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复原方法已显著提升全局图像保真度，但高质量结果仍存在局部失败：语义重要区域（人脸、文字）的伪影、结构不一致、纹理错配。这些错误虽只影响小部分区域，却主导整体感知质量，即全局指标与实用可用性之间存在落差。

Method: 不再对整幅图像施加统一的生成先验，而是以单次（single-pass）复原模型为基座，同时以两类信息作为条件：(1) 局部失败可能发生的位置；(2) 各区域所需的纠正引导。由此实现区域绑定的条件化（region-bound conditioning），获得细粒度、可控的复原能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法可化解常见失败案例，在关键区域提升语义准确性与风格一致性，同时在其他区域保持忠实的高质量复原，整体感知质量优于基线。

Conclusion: FAIR 与现有复原流程互补，将优化重心由全局保真度转向局部感知可接受性，弥合了技术精度与实际可用性之间的差距。

Abstract: Recent advances in image restoration have greatly improved global image fidelity, producing visually plausible results across most regions of an image. Nevertheless, even high-quality restorations often exhibit localized failures, including artifacts in semantically important regions such as faces and text, structural inconsistencies, and texture mismatches. Although these errors typically affect only a small portion of the image, they often dominate the overall perceptual quality of the result. In this work, we propose Failure-Aware Image Restoration (FAIR) to address the "last-mile" problem in image restoration. Rather than applying one uniform generative prior over the whole image, we condition a single-pass restoration model on both where local failures are likely to occur and what corrective guidance each region requires. This region-bound conditioning enables fine-grained and controllable restoration that improves semantic accuracy and stylistic consistency in critical regions while maintaining faithful, high-quality restoration elsewhere. Our method complements existing restoration pipelines by shifting the emphasis from global fidelity to local perceptual acceptability. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach resolves common failure cases and improves perceptual quality, bridging the gap between technical accuracy and practical usability.

</details>


### [165] [SEEC: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Self-Supervised External-Eye Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26386)
*George R. Nahass,Michelle Fleishaker,Sanjay Ganesh,Sasha Hubschman,R. V. Paul Chan,Chad A. Purnell,Pete Setabutr,Ann Q. Tran,Sathya N. Ravi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建435万张图像的外部眼标准化语料库SEEC及表示学习基准，从表示几何与功能两个层面评估自监督编码器，发现两者可分离且无普适最优编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部眼摄影以低成本捕获具有临床意义的眶周解剖信息，但缺乏大规模标准化资源用于评估自监督外部眼表示。

Method: 从14个异构公开人脸数据集构建435万张图像的SEEC语料库；提供留出评估分布、关键点标注解剖探针集及涉及上睑下垂、甲状腺眼病、颅面综合征的疾病分类实验；采用ResNet-101与ViT-B编码器，以InfoNCE、VICReg、LeJEPA目标预训练，并与DINOv2、MAE对比；在表示层面（多分布嵌入几何）与功能层面（解剖恢复、OOD解剖迁移、疾病分类）双层级评估。

Result: 表示层面与功能层面评估可分离：更充分的嵌入空间利用并不一致地对应解剖迁移或疾病分类性能；无单一编码器普适最优；领域特定外部眼编码器在跨数据集解剖迁移上优于DINOv2与MAE（尽管预训练规模显著更小）；外部眼预训练的ViT在冻结疾病分类探针上匹配或超越通用基线。

Conclusion: 外部眼领域自监督预训练具有价值，表示几何指标不能替代功能评估；发布基准代码、评估脚本与预处理流程以复现SEEC。

Abstract: External-eye photography captures clinically relevant periocular anatomy at low acquisition cost, yet no large-scale standardized resource exists for evaluating self-supervised external-eye representations. We introduce a 4.35-million-image Standardized External-Eye Corpus (SEEC) derived from fourteen heterogeneous public face datasets, together with a benchmark for external-eye representation learning. For evaluation, we provide held-out evaluation distributions, landmark-annotated anatomical probe sets, and clinical disease-classification experiments spanning ptosis, thyroid eye disease, and craniofacial syndromes. We evaluate representations at two complementary levels: (a) the representation level, by assessing embedding geometry across multiple data distributions, and (b) the functional level, by assessing anatomical recovery, out-of-distribution anatomical transfer, and disease classification. Our models include ResNet-101 and ViT-B encoders pretrained using InfoNCE, VICReg, and LeJEPA objectives and compared with DINOv2 and MAE. Our results indicate that representation-level and functional-level evaluations are dissociable: broader embedding-space utilization does not consistently correspond to anatomical transfer or disease-classification performance, and no single encoder is universally optimal. Domain-specific external-eye encoders outperform DINOv2 and MAE on cross-dataset anatomical transfer despite being pretrained at substantially smaller scale, while external-eye-pretrained ViT models match or outperform these general-purpose baselines on frozen disease-classification probes. We release benchmark code, evaluation scripts, and preprocessing pipelines to reproduce SEEC at https://monkeygobah.github.io/seec-project/

</details>


### [166] [PaveSplat: Resolution-Robust Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting with Residually Corrected Surface-Aligned Covariances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26045)
*Janghun Kim,Hyun-Cheol Kim,Eunbin An,Myeonghun Oh,Hyon-Gon Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaveSplat 提出一种前馈高斯泼溅方法，在解析的、与表面对齐的协方差基础上学习一个恒等初始化的四元数残差，使每个像素对应的 surfel 在高分辨率重渲染时仍能贴合可见表面，减少空洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素对齐的前馈高斯泼溅每像素预测一个图元，渲染质量取决于这些图元的表面覆盖程度。近期基于 surfel 的方法用预测深度解析计算与表面对齐的协方差，但当深度导出的局部坐标系病态时，该协方差无法校正 surfel 朝向，导致 surfel 松散覆盖表面：训练分辨率下单像素最小足迹可掩盖缝隙，同一预测在更高分辨率重渲染时则出现空洞。

Method: 保留解析协方差作为初始化（其轴对齐于表面切向），并附加一个恒等初始化的单位四元数残差来重定向 surfel；该残差仅在能改善渲染时才重新调整朝向，从而把被解析坐标系错误指向的 surfel 重新转回表面。整个方法为前馈、单次前向、每像素一个高斯。

Result: 在 RealEstate10K 上，PaveSplat 在 1x–8x 各尺度下于单高斯每像素方法中取得最佳 PSNR、SSIM 和 LPIPS；原生分辨率收益较小，优势主要体现于不重跑网络、直接在高显示分辨率重渲染时。零样本 DL3DV 上，该方法在该组内各尺度 PSNR 领先，SSIM/LPIPS 排名第一或第二；覆盖率空洞分析显示高分辨率重渲染下未覆盖像素少于两个基线。

Conclusion: 以解析表面协方差为初始化、辅以轻量学习的朝向残差，是提升前馈逐像素高斯泼溅跨分辨率表面覆盖的有效途径，主要收益体现在高分辨率重渲染的鲁棒性上。

Abstract: We present PaveSplat, a feed-forward Gaussian splatting method that adds a learned residual to the analytic surface-aligned covariance, keeping surfels paving the visible surface across rendering resolutions. Pixel-aligned feed-forward Gaussian splatting predicts one primitive per pixel from a few images in a single pass, and its rendering quality depends on how well these primitives cover the surface. Recent surfel-based methods compute the surface-aligned covariance analytically from predicted depth. However, this covariance has no way to correct the surfel orientation where the depth-derived frame is ill-posed. The surfels then cover the surface loosely; at the training resolution each surfel's one-pixel minimum footprint covers the gaps, but they show up as holes when the same prediction is re-rendered at a higher resolution. To address this, we keep the analytic covariance as an initialization, with its axes aligned to the surface tangents, and add an identity-initialized unit-quaternion residual that reorients a surfel only where this improves rendering, turning surfels that the analytic frame mis-aims back onto the surface. On RealEstate10K, PaveSplat achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS among single-Gaussian-per-pixel methods at every scale (1x-8x). The native-resolution gain is small; the benefit appears when the same prediction is rendered at higher display resolutions without re-running the network. On zero-shot DL3DV, it leads PSNR within this group at every scale and ranks first or second on SSIM/LPIPS. A coverage-hole analysis shows fewer uncovered pixels than both baselines under high-resolution re-rendering.

</details>


### [167] [Multi-View Transformer with Geometry-Guided Multi-Modal ControlNet for 3D Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25979)
*Sarthak Batra,Armin Mustafa,Adrian Hilton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段框架 GS-VTON-MVT，将多视角 Transformer 修复、几何引导 ControlNet 与姿态自适应注意力结合，实现一致的 3DGS 虚拟试穿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 虚拟试穿流程对各训练视角近似独立编辑，再用人工设定的参考视角与固定注意力混合权重的启发式方法融合，导致跨视角不一致被固化进 3D 表示，出现纹理漂移、印花模糊与新视角几何不合理等问题。

Method: 三阶段框架：(i) 多视角修复 Transformer 通过完整跨视角自注意力联合编辑训练视角，使跨视角对应关系被学习而非强加；(ii) 几何引导多模态 ControlNet，以服装图像加上由当前 3DGS 状态渲染的深度与表面法线作为条件，在视觉证据薄弱处提供显式结构；(iii) 姿态自适应人物感知注意力，用基于相机位姿邻近度的权重替代当前视角与参考视角特征间的固定混合权重。

Result: 在 3D-VTONBench 基准上，相比通用 3D 编辑器与既有 3D 试穿方法，在 FID、KID、基于 LPIPS 的跨视角一致性与用户偏好上均取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 以隐式（跨视角注意力）与显式（几何条件）3D 推理相结合的方式，可有效缓解 3DGS 试穿中的跨视角不一致，提升新视角渲染的纹理与几何质量。

Abstract: Editing a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) capture of a person so that they wear a new garment requires every rendered view to agree on the same garment details. Existing 3D virtual try-on (VTON) pipelines, however, edit training views largely independently and fuse them with hand-designed heuristics such as manually chosen reference views and fixed attention-blending weights. The resulting inconsistencies are baked into the 3D representation and surface as texture drift, blurred prints, and implausible geometry in novel views. We present GS-VTON-MVT, a three-stage framework that combines implicit and explicit 3D reasoning for consistent 3DGS try-on. (i) A multi-view inpainting transformer jointly edits a set of training views with full cross-view self-attention, so that cross-view correspondence is learned rather than imposed. (ii) A geometry-guided multi-modal ControlNet conditions the diffusion editor on the garment image together with depth and surface normals rendered from the current 3DGS state, supplying explicit structure where visual evidence is weakest. (iii) A pose-adaptive persona-aware attention replaces the fixed blending weight between current-view and reference-view features with weights derived from camera-pose proximity. On the 3D-VTONBench benchmark, GS-VTON-MVT improves FID, KID, LPIPS-based cross-view consistency, and user preference over general-purpose 3D editors and prior 3D try-on methods.

</details>


### [168] [AGSR: Aperture-Guided Super-Resolution for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25934)
*Ayman Alkhateeb,Hadi Amirpour,Christian Timmerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出推理期插件式框架 AGSR，通过收缩 3DGS 投影协方差实现多孔径渲染并融合，用于提升新视角合成分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 虽支持实时新视角合成，但其高斯光栅化基于每个基元的投影协方差，会削弱细尺度场景结构（有限高斯足迹导致的有效带宽受限）。

Method: 在推理时收缩投影协方差，构建多孔径渲染框架；用不确定性引导的特征聚合，并结合透射率与深度线索进行几何条件化，逐步融合多孔径渲染结果；作为预训练 3DGS 模型的即插即用增强模块。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360 基准上优于轻量级图像空间超分基线，且当底层高斯表征质量越差时增益越大；参数量仅约 139k 且保持实时性能。

Conclusion: 协方差收缩可显露出被原生高斯光栅化衰减的带宽敏感结构线索，验证了多孔径超分思路的有效性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis, but Gaussian rasterization attenuates fine-scale scene structure through the projected covariance of each primitive. We show that contracting these projected covariances at inference time can mitigate the effective bandwidth limitation induced by finite Gaussian footprints, motivating a multi-aperture rendering framework for super-resolution.
  Based on this insight, we propose AGSR (Aperture-Guided Super-Resolution), a lightweight inference-time framework that progressively fuses multi-aperture renderings using uncertainty-guided feature aggregation and geometric conditioning from transmittance and depth cues. AGSR operates as a plug-in enhancement for pre-trained 3DGS models.
  Experiments on the Mip-NeRF 360 benchmark show that AGSR outperforms lightweight image-space super-resolution baselines, with gains increasing as the underlying Gaussian representation deteriorates. The results provide evidence that covariance contraction exposes bandwidth-sensitive structural cues attenuated by native Gaussian rasterization. AGSR achieves these improvements while maintaining real-time performance with up to 139k parameters.

</details>


### [169] [SpatialMem: Building Quantitative Spatial Reasoning Agents with Hierarchical Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25925)
*Zinan Zheng,Nuo Chen,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpatialMem 记忆框架，让 VLM agent 通过积累工具使用经验来编排视觉专家完成定量空间推理，并构建新基准 MetricBench，在三项基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在定量空间推理上能力有限，现有方法依赖调用外部视觉工具，但每个功能需求绑定单一预定义视觉专家，导致工具流水线固定僵化；而提升流水线编排与专家选择的多样性虽能增强鲁棒性，却带来组合爆炸的执行空间，使 agent 难以选出最优编排。

Method: SpatialMem：面向 VLM agent 的两级记忆结构——任务记忆（task memory）捕获工具编排，场景记忆（scene memory）捕获工具选择；并引入双状态轨迹优化（dual-state trajectory optimization）保留成功工具经验、精炼次优经验。同时构建 MetricBench，覆盖室内外场景与三类定量空间推理任务。

Result: 在 MetricBench、Q-Spatial、SpatialRGPT-Bench 上达到 SOTA；MetricBench 上较最佳 VLM 基线提升 32%；记忆设计相较无记忆 agent 在 MetricBench 上带来 9.1% 绝对提升。

Conclusion: 通过记忆机制将工具使用经验结构化沉淀，可有效缓解组合爆炸下的流水线编排难题，显著提升 VLM 定量空间推理性能，并需配套更高质量的基准评估。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities in qualitative spatial reasoning, but remain limited in quantitative spatial reasoning. Current work leverages the VLM agent to invoke external vision tools to perform computation. However, these methods usually bind each functional requirement to a single predefined vision specialist, implicitly resulting in fixed and rigid tool pipelines. In practice, addressing diverse spatial scenarios and tasks demands a broader diversity in pipeline orchestration and vision specialist selection to achieve robust spatial reasoning. However, such diversity leads to a combinatorial execution space, making it challenging for the agent to orchestrate the optimal pipelines and specialists for different scenarios.
  In this work, we propose SpatialMem, a memory framework that enables a VLM agent to accumulate tool-use experience and leverage that memory to orchestrate vision specialists to solve the spatial reasoning problem. SpatialMem builds a two-level memory structure, with task memory capturing tool orchestration and scene memory capturing tool selection. It further introduces a dual-state trajectory optimization process to preserve successful tool experiences and refine suboptimal ones. Moreover, existing spatial reasoning benchmarks suffer from quality issues and limited coverage of scene types and tasks. To address these gap, we propose MetricBench, a benchmark covering both indoor and outdoor scenes and three quantitative spatial reasoning tasks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art results on MetricBench, Q-Spatial, and SpatilRGPT-Bench, outperforming best VLM baselines by 32% on MetricBench. Additionally, the memory design yields a 9.1% absolute gain over a memory-free agent on MetricBench.

</details>


### [170] [EvoMem: Self-Evolving Skills for Time-Aware Long Video Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25889)
*Yutong Guan,Meixing Shi,Haoran Sun,Bing Sun,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoMem：以自演化检索技能引导的时间感知长视频记忆智能体，通过分层时间索引记忆与强化学习式技能演化，改善长视频问答中的证据链连贯性与检索过程控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆增强架构的LVLM长视频检索仍依赖全局语义匹配，缺乏状态驱动的时间控制，导致返回证据碎片化或冗余，难以维持连贯的证据链；长视频问答中还存在检索目标不清、记忆访问路径不当、过早停止等反复出现的流程性错误。

Method: 1) 构建分层、时间索引的记忆结构，将不同类型证据对齐到统一视频时间线；2) 检索时把已获取证据中的时间线索转化为显式信号，约束后续记忆访问；3) 引入检索技能提供过程级指导，使智能体依据当前证据状态动态调整检索内容、记忆操作与目标时间范围，并决定停止时机；4) 借鉴强化学习，将技能演化建模为奖励引导的策略改进：每个候选技能视为外部策略补丁，按验证集上答案正确性的状态转移分配奖励，在不更新模型参数的前提下接受有效技能、拒绝低质量技能。

Result: 在四个长视频问答基准上显著优于现有基线，平均准确率达60.1%，较此前最佳方法平均提升8.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 时间索引记忆结合状态驱动的时间约束与可演化的检索技能，能有效提升长视频推理的证据连贯性与问答性能；且技能可作为外部策略补丁在冻结参数下持续演化，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Although memory-augmented architectures have expanded the ability of large vision-language models (LVLMs) to process long videos, existing retrieval processes still largely rely on global semantic matching and lack state-driven temporal control, often returning fragmented or redundant evidence and failing to maintain a coherent evidence chain. To address this challenge, we propose EvoMem, a time-aware long-video memory agent guided by self-evolving retrieval skills. EvoMem constructs a hierarchical time-indexed memory, where different types of evidence are aligned onto a unified video timeline; during retrieval, temporal cues from acquired evidence are further converted into explicit signals that constrain subsequent memory access. To address recurring procedural errors in long-video question answering, such as unclear retrieval targets, improper memory access paths, or premature stopping, EvoMem introduces retrieval skills to provide process-level guidance, enabling the agent to dynamically adjust retrieval content, memory operations, and target temporal ranges according to the current evidence state, while deciding when to stop. Furthermore, inspired by reinforcement learning, we formulate skill evolution as a reward-guided policy improvement process. Each candidate skill is treated as an external policy patch, and rewards are assigned according to answer-correctness state transitions on the validation set. In this way, effective skills are accepted and low-quality skills are rejected without updating model parameters. Experiments on four long-video question answering benchmarks show that EvoMem significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving an average accuracy of 60.1% and an average improvement of 8.6 percentage points over the previous best methods, demonstrating its effectiveness for long-video reasoning.

</details>


### [171] [WHU-Road3D: A dataset and benchmark towards fine-grained road surface mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25873)
*Xiaoxin Mi,Zhen Cao,Chong Liu,Yuhao Li,Zhen Dong,Bisheng Yang,Yang Jia,Shengwu Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出WHU-Road3D——约100公里、融合MLS点云与全景影像的多模态道路表面要素数据集，覆盖15类要素并含全景图像、轨迹与矢量化/实例级标注，支撑细粒度道路面提取与制图研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路表面要素的精确提取与制图对高精地图、道路资产管理和智能交通系统至关重要；但现有基准数据集在语义丰富度、数据模态多样性和场景复杂度三方面仍存在不足。

Method: 先系统综述现有道路要素提取与制图的基准数据集（俯视—透视、大范围—局部、粗—细粒度）及相关方法，指出数据缺口后构建WHU-Road3D数据集：约100公里中国高速公路与城市街道，融合车载移动激光扫描（MLS）点云、关联全景图像、车辆轨迹、详细矢量化标注与逐点实例标注，标注15类道路要素（路边界、斑马线、车道分界线、停止线等常见标线，以及10类方向符号），并配套基准方法与代码。

Result: 构建了首个面向如此广泛道路表面要素制图的多模态数据集WHU-Road3D，支持实例分割、全要素提取与道路面制图等基准研究，并可扩展用于分层道路拓扑推理、地图更新与街景生成。

Conclusion: WHU-Road3D通过多模态、细粒度、大规模标注弥补现有数据集在语义、模态与场景复杂度上的缺口，为道路表面要素提取与制图的前沿研究提供统一基准，数据集与基准代码将开源。

Abstract: Accurately extracting and mapping road surface elements is crucial for applications such as high-definition maps, road asset management, and intelligent transportation systems. This study first provides a comprehensive review of the benchmark datasets for extracting and mapping road elements, encompassing overhead-to-perspective data, large-to-local scales, and coarse-to-fine details. Subsequently, we thoroughly review the relevant approaches for extracting road surface elements. However, we argue that existing datasets still require enhancements in semantic richness, data modality diversity, and scenario complexity. To bridge these gaps, this paper presents WHU-Road3D, a large-scale multimodal dataset for fine-grained road surface mapping to address these shortcomings. WHU-Road3D provides approximately 100 km of diverse Chinese highways and urban streets. It features a rich combination of MLS point clouds, associated panoramic images, vehicle trajectories, detailed vectorized annotations, and pointwise instance annotations for road surface elements. The dataset carefully annotates 15 distinct categories of road elements, including common markings like road boundaries, zebra crossings, lane dividers, stop lines, as well as 10 types of direction symbols. To our knowledge, WHU-Road3D stands as the first multimodal dataset for mapping such a comprehensive range of road surface elements, designed to facilitate cutting-edge research in instance segmentation, full-element extraction and mapping on road surfaces. Beyond these immediate applications, WHU-Road3D also provides a valuable source for hierarchical road topology reasoning, map updating, and street scene generation. The dataset, along with the code for our benchmark methods, will be made publicly available at \href{https://github.com/WHU-USI3DV/WHU-Road3D.git}{https://github.com/WHU-USI3DV/WHU-Road3D.git}.

</details>


### [172] [Digitization of Analogue Gauges using Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26262)
*Erico Kayumba Mutonkole,Olasupo Ajayi,Omowunmi Isafiade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了基于计算机视觉与深度学习的指针式压力表自动读数系统，比较三种混合方案（Scikit-Image 传统视觉、ResNet18、VGG19），ResNet18 以 97.3% 准确率显著领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业中模拟指针仪表仍广泛使用，但人工读数易产生人为误差且效率低下，亟需自动化读数方案。

Method: 构建包含 1,848 张压力表图像（0–10 BAR）的数据集，通过网页抓取、合成生成与数据增强获得；实现并对比三种混合方案：OpenCV+Scikit-Image（传统图像处理）、OpenCV+ResNet18、OpenCV+VGG19。

Result: ResNet18 准确率达 97.3%，显著优于 VGG19（64.4%）与 Scikit-Image（63.8%）。

Conclusion: 残差学习架构在工业仪表数字化任务中有效，为 Industry 5.0 自动化应用提供可靠基础；但数据集规模有限且来源以合成/抓取为主，结论的泛化性仍需真实工业场景进一步验证。

Abstract: Analogue gauges remain prevalent in manufacturing industries for measuring physical quantities, yet manual monitoring by operators introduces human error and operational inefficiency. This research developed and evaluated an automated gauge reading system using computer vision and deep learning techniques. Three hybrid approaches were implemented and compared: OpenCV with Scikit-Image (traditional computer vision), OpenCV with ResNet18, and OpenCV with VGG19 (deep learning approaches). A comprehensive dataset of 1,848 pressure gauge images (0-10 BAR range) was curated through web scraping, synthetic generation, and augmentation techniques. The experimental results demonstrate that ResNet18 significantly outperforms the alternatives, achieving an accuracy of 97.3%, compared to VGG19's 64.4% and Scikit-Image's 63.8%. The study validates the effectiveness of residual learning architectures for industrial gauge digitization, providing a robust foundation for Industry 5.0 automation applications.

</details>


### [173] [Prism: Hierarchical Text Detection with Minimalist Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26254)
*Yuchen Su,Xuesheng Wang,Zhilong Ji,Jinfeng Bai,Shijian Lu,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出极简分层文本检测框架Prism，用强预训练骨干替代复杂颈部与重型检测头，仅通过单尺度特征上的并行轻量头与几何包含算法完成词/行/段三级检测，在多个基准上取得SOTA精度且推理效率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层文本检测方法为追求精度，依赖复杂的多尺度特征融合颈部和重型检测头，导致结构臃肿、推理效率低。作者认为充分预训练的骨干可替代这些外部模块，且层级结构的解析仅需简单的几何包含关系即可完成。

Method: Prism仅在单尺度骨干特征图上挂载并行轻量检测头，分别负责词、文本行、段落的多粒度检测；再通过高效的几何包含（geometric inclusion）算法重建结构层级。作者还系统对比了多种代表性轻量头范式，发现像素级回归在该设定下取得最优精度—效率权衡。

Result: 在多个公开基准上，简化后的架构达到SOTA：在CTW1500上相比LRANet推理速度快1.7倍且精度更高；在分层数据集HierText上PQ指标超出当前SOTA方法OmniParser V2逾6个点。

Conclusion: 分层文本检测无需复杂多尺度融合与重型检测头，强预训练骨干+并行轻量像素级回归头+几何包含解析即可同时获得SOTA精度与高推理效率，为极简高效的分层文本检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Hierarchical text detection aims to localize words, text lines, and paragraphs while parsing their structural relationships. It provides essential structural cues for visual text understanding. To maximize accuracy, existing methods typically rely on complex multi-scale feature fusion necks and heavy-weight heads, resulting in bloated architectures and low inference efficiency. In this paper, we argue that a sufficiently pre-trained backbone can effectively replace these sophisticated external modules, while simple geometric inclusion suffices for hierarchical text structural parsing. Thus, we propose Prism, a minimalist framework for hierarchical text detection. Prism performs multi-granularity detection by attaching parallel lightweight heads to a single-scale backbone feature map, covering words, text lines, and paragraphs, and reconstructs the structural hierarchy via an efficient geometric inclusion algorithm. In addition, we systematically compare representative lightweight head archetypes and show that pixel-level regression offers the best accuracy-efficiency trade-off in this setting. Experiments on multiple public benchmarks show that our significantly simplified architecture achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance with remarkable efficiency. Specifically, it runs $1.7\times$ faster than LRANet on CTW1500 while achieving higher accuracy, and surpasses the current SOTA method, OmniParser V2, by over 6 PQ points on the hierarchical HierText dataset. Code will be released soon.

</details>
